# Psalms: full Hebrew, English, and pronunciation drafts

All 150 chapters. Hebrew and English are preserved from the pinned source editions; pronunciation is machine-generated and unreviewed. These are not approved app entries. See TRANSLITERATION.md for the sound guide and limitations.

Hebrew: Tanach with Nikkud, citing tanach.us (source reports Public Domain). Pronunciation input: Tanach with Ta'amei Hamikra, same source. English: The Holy Scriptures: A New Translation (JPS 1917), source reports Public Domain. Original language, numbering and superscriptions are retained. Separate source qere forms inform the pronunciation.

## Psalm 1 — Two ways of living; delight in Torah

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.1) · Buckets: learning, wisdom, justice

### 1:1

אַשְׁרֵי־הָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר לֹא הָלַךְ בַּעֲצַת רְשָׁעִים וּבְדֶרֶךְ חַטָּאִים לֹא עָמָד וּבְמוֹשַׁב לֵצִים לֹא יָשָׁב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHSH-rey hah-EESH ah-SHEHR  LOH hah-LAHKH bah-ah-TSAHT re-shah-EEM oo-ve-DEH-rehkh khah-tah-EEM LOH ah-MAHD oo-ve-moh-SHAHV leh-TSEEM LOH yah-SHAHV

HAPPY IS the man that hath not walked in the counsel of the wicked, Nor stood in the way of sinners, nor sat in the seat of the scornful.

### 1:2

כִּי אִם בְּתוֹרַת יְהוָה חֶפְצוֹ וּבְתוֹרָתוֹ יֶהְגֶּה יוֹמָם וָלָיְלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE EEM be-toh-RAHT ah-doh-NAI khehf-TSOH oo-ve-toh-rah-TOH yeh-GEH yoh-MAHM vah-LAH-ye-lah

But his delight is in the law of the LORD; and in His law doth he meditate day and night.

### 1:3

וְהָיָה כְּעֵץ שָׁתוּל עַל־פַּלְגֵי מָיִם אֲשֶׁר פִּרְיוֹ יִתֵּן בְּעִתּוֹ וְעָלֵהוּ לֹא־יִבּוֹל וְכֹל אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲשֶׂה יַצְלִיחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hah-YAH ke-EHTS shah-TOOL ahl pahl-GEY MAH-yeem ah-SHEHR peer-YOH  yee-TEHN be-ee-TOH ve-ah-LEH-hoo loh yee-BOHL ve-KHOHL ah-shehr yah-ah-SEH yahts-LEE-ahkh

And he shall be like a tree planted by streams of water, that bringeth forth its fruit in its season, and whose leaf doth not wither; and in whatsoever he doeth he shall prosper.

### 1:4

לֹא־כֵן הָרְשָׁעִים כִּי אִם־כַּמֹּץ אֲ‍שֶׁר־תִּדְּפֶנּוּ רוּחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh KHEHN hah-re-shah-EEM KEE eem kah-MOHTS ah-shehr ‍tee-de-FEH-noo ROO-ahkh

Not so the wicked; but they are like the chaff which the wind driveth away.

### 1:5

עַל־כֵּן לֹא־יָקֻמוּ רְשָׁעִים בַּמִּשְׁפָּט וְחַטָּאִים בַּעֲדַת צַדִּיקִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl KEHN  loh yah-KOO-moo re-shah-EEM bah-meesh-PAHT ve-khah-tah-EEM bah-ah-DAHT tsah-dee-KEEM

Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment, nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.

### 1:6

כִּי־יוֹדֵעַ יְהוָה דֶּרֶךְ צַדִּיקִים וְדֶרֶךְ רְשָׁעִים תֹּאבֵד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee yoh-DEH-ah ah-doh-NAI DEH-rehkh tsah-dee-KEEM ve-DEH-rehkh re-shah-EEM toh-VEHD

For the LORD regardeth the way of the righteous; but the way of the wicked shall perish.

## Psalm 2 — Kingship and the nations

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.2) · Buckets: community, justice

### 2:1

לָמָּה רָגְשׁוּ גוֹיִם וּלְאֻמִּים יֶהְגּוּ־רִיק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-MAH rah-ge-SHOO goh-YEEM oo-le-oo-MEEM yeh-goo REEK

Why are the nations in an uproar? And why do the peoples mutter in vain?

### 2:2

יִתְיַצְּבוּ מַלְכֵי־אֶרֶץ וְרוֹזְנִים נוֹסְדוּ־יָחַד עַל־יְהוָה וְעַל־מְשִׁיחוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** YEET-yah-tse-VOO  mahl-khey EH-rehts ve-roh-ze-NEEM noh-se-doo YAH-khahd ahl ah-doh-NAI ve-ahl me-shee-KHOH

The kings of the earth stand up, And the rulers take counsel together, Against the LORD, and against His anointed:

### 2:3

נְנַתְּקָה אֶת־מוֹסְרוֹתֵימוֹ וְנַשְׁלִיכָה מִמֶּנּוּ עֲבֹתֵימוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-nah-te-KAH eht moh-se-roh-TEY-moh ve-nahsh-LEE-khah mee-MEH-noo ah-voh-TEY-moh

'Let us break their bands asunder, and cast away their cords from us.'

### 2:4

יוֹשֵׁב בַּשָּׁמַיִם יִשְׂחָק אֲדֹנָי יִלְעַג־לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-SHEHV bah-shah-MAH-yeem yees-KHAHK ah-doh-NAHY yeel-ahg LAH-moh

He that sitteth in heaven laugheth, the Lord hath them in derision.

### 2:5

אָז יְדַבֵּר אֵלֵימוֹ בְאַפּוֹ וּבַחֲרוֹנוֹ יְבַהֲלֵמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHZ ye-dah-BEHR eh-LEY-moh ve-ah-POH oo-vah-khah-roh-NOH ye-vah-hah-LEH-moh

Then will He speak unto them in His wrath, and affright them in His sore displeasure:

### 2:6

וַאֲנִי נָסַכְתִּי מַלְכִּי עַל־צִיּוֹן הַר־קָדְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE nah-SAHKH-tee mahl-KEE ahl tsee-YOHN hahr kohd-SHEE

'Truly it is I that have established My king upon Zion, My holy mountain.'

### 2:7

אֲסַפְּרָה אֶל חֹק יְהוָה אָמַר אֵלַי בְּנִי אַתָּה אֲנִי הַיּוֹם יְלִדְתִּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-sah-pe-RAH EHL KHOHK ah-doh-NAI ah-MAHR eh-LAHY be-NEE AH-tah ah-NEE hah-YOHM ye-leed-TEE-khah

I will tell of the decree: The LORD said unto me: 'Thou art My son, this day have I begotten thee.

### 2:8

שְׁאַל מִמֶּנִּי וְאֶתְּנָה גוֹיִם נַחֲלָתֶךָ וַאֲחֻזָּתְךָ אַפְסֵי־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-AHL mee-MEH-nee ve-eh-te-NAH goh-YEEM nah-khah-lah-TEH-khah vah-ah-khoo-zah-te-KHAH ahf-sey AH-rehts

Ask of Me, and I will give the nations for thine inheritance, and the ends of the earth for thy possession.

### 2:9

תְּרֹעֵם בְּשֵׁבֶט בַּרְזֶל כִּכְלִי יוֹצֵר תְּנַפְּצֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-roh-EHM be-SHEH-veht bahr-ZEHL keekh-LEE yoh-TSEHR te-nah-pe-TSEHM

Thou shalt break them with a rod of iron; Thou shalt dash them in pieces like a potter's vessel.'

### 2:10

וְעַתָּה מְלָכִים הַשְׂכִּילוּ הִוָּסְרוּ שֹׁפְטֵי אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH me-lah-KHEEM hahs-KEE-loo hee-vah-se-ROO SHOH-fe-tey AH-rehts

Now therefore, O ye kings, be wise; be admonished, ye judges of the earth.

### 2:11

עִבְדוּ אֶת־יְהוָה בְּיִרְאָה וְגִילוּ בִּרְעָדָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eev-DOO eht ah-doh-NAI be-yeer-AH ve-GEE-loo beer-ah-DAH

Serve the LORD with fear, and rejoice with trembling.

### 2:12

נַשְּׁקוּ־בַר פֶּן־יֶאֱנַף וְתֹאבְדוּ דֶרֶךְ כִּי־יִבְעַר כִּמְעַט אַפּוֹ אַשְׁרֵי כָּל־חוֹסֵי בוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-she-koo VAHR pehn yeh-eh-NAHF  ve-TOH-ve-doo DEH-rehkh kee yeev-AHR keem-AHT ah-POH ahsh-REY kohl KHOH-sey VOH

Do homage in purity, lest He be angry, and ye perish in the way, when suddenly His wrath is kindled. Happy are all they that take refuge in Him.

## Psalm 3 — Trust amid enemies

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.3) · Buckets: protection, wellness

### 3:1

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד בְּבָרְחוֹ מִפְּנֵי אַבְשָׁלוֹם בְּנוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED be-vah-re-KHOH mee-pe-NEY  ahv-shah-LOHM be-NOH

A Psalm of David, when he fled from Absalom his son.

### 3:2

יְהוָה מָה־רַבּוּ צָרָי רַבִּים קָמִים עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI mah rah-BOO tsah-RAHY rah-BEEM kah-MEEM ah-LAHY

LORD, how many are mine adversaries become! Many are they that rise up against me.

### 3:3

רַבִּים אֹמְרִים לְנַפְשִׁי אֵין יְשׁוּעָתָה לּוֹ בֵאלֹהִים סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BEEM oh-me-REEM le-nahf-SHEE EYN ye-shoo-AH-tah LOH veh-loh-HEEM SEH-lah

Many there are that say of my soul: 'There is no salvation for him in God.' Selah

### 3:4

וְאַתָּה יְהוָה מָגֵן בַּעֲדִי כְּבוֹדִי וּמֵרִים רֹאשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI mah-GEHN bah-ah-DEE ke-voh-DEE oo-meh-REEM roh-SHEE

But thou, O LORD, art a shield about me; My glory, and the lifter up of my head.

### 3:5

קוֹלִי אֶל־יְהוָה אֶקְרָא וַיַּעֲנֵנִי מֵהַר קָדְשׁוֹ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-LEE ehl ah-doh-NAI ehk-RAH vah-yah-ah-NEH-nee meh-HAHR kohd-SHOH SEH-lah

With my voice I call unto the LORD, and He answereth me out of His holy mountain. Selah

### 3:6

אֲנִי שָׁכַבְתִּי וָאִישָׁנָה הֱקִיצוֹתִי כִּי יְהוָה יִסְמְכֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-NEE shah-KHAHV-tee vah-ee-SHAH-nah heh-kee-TSOH-tee KEE ah-doh-NAI yees-me-KHEH-nee

I lay me down, and I sleep; I awake, for the LORD sustaineth me.

### 3:7

לֹא־אִירָא מֵרִבְבוֹת עָם אֲשֶׁר סָבִיב שָׁתוּ עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh ee-RAH meh-reev-VOHT AHM ah-SHEHR sah-VEEV SHAH-too ah-LAHY

I am not afraid of ten thousands of people, that have set themselves against me round about.

### 3:8

קוּמָה יְהוָה הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי אֱלֹהַי כִּי־הִכִּיתָ אֶת־כָּל־אֹיְבַי לֶחִי שִׁנֵּי רְשָׁעִים שִׁבַּרְתָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ah-doh-NAI  hoh-shee-EH-nee eh-loh-HAHY kee hee-KEE-tah eht kohl oh-ye-VAHY LEH-khee shee-NEY re-shah-EEM shee-BAHR-tah

Arise, O LORD; save me, O my God; for Thou hast smitten all mine enemies upon the cheek, Thou hast broken the teeth of the wicked.

### 3:9

לַיהוָה הַיְשׁוּעָה עַל־עַמְּךָ בִרְכָתֶךָ סֶּלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-ah-doh-NAI hah-ye-shoo-AH ahl ah-me-KHAH veer-khah-TEH-khah SEH-lah

Salvation belongeth unto the LORD; Thy blessing be upon Thy people. Selah

## Psalm 4 — Distress, trust, and resting in safety

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.4) · Buckets: wellness, sleep

### 4:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ בִּנְגִינוֹת מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh been-gee-NOHT meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; with string-music. A Psalm of David.

### 4:2

בְּקָרְאִי עֲנֵנִי אֱלֹהֵי צִדְקִי בַּצָּר הִרְחַבְתָּ לִּי חָנֵּנִי וּשְׁמַע תְּפִלָּתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-kah-re-EE ah-NEH-nee  eh-loh-HEY tseed-KEE bah-TSAHR heer-KHAHV-tah LEE khoh-NEH-nee oo-she-MAH te-fee-lah-TEE

Answer me when I call, O God of my righteousness, Thou who didst set me free when I was in distress; be gracious unto me, and hear my prayer.

### 4:3

בְּנֵי אִישׁ עַד־מֶה כְבוֹדִי לִכְלִמָּה תֶּאֱהָבוּן רִיק תְּבַקְשׁוּ כָזָב סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-NEY EESH ahd MEH khe-voh-DEE leekh-lee-MAH teh-eh-hah-VOON REEK te-vahk-SHOO khah-ZAHV SEH-lah

O ye sons of men, how long shall my glory be put to shame, in that ye love vanity, and seek after falsehood? Selah

### 4:4

וּדְעוּ כִּי־הִפְלָה יְהוָה חָסִיד לוֹ יְהוָה יִשְׁמַע בְּקָרְאִי אֵלָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-de-OO kee heef-LAH ah-doh-NAI khah-SEED LOH ah-doh-NAI yeesh-MAH be-kah-re-EE eh-LAHV

But know that the LORD hath set apart the godly man as His own; The LORD will hear when I call unto Him.

### 4:5

רִגְזוּ וְאַל־תֶּחֱטָאוּ אִמְרוּ בִלְבַבְכֶם עַל־מִשְׁכַּבְכֶם וְדֹמּוּ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** reeg-ZOO ve-ahl teh-kheh-TAH-oo eem-ROO veel-vahv-KHEHM ahl meesh-kahv-KHEHM ve-DOH-moo SEH-lah

Tremble, and sin not; commune with your own heart upon your bed, and be still. Selah

### 4:6

זִבְחוּ זִבְחֵי־צֶדֶק וּבִטְחוּ אֶל־יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zeev-KHOO zeev-khey TSEH-dehk oo-veet-KHOO ehl ah-doh-NAI

Offer the sacrifices of righteousness, and put your trust in the LORD.

### 4:7

רַבִּים אֹמְרִים מִי־יַרְאֵנוּ טוֹב נְסָה־עָלֵינוּ אוֹר פָּנֶיךָ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BEEM oh-me-REEM mee yahr-EH-NOO TOHV ne-sah ah-ley-NOO OHR pah-NEY-khah ah-doh-NAI

Many there are that say: 'Oh that we could see some good!' LORD, lift Thou up the light of Thy countenance upon us.

### 4:8

נָתַתָּה שִׂמְחָה בְלִבִּי מֵעֵת דְּגָנָם וְתִירוֹשָׁם רָבּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-TAH-tah seem-KHAH ve-lee-BEE meh-EHT de-gah-NAHM ve-tee-roh-SHAHM RAH-boo

Thou hast put gladness in my heart, more than when their corn and their wine increase.

### 4:9

בְּשָׁלוֹם יַחְדָּו אֶשְׁכְּבָה וְאִישָׁן כִּי־אַתָּה יְהוָה לְבָדָד לָבֶטַח תּוֹשִׁיבֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-shah-LOHM yahkh-DAHV ehsh-ke-VAH ve-ee-SHAHN kee ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI le-vah-DAHD lah-VEH-tahkh toh-shee-VEH-nee

In peace will I both lay me down and sleep; for Thou, LORD, makest me dwell alone in safety.

## Psalm 5 — A morning appeal for guidance and justice

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.5) · Buckets: wisdom, protection

### 5:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אֶל־הַנְּחִילוֹת מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ehl hah-ne-khee-LOHT meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; upon the Nehiloth. A Psalm of David.

### 5:2

אֲמָרַי הַאֲזִינָה יְהוָה בִּינָה הֲגִיגִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-mah-RAHY hah-ah-ZEE-nah  ah-doh-NAI BEE-nah hah-GEE-gee

Give ear to my words, O LORD, consider my meditation.

### 5:3

הַקְשִׁיבָה לְקוֹל שַׁוְעִי מַלְכִּי וֵאלֹהָי כִּי־אֵלֶיךָ אֶתְפַּלָּל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hahk-SHEE-vah  le-KOHL shahv-EE mahl-KEE veh-loh-HAHY kee eh-LEY-khah eht-pah-LAHL

Hearken unto the voice of my cry, my King, and my God; For unto Thee do I pray.

### 5:4

יְהוָה בֹּקֶר תִּשְׁמַע קוֹלִי בֹּקֶר אֶעֱרָךְ־לְךָ וַאֲצַפֶּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI boh-KEHR teesh-MAH koh-LEE BOH-kehr eh-eh-rahkh le-KHAH vah-ah-tsah-PEH

O LORD, in the morning shalt Thou hear my voice; In the morning will I order my prayer unto Thee, and will look forward.

### 5:5

כִּי לֹא אֵל־חָפֵץ רֶשַׁע אָתָּה לֹא יְגֻרְךָ רָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE  LOH ehl khah-FEHTS REH-shah  AH-tah LOH ye-goor-KHAH RAH

For Thou art not a God that hath pleasure in wickedness; Evil shall not sojourn with Thee.

### 5:6

לֹא־יִתְיַצְּבוּ הוֹלְלִים לְנֶגֶד עֵינֶיךָ שָׂנֵאתָ כָּל־פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh yeet-yah-tse-VOO hoh-le-LEEM le-NEH-gehd ey-NEY-khah sah-NEH-tah kohl POH-ah-ley AH-vehn

The boasters shall not stand in Thy sight; Thou hatest all workers of iniquity.

### 5:7

תְּאַבֵּד דֹּבְרֵי כָזָב אִישׁ־דָּמִים וּמִרְמָה יְתָעֵב יְהוָה׃

### 8:6

וַתְּחַסְּרֵהוּ מְּעַט מֵאֱלֹהִים וְכָבוֹד וְהָדָר תְּעַטְּרֵהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-te-khah-se-REH-hoo me-AHT meh-eh-loh-HEEM ve-khah-VOHD ve-hah-DAHR te-ah-te-REH-hoo

Yet Thou hast made him but little lower than the angels, And hast crowned him with glory and honour.

### 8:7

תַּמְשִׁילֵהוּ בְּמַעֲשֵׂי יָדֶיךָ כֹּל שַׁתָּה תַחַת־רַגְלָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tahm-shee-leh-HOO be-mah-ah-SEY yah-DEY-khah KOHL SHAH-tah tah-khaht rahg-LAHV

Thou hast made him to have dominion over the works of Thy hands; Thou hast put all things under His feet:

### 8:8

צֹנֶה וַאֲלָפִים כֻּלָּם וְגַם בַּהֲמוֹת שָׂדָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsoh-NEH vah-ah-lah-FEEM koo-LAHM ve-GAHM bah-hah-MOHT sah-DAHY

Sheep and oxen, all of them, Yea, and the beasts of the field;

### 8:9

צִפּוֹר שָׁמַיִם וּדְגֵי הַיָּם עֹבֵר אָרְחוֹת יַמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsee-POHR shah-mah-YEEM oo-de-GEY hah-YAHM oh-VEHR ohr-KHOHT yah-MEEM

The fowl of the air, and the fish of the sea; Whatsoever passeth through the paths of the seas.

### 8:10

יְהוָה אֲדֹנֵינוּ מָה־אַדִּיר שִׁמְךָ בְּכָל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI ah-doh-NEY-noo mah ah-DEER sheem-KHAH be-khohl hah-AH-rehts

O LORD, our Lord, How glorious is Thy name in all the earth!

## Psalm 9 — Thanksgiving and justice for the oppressed

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.9) · Buckets: gratitude, justice, health

### 9:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַלְמוּת לַבֵּן מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-tseh-AHKH ahl-MOOT lah-BEHN meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; upon Muthlabben. A Psalm of David.

### 9:2

אוֹדֶה יְהוָה בְּכָל־לִבִּי אֲסַפְּרָה כָּל־נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-DEH ah-doh-NAI be-khohl lee-BEE ah-sah-pe-RAH kohl neef-le-oh-TEY-khah

I will give thanks unto the LORD with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvellous works.

### 9:3

אֶשְׂמְחָה וְאֶעֶלְצָה בָךְ אֲזַמְּרָה שִׁמְךָ עֶלְיוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehs-me-KHAH ve-eh-ehl-TSAH VAHKH ah-zah-me-RAH sheem-KHAH ehl-YOHN

I will be glad and exult in Thee; I will sing praise to Thy name, O Most High:

### 9:4

בְּשׁוּב־אוֹיְבַי אָחוֹר יִכָּשְׁלוּ וְיֹאבְדוּ מִפָּנֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-shoov oh-ye-VAHY ah-KHOHR yee-kah-she-LOO ve-yoh-ve-DOO mee-pah-NEY-khah

When mine enemies are turned back, They stumble and perish at Thy presence;

### 9:5

כִּי־עָשִׂיתָ מִשְׁפָּטִי וְדִינִי יָשַׁבְתָּ לְכִסֵּא שׁוֹפֵט צֶדֶק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-see-TAH meesh-pah-TEE ve-dee-NEE yah-SHAHV-tah le-khee-SEH shoh-FEHT TSEH-dehk

For Thou hast maintained my right and my cause; Thou sattest upon the throne as the righteous Judge.

### 9:6

גָּעַרְתָּ גוֹיִם אִבַּדְתָּ רָשָׁע שְׁמָם מָחִיתָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-AHR-tah goh-YEEM ee-BAHD-tah rah-SHAH she-MAHM mah-KHEE-tah le-oh-LAHM vah-EHD

Thou hast rebuked the nations, Thou hast destroyed the wicked, Thou hast blotted out their name for ever and ever.

### 9:7

הָאוֹיֵב תַּמּוּ חֳרָבוֹת לָנֶצַח וְעָרִים נָתַשְׁתָּ אָבַד זִכְרָם הֵמָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-oh-YEHV  TAH-moo khoh-rah-VOHT lah-NEH-tsahkh ve-ah-REEM nah-TAHSH-tah ah-VAHD zeekh-RAHM HEH-mah

O thou enemy, the waste places are come to an end for ever; And the cities which thou didst uproot, Their very memorial is perished.

### 9:8

וַיהוָה לְעוֹלָם יֵשֵׁב כּוֹנֵן לַמִּשְׁפָּט כִּסְאוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-doh-NAI le-oh-LAHM yeh-SHEHV koh-NEHN lah-meesh-PAHT kees-OH

But the LORD is enthroned for ever; He hath established His throne for judgment.

### 9:9

וְהוּא יִשְׁפֹּט־תֵּבֵל בְּצֶדֶק יָדִין לְאֻמִּים בְּמֵישָׁרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-HOO yeesh-poht teh-VEHL be-TSEH-dehk yah-DEEN le-oo-MEEM be-mey-shah-REEM

And He will judge the world in righteousness, He will minister judgment to the peoples with equity.

### 9:10

וִיהִי יְהוָה מִשְׂגָּב לַדָּךְ מִשְׂגָּב לְעִתּוֹת בַּצָּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vee-HEE ah-doh-NAI mees-GAHV lah-DAHKH mees-GAHV le-ee-TOHT bah-tsah-RAH

The LORD also will be a high tower for the oppressed, A high tower in times of trouble;

### 9:11

וְיִבְטְחוּ בְךָ יוֹדְעֵי שְׁמֶךָ כִּי לֹא־עָזַבְתָּ דֹרְשֶׁיךָ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yeev-te-KHOO ve-KHAH yoh-de-EY she-MEH-khah KEE loh ah-ZAHV-tah doh-re-SHEY-khah ah-doh-NAI

And they that know Thy name will put their trust in Thee; For thou, LORD, hast not forsaken them that seek Thee.

### 9:12

זַמְּרוּ לַיהוָה יֹשֵׁב צִיּוֹן הַגִּידוּ בָעַמִּים עֲלִילוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-me-ROO lah-ah-doh-NAI yoh-SHEHV tsee-YOHN hah-GEE-doo vah-ah-MEEM ah-lee-loh-TAHV

Sing praises to the LORD, who dwelleth in Zion; Declare among the peoples His doings.

### 9:13

כִּי־דֹרֵשׁ דָּמִים אוֹתָם זָכָר לֹא־שָׁכַח צַעֲקַת עניים [עֲנָוִים׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** kee doh-REHSH dah-MEEM oh-TAHM zah-KHAHR loh shah-KHAHKH tsah-ah-KAHT ah-nah-VEEM

For He that avengeth blood hath remembered them; He hath not forgotten the cry of the humble.

Reading note: written עניים → read עֲנָוִֽים׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 9:14

חָנְנֵנִי יְהוָה רְאֵה עָנְיִי מִשֹּׂנְאָי מְרוֹמְמִי מִשַּׁעֲרֵי מָוֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-ne-NEH-nee ah-doh-NAI re-EH ohn-YEE mee-soh-ne-AHY me-roh-me-MEE mee-SHAH-ah-rey MAH-veht

Be gracious unto me, O LORD, Behold mine affliction at the hands of them that hate me; Thou that liftest me up from the gates of death;

### 9:15

לְמַעַן אֲסַפְּרָה כָּל־תְּהִלָּתֶיךָ בְּשַׁעֲרֵי בַת־צִיּוֹן אָגִילָה בִּישׁוּעָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-MAH-ahn ah-sah-pe-RAH kahl te-hee-lah-TEY-khah be-shah-ah-REY vaht tsee-YOHN ah-GEE-lah bee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah

That I may tell of all Thy praise in the gates of the daughter of Zion, That I may rejoice in Thy salvation.

### 9:16

טָבְעוּ גוֹיִם בְּשַׁחַת עָשׂוּ בְּרֶשֶׁת־זוּ טָמָנוּ נִלְכְּדָה רַגְלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-ve-OO goh-YEEM be-SHAH-khaht ah-SOO be-reh-sheht ZOO tah-MAH-noo neel-ke-DAH rahg-LAHM

The nations are sunk down in the pit that they made; In the net which they hid is their own foot taken.

### 9:17

נוֹדַע יְהוָה מִשְׁפָּט עָשָׂה בְּפֹעַל כַּפָּיו נוֹקֵשׁ רָשָׁע הִגָּיוֹן סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** NOH-DAH  ah-doh-NAI meesh-PAHT ah-SAH be-FOH-ahl kah-PAHV noh-KEHSH rah-SHAH hee-gah-YOHN SEH-lah

The LORD hath made Himself known, He hath executed judgment, The wicked is snared in the work of his own hands. Higgaion. Selah

### 9:18

יָשׁוּבוּ רְשָׁעִים לִשְׁאוֹלָה כָּל־גּוֹיִם שְׁכֵחֵי אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SHOO-voo re-shah-EEM leesh-OH-lah kohl goh-YEEM she-kheh-KHEY eh-loh-HEEM

The wicked shall return to the nether-world, Even all the nations that forget God.

### 9:19

כִּי לֹא לָנֶצַח יִשָּׁכַח אֶבְיוֹן תִּקְוַת ענוים [עֲנִיִּים] תֹּאבַד לָעַד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE LOH lah-neh-TSAHKH yee-shah-KHAHKH ehv-YOHN teek-VAHT ah-nee-YEEM toh-VAHD lah-AHD

For the needy shall not alway be forgotten, Nor the expectation of the poor perish for ever.

Reading note: written ענוים → read עֲ֝נִיִּ֗ים. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 9:20

קוּמָה יְהוָה אַל־יָעֹז אֱנוֹשׁ יִשָּׁפְטוּ גוֹיִם עַל־פָּנֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ah-doh-NAI ahl yah-OHZ eh-NOHSH yee-shah-fe-TOO goh-YEEM ahl pah-NEY-khah

Arise, O LORD, let not man prevail; Let the nations be judged in Thy sight.

### 9:21

שִׁיתָה יְהוָה מוֹרָה לָהֶם יֵדְעוּ גוֹיִם אֱנוֹשׁ הֵמָּה סֶּלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-TAH ah-doh-NAI  moh-RAH lah-HEHM yeh-de-OO goh-YEEM eh-NOHSH HEH-mah SEH-lah

Set terror over them, O LORD; Let the nations know they are but men. Selah

## Psalm 10 — A plea for those threatened by the wicked

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.10) · Buckets: justice, protection

### 10:1

לָמָה יְהוָה תַּעֲמֹד בְּרָחוֹק תַּעְלִים לְעִתּוֹת בַּצָּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-MAH ah-doh-NAI tah-ah-MOHD be-rah-KHOHK tah-LEEM le-ee-TOHT bah-tsah-RAH

Why standest Thou afar off, O LORD? Why hidest Thou Thyself in times of trouble?

### 10:2

בְּגַאֲוַת רָשָׁע יִדְלַק עָנִי יִתָּפְשׂוּ בִּמְזִמּוֹת זוּ חָשָׁבוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-gah-ah-VAHT rah-SHAH yeed-LAHK ah-NEE yee-tah-fe-SOO  beem-zee-MOHT ZOO khah-SHAH-voo

Through the pride of the wicked the poor is hotly pursued, They are taken in the devices that they have imagined.

### 10:3

כִּי־הִלֵּל רָשָׁע עַל־תַּאֲוַת נַפְשׁוֹ וּבֹצֵעַ בֵּרֵךְ נִאֵץ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hee-LEHL rah-SHAH ahl tah-ah-VAHT nahf-SHOH oo-voh-TSEH-ah beh-REHKH nee-EHTS  ah-doh-NAI

For the wicked boasteth of his heart's desire, And the covetous vaunteth himself, though he contemn the LORD.

### 10:4

רָשָׁע כְּגֹבַהּ אַפּוֹ בַּל־יִדְרֹשׁ אֵין אֱלֹהִים כָּל־מְזִמּוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-SHAH ke-GOH-vahh ah-POH bahl yeed-ROHSH EYN eh-loh-HEEM kohl me-zee-moh-TAHV

The wicked, in the pride of his countenance [, saith]: 'He will not require'; All his thoughts are: 'There is no God.'

### 10:5

יָחִילוּ דרכו [דְרָכָיו ] בְּכָל־עֵת מָרוֹם מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ מִנֶּגְדּוֹ כָּל־צוֹרְרָיו יָפִיחַ בָּהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-KHEE-loo de-rah-KHAHV  be-khohl EHT mah-ROHM meesh-pah-tey-KHAH mee-nehg-DOH kohl tsoh-re-RAHV yah-FEE-ahkh bah-HEHM

His ways prosper at all times; Thy judgments are far above out of his sight; As for all his adversaries, he puffeth at them.

Reading note: written דרכו → read דְרָכָ֨יו ׀. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 10:6

אָמַר בְּלִבּוֹ בַּל־אֶמּוֹט לְדֹר וָדֹר אֲשֶׁר לֹא־בְרָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR be-lee-BOH bahl eh-MOHT le-DOHR vah-DOHR ah-SHEHR loh ve-RAH

He saith in his heart: 'I shall not be moved, I who to all generations shall not be in adversity.'

### 10:7

אָלָה פִּיהוּ מָלֵא וּמִרְמוֹת וָתֹךְ תַּחַת לְשׁוֹנוֹ עָמָל וָאָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-LAH PEE-hoo mah-LEH oo-meer-MOHT vah-TOHKH TAH-khaht le-shoh-NOH ah-MAHL vah-AH-vehn

His mouth is full of cursing and deceit and oppression; Under his tongue is mischief and iniquity.

### 10:8

יֵשֵׁב בְּמַאְרַב חֲצֵרִים בַּמִּסְתָּרִים יַהֲרֹג נָקִי עֵינָיו לְחֵלְכָה יִצְפֹּנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-SHEHV  be-mah-RAHV khah-tseh-REEM bah-mees-tah-REEM yah-hah-ROHG nah-KEE ey-NAHV le-kheh-le-KHAH yeets-POH-noo

He sitteth in the lurking-places of the villages; In secret places doth he slay the innocent; His eyes are on the watch for the helpless.

### 10:9

יֶאֱרֹב בַּמִּסְתָּר כְּאַרְיֵה בְסֻכֹּה יֶאֱרֹב לַחֲטוֹף עָנִי יַחְטֹף עָנִי בְּמָשְׁכוֹ בְרִשְׁתּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-eh-ROHV bah-mees-TAHR  ke-ahr-YEH ve-soo-KOHH yeh-eh-ROHV lah-khah-TOHF ah-NEE yahkh-TOHF ah-NEE be-mah-she-KHOH ve-reesh-TOH

He lieth in wait in a secret place as a lion in his lair, He lieth in wait to catch the poor; He doth catch the poor, when he draweth him up in his net.

### 10:10

ודכה [יִדְכֶּה] יָשֹׁחַ וְנָפַל בַּעֲצוּמָיו חלכאים [חֵיל] [כָּאִים׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** yeed-KEH yah-SHOH-ahkh ve-nah-FAHL bah-ah-tsoo-MAHV KHEYL kah-EEM

He croucheth, he boweth down, And the helpless fall into his mighty claws.

Reading note: written ודכה → read יִדְכֶּ֥ה; written חלכאים → read חֵ֣יל כָּאִֽים׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 10:11

אָמַר בְּלִבּוֹ שָׁכַח אֵל הִסְתִּיר פָּנָיו בַּל־רָאָה לָנֶצַח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR be-lee-BOH SHAH-khahkh EHL hees-TEER pah-NAHV bahl rah-AH lah-NEH-tsahkh

He hath said in his heart: 'God hath forgotten; He hideth His face; He will never see.'

### 10:12

קוּמָה יְהוָה אֵל נְשָׂא יָדֶךָ אַל־תִּשְׁכַּח עניים [עֲנָוִים׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ah-doh-NAI EHL ne-SAH yah-DEH-khah ahl teesh-KAHKH ah-nah-VEEM

Arise, O LORD; O God, lift up Thy hand; Forget not the humble.

Reading note: written עניים → read עֲנָוִֽים׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 10:13

עַל־מֶה נִאֵץ רָשָׁע אֱלֹהִים אָמַר בְּלִבּוֹ לֹא תִּדְרֹשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl MEH  nee-EHTS rah-SHAH  eh-loh-HEEM ah-MAHR be-lee-BOH LOH teed-ROHSH

Wherefore doth the wicked contemn God, And say in his heart: 'Thou wilt not require'?

### 10:14

רָאִתָה כִּי־אַתָּה עָמָל וָכַעַס תַּבִּיט לָתֵת בְּיָדֶךָ עָלֶיךָ יַעֲזֹב חֵלֶכָה יָתוֹם אַתָּה הָיִיתָ עוֹזֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-EE-tah kee ah-TAH  ah-MAHL vah-KHAH-ahs  tah-BEET lah-TEHT be-yah-DEH-khah ah-ley-KHAH yah-ah-ZOHV kheh-LEH-khah yah-TOHM ah-TAH  hah-YEE-tah oh-ZEHR

Thou hast seen; for Thou beholdest trouble and vexation, to requite them with Thy hand; Unto Thee the helpless committeth himself; Thou hast been the helper of the fatherless.

### 10:15

שְׁבֹר זְרוֹעַ רָשָׁע וָרָע תִּדְרוֹשׁ־רִשְׁעוֹ בַל־תִּמְצָא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-VOHR ze-ROH-ah rah-SHAH vah-RAH tee-de-rohsh reesh-OH vahl teem-TSAH

Break Thou the arm of the wicked; And as for the evil man, search out his wickedness, till none be found.

### 10:16

יְהוָה מֶלֶךְ עוֹלָם וָעֶד אָבְדוּ גוֹיִם מֵאַרְצוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI meh-LEHKH oh-LAHM vah-EHD ah-ve-DOO goh-YEEM meh-ahr-TSOH

The LORD is King for ever and ever; The nations are perished out of His land.

### 10:17

### 10:18

### 12:1

יִרְבּוּ עַצְּבוֹתָם אַחֵר מָהָרוּ בַּל־אַסִּיךְ נִסְכֵּיהֶם מִדָּם וּבַל־אֶשָּׂא אֶת־שְׁמוֹתָם עַל־שְׂפָתָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeer-BOO ah-tse-voh-TAHM ah-KHEHR mah-HAH-roo bahl ah-SEEKH nees-key-HEHM mee-DAHM oo-vahl eh-SAH eht she-moh-TAHM ahl se-fah-TAHY

Let the idols of them be multiplied that make suit unto another; Their drink-offerings of blood will I not offer, Nor take their names upon my lips.

### 16:5

יְהוָה מְנָת־חֶלְקִי וְכוֹסִי אַתָּה תּוֹמִיךְ גּוֹרָלִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI me-naht khehl-KEE ve-khoh-SEE ah-TAH toh-MEEKH goh-rah-LEE

O LORD, the portion of mine inheritance and of my cup, Thou maintainest my lot.

### 16:6

חֲבָלִים נָפְלוּ־לִי בַּנְּעִמִים אַף־נַחֲלָת שָׁפְרָה עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-vah-LEEM nah-fe-loo LEE bah-ne-ee-MEEM ahf nah-khah-LAHT shah-fe-RAH ah-LAHY

The lines are fallen unto me in pleasant places; Yea, I have a goodly heritage.

### 16:7

אֲבָרֵךְ אֶת־יְהוָה אֲשֶׁר יְעָצָנִי אַף־לֵילוֹת יִסְּרוּנִי כִלְיוֹתָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-vah-REHKH eht ah-doh-NAI ah-SHEHR ye-ah-TSAH-nee ahf ley-LOHT yee-se-ROO-nee kheel-yoh-TAHY

I will bless the LORD, who hath given me counsel; Yea, in the night seasons my reins instruct me.

### 16:8

שִׁוִּיתִי יְהוָה לְנֶגְדִּי תָמִיד כִּי מִימִינִי בַּל־אֶמּוֹט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-VEE-tee ah-doh-NAI le-nehg-DEE tah-MEED KEE mee-mee-NEE bahl eh-MOHT

I have set the LORD always before me; Surely He is at my right hand, I shall not be moved.

### 16:9

לָכֵן שָׂמַח לִבִּי וַיָּגֶל כְּבוֹדִי אַף־בְּשָׂרִי יִשְׁכֹּן לָבֶטַח׃

### 16:10

כִּי לֹא־תַעֲזֹב נַפְשִׁי לִשְׁאוֹל לֹא־תִתֵּן חֲסִידְךָ לִרְאוֹת שָׁחַת׃

### 16:11

תּוֹדִיעֵנִי אֹרַח חַיִּים שֹׂבַע שְׂמָחוֹת אֶת־פָּנֶיךָ נְעִמוֹת בִּימִינְךָ נֶצַח׃

## Psalm 17 — An appeal for protection and vindication

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.17) · Buckets: protection, justice, health

### 17:1

תְּפִלָּה לְדָוִד שִׁמְעָה יְהוָה צֶדֶק הַקְשִׁיבָה רִנָּתִי הַאֲזִינָה תְפִלָּתִי בְּלֹא שִׂפְתֵי מִרְמָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-fee-LAH le-dah-VEED sheem-AH ah-doh-NAI  TSEH-dehk hahk-SHEE-vah ree-nah-TEE hah-ah-ZEE-nah te-fee-lah-TEE be-LOH seef-TEY meer-MAH

A Prayer of David. Hear the right, O LORD, attend unto my cry; Give ear unto my prayer from lips without deceit.

### 17:2

מִלְּפָנֶיךָ מִשְׁפָּטִי יֵצֵא עֵינֶיךָ תֶּחֱזֶינָה מֵישָׁרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-le-fah-ney-KHAH meesh-pah-TEE yeh-TSEH ey-NEY-khah teh-kheh-ZEY-nah mey-shah-REEM

Let my judgment come forth from Thy presence; Let Thine eyes behold equity.

### 17:3

בָּחַנְתָּ לִבִּי פָּקַדְתָּ לַּיְלָה צְרַפְתַּנִי בַל־תִּמְצָא זַמֹּתִי בַּל־יַעֲבָר־פִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-KHAHN-tah lee-BEE  pah-KAHD-tah LAHY-lah tse-rahf-TAH-nee vahl teem-TSAH zah-moh-TEE bahl yah-ah-vahr PEE

Thou hast tried my heart, Thou hast visited it in the night; Thou hast tested me, and Thou findest not That I had a thought which should not pass my mouth.

### 17:4

לִפְעֻלּוֹת אָדָם בִּדְבַר שְׂפָתֶיךָ אֲנִי שָׁמַרְתִּי אָרְחוֹת פָּרִיץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leef-oo-LOHT ah-DAHM beed-VAHR se-fah-TEY-khah ah-NEE shah-MAHR-tee ohr-KHOHT pah-REETS

As for the doings of men, by the word of Thy lips I have kept me from the ways of the violent.

### 17:5

תָּמֹךְ אֲשֻׁרַי בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתֶיךָ בַּל־נָמוֹטּוּ פְעָמָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-MOHKH ah-shoo-RAHY be-mah-ge-loh-TEY-khah bahl nah-MOH-too fe-ah-MAHY

My steps have held fast to Thy paths, My feet have not slipped.

### 17:6

אֲנִי־קְרָאתִיךָ כִי־תַעֲנֵנִי אֵל הַט־אָזְנְךָ לִי שְׁמַע אִמְרָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-nee ke-rah-TEE-khah khee tah-ah-NEH-nee EHL haht ohz-ne-KHAH LEE she-MAH eem-rah-TEE

As for me, I call upon Thee, for Thou wilt answer me, O God; Incline Thine ear unto me, hear my speech.

### 17:7

הַפְלֵה חֲסָדֶיךָ מוֹשִׁיעַ חוֹסִים מִמִּתְקוֹמְמִים בִּימִינֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hahf-LEH khah-sah-dey-KHAH moh-SHEE-ah khoh-SEEM mee-meet-koh-me-MEEM bee-mee-NEH-khah

Make passing great Thy mercies, O Thou that savest by Thy right hand From assailants them that take refuge in Thee.

### 17:8

שָׁמְרֵנִי כְּאִישׁוֹן בַּת־עָיִן בְּצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ תַּסְתִּירֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-me-reh-NEE ke-ee-SHOHN baht AH-yeen be-TSEHL ke-nah-FEY-khah tahs-tee-REH-nee

Keep me as the apple of the eye, Hide me in the shadow of Thy wings,

### 17:9

מִפְּנֵי רְשָׁעִים זוּ שַׁדּוּנִי אֹיְבַי בְּנֶפֶשׁ יַקִּיפוּ עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-pe-NEY re-shah-EEM ZOO shah-DOO-nee oh-ye-VAHY be-NEH-fehsh yah-KEE-foo ah-LAHY

From the wicked that oppress, My deadly enemies, that compass me about.

### 17:10

**Pronunciation draft:** khehl-BAH-moh sah-ge-ROO pee-MOH dee-be-ROO ve-geh-OOT

Their gross heart they have shut tight, With their mouth they speak proudly.

### 17:11

אַשֻּׁרֵינוּ עַתָּה סבבוני [סְבָבוּנוּ] עֵינֵיהֶם יָשִׁיתוּ לִנְטוֹת בָּאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shoo-rey-NOO ah-TAH se-vah-VOO-noo ey-ney-HEHM yah-SHEE-too leen-TOHT bah-AH-rehts

At our every step they have now encompassed us; They set their eyes to cast us down to the earth.

Reading note: written סבבוני → read סְבָב֑וּנוּ. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 17:12

דִּמְיֹנוֹ כְּאַרְיֵה יִכְסוֹף לִטְרוֹף וְכִכְפִיר יֹשֵׁב בְּמִסְתָּרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deem-yoh-NOH ke-ahr-YEH yeekh-SOHF leet-ROHF ve-kheekh-FEER yoh-SHEHV be-mees-tah-REEM

He is like a lion that is eager to tear in pieces, And like a young lion lurking in secret places.

### 17:13

קוּמָה יְהוָה קַדְּמָה פָנָיו הַכְרִיעֵהוּ פַּלְּטָה נַפְשִׁי מֵרָשָׁע חַרְבֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ah-doh-NAI kah-de-MAH fah-NAHV hahkh-ree-EH-hoo pah-le-TAH nahf-SHEE meh-rah-SHAH khahr-BEH-khah

Arise, O LORD, confront him, cast him down; Deliver my soul from the wicked, by Thy sword;

### 17:14

מִמְתִים יָדְךָ יְהוָה מִמְתִים מֵחֶלֶד חֶלְקָם בַּחַיִּים וצפינך [וּצְפוּנְךָ] תְּמַלֵּא בִטְנָם יִשְׂבְּעוּ בָנִים וְהִנִּיחוּ יִתְרָם לְעוֹלְלֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-me-TEEM yah-de-KHAH  ah-doh-NAI mee-me-TEEM meh-KHEH-lehd khehl-KAHM bah-khah-YEEM oo-tse-foo-ne-KHAH te-mah-LEH veet-NAHM yees-be-OO vah-NEEM ve-hee-NEE-khoo yeet-RAHM le-oh-le-ley-HEHM

From men, by Thy hand, O LORD, From men of the world, whose portion is in this life, And whose belly Thou fillest with Thy treasure; Who have children in plenty, And leave their abundance to their babes.

Reading note: written וצפינך → read וּֽצְפוּנְךָ֮. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 17:15

אֲנִי בְּצֶדֶק אֶחֱזֶה פָנֶיךָ אֶשְׂבְּעָה בְהָקִיץ תְּמוּנָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-NEE be-tseh-DEHK eh-kheh-ZEH fah-NEY-khah ehs-be-AH ve-hah-KEETS te-moo-nah-TEH-khah

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YEH-rah-OO  ah-FEE-key MAH-yeem vah-yee-gah-LOO moh-se-DOHT teh-VEHL mee-gah-ah-RAH-te-KHAH ah-doh-NAI mee-neesh-MAHT ROO-ahkh ah-PEH-khah

### 18:17

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-tsee-EH-nee lah-mehr-KHAHV ye-khah-le-TSEH-nee KEE KHAH-fehts BEE

### 18:45

**Pronunciation draft:** le-SHEH-mah oh-ZEHN yee-SHAH-me-oo LEE be-ney neh-KHAHR ye-khah-khah-shoo LEE

### 18:48

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-EHL hah-noh-TEHN ne-kah-MOHT LEE vah-yahd-BEHR ah-MEEM tahkh-TAHY

### 18:49

**Pronunciation draft:** me-fah-le-TEE meh-oh-ye-VAHY AHF meen kah-MAHY te-roh-me-MEH-nee meh-EESH khah-MAHS tah-tsee-LEH-nee

## Psalm 19 — Creation, Torah, and acceptable words

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.19) · Buckets: nature, learning, repentance

### 19:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

### 19:2

הַשָּׁמַיִם מְסַפְּרִים כְּבוֹד־אֵל וּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו מַגִּיד הָרָקִיעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-shah-MAH-yeem me-sah-pe-REEM ke-vohd EHL oo-mah-ah-SEH yah-DAHV mah-GEED hah-rah-KEE-ah

The heavens declare the glory of God, and the firmament showeth His handiwork;

### 19:3

יוֹם לְיוֹם יַבִּיעַ אֹמֶר וְלַיְלָה לְּלַיְלָה יְחַוֶּה־דָּעַת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** YOHM le-YOHM yah-BEE-ah OH-mehr ve-LAHY-lah le-LAHY-lah ye-khah-veh DAH-aht

Day unto day uttereth speech, and night unto night revealeth knowledge;

### 19:4

אֵין־אֹמֶר וְאֵין דְּבָרִים בְּלִי נִשְׁמָע קוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eyn oh-MEHR ve-EYN de-vah-REEM be-LEE neesh-MAH koh-LAHM

There is no speech, there are no words, neither is their voice heard.

### 19:5

בְּכָל־הָאָרֶץ יָצָא קַוָּם וּבִקְצֵה תֵבֵל מִלֵּיהֶם לַשֶּׁמֶשׁ שָׂם־אֹהֶל בָּהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khohl hah-AH-rehts  yah-TSAH kah-VAHM oo-veek-TSEH teh-VEHL mee-ley-HEHM lah-SHEH-mehsh sahm OH-hehl bah-HEHM

Their line is gone out through all the earth, and their words to the end of the world. In them hath He set a tent for the sun,

### 19:6

וְהוּא כְּחָתָן יֹצֵא מֵחֻפָּתוֹ יָשִׂישׂ כְּגִבּוֹר לָרוּץ אֹרַח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-HOO ke-khah-TAHN yoh-TSEH meh-khoo-pah-TOH yah-SEES ke-gee-BOHR lah-ROOTS OH-rahkh

Which is as a bridegroom coming out of his chamber, and rejoiceth as a strong man to run his course.

### 19:7

מִקְצֵה הַשָּׁמַיִם מוֹצָאוֹ וּתְקוּפָתוֹ עַל־קְצוֹתָם וְאֵין נִסְתָּר מֵחַמָּתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meek-TSEH hah-shah-MAH-yeem  moh-tsah-OH oo-te-koo-fah-TOH ahl ke-tsoh-TAHM ve-EYN nees-TAHR MEH-khah-mah-toh

His going forth is from the end of the heaven, and his circuit unto the ends of it; and there is nothing hid from the heat thereof.

### 19:8

תּוֹרַת יְהוָה תְּמִימָה מְשִׁיבַת נָפֶשׁ עֵדוּת יְהוָה נֶאֱמָנָה מַחְכִּימַת פֶּתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** toh-RAHT ah-doh-NAI te-mee-MAH me-SHEE-vaht NAH-fehsh eh-DOOT ah-doh-NAI neh-eh-mah-NAH mahkh-KEE-maht PEH-tee

The law of the LORD is perfect, restoring the soul; the testimony of the LORD is sure, making wise the simple. .

### 19:9

פִּקּוּדֵי יְהוָה יְשָׁרִים מְשַׂמְּחֵי־לֵב מִצְוַת יְהוָה בָּרָה מְאִירַת עֵינָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pee-koo-DEY ah-doh-NAI ye-shah-REEM me-sah-me-khey LEHV meets-VAHT ah-doh-NAI bah-RAH me-ee-RAHT ey-NAH-yeem

The precepts of the LORD are right, rejoicing the heart; the commandment of the LORD is pure, enlightening the eyes.

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

## Psalm 22 — Abandonment, suffering, and eventual praise

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.22) · Buckets: wellness, hope, health

### 22:1

For the Leader; upon Aijeleth ha-Shahar. A Psalm of David.

### 22:2

אֵלִי אֵלִי לָמָה עֲזַבְתָּנִי רָחוֹק מִישׁוּעָתִי דִּבְרֵי שַׁאֲגָתִי׃

### 22:4

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH kah-DOHSH yoh-SHEHV te-hee-LOHT yees-rah-EHL

### 22:5

בְּךָ בָּטְחוּ אֲבֹתֵינוּ בָּטְחוּ וַתְּפַלְּטֵמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-KHAH bah-te-KHOO ah-voh-TEY-noo bah-te-KHOO vah-te-fah-le-TEH-moh

### 22:6

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-LEY-khah zah-ah-KOO ve-neem-LAH-too be-KHAH vah-te-KHOO ve-loh VOH-shoo

### 22:7

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-noh-KHEE toh-LAH-aht ve-loh EESH khehr-PAHT ah-DAHM oo-ve-ZOOY AHM

### 22:8

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl roh-AHY yahl-EE-goo LEE yahf-TEE-roo ve-sah-FAH yah-NEE-oo ROHSH

### 22:9

גֹּל אֶל־יְהוָה יְפַלְּטֵהוּ יַצִּילֵהוּ כִּי חָפֵץ בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** GOHL ehl ah-doh-NAI ye-fah-le-TEH-hoo yah-tsee-LEH-hoo KEE KHAH-fehts BOH

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ley-KHAH hah-she-LAHKH-tee meh-RAH-khehm mee-BEH-tehn ee-MEE EH-lee AH-tah

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-TSOO ah-LAHY pee-HEHM ahr-YEH toh-REHF ve-shoh-EHG

## Psalm 26 — Integrity and love of God's house

### 26:1

לְדָוִד שָׁפְטֵנִי יְהוָה כִּי־אֲנִי בְּתֻמִּי הָלַכְתִּי וּבַיהוָה בָּטַחְתִּי לֹא אֶמְעָד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED  shah-fe-TEH-nee ah-doh-NAI kee ah-NEE be-too-MEE hah-LAHKH-tee oo-ah-doh-NAI bah-TAHKH-tee LOH ehm-AHD

[A Psalm] of David. Judge me, O LORD, for I have walked in mine integrity, and I have trusted in the LORD without wavering.

Examine me, O LORD, and try me; test my reins and my heart.

### 26:3

For Thy mercy is before mine eyes; and I have walked in Thy truth.

### 26:4

**Pronunciation draft:** loh yah-shahv-TEE eem me-tey SHAHV ve-EEM nah-ah-lah-MEEM LOH ah-VOH

### 26:5

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-neh-TEE ke-HAHL me-reh-EEM ve-eem re-shah-EEM LOH eh-SHEHV

### 26:6

**Pronunciation draft:** ehr-KHAHTS be-nee-kah-YOHN kah-PAHY vah-ah-soh-ve-VAH eht meez-bah-khah-KHAH ah-doh-NAI

I will wash my hands in innocency; so will I compass Thine altar, O LORD,

### 26:7

**Pronunciation draft:** lahsh-mee-AH be-KOHL toh-DAH oo-le-sah-PEHR kohl neef-le-oh-TEY-khah

That I may make the voice of thanksgiving to be heard, and tell of all Thy wondrous works.

### 26:8

יְהוָה אָהַבְתִּי מְעוֹן בֵּיתֶךָ וּמְקוֹם מִשְׁכַּן כְּבוֹדֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI ah-hahv-TEE me-OHN bey-TEH-khah oo-me-KOHM meesh-KAHN ke-voh-DEH-khah

### 26:9

### 27:1

### 27:6

וְעַתָּה יָרוּם רֹאשִׁי עַל אֹיְבַי סְבִיבוֹתַי וְאֶזְבְּחָה בְאָהֳלוֹ זִבְחֵי תְרוּעָה אָשִׁירָה וַאֲזַמְּרָה לַיהוָה׃

### 27:10

## Psalm 28 — A plea for help and blessing for the people

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.28) · Buckets: protection, community, health

### 28:1

לְדָוִד אֵלֶיךָ יְהוָה אֶקְרָא צוּרִי אַל־תֶּחֱרַשׁ מִמֶּנִּי פֶּן־תֶּחֱשֶׁה מִמֶּנִּי וְנִמְשַׁלְתִּי עִם־יוֹרְדֵי בוֹר׃

כִּי לֹא יָבִינוּ אֶל־פְּעֻלֹּת יְהוָה וְאֶל־מַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו יֶהֶרְסֵם וְלֹא יִבְנֵם׃

## Psalm 31 — Refuge amid distress and betrayal

### 31:9

וְלֹא הִסְגַּרְתַּנִי בְּיַד־אוֹיֵב הֶעֱמַדְתָּ בַמֶּרְחָב רַגְלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-LOH hees-gahr-tah-NEE be-yahd oh-YEHV heh-eh-MAHD-tah vah-mehr-KHAHV rahg-LAHY

And Thou hast not given me over into the hand of the enemy; Thou hast set my feet in a broad place.

### 31:10

חָנֵּנִי יְהוָה כִּי צַר־לִי עָשְׁשָׁה בְכַעַס עֵינִי נַפְשִׁי וּבִטְנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khoh-NEH-nee ah-doh-NAI KEE TSAHR LEE ah-she-SHAH ve-KHAH-ahs ey-NEE nahf-SHEE oo-veet-NEE

Be gracious unto me, O LORD, for I am in distress; Mine eye wasteth away with vexation, yea, my soul and my body.

### 31:11

כִּי כָלוּ בְיָגוֹן חַיַּי וּשְׁנוֹתַי בַּאֲנָחָה כָּשַׁל בַּעֲוֺנִי כֹחִי וַעֲצָמַי עָשֵׁשׁוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE khah-LOO ve-yah-GOHN khah-YAHY oo-she-noh-TAHY bah-ah-nah-KHAH kah-SHAHL bah-ah-voh-NEE khoh-KHEE vah-ah-tsah-MAHY ah-SHEH-shoo

For my life is spent in sorrow, and my years in sighing; My strength faileth because of mine iniquity, and my bones are wasted away.

### 31:12

מִכָּל־צֹרְרַי הָיִיתִי חֶרְפָּה וְלִשֲׁכֵנַי מְאֹד וּפַחַד לִמְיֻדָּעָי רֹאַי בַּחוּץ נָדְדוּ מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-kohl tsoh-re-RAHY hah-YEE-tee khehr-PAH ve-lee-shah-kheh-NAHY  me-OHD oo-FAH-khahd lee-me-yoo-dah-AHY roh-AHY bah-KHOOTS nah-de-DOO mee-MEH-nee

Because of all mine adversaries I am become a reproach, Yea, unto my neighbours exceedingly, and a dread to mine acquaintance; They that see me without flee from me.

### 31:13

נִשְׁכַּחְתִּי כְּמֵת מִלֵּב הָיִיתִי כִּכְלִי אֹבֵד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neesh-kahkh-TEE ke-MEHT mee-LEHV hah-YEE-tee keekh-LEE oh-VEHD

I am forgotten as a dead man out of mind; I am like a useless vessel.

### 31:14

כִּי שָׁמַעְתִּי דִּבַּת רַבִּים מָגוֹר מִסָּבִיב בְּהִוָּסְדָם יַחַד עָלַי לָקַחַת נַפְשִׁי זָמָמוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE shah-MAH-tee  dee-BAHT rah-BEEM mah-GOHR mee-sah-VEEV be-hee-vah-se-DAHM YAH-khahd ah-LAHY lah-KAH-khaht nahf-SHEE zah-MAH-moo

For I have heard the whispering of many, Terror on every side; While they took counsel together against me, They devised to take away my life.

### 31:15

וַאֲנִי עָלֶיךָ בָטַחְתִּי יְהוָה אָמַרְתִּי אֱלֹהַי אָתָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  ah-LEY-khah vah-TAHKH-tee ah-doh-NAI ah-MAHR-tee eh-loh-HAHY AH-tah

But as for me, I have trusted in Thee, O LORD; I have said: 'Thou art my God.'

### 31:16

בְּיָדְךָ עִתֹּתָי הַצִּילֵנִי מִיַּד־אוֹיְבַי וּמֵרֹדְפָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-yah-de-KHAH ee-toh-TAHY hah-tsee-LEH-nee mee-yahd oh-ye-VAHY oo-meh-roh-de-FAHY

My times are in Thy hand; Deliver me from the hand of mine enemies, and from them that persecute me.

### 31:17

הָאִירָה פָנֶיךָ עַל־עַבְדֶּךָ הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי בְחַסְדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-EE-rah fah-ney-KHAH ahl ahv-DEH-khah HOH-shee-EH-nee ve-khahs-DEH-khah

Make Thy face to shine upon Thy servant; Save me in Thy lovingkindness.

### 31:18

יְהוָה אַל־אֵבוֹשָׁה כִּי קְרָאתִיךָ יֵבֹשׁוּ רְשָׁעִים יִדְּמוּ לִשְׁאוֹל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI ahl eh-voh-SHAH KEE ke-rah-TEE-khah yeh-VOH-shoo re-shah-EEM yee-de-MOO leesh-OHL

O LORD, let me not be ashamed, for I have called upon Thee; Let the wicked be ashamed, let them be put to silence in the nether-world.

### 31:19

תֵּאָלַמְנָה שִׂפְתֵי שָׁקֶר הַדֹּבְרוֹת עַל־צַדִּיק עָתָק בְּגַאֲוָה וָבוּז׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TEH-ah-LAHM-nah seef-TEY SHAH-kehr hah-doh-ve-ROHT ahl tsah-DEEK ah-TAHK be-gah-ah-VAH vah-VOOZ

Let the lying lips be dumb, Which speak arrogantly against the righteous, With pride and contempt.

### 31:20

מָה רַב־טוּבְךָ אֲשֶׁר־צָפַנְתָּ לִּירֵאֶיךָ פָּעַלְתָּ לַחֹסִים בָּךְ נֶגֶד בְּנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** MAH rahv too-ve-KHAH ah-shehr tsah-FAHN-tah lee-reh-EY-khah pah-ahl-TAH lah-khoh-SEEM BAHKH neh-GEHD be-NEY ah-dahm

Oh how abundant is Thy goodness, which Thou hast laid up for them that fear Thee; Which Thou hast wrought for them that take their refuge in Thee, in the sight of the sons of men!

### 31:21

תַּסְתִּירֵם בְּסֵתֶר פָּנֶיךָ מֵרֻכְסֵי אִישׁ תִּצְפְּנֵם בְּסֻכָּה מֵרִיב לְשֹׁנוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tahs-tee-REHM  be-SEH-tehr pah-ney-KHAH meh-rookh-SEY EESH teets-pe-NEHM be-soo-KAH meh-REEV le-shoh-NOHT

Thou hidest them in the covert of Thy presence from the plottings of man; Thou concealest them in a pavilion from the strife of tongues.

### 31:22

בָּרוּךְ יְהוָה כִּי הִפְלִיא חַסְדּוֹ לִי בְּעִיר מָצוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NAI KEE heef-LEE khahs-DOH LEE be-EER mah-TSOHR

Blessed be the LORD; For He hath shown me His wondrous lovingkindness in an entrenched city.

### 31:23

וַאֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי בְחָפְזִי נִגְרַזְתִּי מִנֶּגֶד עֵינֶיךָ אָכֵן שָׁמַעְתָּ קוֹל תַּחֲנוּנַי בְּשַׁוְּעִי אֵלֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  ah-MAHR-tee ve-khah-fe-ZEE neeg-rahz-TEE mee-NEH-gehd ey-NEY-khah ah-KHEHN shah-mah-TAH KOHL tah-khah-noo-NAHY be-shah-ve-EE eh-LEY-khah

As for me, I said in my haste: 'I am cut off from before Thine eyes'; Nevertheless Thou heardest the voice of my supplications when I cried unto Thee.

### 31:24

אֶהֱבוּ אֶת־יְהוָה כָּל־חֲסִידָיו אֱמוּנִים נֹצֵר יְהוָה וּמְשַׁלֵּם עַל־יֶתֶר עֹשֵׂה גַאֲוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-heh-VOO eht ah-doh-NAI kahl khah-see-DAHV eh-moo-NEEM noh-TSEHR ah-doh-NAI oo-me-shah-LEHM ahl yeh-TEHR oh-SEH gah-ah-VAH

O love the LORD, all ye His godly ones; The LORD preserveth the faithful, And plentifully repayeth him that acteth haughtily.

### 31:25

חִזְקוּ וְיַאֲמֵץ לְבַבְכֶם כָּל־הַמְיַחֲלִים לַיהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kheez-KOO ve-yah-ah-MEHTS le-vahv-KHEHM kohl hah-me-yah-khah-LEEM lah-ah-doh-NAI

Be strong, and let your heart take courage, All ye that wait for the LORD.

## Psalm 32 — Confession, forgiveness, and instruction

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.32) · Buckets: repentance, wisdom, health

### 32:1

לְדָוִד מַשְׂכִּיל אַשְׁרֵי נְשׂוּי־פֶּשַׁע כְּסוּי חֲטָאָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED mahs-KEEL ahsh-REY ne-sooy PEH-shah ke-SOOY khah-tah-AH

[A Psalm] of David. Maschil. Happy is he whose transgression is forgiven, whose sin is pardoned.

### 32:2

אַשְׁרֵי אָדָם לֹא יַחְשֹׁב יְהוָה לוֹ עָוֺן וְאֵין בְּרוּחוֹ רְמִיָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHSH-rey ah-DAHM LOH yahkh-SHOHV ah-doh-NAI LOH ah-VOHN ve-EYN be-roo-KHOH re-mee-yah

Happy is the man unto whom the LORD counteth not iniquity, And in whose spirit there is no guile.

### 32:3

כִּי־הֶחֱרַשְׁתִּי בָּלוּ עֲצָמָי בְּשַׁאֲגָתִי כָּל־הַיּוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee heh-kheh-rahsh-TEE bah-LOO ah-tsah-MAHY be-shah-ah-gah-TEE kohl hah-YOHM

When I kept silence, my bones wore away Through my groaning all the day long.

### 32:4

כִּי יוֹמָם וָלַיְלָה תִּכְבַּד עָלַי יָדֶךָ נֶהְפַּךְ לְשַׁדִּי בְּחַרְבֹנֵי קַיִץ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE  yoh-MAHM vah-lahy-LAH teekh-BAHD ah-LAHY yah-DEH-khah neh-PAHKH le-shah-DEE be-khahr-VOH-ney KAH-yeets SEH-lah

For day and night Thy hand was heavy upon me; My sap was turned as in the droughts of summer. Selah

### 32:5

חַטָּאתִי אוֹדִיעֲךָ וַעֲוֺנִי לֹא־כִסִּיתִי אָמַרְתִּי אוֹדֶה עֲלֵי פְשָׁעַי לַיהוָה וְאַתָּה נָשָׂאתָ עֲוֺן חַטָּאתִי סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-tah-TEE oh-DEE-ah-KHAH vah-ah-voh-NEE loh khee-SEE-tee ah-MAHR-tee oh-DEH ah-LEY fe-shah-AHY lah-ah-doh-NAI ve-ah-TAH nah-SAH-tah ah-VOHN khah-tah-TEE SEH-lah

I acknowledged my sin unto Thee, and mine iniquity have I not hid; I said: 'I will make confession concerning my transgressions unto the LORD' — And Thou, Thou forgavest the iniquity of my sin. Selah

### 32:6

עַל־זֹאת יִתְפַּלֵּל כָּל־חָסִיד אֵלֶיךָ לְעֵת מְצֹא רַק לְשֵׁטֶף מַיִם רַבִּים אֵלָיו לֹא יַגִּיעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl ZOHT yeet-pah-LEHL kohl khah-SEED  eh-ley-KHAH le-EHT me-TSOH RAHK le-sheh-TEHF MAH-yeem rah-BEEM eh-LAHV LOH yah-GEE-oo

For this let every one that is godly pray unto Thee in a time when Thou mayest be found; Surely, when the great waters overflow, they will not reach unto him.

### 32:7

אַתָּה סֵתֶר לִי מִצַּר תִּצְּרֵנִי רָנֵּי פַלֵּט תְּסוֹבְבֵנִי סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH  SEH-tehr LEE mee-TSAHR tee-tse-REH-nee rah-NEY fah-LEHT te-SOH-ve-VEH-nee SEH-lah

Thou art my hiding-place; Thou wilt preserve me from the adversary; With songs of deliverance Thou wilt compass me about. Selah

### 32:8

אַשְׂכִּילְךָ וְאוֹרְךָ בְּדֶרֶךְ־זוּ תֵלֵךְ אִיעֲצָה עָלֶיךָ עֵינִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahs-kee-le-KHAH  ve-oh-re-KHAH be-deh-rehkh ZOO teh-LEHKH ee-ah-TSAH ah-LEY-khah ey-NEE

'I will instruct thee and teach thee in the way which thou shalt go; I will give counsel, Mine eye being upon thee.'

### 32:9

אַל־תִּהְיוּ כְּסוּס כְּפֶרֶד אֵין הָבִין בְּמֶתֶג־וָרֶסֶן עֶדְיוֹ לִבְלוֹם בַּל קְרֹב אֵלֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tee-he-YOO  ke-SOOS ke-feh-REHD EYN hah-VEEN be-meh-tehg vah-REH-sehn ehd-YOH leev-LOHM BAHL ke-ROHV eh-LEY-khah

Be ye not as the horse, or as the mule, which have no understanding; Whose mouth must be held in with bit and bridle, That they come not near unto thee.

### 32:10

רַבִּים מַכְאוֹבִים לָרָשָׁע וְהַבּוֹטֵחַ בַּיהוָה חֶסֶד יְסוֹבְבֶנּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BEEM mahkh-oh-VEEM lah-rah-SHAH ve-hah-boh-TEH-ahkh bah-ah-doh-NAI kheh-SEHD ye-soh-ve-VEH-noo

Many are the sorrows of the wicked; But he that trusteth in the LORD, mercy compasseth him about.

### 32:11

שִׂמְחוּ בַיהוָה וְגִילוּ צַדִּיקִים וְהַרְנִינוּ כָּל־יִשְׁרֵי־לֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** seem-KHOO vah-ah-doh-NAI ve-gee-LOO tsah-dee-KEEM ve-hahr-NEE-noo kohl yeesh-rey LEHV

Be glad in the LORD, and rejoice, ye righteous; And shout for joy, all ye that are upright in heart.

## Psalm 33 — Creation, trust, and communal praise

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.33) · Buckets: gratitude, community, health

### 33:1

רַנְּנוּ צַדִּיקִים בַּיהוָה לַיְשָׁרִים נָאוָה תְהִלָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-ne-NOO tsah-dee-KEEM bah-ah-doh-NAI lahy-shah-REEM nah-VAH te-hee-LAH

Rejoice in the LORD, O ye righteous, Praise is comely for the upright.

### 33:2

הוֹדוּ לַיהוָה בְּכִנּוֹר בְּנֵבֶל עָשׂוֹר זַמְּרוּ־לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI be-khee-NOHR be-NEH-vehl ah-SOHR zah-me-roo LOH

Give thanks unto the LORD with harp, Sing praises unto Him with the psaltery of ten strings.

### 33:3

שִׁירוּ־לוֹ שִׁיר חָדָשׁ הֵיטִיבוּ נַגֵּן בִּתְרוּעָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-roo LOH SHEER khah-DAHSH hey-TEE-voo nah-GEHN beet-roo-AH

Sing unto Him a new song; Play skilfully amid shouts of joy.

### 33:4

כִּי־יָשָׁר דְּבַר־יְהוָה וְכָל־מַעֲשֵׂהוּ בֶּאֱמוּנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee yah-SHAHR de-vahr ah-doh-NAI ve-khohl mah-ah-SEH-hoo beh-eh-moo-NAH

For the word of the LORD is upright; And all His work is done in faithfulness.

### 33:5

אֹהֵב צְדָקָה וּמִשְׁפָּט חֶסֶד יְהוָה מָלְאָה הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-HEHV tse-dah-KAH oo-meesh-PAHT KHEH-sehd ah-doh-NAI mah-le-AH hah-AH-rehts

He loveth righteousness and justice; The earth is full of the lovingkindness of the LORD.

### 33:6

בִּדְבַר יְהוָה שָׁמַיִם נַעֲשׂוּ וּבְרוּחַ פִּיו כָּל־צְבָאָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** beed-VAHR ah-doh-NAI shah-MAH-yeem nah-ah-SOO oo-ve-ROO-ahkh PEEV kohl tse-vah-AHM

By the word of the LORD were the heavens made; And all the host of them by the breath of His mouth.

### 33:7

כֹּנֵס כַּנֵּד מֵי הַיָּם נֹתֵן בְּאֹצָרוֹת תְּהוֹמוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-NEHS kah-NEHD MEY hah-YAHM noh-TEHN be-oh-tsah-ROHT te-hoh-MOHT

He gathereth the waters of the sea together as a heap; He layeth up the deeps in storehouses.

### 33:8

יִירְאוּ מֵיְהוָה כָּל־הָאָרֶץ מִמֶּנּוּ יָגוּרוּ כָּל־יֹשְׁבֵי תֵבֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-re-OO meh-ah-doh-NAI kohl hah-AH-rehts mee-MEH-noo yah-GOO-roo kohl yoh-she-VEY teh-VEHL

Let all the earth fear the LORD; Let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of Him.

### 33:9

כִּי הוּא אָמַר וַיֶּהִי הוּא־צִוָּה וַיַּעֲמֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE HOO ah-MAHR vah-YEH-hee hoo tsee-VAH vah-yah-ah-MOHD

For He spoke, and it was; He commanded, and it stood.

### 33:10

יְהוָה הֵפִיר עֲצַת־גּוֹיִם הֵנִיא מַחְשְׁבוֹת עַמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI heh-FEER ah-tsaht goh-YEEM heh-NEE mahkh-she-VOHT ah-MEEM

The LORD bringeth the counsel of the nations to nought; He maketh the thoughts of the peoples to be of no effect.

### 33:11

עֲצַת יְהוָה לְעוֹלָם תַּעֲמֹד מַחְשְׁבוֹת לִבּוֹ לְדֹר וָדֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TSAHT ah-doh-NAI le-oh-LAHM tah-ah-MOHD mahkh-she-VOHT lee-BOH le-DOHR vah-DOHR

The counsel of the LORD standeth for ever, The thoughts of His heart to all generations.

### 33:12

אַשְׁרֵי הַגּוֹי אֲשֶׁר־יְהוָה אֱלֹהָיו הָעָם בָּחַר לְנַחֲלָה לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY hah-GOHY ah-shehr ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHV hah-AHM  bah-KHAHR le-nah-khah-LAH LOH

Happy is the nation whose God is the LORD; The people whom He hath chosen for His own inheritance.

### 33:13

מִשָּׁמַיִם הִבִּיט יְהוָה רָאָה אֶת־כָּל־בְּנֵי הָאָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-shah-mah-YEEM hee-BEET ah-doh-NAI rah-AH eht kohl be-NEY hah-ah-DAHM

The LORD looketh from heaven; He beholdeth all the sons of men;

### 33:14

מִמְּכוֹן־שִׁבְתּוֹ הִשְׁגִּיחַ אֶל כָּל־יֹשְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-me-khohn sheev-TOH heesh-GEE-ahkh EHL kohl yoh-she-VEY hah-AH-rehts

From the place of His habitation He looketh intently Upon all the inhabitants of the earth;

### 33:15

הַיֹּצֵר יַחַד לִבָּם הַמֵּבִין אֶל־כָּל־מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-yoh-TSEHR YAH-khahd lee-BAHM hah-meh-VEEN ehl kohl mah-ah-sey-HEHM

He that fashioneth the hearts of them all, That considereth all their doings.

### 33:16

אֵין־הַמֶּלֶךְ נוֹשָׁע בְּרָב־חָיִל גִּבּוֹר לֹא־יִנָּצֵל בְּרָב־כֹּחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eyn hah-meh-LEHKH noh-SHAH be-rohv KHAH-yeel gee-BOHR loh yee-nah-TSEHL be-rohv KOH-ahkh

A king is not saved by the multitude of a host; A mighty man is not delivered by great strength.

### 33:17

שֶׁקֶר הַסּוּס לִתְשׁוּעָה וּבְרֹב חֵילוֹ לֹא יְמַלֵּט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEH-kehr hah-SOOS leet-shoo-AH oo-ve-ROHV khey-LOH LOH ye-mah-LEHT

A horse is a vain thing for safety; Neither doth it afford escape by its great strength.

### 33:18

הִנֵּה עֵין יְהוָה אֶל־יְרֵאָיו לַמְיַחֲלִים לְחַסְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH EYN ah-doh-NAI ehl ye-reh-AHV lah-me-yah-khah-LEEM le-khahs-DOH

Behold, the eye of the LORD is toward them that fear Him, Toward them that wait for His mercy;

### 33:19

לְהַצִּיל מִמָּוֶת נַפְשָׁם וּלְחַיּוֹתָם בָּרָעָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-hah-TSEEL mee-MAH-veht nahf-SHAHM oo-le-khah-yoh-TAHM bah-rah-AHV

To deliver their soul from death, And to keep them alive in famine.

### 33:20

נַפְשֵׁנוּ חִכְּתָה לַיהוָה עֶזְרֵנוּ וּמָגִנֵּנוּ הוּא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nahf-sheh-NOO khee-ke-TAH lah-ah-doh-NAI ehz-REH-noo oo-mah-gee-NEH-noo HOO

Our soul hath waited for the LORD; He is our help and our shield.

### 33:21

כִּי־בוֹ יִשְׂמַח לִבֵּנוּ כִּי בְשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ בָטָחְנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee VOH yees-MAHKH lee-BEH-noo KEE ve-SHEHM kohd-SHOH vah-TAH-khe-noo

For in Him doth our heart rejoice, Because we have trusted in His holy name.

### 33:22

יְהִי־חַסְדְּךָ יְהוָה עָלֵינוּ כַּאֲשֶׁר יִחַלְנוּ לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-hee khahs-de-KHAH ah-doh-NAI ah-LEY-noo kah-ah-SHEHR yee-KHAHL-noo LAHKH

Let Thy mercy, O LORD, be upon us, According as we have waited for Thee.

## Psalm 34 — Rescue, ethical speech, and care for the brokenhearted

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.34) · Buckets: wellness, relationships, gratitude

### 34:1

לְדָוִד בְּשַׁנּוֹתוֹ אֶת־טַעְמוֹ לִפְנֵי אֲבִימֶלֶךְ וַיְגָרֲשֵׁהוּ וַיֵּלַךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED be-shah-noh-TOH eht tah-MOH leef-NEY ah-vee-MEH-lehkh vah-ye-gah-rah-SHEH-hoo vah-yeh-LAHKH

[A Psalm] of David; when he changed his demeanour before Abimelech, who drove him away, and he departed.

### 34:2

אֲבָרֲכָה אֶת־יְהוָה בְּכָל־עֵת תָּמִיד תְּהִלָּתוֹ בְּפִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-vah-rah-KHAH eht ah-doh-NAI be-khohl EHT tah-MEED te-hee-lah-TOH be-FEE

I will bless the LORD at all times; His praise shall continually be in my mouth.

### 34:3

בַּיהוָה תִּתְהַלֵּל נַפְשִׁי יִשְׁמְעוּ עֲנָוִים וְיִשְׂמָחוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ah-doh-NAI teet-hah-LEHL nahf-SHEE yeesh-me-OO ah-nah-VEEM ve-yees-MAH-khoo

My soul shall glory in the LORD; The humble shall hear thereof, and be glad.

### 34:4

גַּדְּלוּ לַיהוָה אִתִּי וּנְרוֹמְמָה שְׁמוֹ יַחְדָּו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-de-LOO lah-ah-doh-NAI ee-TEE oo-ne-roh-me-MAH she-MOH yahkh-DAHV

O magnify the LORD with me, And let us exalt His name together.

### 34:5

דָּרַשְׁתִּי אֶת־יְהוָה וְעָנָנִי וּמִכָּל־מְגוּרוֹתַי הִצִּילָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-RAHSH-tee eht ah-doh-NAI ve-ah-NAH-nee oo-mee-kohl me-goo-roh-TAHY hee-tsee-LAH-nee

I sought the LORD, and He answered me, And delivered me from all my fears.

### 34:6

הִבִּיטוּ אֵלָיו וְנָהָרוּ וּפְנֵיהֶם אַל־יֶחְפָּרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-BEE-too eh-LAHV ve-nah-HAH-roo oo-fe-ney-HEHM ahl yehkh-PAH-roo

They looked unto Him, and were radiant; And their faces shall never be abashed.

### 34:7

זֶה עָנִי קָרָא וַיהוָה שָׁמֵעַ וּמִכָּל־צָרוֹתָיו הוֹשִׁיעוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZEH ah-NEE kah-RAH vah-ah-doh-NAI shah-MEH-ah oo-mee-kohl tsah-roh-TAHV hoh-shee-OH

This poor man cried, and the LORD heard, And saved him out of all his troubles.

### 34:8

חֹנֶה מַלְאַךְ־יְהוָה סָבִיב לִירֵאָיו וַיְחַלְּצֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khoh-NEH mahl-ahkh ah-doh-NAI sah-VEEV lee-reh-AHV vah-ye-khah-le-TSEHM

The angel of the LORD encampeth round about them that fear Him, And delivereth them.

### 34:9

טַעֲמוּ וּרְאוּ כִּי־טוֹב יְהוָה אַשְׁרֵי הַגֶּבֶר יֶחֱסֶה־בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-ah-MOO oo-re-OO kee TOHV ah-doh-NAI ah-she-REY hah-GEH-vehr yeh-kheh-seh BOH

O consider and see that the LORD is good; Happy is the man that taketh refuge in Him.

### 34:10

יְראוּ אֶת־יְהוָה קְדֹשָׁיו כִּי־אֵין מַחְסוֹר לִירֵאָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-R-oo eht ah-doh-NAI ke-doh-SHAHV kee EYN mahkh-SOHR lee-reh-AHV

O fear the LORD, ye His holy ones; For there is no want to them that fear Him.

### 34:11

כְּפִירִים רָשׁוּ וְרָעֵבוּ וְדֹרְשֵׁי יְהוָה לֹא־יַחְסְרוּ כָל־טוֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-fee-REEM rah-SHOO ve-rah-EH-voo ve-doh-re-SHEY ah-doh-NAI loh yahkh-se-ROO khohl TOHV

The young lions do lack, and suffer hunger; But they that seek the LORD want not any good thing.

### 34:12

לְכוּ־בָנִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִי יִרְאַת יְהוָה אֲלַמֶּדְכֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-khoo vah-NEEM sheem-oo LEE yee-re-AHT ah-doh-NAI ah-lah-mehd-KHEHM

Come, ye children, hearken unto me; I will teach you the fear of the LORD.

### 34:13

מִי־הָאִישׁ הֶחָפֵץ חַיִּים אֹהֵב יָמִים לִרְאוֹת טוֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee hah-EESH heh-khah-FEHTS khah-YEEM oh-HEHV yah-MEEM leer-OHT TOHV

Who is the man that desireth life, and loveth days, That he may see good therein?

### 34:14

נְצֹר לְשׁוֹנְךָ מֵרָע וּשְׂפָתֶיךָ מִדַּבֵּר מִרְמָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-TSOHR le-shoh-ne-KHAH meh-RAH oo-se-fah-TEY-khah mee-dah-BEHR meer-MAH

Keep thy tongue from evil, And thy lips from speaking guile.

### 34:15

סוּר מֵרָע וַעֲשֵׂה־טוֹב בַּקֵּשׁ שָׁלוֹם וְרָדְפֵהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SOOR meh-RAH vah-ah-seh TOHV bah-KEHSH shah-LOHM ve-rah-de-FEH-hoo

Depart from evil, and do good; Seek peace, and pursue it.

### 34:16

עֵינֵי יְהוָה אֶל־צַדִּיקִים וְאָזְנָיו אֶל־שַׁוְעָתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ey-NEY ah-doh-NAI ehl tsah-dee-KEEM ve-ohz-NOHYV ehl shahv-ah-TAHM

The eyes of the LORD are toward the righteous, And His ears are open unto their cry.

### 34:17

פְּנֵי יְהוָה בְּעֹשֵׂי רָע לְהַכְרִית מֵאֶרֶץ זִכְרָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-NEY ah-doh-NAI be-OH-sey RAH le-hahkh-REET meh-EH-rehts zeekh-RAHM

The face of the LORD is against them that do evil, To cut off the remembrance of them from the earth.

### 34:18

צָעֲקוּ וַיהוָה שָׁמֵעַ וּמִכָּל־צָרוֹתָם הִצִּילָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsah-ah-KOO vah-ah-doh-NAI shah-MEH-ah oo-mee-kohl tsah-roh-TAHM hee-tsee-LAHM

They cried, and the LORD heard, And delivered them out of all their troubles.

### 34:19

קָרוֹב יְהוָה לְנִשְׁבְּרֵי־לֵב וְאֶת־דַּכְּאֵי־רוּחַ יוֹשִׁיעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-ROHV ah-doh-NAI le-neesh-be-rey LEHV ve-eht dah-ke-ey ROO-ahkh yoh-SHEE-ah

The LORD is nigh unto them that are of a broken heart, And saveth such as are of a contrite spirit.

### 34:20

רַבּוֹת רָעוֹת צַדִּיק וּמִכֻּלָּם יַצִּילֶנּוּ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BOHT rah-OHT tsah-DEEK oo-mee-koo-LAHM yah-tsee-LEH-noo ah-doh-NAI

Many are the ills of the righteous, But the LORD delivereth him out of them all.

### 34:21

שֹׁמֵר כָּל־עַצְמוֹתָיו אַחַת מֵהֵנָּה לֹא נִשְׁבָּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shoh-MEHR kohl ahts-moh-TAHV ah-KHAHT meh-HEH-nah LOH neesh-BAH-rah

He keepeth all his bones; Not one of them is broken.

### 34:22

תְּמוֹתֵת רָשָׁע רָעָה וְשֹׂנְאֵי צַדִּיק יֶאְשָׁמוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-moh-TEHT rah-SHAH rah-AH ve-soh-ne-EY tsah-DEEK yeh-SHAH-moo

Evil shall kill the wicked; And they that hate the righteous shall be held guilty.

### 34:23

פּוֹדֶה יְהוָה נֶפֶשׁ עֲבָדָיו וְלֹא יֶאְשְׁמוּ כָּל־הַחֹסִים בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** poh-DEH ah-doh-NAI NEH-fehsh ah-vah-DAHV ve-LOH yeh-she-MOO kahl hah-khoh-SEEM BOH

The LORD redeemeth the soul of His servants; And none of them that take refuge in Him shall be desolate.

## Psalm 35 — Help against unjust opponents

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.35) · Buckets: justice, protection

### 35:1

לְדָוִד רִיבָה יְהוָה אֶת־יְרִיבַי לְחַם אֶת־לֹחֲמָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED  ree-VAH ah-doh-NAI eht ye-ree-VAHY le-KHAHM eht loh-khah-MAHY

[A Psalm] of David. Strive, O LORD, with them that strive with me; Fight against them that fight against me.

### 35:2

הַחֲזֵק מָגֵן וְצִנָּה וְקוּמָה בְּעֶזְרָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-khah-ZEHK mah-GEHN ve-tsee-NAH ve-KOO-mah be-ehz-rah-TEE

Take hold of shield and buckler, And rise up to my help.

### 35:3

וְהָרֵק חֲנִית וּסְגֹר לִקְרַאת רֹדְפָי אֱמֹר לְנַפְשִׁי יְשֻׁעָתֵךְ אָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hah-REHK khah-NEET oo-se-GOHR leek-RAHT roh-de-FAHY eh-MOHR le-nahf-SHEE ye-shoo-ah-TEHKH AH-nee

Draw out also the spear, and the battle-axe, against them that pursue me; Say unto my soul: 'I am Thy salvation.'

### 35:4

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיִכָּלְמוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי יִסֹּגוּ אָחוֹר וְיַחְפְּרוּ חֹשְׁבֵי רָעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo ve-yee-kah-le-MOO me-vahk-SHEY nahf-SHEE yee-SOH-goo ah-KHOHR ve-yahkh-pe-ROO khoh-she-VEY rah-ah-TEE

Let them be ashamed and brought to confusion that seek after my soul; Let them be turned back and be abashed that devise my hurt.

### 35:5

יִהְיוּ כְּמֹץ לִפְנֵי־רוּחַ וּמַלְאַךְ יְהוָה דּוֹחֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-he-YOO ke-MOHTS leef-ney ROO-ahkh oo-mahl-AHKH ah-doh-NAI doh-KHEH

Let them be as chaff before the wind, The angel of the LORD thrusting them.

### 35:6

יְהִי־דַרְכָּם חֹשֶׁךְ וַחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת וּמַלְאַךְ יְהוָה רֹדְפָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-hee dahr-KAHM KHOH-shehkh vah-khah-lahk-lah-KOHT oo-mahl-AHKH ah-doh-NAI roh-de-FAHM

Let their way be dark and slippery, The angel of the LORD pursuing them.

### 35:7

כִּי־חִנָּם טָמְנוּ־לִי שַׁחַת רִשְׁתָּם חִנָּם חָפְרוּ לְנַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khee-NAHM tah-me-noo LEE SHAH-khaht reesh-TAHM khee-NAHM khah-fe-ROO le-nahf-SHEE

For without cause have they hid for me the pit, even their net, Without cause have they digged for my soul. .

### 35:8

תְּבוֹאֵהוּ שׁוֹאָה לֹא־יֵדָע וְרִשְׁתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר־טָמַן תִּלְכְּדוֹ בְּשׁוֹאָה יִפָּל־בָּהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-voh-EH-hoo shoh-AH loh yeh-DAH ve-reesh-TOH ah-shehr tah-MAHN teel-ke-DOH be-shoh-AH yee-pahl BAHH

Let destruction come upon him unawares; And let his net that he hath hid catch himself; With destruction let him fall therein.

### 35:9

וְנַפְשִׁי תָּגִיל בַּיהוָה תָּשִׂישׂ בִּישׁוּעָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-nahf-SHEE tah-GEEL bah-ah-doh-NAI tah-SEES bee-shoo-ah-TOH

And my soul shall be joyful in the LORD; It shall rejoice in His salvation.

### 35:10

כָּל עַצְמוֹתַי תֹּאמַרְנָה יְהוָה מִי כָמוֹךָ מַצִּיל עָנִי מֵחָזָק מִמֶּנּוּ וְעָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן מִגֹּזְלוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOHL ahts-moh-TAHY  toh-mahr-NAH ah-doh-NAI MEE khah-MOH-khah mah-TSEEL ah-NEE meh-khah-ZAHK mee-MEH-noo ve-ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN mee-goh-ze-LOH

All my bones shall say: 'LORD, who is like unto Thee, Who deliverest the poor from him that is too strong for him, Yea, the poor and the needy from him that spoileth him?'

### 35:11

יְקוּמוּן עֵדֵי חָמָס אֲשֶׁר לֹא־יָדַעְתִּי יִשְׁאָלוּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-koo-MOON eh-DEY khah-MAHS ah-SHEHR loh yah-DAH-tee yeesh-ah-LOO-nee

Unrighteous witnesses rise up; They ask me of things that I know not.

### 35:12

יְשַׁלְּמוּנִי רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה שְׁכוֹל לְנַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-shah-le-MOO-nee rah-AH TAH-khaht toh-VAH she-KHOHL le-nahf-SHEE

They repay me evil for good; Bereavement is come to my soul.

### 35:13

וַאֲנִי בַּחֲלוֹתָם לְבוּשִׁי שָׂק עִנֵּיתִי בַצּוֹם נַפְשִׁי וּתְפִלָּתִי עַל־חֵיקִי תָשׁוּב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  bah-khah-loh-TAHM le-VOO-shee SAHK ee-NEY-tee vah-TSOHM nahf-SHEE oo-te-fee-lah-TEE ahl khey-KEE tah-SHOOV

But as for me, when they were sick, my clothing was sackcloth, I afflicted my soul with fasting; And my prayer, may it return into mine own bosom.

### 35:14

כְּרֵעַ־כְּאָח לִי הִתְהַלָּכְתִּי כַּאֲבֶל־אֵם קֹדֵר שַׁחוֹתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-reh-ah ke-AHKH LEE heet-hah-LAH-khe-tee kah-ah-vehl EHM koh-DEHR shah-KHOH-tee

I went about as though it had been my friend or my brother; I bowed down mournful, as one that mourneth for his mother.

### 35:15

וּבְצַלְעִי שָׂמְחוּ וְנֶאֱסָפוּ נֶאֶסְפוּ עָלַי נֵכִים וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי קָרְעוּ וְלֹא־דָמּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-ve-tsahl-EE sah-me-KHOO ve-neh-eh-SAH-foo neh-ehs-FOO ah-LAHY neh-KHEEM ve-LOH yah-DAH-tee kah-re-OO ve-loh DAH-moo

But when I halt they rejoice, and gather themselves together; The abjects gather themselves together against me, and those whom I know not; They tear me, and cease not;

### 35:16

בְּחַנְפֵי לַעֲגֵי מָעוֹג חָרֹק עָלַי שִׁנֵּימוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khahn-FEY lah-ah-GEY mah-OHG khah-ROHK ah-LAHY shee-NEY-moh

With the profanest mockeries of backbiting They gnash at me with their teeth.

### 35:17

אֲדֹנָי כַּמָּה תִּרְאֶה הָשִׁיבָה נַפְשִׁי מִשֹּׁאֵיהֶם מִכְּפִירִים יְחִידָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAHY kah-MAH teer-EH hah-SHEE-vah nahf-SHEE mee-shoh-ey-HEHM mee-ke-fee-REEM ye-khee-dah-TEE

Lord, how long wilt Thou look on? Rescue my soul from their destructions, Mine only one from the lions.

### 35:18

אוֹדְךָ בְּקָהָל רָב בְּעַם עָצוּם אֲהַלְלֶךָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-de-KHAH be-kah-HAHL RAHV be-AHM ah-TSOOM ahhah-le-LEH-kah

I will give Thee thanks in the great congregation; I will praise Thee among a numerous people.

### 35:19

אַל־יִשְׂמְחוּ־לִי אֹיְבַי שֶׁקֶר שֹׂנְאַי חִנָּם יִקְרְצוּ־עָיִן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl yees-me-khoo LEE oh-ye-VAHY SHEH-kehr soh-ne-AHY khee-NAHM yeek-re-tsoo AH-yeen

Let not them that are wrongfully mine enemies rejoice over me; Neither let them wink with the eye that hate me without a cause.

### 35:20

כִּי לֹא שָׁלוֹם יְדַבֵּרוּ וְעַל רִגְעֵי־אֶרֶץ דִּבְרֵי מִרְמוֹת יַחֲשֹׁבוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE LOH shah-LOHM ye-dah-BEH-roo ve-AHL reeg-ey EH-rehts deev-REY meer-MOHT yah-khah-shoh-VOON

For they speak not peace; But they devise deceitful matters against them that are quiet in the land.

### 35:21

וַיַּרְחִיבוּ עָלַי פִּיהֶם אָמְרוּ הֶאָח הֶאָח רָאֲתָה עֵינֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahr-KHEE-voo ah-LAHY pee-HEHM ah-me-ROO heh-AHKH  heh-AHKH rah-ah-TAH ey-NEY-noo

Yea, they open their mouth wide against me; They say: 'Aha, aha, our eye hath seen it.'

### 35:22

רָאִיתָה יְהוָה אַל־תֶּחֱרַשׁ אֲדֹנָי אֲל־תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-EE-tah ah-doh-NAI ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH ah-doh-NAHY ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee

Thou hast seen, O LORD; keep not silence; O Lord, be not far from me.

### 35:23

הָעִירָה וְהָקִיצָה לְמִשְׁפָּטִי אֱלֹהַי וַאדֹנָי לְרִיבִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-EE-rah ve-hah-kee-TSAH le-meesh-pah-TEE eh-loh-HAHY vah-doh-NAHY le-ree-VEE

Rouse Thee, and awake to my judgment, Even unto my cause, my God and my Lord.

### 35:24

שָׁפְטֵנִי כְצִדְקְךָ יְהוָה אֱלֹהָי וְאַל־יִשְׂמְחוּ־לִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-fe-TEH-nee khe-tseed-ke-KHAH ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHY ve-ahl yees-me-khoo LEE

Judge me, O LORD my God, according to Thy righteousness; And let them not rejoice over me.

### 35:25

אַל־יֹאמְרוּ בְלִבָּם הֶאָח נַפְשֵׁנוּ אַל־יֹאמְרוּ בִּלַּעֲנוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl yoh-me-ROO ve-lee-BAHM heh-AHKH nahf-SHEH-noo ahl yoh-me-ROO bee-lah-ah-NOO-hoo

Let them not say in their heart: 'Aha, we have our desire'; Let them not say: 'We have swallowed him up.'

### 35:26

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ יַחְדָּו שְׂמֵחֵי רָעָתִי יִלְבְּשׁוּ־בֹשֶׁת וּכְלִמָּה הַמַּגְדִּילִים עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo ve-yahkh-pe-ROO  yahkh-DAHV se-meh-KHEY rah-ah-TEE yee-le-be-shoo VOH-sheht oo-khe-lee-MAH hah-mahg-dee-LEEM ah-LAHY

Let them be ashamed and abashed together that rejoice at my hurt; Let them be clothed with shame and confusion that magnify themselves against me. .

### 35:27

יָרֹנּוּ וְיִשְׂמְחוּ חֲפֵצֵי צִדְקִי וְיֹאמְרוּ תָמִיד יִגְדַּל יְהוָה הֶחָפֵץ שְׁלוֹם עַבְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-ROH-noo ve-yees-me-KHOO khah-feh-TSEY tseed-KEE ve-yoh-me-ROO tah-MEED yeeg-DAHL ah-doh-NAI heh-khah-FEHTS she-LOHM ahv-DOH

Let them shout for joy, and be glad, that delight in my righteousness; Yea, let them say continually: 'Magnified be the LORD, Who delighteth in the peace of His servant.'

### 35:28

וּלְשׁוֹנִי תֶּהְגֶּה צִדְקֶךָ כָּל־הַיּוֹם תְּהִלָּתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-le-shoh-NEE teh-GEH tseed-KEH-khah kohl hah-YOHM te-hee-lah-TEH-khah

And my tongue shall speak of Thy righteousness, And of Thy praise all the day.

## Psalm 36 — Human wrongdoing and divine lovingkindness

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.36) · Buckets: justice, hope

### 36:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְעֶבֶד־יְהוָה לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh  le-eh-vehd ah-doh-NAI le-dah-VEED

For the Leader. [A Psalm] of David the servant of the LORD.

### 36:2

נְאֻם־פֶּשַׁע לָרָשָׁע בְּקֶרֶב לִבִּי אֵין־פַּחַד אֱלֹהִים לְנֶגֶד עֵינָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-oom PEH-shah lah-rah-SHAH be-KEH-rehv lee-BEE eyn PAH-khahd eh-loh-HEEM le-NEH-gehd ey-NAHV

Transgression speaketh to the wicked, methinks— There is no fear of God before his eyes.

### 36:3

כִּי־הֶחֱלִיק אֵלָיו בְּעֵינָיו לִמְצֹא עֲוֺנוֹ לִשְׂנֹא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee heh-kheh-LEEK eh-LAHV be-ey-NAHV leem-TSOH ah-voh-NOH lees-NOH

For it flattereth him in his eyes, Until his iniquity be found, and he be hated.

### 36:4

דִּבְרֵי־פִיו אָוֶן וּמִרְמָה חָדַל לְהַשְׂכִּיל לְהֵיטִיב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deev-rey FEEV AH-vehn oo-meer-MAH khah-DAHL le-hahs-KEEL le-hey-TEEV

The words of his mouth are iniquity and deceit; He hath left off to be wise, to do good.

### 36:5

אָוֶן יַחְשֹׁב עַל־מִשְׁכָּבוֹ יִתְיַצֵּב עַל־דֶּרֶךְ לֹא־טוֹב רָע לֹא יִמְאָס׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AH-vehn  yahkh-SHOHV ahl meesh-kah-VOH yeet-yah-TSEHV ahl DEH-rehkh loh TOHV RAH LOH yeem-AHS

He deviseth iniquity upon his bed; He setteth himself in a way that is not good; He abhorreth not evil.

### 36:6

יְהוָה בְּהַשָּׁמַיִם חַסְדֶּךָ אֱמוּנָתְךָ עַד־שְׁחָקִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI be-hah-shah-MAH-yeem khahs-DEH-khah eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH ahd she-khah-KEEM

Thy lovingkindness, O LORD, is in the heavens; Thy faithfulness reacheth unto the skies.

### 36:7

צִדְקָתְךָ כְּהַרְרֵי־אֵל מִשְׁפָּטֶךָ תְּהוֹם רַבָּה אָדָם־וּבְהֵמָה תוֹשִׁיעַ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tseed-kah-te-KHAH  ke-hahr-rey EHL meesh-pah-teh-KHAH te-HOHM rah-BAH AH-dahm oo-ve-heh-MAH toh-SHEE-ah ah-doh-NAI

Thy righteousness is like the mighty mountains; Thy judgments are like the great deep; Man and beast Thou preservest, O LORD.

### 36:8

מַה־יָּקָר חַסְדְּךָ אֱלֹהִים וּבְנֵי אָדָם בְּצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ יֶחֱסָיוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahh yah-KAHR khahs-de-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM oo-ve-NEY ah-DAHM be-TSEHL ke-nah-FEY-khah yeh-kheh-sah-YOON

How precious is Thy lovingkindness, O God! And the children of men take refuge in the shadow of Thy wings.

### 36:9

יִרְוְיֻן מִדֶּשֶׁן בֵּיתֶךָ וְנַחַל עֲדָנֶיךָ תַשְׁקֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeer-ve-YOON mee-DEH-shehn bey-TEH-khah ve-NAH-khahl ah-dah-NEY-khah tahsh-KEHM

They are abundantly satisfied with the fatness of Thy house; And Thou makest them drink of the river of Thy pleasures.

### 36:10

כִּי־עִמְּךָ מְקוֹר חַיִּים בְּאוֹרְךָ נִרְאֶה־אוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ee-me-KHAH me-KOHR khah-YEEM be-oh-re-KHAH neer-eh OHR

For with Thee is the fountain of life; In Thy light do we see light.

### 36:11

מְשֹׁךְ חַסְדְּךָ לְיֹדְעֶיךָ וְצִדְקָתְךָ לְיִשְׁרֵי־לֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** me-SHOHKH khahs-de-KHAH le-yoh-de-EY-khah ve-tseed-kah-te-KHAH le-yeesh-rey LEHV

O continue Thy lovingkindness unto them that know Thee; And Thy righteousness to the upright in heart.

### 36:12

אַל־תְּבוֹאֵנִי רֶגֶל גַּאֲוָה וְיַד־רְשָׁעִים אַל־תְּנִדֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl te-voh-eh-NEE REH-gehl gah-ah-VAH ve-yahd re-shah-EEM ahl te-nee-DEH-nee

Let not the foot of pride overtake me, And let not the hand of the wicked drive me away.

### 36:13
