**Pronunciation draft:** SHAHM nah-fe-LOO POH-ah-ley AH-vehn doh-KHOO ve-loh YAH-khe-loo KOOM

There are the workers of iniquity fallen; They are thrust down, and are not able to rise.

## Psalm 37 — Patience, upright conduct, and provision

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.37) · Buckets: provision, opportunity, wisdom, health

### 37:1

לְדָוִד אַל־תִּתְחַר בַּמְּרֵעִים אַל־תְּקַנֵּא בְּעֹשֵׂי עַוְלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED  ahl teet-KHAHR bah-me-reh-EEM ahl te-kah-NEH be-oh-SEY ahv-LAH

[A Psalm] of David. Fret not thyself because of evil-doers, neither be thou envious against them that work unrighteousness.

### 37:2

כִּי כֶחָצִיר מְהֵרָה יִמָּלוּ וּכְיֶרֶק דֶּשֶׁא יִבּוֹלוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE kheh-khah-TSEER me-heh-RAH yee-MAH-loo oo-khe-YEH-rehk deh-SHEH yee-boh-LOON

For they shall soon wither like the grass, and fade as the green herb.

### 37:3

בְּטַח בַּיהוָה וַעֲשֵׂה־טוֹב שְׁכָן־אֶרֶץ וּרְעֵה אֱמוּנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-TAHKH bah-ah-doh-NAI vah-ah-seh TOHV she-khahn eh-REHTS oo-re-EH eh-moo-NAH

Trust in the LORD, and do good; dwell in the land, and cherish faithfulness.

### 37:4

וְהִתְעַנַּג עַל־יְהוָה וְיִתֶּן־לְךָ מִשְׁאֲלֹת לִבֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-heet-ah-NAHG ahl ah-doh-NAI ve-yee-tehn le-KHAH meesh-ah-LOHT lee-BEH-khah

So shalt thou delight thyself in the LORD; and He shall give thee the petitions of thy heart.

### 37:5

גּוֹל עַל־יְהוָה דַּרְכֶּךָ וּבְטַח עָלָיו וְהוּא יַעֲשֶׂה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** GOHL ahl ah-doh-NAI dahr-KEH-khah oo-ve-TAHKH ah-LAHV ve-HOO yah-ah-SEH

Commit thy way unto the LORD; trust also in Him, and He will bring it to pass.

### 37:6

וְהוֹצִיא כָאוֹר צִדְקֶךָ וּמִשְׁפָּטֶךָ כַּצָּהֳרָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hoh-TSEE khah-OHR tseed-KEH-khah oo-meesh-pah-TEH-khah kah-tsoh-hoh-RAH-yeem

And He will make thy righteousness to go forth as the light, and thy right as the noonday.

### 37:7

דּוֹם לַיהוָה וְהִתְחוֹלֵל לוֹ אַל־תִּתְחַר בְּמַצְלִיחַ דַּרְכּוֹ בְּאִישׁ עֹשֶׂה מְזִמּוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** DOHM  lah-ah-doh-NAI ve-heet-KHOH-LEHL LOH ahl teet-KHAHR be-mahts-LEE-ahkh dahr-KOH be-EESH oh-SEH me-zee-MOHT

Resign thyself unto the LORD, and wait patiently for Him; fret not thyself because of him who prospereth in his way, because of the man who bringeth wicked devices to pass.

### 37:8

הֶרֶף מֵאַף וַעֲזֹב חֵמָה אַל־תִּתְחַר אַךְ־לְהָרֵעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HEH-rehf meh-AHF vah-ah-ZOHV kheh-MAH ahl teet-KHAHR ahkh le-hah-REH-ah

Cease from anger, and forsake wrath; fret not thyself, it tendeth only to evil-doing.

### 37:9

כִּי־מְרֵעִים יִכָּרֵתוּן וְקֹוֵי יְהוָה הֵמָּה יִירְשׁוּ־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee me-reh-EEM yee-kah-reh-TOON ve-koh-VEY ah-doh-NAI HEH-mah yee-re-shoo AH-rehts

For evil-doers shall be cut off; but those that wait for the LORD, they shall inherit the land.

### 37:10

וְעוֹד מְעַט וְאֵין רָשָׁע וְהִתְבּוֹנַנְתָּ עַל־מְקוֹמוֹ וְאֵינֶנּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-OHD me-AHT ve-EYN rah-SHAH ve-heet-boh-NAHN-tah ahl me-koh-MOH ve-ey-NEH-noo

And yet a little while, and the wicked is no more; yea, thou shalt look well at his place, and he is not.

### 37:11

וַעֲנָוִים יִירְשׁוּ־אָרֶץ וְהִתְעַנְּגוּ עַל־רֹב שָׁלוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-nah-VEEM yee-re-shoo AH-rehts ve-heet-ah-ne-GOO ahl ROHV shah-LOHM

But the humble shall inherit the land, and delight themselves in the abundance of peace.

### 37:12

זֹמֵם רָשָׁע לַצַּדִּיק וְחֹרֵק עָלָיו שִׁנָּיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zoh-MEHM rah-SHAH lah-tsah-DEEK ve-khoh-REHK ah-LAHV shee-NAHV

The wicked plotteth against the righteous, and gnasheth at him with his teeth.

### 37:13

אֲדֹנָי יִשְׂחַק־לוֹ כִּי־רָאָה כִּי־יָבֹא יוֹמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAHY yees-khahk LOH kee rah-AH kee yah-VOH yoh-MOH

The Lord doth laugh at him; for He seeth that his day is coming.

### 37:14

חֶרֶב פָּתְחוּ רְשָׁעִים וְדָרְכוּ קַשְׁתָּם לְהַפִּיל עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן לִטְבוֹחַ יִשְׁרֵי־דָרֶךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KHEH-rehv  pah-te-KHOO re-shah-EEM ve-dah-re-KHOO kahsh-TAHM le-hah-PEEL ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN leet-VOH-ahkh yeesh-rey DAH-rehkh

The wicked have drawn out the sword, and have bent their bow; to cast down the poor and needy, to slay such as are upright in the way;

### 37:15

חַרְבָּם תָּבוֹא בְלִבָּם וְקַשְּׁתוֹתָם תִּשָּׁבַרְנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khahr-BAHM tah-VOH ve-lee-BAHM ve-kah-she-toh-TAHM tee-shah-VAHR-nah

Their sword shall enter into their own heart, and their bows shall be broken.

### 37:16

טוֹב־מְעַט לַצַּדִּיק מֵהֲמוֹן רְשָׁעִים רַבִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tohv me-AHT lah-tsah-DEEK meh-hah-MOHN re-shah-EEM rah-BEEM

Better is a little that the righteous hath than the abundance of many wicked.

### 37:17

כִּי זְרוֹעוֹת רְשָׁעִים תִּשָּׁבַרְנָה וְסוֹמֵךְ צַדִּיקִים יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ze-roh-OHT re-shah-EEM tee-shah-VAHR-nah ve-soh-MEHKH tsah-dee-KEEM ah-doh-NAI

For the arms of the wicked shall be broken; but the LORD upholdeth the righteous.

### 37:18

יוֹדֵעַ יְהוָה יְמֵי תְמִימִם וְנַחֲלָתָם לְעוֹלָם תִּהְיֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DEH-ah ah-doh-NAI ye-MEY te-mee-MEEM ve-nah-khah-lah-TAHM le-oh-LAHM teeh-YEH

The LORD knoweth the days of them that are wholehearted; and their inheritance shall be for ever.

### 37:19

לֹא־יֵבֹשׁוּ בְּעֵת רָעָה וּבִימֵי רְעָבוֹן יִשְׂבָּעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh yeh-voh-SHOO be-EHT rah-AH oo-vee-MEY re-ah-VOHN yees-BAH-oo

They shall not be ashamed in the time of evil; and in the days of famine they shall be satisfied.

### 37:20

כִּי רְשָׁעִים יֹאבֵדוּ וְאֹיְבֵי יְהוָה כִּיקַר כָּרִים כָּלוּ בֶעָשָׁן כָּלוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE re-shah-EEM  yoh-VEH-doo ve-oh-ye-VEY ah-doh-NAI kee-KAHR kah-REEM kah-LOO veh-ah-SHAHN KAH-loo

For the wicked shall perish, and the enemies of the LORD shall be as the fat of lambs— They shall pass away in smoke, they shall pass away.

### 37:21

לֹוֶה רָשָׁע וְלֹא יְשַׁלֵּם וְצַדִּיק חוֹנֵן וְנוֹתֵן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh-VEH rah-SHAH ve-LOH ye-shah-LEHM ve-tsah-DEEK khoh-NEHN ve-noh-TEHN

The wicked borroweth, and payeth not; but the righteous dealeth graciously, and giveth.

### 37:22

כִּי מְבֹרָכָיו יִירְשׁוּ אָרֶץ וּמְקֻלָּלָיו יִכָּרֵתוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE me-voh-rah-KHAHV YEE-re-shoo AH-rehts oo-me-koo-lah-LAHV yee-kah-REH-too

For such as are blessed of Him shall inherit the land; and they that are cursed of Him shall be cut off.

### 37:23

מֵיְהוָה מִצְעֲדֵי־גֶבֶר כּוֹנָנוּ וְדַרְכּוֹ יֶחְפָּץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meh-ah-doh-NAI mee-tse-ah-dey GEH-vehr koh-NAH-noo ve-dahr-KOH yehkh-PAHTS

It is of the LORD that a man's goings are established; and He delighted in his way.

### 37:24

כִּי־יִפֹּל לֹא־יוּטָל כִּי־יְהוָה סוֹמֵךְ יָדוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee yee-POHL loh yoo-TAHL kee ah-doh-NAI soh-MEHKH yah-DOH

Though he fall, he shall not be utterly cast down; for the LORD upholdeth his hand.

### 37:25

נַעַר הָיִיתִי גַּם־זָקַנְתִּי וְלֹא־רָאִיתִי צַדִּיק נֶעֱזָב וְזַרְעוֹ מְבַקֶּשׁ־לָחֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** NAH-ahr  hah-YEE-tee gahm zah-KAHN-tee ve-loh rah-ee-TEE tsah-DEEK neh-eh-ZAHV ve-zahr-OH me-vah-kehsh LAH-khehm

I have been young, and now am old; yet have I not seen the righteous forsaken, nor his seed begging bread.

### 37:26

כָּל־הַיּוֹם חוֹנֵן וּמַלְוֶה וְזַרְעוֹ לִבְרָכָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl hah-YOHM khoh-NEHN oo-mahl-VEH ve-zahr-OH leev-rah-KHAH

All the day long he dealeth graciously, and lendeth; and his seed is blessed.

### 37:27

סוּר מֵרָע וַעֲשֵׂה־טוֹב וּשְׁכֹן לְעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SOOR meh-RAH vah-ah-seh TOHV oo-she-KHOHN le-oh-LAHM

Depart from evil, and do good; and dwell for evermore.

### 37:28

כִּי יְהוָה אֹהֵב מִשְׁפָּט וְלֹא־יַעֲזֹב אֶת־חֲסִידָיו לְעוֹלָם נִשְׁמָרוּ וְזֶרַע רְשָׁעִים נִכְרָת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ah-doh-NAI  oh-HEHV meesh-PAHT ve-loh yah-ah-ZOHV eht khah-see-DAHV le-oh-LAHM neesh-MAH-roo ve-ZEH-rah re-shah-EEM neekh-RAHT

For the LORD loveth justice, And forsaketh not His saints; they are preserved for ever; but the seed of the wicked shall be cut off.

### 37:29

צַדִּיקִים יִירְשׁוּ־אָרֶץ וְיִשְׁכְּנוּ לָעַד עָלֶיהָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsah-dee-KEEM yee-re-shoo AH-rehts ve-yeesh-ke-NOO lah-AHD ah-LEY-hah

The righteous shall inherit the land, and dwell therein for ever.

### 37:30

פִּי־צַדִּיק יֶהְגֶּה חָכְמָה וּלְשׁוֹנוֹ תְּדַבֵּר מִשְׁפָּט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pee tsah-DEEK yeh-GEH khohkh-MAH oo-le-shoh-NOH te-dah-BEHR meesh-PAHT

The mouth of the righteous uttereth wisdom, and his tongue speaketh justice.

### 37:31

תּוֹרַת אֱלֹהָיו בְּלִבּוֹ לֹא תִמְעַד אֲשֻׁרָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** toh-RAHT eh-loh-HAHV be-lee-BOH LOH teem-AHD ah-shoo-rahv

The law of his God is in his heart; none of his steps slide.

### 37:32

צוֹפֶה רָשָׁע לַצַּדִּיק וּמְבַקֵּשׁ לַהֲמִיתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsoh-FEH rah-SHAH lah-tsah-DEEK oo-me-vah-KEHSH lah-hah-mee-toh

The wicked watcheth the righteous, and seeketh to slay him.

### 37:33

יְהוָה לֹא־יַעַזְבֶנּוּ בְיָדוֹ וְלֹא יַרְשִׁיעֶנּוּ בְּהִשָּׁפְטוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI loh yah-ahz-VEH-noo ve-yah-DOH ve-LOH yahr-shee-EH-noo be-hee-shah-fe-TOH

The LORD will not leave him in his hand, nor suffer him to be condemned when he is judged.

### 37:34

קַוֵּה אֶל־יְהוָה וּשְׁמֹר דַּרְכּוֹ וִירוֹמִמְךָ לָרֶשֶׁת אָרֶץ בְּהִכָּרֵת רְשָׁעִים תִּרְאֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-VEH ehl ah-doh-NAI  oo-she-MOHR dahr-KOH vee-roh-meem-KHAH lah-REH-sheht AH-rehts be-hee-kah-REHT re-shah-EEM teer-EH

Wait for the LORD, and keep His way, and He will exalt thee to inherit the land; when the wicked are cut off, thou shalt see it.

### 37:35

רָאִיתִי רָשָׁע עָרִיץ וּמִתְעָרֶה כְּאֶזְרָח רַעֲנָן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-ee-TEE rah-SHAH ah-REETS oo-meet-ah-REH ke-ehz-RAHKH rah-ah-NAHN

I have seen the wicked in great power, and spreading himself like a leafy tree in its native soil.

### 37:36

וַיַּעֲבֹר וְהִנֵּה אֵינֶנּוּ וָאֲבַקְשֵׁהוּ וְלֹא נִמְצָא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ah-VOHR ve-hee-NEH ey-NEH-noo vah-ah-vahk-SHEH-hoo ve-LOH neem-TSAH

But one passed by, and, lo, he was not; yea, I sought him, but he could not be found.

### 37:37

שְׁמָר־תָּם וּרְאֵה יָשָׁר כִּי־אַחֲרִית לְאִישׁ שָׁלוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-mohr TAHM oo-re-EH yah-SHAHR kee ah-khah-REET le-EESH shah-LOHM

Mark the man of integrity, and behold the upright; for there is a future for the man of peace.

### 37:38

וּפֹשְׁעִים נִשְׁמְדוּ יַחְדָּו אַחֲרִית רְשָׁעִים נִכְרָתָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-foh-she-EEM neesh-me-DOO yahkh-DAHV ah-khah-REET re-shah-EEM neekh-RAH-tah

But transgressors shall be destroyed together; the future of the wicked shall be cut off.

### 37:39

וּתְשׁוּעַת צַדִּיקִים מֵיְהוָה מָעוּזָּם בְּעֵת צָרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-te-shoo-AHT tsah-dee-KEEM meh-ah-doh-NAI mah-oo-ZAHM be-EHT tsah-RAH

But the salvation of the righteous is of the LORD; He is their stronghold in the time of trouble.

### 37:40

וַיַּעְזְרֵם יְהוָה וַיְפַלְּטֵם יְפַלְּטֵם מֵרְשָׁעִים וְיוֹשִׁיעֵם כִּי־חָסוּ בוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ze-REHM ah-doh-NAI vah-ye-fah-le-TEHM ye-fah-le-TEHM meh-re-shah-EEM ve-yoh-shee-EHM kee KHAH-soo VOH

And the LORD helpeth them, and delivereth them; He delivereth them from the wicked, and saveth them, because they have taken refuge in Him.

## Psalm 38 — Affliction, guilt, and a plea for help

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.38) · Buckets: health, repentance

### 38:1

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד לְהַזְכִּיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED le-hahz-KEER

A Psalm of David, to make memorial.

### 38:2

יְהוָה אַל־בְּקֶצְפְּךָ תוֹכִיחֵנִי וּבַחֲמָתְךָ תְיַסְּרֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI ahl be-kehts-pe-KHAH toh-khee-KHEH-nee oo-vah-khah-mah-te-KHAH te-yah-se-REH-nee

O LORD, rebuke me not in Thine anger; neither chasten me in Thy wrath.

### 38:3

כִּי־חִצֶּיךָ נִחֲתוּ בִי וַתִּנְחַת עָלַי יָדֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khee-tseh-KHAH NEE-khah-too VEE vah-teen-KHAHT ah-LAHY yah-DEH-khah

For Thine arrows are gone deep into me, and Thy hand is come down upon me.

### 38:4

אֵין־מְתֹם בִּבְשָׂרִי מִפְּנֵי זַעְמֶךָ אֵין־שָׁלוֹם בַּעֲצָמַי מִפְּנֵי חַטָּאתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eyn me-TOHM beev-sah-REE mee-pe-NEY zah-MEH-khah eyn shah-LOHM bah-ah-tsah-MAHY mee-pe-NEY khah-tah-TEE

There is no soundness in my flesh because of Thine indignation; neither is there any health in my bones because of my sin.

### 38:5

כִּי עֲוֺנֹתַי עָבְרוּ רֹאשִׁי כְּמַשָּׂא כָבֵד יִכְבְּדוּ מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ah-voh-noh-TAHY ah-ve-ROO roh-SHEE ke-mah-SAH khah-VEHD yeekh-be-DOO mee-MEH-nee

For mine iniquities are gone over my head; as a heavy burden they are too heavy for me.

### 38:6

הִבְאִישׁוּ נָמַקּוּ חַבּוּרֹתָי מִפְּנֵי אִוַּלְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heev-EE-shoo nah-mah-KOO khah-boo-roh-TAHY mee-pe-NEY ee-vahl-TEE

My wounds are noisome, they fester, because of my foolishness.

### 38:7

נַעֲוֵיתִי שַׁחֹתִי עַד־מְאֹד כָּל־הַיּוֹם קֹדֵר הִלָּכְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-ah-VEY-tee shah-KHOH-tee ahd me-OHD kohl hah-YOHM koh-DEHR hee-LAH-khe-tee

I am bent and bowed down greatly; I go mourning all the day.

### 38:8

כִּי־כְסָלַי מָלְאוּ נִקְלֶה וְאֵין מְתֹם בִּבְשָׂרִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khe-sah-LAHY mah-le-OO neek-LEH ve-EYN me-TOHM beev-sah-REE

For my loins are filled with burning; and there is no soundness in my flesh.

### 38:9

נְפוּגוֹתִי וְנִדְכֵּיתִי עַד־מְאֹד שָׁאַגְתִּי מִנַּהֲמַת לִבִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-foo-GOH-tee ve-need-KEY-tee ahd me-OHD shah-AHG-tee mee-nah-hah-MAHT lee-BEE

I am benumbed and sore crushed; I groan by reason of the moaning of my heart.

### 38:10

אֲ‍דֹנָי נֶגְדְּךָ כָל־תַּאֲוָתִי וְאַנְחָתִי מִמְּךָ לֹא־נִסְתָּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-nahy‍ nehg-de-KHAH khohl tah-ah-vah-TEE ve-ahn-khah-TEE mee-me-KHAH loh nees-TAH-rah

Lord, all my desire is before Thee; and my sighing is not hid from Thee.

### 38:11

לִבִּי סְחַרְחַר עֲזָבַנִי כֹחִי וְאוֹר־עֵינַי גַּם־הֵם אֵין אִתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lee-BEE se-khahr-KHAHR ah-zah-VAH-nee khoh-KHEE ve-ohr ey-NAHY gahm HEHM EYN ee-TEE

My heart fluttereth, my strength faileth me; as for the light of mine eyes, it also is gone from me.

### 38:12

אֹהֲבַי וְרֵעַי מִנֶּגֶד נִגְעִי יַעֲמֹדוּ וּקְרוֹבַי מֵרָחֹק עָמָדוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-hah-VAHY  ve-reh-AHY mee-NEH-gehd neeg-EE yah-ah-MOH-doo oo-ke-roh-VAHY meh-rah-KHOHK ah-MAH-doo

My friends and my companions stand aloof from my plague; and my kinsmen stand afar off.

### 38:13

וַיְנַקְשׁוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי וְדֹרְשֵׁי רָעָתִי דִּבְּרוּ הַוּוֹת וּמִרְמוֹת כָּל־הַיּוֹם יֶהְגּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-nahk-SHOO  me-vahk-SHEY nahf-SHEE ve-doh-re-SHEY rah-ah-TEE dee-be-ROO hah-VOHT oo-meer-MOHT kohl hah-YOHM yeh-GOO

They also that seek after my life lay snares for me; and they that seek my hurt speak crafty devices, and utter deceits all the day.

### 38:14

וַאֲנִי כְחֵרֵשׁ לֹא אֶשְׁמָע וּכְאִלֵּם לֹא יִפְתַּח־פִּיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE khe-kheh-REHSH LOH ehsh-MAH oo-khe-ee-LEHM LOH yeef-tahkh PEEV

But I am as a deaf man, I hear not; and I am as a dumb man that openeth not his mouth.

### 38:15

וָאֱהִי כְּאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר לֹא־שֹׁמֵעַ וְאֵין בְּפִיו תּוֹכָחוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-eh-HEE ke-EESH ah-SHEHR loh shoh-MEH-ah ve-EYN be-FEEV toh-khah-KHOHT

Yea, I am become as a man that heareth not, and in whose mouth are no arguments.

### 38:16

כִּי־לְךָ יְהוָה הוֹחָלְתִּי אַתָּה תַעֲנֶה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee le-KHAH ah-doh-NAI hoh-KHAH-le-tee ah-TAH tah-ah-NEH ah-doh-NAHY eh-loh-HAHY

For in Thee, O LORD, do I hope; Thou wilt answer, O Lord my God.

### 38:17

כִּי־אָמַרְתִּי פֶּן־יִשְׂמְחוּ־לִי בְּמוֹט רַגְלִי עָלַי הִגְדִּילוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-mahr-TEE pehn yees-me-khoo LEE be-MOHT rahg-LEE ah-LAHY heeg-DEE-loo

For I said: 'Lest they rejoice over me; when my foot slippeth, they magnify themselves against me.'

### 38:18

כִּי־אֲנִי לְצֶלַע נָכוֹן וּמַכְאוֹבִי נֶגְדִּי תָמִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-NEE le-TSEH-lah nah-KHOHN oo-mahkh-oh-VEE nehg-DEE tah-MEED

For I am ready to halt, and my pain is continually before me.

### 38:19

כִּי־עֲוֺנִי אַגִּיד אֶדְאַג מֵחַטָּאתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-voh-NEE ah-GEED ehd-AHG meh-khah-tah-TEE

For I do declare mine iniquity; I am full of care because of my sin.

### 38:20

וְאֹיְבַי חַיִּים עָצֵמוּ וְרַבּוּ שֹׂנְאַי שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-oh-ye-VAHY khah-YEEM ah-TSEH-moo ve-rah-BOO soh-ne-AHY SHAH-kehr

But mine enemies are strong in health; and they that hate me wrongfully are multiplied.

### 38:21

וּמְשַׁלְּמֵי רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה יִשְׂטְנוּנִי תַּחַת רדופי־[רָדְפִי־] טוֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-me-shah-le-MEY rah-AH TAH-khaht toh-VAH yees-te-NOO-nee TAH-khaht rah-de-fee  TOHV

They also that repay evil for good are adversaries unto me, because I follow the thing that is good.

Reading note: written רדופי־ → read רָֽדְפִי־. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 38:22

אַל־תַּעַזְבֵנִי יְהוָה אֱלֹהַי אַל־תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHY ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee

Forsake me not, O LORD; O my God, be not far from me.

### 38:23

חוּשָׁה לְעֶזְרָתִי אֲדֹנָי תְּשׁוּעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KHOO-shah le-ehz-rah-TEE ah-doh-NAHY te-shoo-ah-TEE

Make haste to help me, O Lord, my salvation.

## Psalm 39 — Human frailty and the brevity of life

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.39) · Buckets: grief, wellness, health

### 39:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לידיתון [לִידוּתוּן] מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh lee-doo-TOON meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader, for Jeduthun. A Psalm of David.

Reading note: written לידיתון → read לִֽידוּת֗וּן. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 39:2

אָמַרְתִּי אֶשְׁמְרָה דְרָכַי מֵחֲטוֹא בִלְשׁוֹנִי אֶשְׁמְרָה לְפִי מַחְסוֹם בְּעֹד רָשָׁע לְנֶגְדִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR-tee eh-she-me-RAH de-rah-KHAHY meh-khah-TOH veel-shoh-NEE ehsh-me-RAH le-FEE mahkh-SOHM be-OHD rah-SHAH le-nehg-DEE

I said: 'I will take heed to my ways, that I sin not with my tongue; I will keep a curb upon my mouth, while the wicked is before me.'

### 39:3

נֶאֱלַמְתִּי דוּמִיָּה הֶחֱשֵׁיתִי מִטּוֹב וּכְאֵבִי נֶעְכָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neh-eh-LAHM-tee doo-mee-YAH heh-kheh-SHEY-tee mee-TOHV oo-khe-eh-VEE neh-KAHR

I was dumb with silence; I held my peace, had no comfort; and my pain was held in check.

### 39:4

חַם־לִבִּי בְּקִרְבִּי בַּהֲגִיגִי תִבְעַר־אֵשׁ דִּבַּרְתִּי בִּלְשׁוֹנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khahm lee-BEE  be-keer-BEE bah-hah-gee-GEE teev-ahr EHSH dee-BAHR-tee beel-SHOH-nee

My heart waxed hot within me; while I was musing, the fire kindled; Then spoke I with my tongue:

### 39:5

הוֹדִיעֵנִי יְהוָה קִצִּי וּמִדַּת יָמַי מַה־הִיא אֵדְעָה מֶה־חָדֵל אָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-dee-EH-nee ah-doh-NAI  kee-TSEE oo-mee-DAHT yah-MAHY mahh HEE eh-de-AH meh khah-DEHL AH-nee

'LORD, make me to know mine end, And the measure of my days, what it is; Let me know how short-lived I am.

### 39:6

הִנֵּה טְפָחוֹת נָתַתָּה יָמַי וְחֶלְדִּי כְאַיִן נֶגְדֶּךָ אַךְ כָּל־הֶבֶל כָּל־אָדָם נִצָּב סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH te-fah-KHOHT  nah-TAH-tah yah-MAHY ve-khehl-DEE khe-AH-yeen nehg-DEH-khah AHKH kahl HEH-vehl kohl ah-DAHM nee-TSAHV SEH-lah

Behold, Thou hast made my days as hand-breadths; and mine age is as nothing before Thee; surely every man at his best estate is altogether vanity. Selah

### 39:7

אַךְ־בְּצֶלֶם יִתְהַלֶּךְ־אִישׁ אַךְ־הֶבֶל יֶהֱמָיוּן יִצְבֹּר וְלֹא־יֵדַע מִי־אֹסְפָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahkh be-TSEH-lehm  yee-te-hah-lehkh EESH ahkh HEH-vehl yeh-heh-mah-YOON yeets-BOHR ve-loh yeh-DAH mee oh-se-FAHM

Surely man walketh as a mere semblance; surely for vanity they are in turmoil; He heapeth up riches, and knoweth not who shall gather them.

### 39:8

וְעַתָּה מַה־קִּוִּיתִי אֲדֹנָי תּוֹחַלְתִּי לְךָ הִיא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH mahh kee-VEE-tee ah-doh-NAHY toh-khahl-TEE le-KHAH HEE

And now, Lord, what wait I for? My hope, it is in Thee.

### 39:9

מִכָּל־פְּשָׁעַי הַצִּילֵנִי חֶרְפַּת נָבָל אַל־תְּשִׂימֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-kohl pe-shah-AHY hah-tsee-LEH-nee khehr-PAHT nah-VAHL ahl te-see-MEH-nee

Deliver me from all my transgressions; make me not the reproach of the base.

### 39:10

נֶאֱלַמְתִּי לֹא אֶפְתַּח־פִּי כִּי אַתָּה עָשִׂיתָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neh-eh-lahm-TEE LOH ehf-tahkh PEE KEE ah-TAH ah-SEE-tah

I am dumb, I open not my mouth; because Thou hast done it.

### 39:11

הָסֵר מֵעָלַי נִגְעֶךָ מִתִּגְרַת יָדְךָ אֲנִי כָלִיתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-SEHR meh-ah-LAHY neeg-EH-khah mee-teeg-RAHT yah-de-KHAH ah-NEE khah-LEE-tee

Remove Thy stroke from off me; I am consumed by the blow of Thy hand.

### 39:12

בְּתוֹכָחוֹת עַל־עָוֺן יִסַּרְתָּ אִישׁ וַתֶּמֶס כָּעָשׁ חֲמוּדוֹ אַךְ הֶבֶל כָּל־אָדָם סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-toh-khah-KHOHT ahl ah-VOHN  yee-SAHR-tah EESH vah-TEH-mehs kah-AHSH khah-moo-DOH AHKH HEH-vehl kohl ah-DAHM SEH-lah

With rebukes dost Thou chasten man for iniquity, and like a moth Thou makest his beauty to consume away; surely every man is vanity. Selah

### 39:13

שִׁמְעָה־תְפִלָּתִי יְהוָה וְשַׁוְעָתִי הַאֲזִינָה אֶל־דִּמְעָתִי אַל־תֶּחֱרַשׁ כִּי גֵר אָנֹכִי עִמָּךְ תּוֹשָׁב כְּכָל־אֲבוֹתָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-me-AH te-fee-lah-TEE  ah-doh-NAI ve-shahv-ah-TEE  hah-ah-zee-NAH ehl deem-ah-TEE ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH KEE GEHR ah-noh-KHEE ee-MAHKH toh-SHAHV ke-khohl ah-voh-TAHY

Hear my prayer, O LORD, and give ear unto my cry; keep not silence at my tears; for I am a stranger with Thee, a sojourner, as all my fathers were.

### 39:14

הָשַׁע מִמֶּנִּי וְאַבְלִיגָה בְּטֶרֶם אֵלֵךְ וְאֵינֶנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-SHAH mee-MEH-nee ve-ahv-LEE-gah be-TEH-rehm eh-LEHKH ve-ey-NEH-nee

Look away from me, that I may take comfort, before I go hence, and be no more.'

## Psalm 40 — Rescue, willingness, and renewed need

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.40) · Buckets: gratitude, hope

### 40:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh le-dah-VEED meez-MOHR

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

### 40:2

קַוֺּה קִוִּיתִי יְהוָה וַיֵּט אֵלַי וַיִּשְׁמַע שַׁוְעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-VOHH kee-VEE-tee ah-doh-NAI vah-YEHT eh-LAHY vah-yeesh-MAH shahv-ah-TEE

I waited patiently for the LORD; and He inclined unto me, and heard my cry.

### 40:3

וַיַּעֲלֵנִי מִבּוֹר שָׁאוֹן מִטִּיט הַיָּוֵן וַיָּקֶם עַל־סֶלַע רַגְלַי כּוֹנֵן אֲשֻׁרָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ah-LEH-nee  mee-BOHR shah-OHN mee-TEET hah-yah-VEHN vah-YAH-kehm ahl SEH-lah rahg-LAHY koh-NEHN ah-shoo-RAHY

He brought me up also out of the tumultuous pit, out of the miry clay; and He set my feet upon a rock, He established my goings.

### 40:4

וַיִּתֵּן בְּפִי שִׁיר חָדָשׁ תְּהִלָּה לֵאלֹהֵינוּ יִרְאוּ רַבִּים וְיִירָאוּ וְיִבְטְחוּ בַּיהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN be-FEE  SHEER khah-DAHSH te-hee-LAH leh-loh-HEY-noo yeer-OO rah-BEEM ve-yee-RAH-oo ve-yeev-te-KHOO bah-ah-doh-NAI

And He hath put a new song in my mouth, even praise unto our God; many shall see, and fear, and shall trust in the LORD.

### 40:5

אַשְׁרֵי הַגֶּבֶר אֲשֶׁר־שָׂם יְהֹוָה מִבְטַחוֹ וְלֹא־פָנָה אֶל־רְהָבִים וְשָׂטֵי כָזָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHSH-rey hah-GEH-vehr ah-shehr SAHM ah-doh-NAI meev-tah-KHOH ve-loh fah-NAH ehl re-hah-VEEM ve-sah-TEY khah-ZAHV

Happy is the man that hath made the LORD his trust, and hath not turned unto the arrogant, nor unto such as fall away treacherously.

### 40:6

רַבּוֹת עָשִׂיתָ אַתָּה יְהוָה אֱלֹהַי נִפְלְאֹתֶיךָ וּמַחְשְׁבֹתֶיךָ אֵלֵינוּ אֵין עֲרֹךְ אֵלֶיךָ אַגִּידָה וַאֲדַבֵּרָה עָצְמוּ מִסַּפֵּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BOHT ah-SEE-tah  ah-TAH  ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHY nee-fe-le-oh-TEY-khah oo-mahkh-she-voh-TEY-khah eh-LEY-noo EYN  ah-ROHKH eh-LEY-khah ah-GEE-dah vah-ah-dah-BEH-rah ah-tse-MOO mee-sah-PEHR

Many things hast Thou done, O LORD my God, even Thy wonderful works, and Thy thoughts toward us; there is none to be compared unto Thee! If I would declare and speak of them, they are more than can be told.

### 40:7

זֶבַח וּמִנְחָה לֹא־חָפַצְתָּ אָזְנַיִם כָּרִיתָ לִּי עוֹלָה וַחֲטָאָה לֹא שָׁאָלְתָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZEH-vahkh oo-meen-KHAH  loh khah-FAHTS-tah ohz-nah-YEEM kah-REE-tah LEE oh-LAH vah-khah-tah-AH LOH shah-AH-le-tah

Sacrifice and meal-offering Thou hast no delight in; mine ears hast Thou opened; burnt-offering and sin-offering hast Thou not required.

### 40:8

אָז אָמַרְתִּי הִנֵּה־בָאתִי בִּמְגִלַּת־סֵפֶר כָּתוּב עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHZ ah-mahr-TEE hee-neh VAH-tee beem-gee-laht seh-FEHR kah-TOOV ah-LAHY

Then said I: 'Lo, I am come with the roll of a book which is prescribed for me;

### 40:9

לַעֲשׂוֹת־רְצוֹנְךָ אֱלֹהַי חָפָצְתִּי וְתוֹרָתְךָ בְּתוֹךְ מֵעָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-ah-soht re-tsoh-ne-KHAH eh-loh-HAHY khah-FAH-tse-tee ve-TOH-rah-te-KHAH be-TOHKH meh-AHY

I delight to do Thy will, O my God; yea, Thy law is in my inmost parts.'

### 40:10

בִּשַּׂרְתִּי צֶדֶק בְּקָהָל רָב הִנֵּה שְׂפָתַי לֹא אֶכְלָא יְהוָה אַתָּה יָדָעְתָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bee-SAHR-tee TSEH-dehk  be-kah-HAHL RAHV hee-NEH se-fah-TAHY LOH ehkh-LAH ah-doh-NAI ah-TAH yah-DAH-e-tah

I have preached righteousness in the great congregation, Lo, I did not refrain my lips; O LORD, Thou knowest.

### 40:11

צִדְקָתְךָ לֹא־כִסִּיתִי בְּתוֹךְ לִבִּי אֱמוּנָתְךָ וּתְשׁוּעָתְךָ אָמָרְתִּי לֹא־כִחַדְתִּי חַסְדְּךָ וַאֲמִתְּךָ לְקָהָל רָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tseed-kah-te-KHAH loh khee-SEE-tee  be-TOHKH lee-BEE eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH oo-te-shoo-ah-te-KHAH ah-MAH-re-tee loh khee-KHAHD-tee khahs-de-KHAH vah-ah-mee-te-KHAH le-kah-HAHL RAHV

I have not hid Thy righteousness within my heart; I have declared Thy faithfulness and Thy salvation; I have not concealed Thy mercy and Thy truth from the great congregation.

### 40:12

אַתָּה יְהוָה לֹא־תִכְלָא רַחֲמֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי חַסְדְּךָ וַאֲמִתְּךָ תָּמִיד יִצְּרוּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI loh teekh-LAH rah-khah-MEY-khah mee-MEH-nee khahs-de-KHAH vah-ah-mee-te-KHAH tah-MEED yee-tse-ROO-nee

Thou, O LORD, wilt not withhold Thy compassions from me; let Thy mercy and Thy truth continually preserve me.

### 40:13

כִּי אָפְפוּ־עָלַי רָעוֹת עַד־אֵין מִסְפָּר הִשִּׂיגוּנִי עֲוֺנֹתַי וְלֹא־יָכֹלְתִּי לִרְאוֹת עָצְמוּ מִשַּׂעֲרוֹת רֹאשִׁי וְלִבִּי עֲזָבָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ah-fe-FOO ah-LAHY  rah-OHT ahd EYN mees-PAHR hee-see-GOO-nee ah-voh-noh-TAHY ve-loh yah-KHOH-le-tee leer-OHT ah-tse-MOO mee-sah-ah-ROHT roh-SHEE ve-lee-BEE ah-zah-VAH-nee

For innumerable evils have compassed me about, mine iniquities have overtaken me, so that I am not able to look up; they are more than the hairs of my head, and my heart hath failed me.

### 40:14

רְצֵה יְהוָה לְהַצִּילֵנִי יְהוָה לְעֶזְרָתִי חוּשָׁה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** re-TSEH ah-doh-NAI le-hah-tsee-LEH-nee ah-doh-NAI le-ehz-RAH-tee KHOO-shah

Be pleased, O LORD, to deliver me; O LORD, make haste to help me.

### 40:15

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ יַחַד מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי לִסְפּוֹתָהּ יִסֹּגוּ אָחוֹר וְיִכָּלְמוּ חֲפֵצֵי רָעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo ve-yahkh-pe-ROO  yah-KHAHD me-vahk-SHEY nahf-SHEE lees-poh-TAHH yee-SOH-goo ah-KHOHR ve-yee-kah-le-MOO khah-feh-TSEY rah-ah-TEE

Let them be ashamed and abashed together that seek after my soul to sweep it away; let them be turned backward and brought to confusion that delight in my hurt.

### 40:16

יָשֹׁמּוּ עַל־עֵקֶב בָּשְׁתָּם הָאֹמְרִים לִי הֶאָח הֶאָח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-shoh-MOO ahl EH-kehv bohsh-TAHM hah-oh-me-REEM LEE heh-AHKH  heh-AHKH

Let them be appalled by reason of their shame that say unto me: 'Aha, aha.'

### 40:17

יָשִׂישׂוּ וְיִשְׂמְחוּ בְּךָ כָּל־מְבַקְשֶׁיךָ יֹאמְרוּ תָמִיד יִגְדַּל יְהוָה אֹהֲבֵי תְּשׁוּעָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SEE-soo ve-yees-me-KHOO  be-KHAH kahl me-vahk-SHEY-khah yoh-me-ROO tah-MEED yeeg-DAHL ah-doh-NAI oh-hah-VEY te-shoo-ah-TEH-khah

Let all those that seek Thee rejoice and be glad in Thee; let such as love Thy salvation say continually: 'The LORD be magnified.'

### 40:18

וַאֲנִי עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן אֲדֹנָי יַחֲשָׁב לִי עֶזְרָתִי וּמְפַלְטִי אַתָּה אֱלֹהַי אַל־תְּאַחַר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN ah-doh-NAHY yah-khah-SHAHV LEE ehz-rah-TEE oo-me-fahl-TEE AH-tah eh-loh-HAHY ahl te-ah-KHAHR

But, as for me, that am poor and needy, the Lord will account it unto me; Thou art my help and my deliverer; O my God, tarry not.

## Psalm 41 — Care for the vulnerable and sickness

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.41) · Buckets: health, caregiving

### 41:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

### 41:2

אַשְׁרֵי מַשְׂכִּיל אֶל־דָּל בְּיוֹם רָעָה יְמַלְּטֵהוּ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY mahs-KEEL ehl DAHL be-YOHM rah-AH ye-mah-le-TEH-hoo ah-doh-NAI

Happy is he that considereth the poor; the LORD will deliver him in the day of evil.

### 41:3

יְהוָה יִשְׁמְרֵהוּ וִיחַיֵּהוּ יאשר [וְאֻשַּׁר] בָּאָרֶץ וְאַל־תִּתְּנֵהוּ בְּנֶפֶשׁ אֹיְבָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI  yeesh-me-REH-hoo vee-khah-yeh-HOO ve-oo-SHAHR bah-AH-rehts ve-ahl tee-te-NEH-hoo be-NEH-fehsh oh-ye-VAHV

The LORD preserve him, and keep him alive, let him be called happy in the land; and deliver not Thou him unto the greed of his enemies.

Reading note: written יאשר → read וְאֻשַּׁ֣ר. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 41:4

יְהוָה יִסְעָדֶנּוּ עַל־עֶרֶשׂ דְּוָי כָּל־מִשְׁכָּבוֹ הָפַכְתָּ בְחָלְיוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI yees-ah-deh-NOO ahl EH-rehs de-VAHY kohl meesh-kah-VOH hah-FAHKH-tah ve-khohl-YOH

The LORD support him upon the bed of illness; mayest Thou turn all his lying down in his sickness.

### 41:5

אֲ‍נִי־אָמַרְתִּי יְהוָה חָנֵּנִי רְפָאָה נַפְשִׁי כִּי־חָטָאתִי לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-ye-VAHY yoh-me-ROO RAH LEE mah-TAHY yah-MOOT ve-ah-VAHD she-MOH

### 41:8

יַחַד עָלַי יִתְלַחֲשׁוּ כָּל־שֹׂנְאָי עָלַי יַחְשְׁבוּ רָעָה לִי׃

### 41:11

**Pronunciation draft:** be-ZOHT yah-dah-TEE kee khah-FAHTS-tah BEE KEE loh yah-REE-ah oh-ye-VEE ah-LAHY

## Psalm 42 — Longing, tears, and hope

### 42:1

### 42:6

### 43:1

Yet Thou hast cast off, and brought us to confusion; and goest not forth with our hosts.

### 44:11

תְּשִׁיבֵנוּ אָחוֹר מִנִּי־צָר וּמְשַׂנְאֵינוּ שָׁסוּ לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-shee-VEH-noo ah-KHOHR mee-nee TSAHR oo-me-sahn-EY-noo SHAH-soo LAH-moh

Thou makest us to turn back from the adversary; and they that hate us spoil at their will.

### 44:12

תִּתְּנֵנוּ כְּצֹאן מַאֲכָל וּבַגּוֹיִם זֵרִיתָנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tee-te-neh-NOO ke-TSOHN mah-ah-KHAHL oo-vah-goh-YEEM zeh-ree-TAH-noo

Thou hast given us like sheep to be eaten; and hast scattered us among the nations.

### 44:13

תִּמְכֹּר־עַמְּךָ בְלֹא־הוֹן וְלֹא־רִבִּיתָ בִּמְחִירֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** teem-kohr ah-me-KHAH ve-loh HOHN ve-LOH ree-BEE-tah beem-khee-rey-HEHM

Thou sellest Thy people for small gain, and hast not set their prices high.

### 44:14

תְּשִׂימֵנוּ חֶרְפָּה לִשְׁכֵנֵינוּ לַעַג וָקֶלֶס לִסְבִיבוֹתֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-see-MEH-noo khehr-PAH leesh-kheh-NEY-noo LAH-ahg vah-KEH-lehs lees-vee-voh-TEY-noo

Thou makest us a taunt to our neighbours, a scorn and a derision to them that are round about us.

### 44:15

תְּשִׂימֵנוּ מָשָׁל בַּגּוֹיִם מְנוֹד־רֹאשׁ בַּל־אֻמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-see-MEH-noo mah-SHAHL bah-goh-YEEM me-nohd ROHSH bahl oo-MEEM

Thou makest us a byword among the nations, a shaking of the head among the peoples.

### 44:16

כָּל־הַיּוֹם כְּלִמָּתִי נֶגְדִּי וּבֹשֶׁת פָּנַי כִּסָּתְנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl hah-YOHM ke-lee-mah-TEE nehg-DEE oo-VOH-sheht pah-NAHY kee-SAH-te-nee

All the day is my confusion before me, and the shame of my face hath covered me,

### 44:17

מִקּוֹל מְחָרֵף וּמְגַדֵּף מִפְּנֵי אוֹיֵב וּמִתְנַקֵּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-KOHL me-khah-REHF oo-me-gah-DEHF mee-pe-NEY oh-YEHV oo-meet-nah-KEHM

For the voice of him that taunteth and blasphemeth; by reason of the enemy and the revengeful.

### 44:18

כָּל־זֹאת בָּאַתְנוּ וְלֹא שְׁכַחֲנוּךָ וְלֹא־שִׁקַּרְנוּ בִּבְרִיתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl ZOHT bah-aht-NOO ve-LOH she-khah-khah-NOO-khah ve-loh shee-KAHR-noo beev-ree-TEH-khah

All this is come upon us; yet have we not forgotten Thee, neither have we been false to Thy covenant.

### 44:19

לֹא־נָסוֹג אָחוֹר לִבֵּנוּ וַתֵּט אֲשֻׁרֵינוּ מִנִּי אָרְחֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh nah-SOHG ah-KHOHR lee-BEH-noo vah-TEHT ah-shoo-REY-noo mee-NEE ohr-KHEH-khah

Our heart is not turned back, neither have our steps declined from Thy path;

### 44:20

כִּי דִכִּיתָנוּ בִּמְקוֹם תַּנִּים וַתְּכַס עָלֵינוּ בְצַלְמָוֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE dee-kee-tah-NOO beem-KOHM tah-NEEM vah-te-KHAHS ah-LEY-noo ve-tsahl-MAH-veht

Though Thou hast crushed us into a place of jackals, and covered us with the shadow of death.

### 44:21

אִם־שָׁכַחְנוּ שֵׁם אֱלֹהֵינוּ וַנִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפֵּינוּ לְאֵל זָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eem shah-khahkh-NOO SHEHM eh-loh-HEY-noo vah-neef-ROHS kah-PEY-noo le-EHL ZAHR

If we had forgotten the name of our God, or spread forth our hands to a strange god; .

### 44:22

הֲלֹא אֱלֹהִים יַחֲקָר־זֹאת כִּי־הוּא יֹדֵעַ תַּעֲלֻמוֹת לֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-LOH eh-loh-HEEM yah-khah-kahr ZOHT kee HOO yoh-DEH-ah tah-ah-loo-MOHT LEHV

Would not God search this out? For He knoweth the secrets of the heart.

### 44:23

כִּי־עָלֶיךָ הֹרַגְנוּ כָל־הַיּוֹם נֶחְשַׁבְנוּ כְּצֹאן טִבְחָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-ley-KHAH hoh-RAHG-noo khohl hah-YOHM nehkh-SHAHV-noo ke-TSOHN teev-KHAH

Nay, but for Thy sake are we killed all the day; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter.

### 44:24

עוּרָה לָמָּה תִישַׁן אֲדֹנָי הָקִיצָה אַל־תִּזְנַח לָנֶצַח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** OO-rah  LAH-mah tee-SHAHN  ah-doh-NAHY hah-KEE-tsah ahl teez-NAHKH lah-NEH-tsahkh

Awake, why sleepest Thou, O Lord? Arouse Thyself, cast not off for ever.

### 44:25

לָמָּה־פָנֶיךָ תַסְתִּיר תִּשְׁכַּח עָנְיֵנוּ וְלַחֲצֵנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-mah fah-NEY-khah tahs-TEER teesh-KAHKH ohn-YEH-noo ve-lah-khah-TSEH-noo

Wherefore hidest Thou Thy face, and forgettest our affliction and our oppression?

### 44:26

כִּי שָׁחָה לֶעָפָר נַפְשֵׁנוּ דָּבְקָה לָאָרֶץ בִּטְנֵנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE SHAH-khah leh-ah-FAHR nahf-SHEH-noo dah-ve-KAH lah-AH-rehts beet-NEH-noo

For our soul is bowed down to the dust; our belly cleaveth unto the earth.

### 44:27

קוּמָה עֶזְרָתָה לָּנוּ וּפְדֵנוּ לְמַעַן חַסְדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ehz-RAH-tah LAH-noo oo-fe-DEH-noo le-MAH-ahn khahs-DEH-khah

Arise for our help, and redeem us for Thy mercy's sake.

## Psalm 45 — A royal wedding song

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.45) · Buckets: relationships, life-cycle

### 45:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־שֹׁשַׁנִּים לִבְנֵי־קֹרַח מַשְׂכִּיל שִׁיר יְדִידֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl shoh-shah-NEEM leev-ney KOH-rahkh mahs-KEEL SHEER ye-dee-DOHT

For the Leader; upon Shoshannim; [a Psalm] of the sons of Korah. Maschil. A Song of loves.

### 45:2

רָחַשׁ לִבִּי דָּבָר טוֹב אֹמֵר אָנִי מַעֲשַׂי לְמֶלֶךְ לְשׁוֹנִי עֵט סוֹפֵר מָהִיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-KHAHSH lee-BEE  dah-VAHR TOHV oh-MEHR ah-NEE mah-ah-SAHY le-MEH-lehkh le-shoh-NEE EHT  soh-FEHR mah-HEER

My heart overfloweth with a goodly matter; I say: 'My work is concerning a king'; My tongue is the pen of a ready writer.

### 45:3

יָפְיָפִיתָ מִבְּנֵי אָדָם הוּצַק חֵן בְּשְׂפְתוֹתֶיךָ עַל־כֵּן בֵּרַכְךָ אֱלֹהִים לְעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yohf-yah-FEE-tah mee-be-NEY ah-DAHM HOO-tsahk KHEHN be-se-fe-toh-TEY-khah ahl KEHN beh-rahkh-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM le-oh-LAHM

Thou art fairer than the children of men; Grace is poured upon thy lips; Therefore God hath blessed thee for ever.

### 45:4

חֲגוֹר־חַרְבְּךָ עַל־יָרֵךְ גִּבּוֹר הוֹדְךָ וַהֲדָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-gohr khahr-be-KHAH ahl yah-REHKH gee-BOHR hoh-de-KHAH vah-hah-dah-REH-khah

Gird thy sword upon thy thigh, O mighty one, Thy glory and thy majesty.

### 45:5

וַהֲדָרְךָ צְלַח רְכַב עַל־דְּבַר־אֱמֶת וְעַנְוָה־צֶדֶק וְתוֹרְךָ נוֹרָאוֹת יְמִינֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-hah-DAH-re-KHAH  tse-LAHKH re-KHAHV ahl de-vahr eh-MEHT ve-ahn-vah TSEH-dehk ve-toh-re-KHAH noh-rah-OHT ye-mee-NEH-khah

And in thy majesty prosper, ride on, In behalf of truth and meekness and righteousness; And let thy right hand teach thee tremendous things.

### 45:6

חִצֶּיךָ שְׁנוּנִים עַמִּים תַּחְתֶּיךָ יִפְּלוּ בְּלֵב אוֹיְבֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khee-TSEH-khah she-noo-NEEM ah-MEEM tahkh-TEY-khah yee-pe-LOO be-LEHV oh-ye-VEY hah-MEH-lehkh

Thine arrows are sharp— The peoples fall under thee— [They sink] into the heart of the king's enemies.

### 45:7

כִּסְאֲךָ אֱלֹהִים עוֹלָם וָעֶד שֵׁבֶט מִישֹׁר שֵׁבֶט מַלְכוּתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kees-ah-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM oh-LAHM vah-EHD SHEH-veht mee-SHOHR SHEH-veht mahl-khoo-TEH-khah

Thy throne given of God is for ever and ever; A sceptre of equity is the sceptre of thy kingdom.

### 45:8

אָהַבְתָּ צֶּדֶק וַתִּשְׂנָא רֶשַׁע עַל־כֵּן מְשָׁחֲךָ אֱלֹהִים אֱלֹהֶיךָ שֶׁמֶן שָׂשׂוֹן מֵחֲבֵרֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-HAHV-tah tseh-DEHK vah-tees-NAH REH-shah ahl KEHN  me-shah-khah-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM eh-loh-hey-KHAH SHEH-mehn sah-SOHN meh-khah-veh-REY-khah

Thou hast loved righteousness, and hated wickedness; Therefore God, thy God, hath anointed thee With the oil of gladness above thy fellows.

### 45:9

מֹר־וַאֲהָלוֹת קְצִיעוֹת כָּל־בִּגְדֹתֶיךָ מִן־הֵיכְלֵי שֵׁן מִנִּי שִׂמְּחוּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mohr vah-ah-hah-LOHT ke-tsee-OHT kohl beeg-doh-TEY-khah meen HEY-khe-ley SHEHN mee-NEE see-me-KHOO-khah

Myrrh, and aloes, and cassia are all thy garments; Out of ivory palaces stringed instruments have made thee glad.

### 45:10

בְּנוֹת מְלָכִים בְּיִקְּרוֹתֶיךָ נִצְּבָה שֵׁגַל לִימִינְךָ בְּכֶתֶם אוֹפִיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-NOHT me-lah-KHEEM be-yee-ke-roh-TEY-khah nee-tse-VAH sheh-GAHL lee-mee-ne-KHAH be-KHEH-tehm oh-FEER

Kings' daughters are among thy favourites; At thy right hand doth stand the queen in gold of Ophir.

### 45:11

שִׁמְעִי־בַת וּרְאִי וְהַטִּי אָזְנֵךְ וְשִׁכְחִי עַמֵּךְ וּבֵית אָבִיךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheem-ee VAHT oo-re-EE ve-hah-TEE ohz-NEHKH ve-sheekh-KHEE ah-MEHKH oo-VEYT ah-VEEKH

'Hearken, O daughter, and consider, and incline thine ear; Forget also thine own people, and thy father's house;

### 45:12

וְיִתְאָו הַמֶּלֶךְ יָפְיֵךְ כִּי־הוּא אֲדֹנַיִךְ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִי־לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yeet-AHV hah-MEH-lehkh yohf-YEHKH kee HOO ah-doh-NAH-yeekh ve-heesh-tah-khah-vee LOH

So shall the king desire thy beauty; For he is thy lord; and do homage unto him. .

### 45:13

וּבַת־צֹר בְּמִנְחָה פָּנַיִךְ יְחַלּוּ עֲשִׁירֵי עָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-vaht TSOHR  be-meen-KHAH pah-NAH-yeekh ye-khah-LOO ah-SHEE-rey AHM

And, O daughter of Tyre, the richest of the people Shall entreat thy favour with a gift.'

### 45:14

כָּל־כְּבוּדָּה בַת־מֶלֶךְ פְּנִימָה מִמִּשְׁבְּצוֹת זָהָב לְבוּשָׁהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl ke-voo-DAH vaht MEH-lehkh pe-NEE-mah mee-meesh-be-TSOHT zah-HAHV le-voo-SHAHH

All glorious is the king's daughter within the palace; Her raiment is of chequer work inwrought with gold.

### 45:15

לִרְקָמוֹת תּוּבַל לַמֶּלֶךְ בְּתוּלוֹת אַחֲרֶיהָ רֵעוֹתֶיהָ מוּבָאוֹת לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leer-kah-MOHT too-VAHL lah-MEH-lehkh be-too-LOHT ah-khah-rey-HAH reh-oh-TEY-hah MOO-vah-OHT LAHKH

She shall be led unto the king on richly woven stuff; The virgins her companions in her train being brought unto thee.

### 45:16

תּוּבַלְנָה בִּשְׂמָחֹת וָגִיל תְּבֹאֶינָה בְּהֵיכַל מֶלֶךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** too-vahl-NAH bees-mah-KHOHT vah-GEEL te-voh-EY-nah be-HEY-khahl MEH-lehkh

They shall be led with gladness and rejoicing; They shall enter into the king's palace.

### 45:17

תַּחַת אֲבֹתֶיךָ יִהְיוּ בָנֶיךָ תְּשִׁיתֵמוֹ לְשָׂרִים בְּכָל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TAH-khaht ah-voh-tey-KHAH yeeh-YOO vah-NEY-khah te-shee-TEH-moh le-sah-REEM be-khohl hah-AH-rehts

Instead of thy fathers shall be thy sons, Whom thou shalt make princes in all the land.

### 45:18

אַזְכִּירָה שִׁמְךָ בְּכָל־דֹּר וָדֹר עַל־כֵּן עַמִּים יְהוֹדֻךָ לְעֹלָם וָעֶד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahz-KEE-rah sheem-KHAH be-khohl DOHR vah-DOHR ahl KEHN ah-MEEM ye-hoh-DOO-khah le-oh-LAHM vah-EHD

I will make thy name to be remembered in all generations; Therefore shall the peoples praise thee for ever and ever.

## Psalm 46 — Refuge amid upheaval

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.46) · Buckets: protection, peace

### 46:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לִבְנֵי־קֹרַח עַל־עֲלָמוֹת שִׁיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh leev-ney KOH-rahkh ahl ah-lah-MOHT SHEER

For the Leader; [a Psalm] of the sons of Korah; upon Alamoth. A Song.

### 46:2

אֱלֹהִים לָנוּ מַחֲסֶה וָעֹז עֶזְרָה בְצָרוֹת נִמְצָא מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM lah-NOO mah-khah-SEH vah-OHZ ehz-RAH ve-tsah-ROHT neem-TSAH me-OHD

God is our refuge and strength, A very present help in trouble.

### 46:3

עַל־כֵּן לֹא־נִירָא בְּהָמִיר אָרֶץ וּבְמוֹט הָרִים בְּלֵב יַמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl KEHN loh nee-RAH be-hah-MEER AH-rehts oo-ve-MOHT hah-REEM be-LEHV yah-MEEM

Therefore will we not fear, though the earth do change, And though the mountains be moved into the heart of the seas;

### 46:4

יֶהֱמוּ יֶחְמְרוּ מֵימָיו יִרְעֲשׁוּ־הָרִים בְּגַאֲוָתוֹ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-heh-MOO yehkh-me-ROO mey-MAHV yee-re-ah-shoo hah-REEM be-gah-ah-vah-TOH SEH-lah

Though the waters thereof roar and foam, Though the mountains shake at the swelling thereof. Selah

### 46:5

נָהָר פְּלָגָיו יְשַׂמְּחוּ עִיר־אֱלֹהִים קְדֹשׁ מִשְׁכְּנֵי עֶלְיוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-HAHR pe-lah-GAHV ye-sah-me-KHOO eer eh-loh-HEEM ke-DOHSH meesh-ke-NEY ehl-YOHN

There is a river, the streams whereof make glad the city of God, The holiest dwelling-place of the Most High.

### 46:6

אֱלֹהִים בְּקִרְבָּהּ בַּל־תִּמּוֹט יַעְזְרֶהָ אֱלֹהִים לִפְנוֹת בֹּקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM be-keer-BAHH bahl tee-MOHT yah-ze-REH-hah eh-loh-HEEM leef-NOHT BOH-kehr

God is in the midst of her, she shall not be moved; God shall help her, at the approach of morning.

### 46:7

הָמוּ גוֹיִם מָטוּ מַמְלָכוֹת נָתַן בְּקוֹלוֹ תָּמוּג אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-MOO goh-YEEM MAH-too mahm-lah-KHOHT nah-TAHN be-koh-LOH tah-MOOG AH-rehts

Nations were in tumult, kingdoms were moved; He uttered His voice, the earth melted.

### 46:8

יְהוָה צְבָאוֹת עִמָּנוּ מִשְׂגָּב־לָנוּ אֱלֹהֵי יַעֲקֹב סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI tse-vah-OHT ee-MAH-noo mees-gahv lah-NOO eh-loh-HEY yah-ah-KOHV SEH-lah

The LORD of hosts is with us; The God of Jacob is our high tower. Selah

### 46:9

לְכוּ־חֲזוּ מִפְעֲלוֹת יְהוָה אֲשֶׁר־שָׂם שַׁמּוֹת בָּאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-khoo khah-ZOO meef-ah-LOHT ah-doh-NAI ah-shehr SAHM shah-MOHT bah-AH-rehts

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-TSOHN  lee-she-OHL shah-TOO MAH-veht yeer-EHM vah-yeer-DOO VAHM ye-shah-REEM  lah-BOH-kehr ve-tsoo-RAHM le-vah-LOHT she-OHL mee-ze-VOOL LOH

### 56:1

## Psalm 58 — Condemnation of unjust judgment

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.58) · Buckets: justice

### 58:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אַל־תַּשְׁחֵת לְדָוִד מִכְתָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl tahsh-KHEHT le-dah-VEED meekh-TAHM

For the Leader; Al-tashheth. [A Psalm] of David; Michtam.

### 58:2

הַאֻמְנָם אֵלֶם צֶדֶק תְּדַבֵּרוּן מֵישָׁרִים תִּשְׁפְּטוּ בְּנֵי אָדָם׃

### 58:3

אַף־בְּלֵב עוֹלֹת תִּפְעָלוּן בָּאָרֶץ חֲמַס יְדֵיכֶם תְּפַלֵּסוּן׃

### 58:4

זֹרוּ רְשָׁעִים מֵרָחֶם תָּעוּ מִבֶּטֶן דֹּבְרֵי כָזָב׃

The wicked are estranged from the womb; The speakers of lies go astray as soon as they are born.

### 58:5

חֲמַת־לָמוֹ כִּדְמוּת חֲמַת־נָחָשׁ כְּמוֹ־פֶתֶן חֵרֵשׁ יַאְטֵם אָזְנוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-maht LAH-moh keed-MOOT khah-maht nah-KHAHSH ke-moh FEH-tehn kheh-REHSH yah-TEHM ohz-NOH

Their venom is like the venom of a serpent; They are like the deaf asp that stoppeth her ear;

### 58:6

אֲשֶׁר לֹא־יִשְׁמַע לְקוֹל מְלַחֲשִׁים חוֹבֵר חֲבָרִים מְחֻכָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR loh yeesh-MAH le-KOHL me-lah-khah-SHEEM khoh-VEHR khah-vah-REEM me-khoo-KAHM

Which hearkeneth not to the voice of charmers, Or of the most cunning binder of spells.

### 58:7

אֱ‍לֹהִים הֲרָס־שִׁנֵּימוֹ בְּפִימוֹ מַלְתְּעוֹת כְּפִירִים נְתֹץ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM‍ hah-rahs shee-NEY-moh be-FEE-moh mahl-te-OHT ke-fee-REEM ne-TOHTS  ah-doh-NAI

Break their teeth, O God, in their mouth; Break out the cheek-teeth of the young lions, O LORD.

### 58:8

יִמָּאֲסוּ כְמוֹ־מַיִם יִתְהַלְּכוּ־לָמוֹ יִדְרֹךְ חצו [חִצָּיו] כְּמוֹ יִתְמֹלָלוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-mah-ah-SOO khe-moh mah-YEEM yeet-hah-le-khoo LAH-moh yeed-ROHKH khee-TSAHV ke-MOH yeet-moh-LAH-loo

### 58:9

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-MOH shah-be-LOOL TEH-mehs yah-hah-LOHKH NEH-fehl eh-SHEHT bahl KHAH-zoo SHAH-mehsh

Let them be as a snail which melteth and passeth away; Like the untimely births of a woman, that have not seen the sun.

### 58:10

בְּטֶרֶם יָבִינוּ סִּירֹתֵיכֶם אָטָד כְּמוֹ־חַי כְּמוֹ־חָרוֹן יִשְׂעָרֶנּוּ׃

Before your pots can feel the thorns, He will sweep it away with a whirlwind, the raw and the burning alike.

### 58:11

**Pronunciation draft:** yees-MAHKH tsah-DEEK kee khah-ZAH nah-KAHM pe-ah-MAHV yeer-KHAHTS be-DAHM hah-rah-SHAH

The righteous shall rejoice when he seeth the vengeance; He shall wash his feet in the blood of the wicked.

### 58:12

וְיֹאמַר אָדָם אַךְ־פְּרִי לַצַּדִּיק אַךְ יֵשׁ־אֱלֹהִים שֹׁפְטִים בָּאָרֶץ׃

## Psalm 59 — Protection from violent enemies

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.59) · Buckets: protection, justice

### 59:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אַל־תַּשְׁחֵת לְדָוִד מִכְתָּם בִּשְׁלֹחַ שָׁאוּל וַיִּשְׁמְרוּ אֶת־הַבַּיִת לַהֲמִיתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl tahsh-KHEHT le-dah-VEED meekh-TAHM beesh-LOH-ahkh shah-OOL vah-yeesh-me-ROO eht hah-BAH-yeet lah-hah-mee-TOH

For the Leader; Al-tashheth. [A Psalm] of David; Michtam; when Saul sent, and they watched the house to kill him.

### 59:2

הַצִּילֵנִי מֵאֹיְבַי אֱלֹהָי מִּמִתְקוֹמְמַי תְּשַׂגְּבֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-tsee-LEH-nee meh-oh-ye-VAHY  eh-loh-HAHY mee-meet-koh-me-MAHY te-sah-ge-VEH-nee

Deliver me from mine enemies, O my God; set me on high from them that rise up against me.

### 59:3

הַצִּילֵנִי מִפֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן וּמֵאַנְשֵׁי דָמִים הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-tsee-leh-NEE mee-POH-ah-ley AH-vehn oo-meh-ahn-SHEY dah-MEEM hoh-shee-EH-nee

Deliver me from the workers of iniquity, and save me from the men of blood.

### 59:4

כִּי הִנֵּה אָרְבוּ לְנַפְשִׁי יָגוּרוּ עָלַי עַזִים לֹא־פִשְׁעִי וְלֹא־חַטָּאתִי יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE hee-NEH ah-re-VOO le-nahf-SHEE yah-GOO-roo ah-LAHY ah-ZEEM loh feesh-EE ve-loh khah-tah-TEE ah-doh-NAI

For, lo, they lie in wait for my soul; the impudent gather themselves together against me; not for my transgression, nor for my sin, O LORD.

### 59:5

בְּלִי־עָוֺן יְרוּצוּן וְיִכּוֹנָנוּ עוּרָה לִקְרָאתִי וּרְאֵה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-lee ah-VOHN ye-roo-TSOON ve-yee-koh-NAH-noo OO-rah leek-rah-TEE oo-re-eh

Without my fault, they run and prepare themselves; awake Thou to help me, and behold.

### 59:6

וְאַתָּה יְהוָה־אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הָקִיצָה לִפְקֹד כָּל־הַגּוֹיִם אַל־תָּחֹן כָּל־בֹּגְדֵי אָוֶן סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH ah-doh-NAIeh-loh-HEEM  tse-vah-OHT eh-loh-HEY yees-rah-EHL hah-KEE-tsah leef-KOHD kahl hah-goh-YEEM ahl tah-KHOHN kohl BOH-ge-dey AH-vehn SEH-lah

Thou therefore, O LORD God of hosts, the God of Israel, arouse Thyself to punish all the nations; show no mercy to any iniquitous traitors. Selah

### 59:7

יָשׁוּבוּ לָעֶרֶב יֶהֱמוּ כַכָּלֶב וִיסוֹבְבוּ עִיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SHOO-voo lah-eh-REHV yeh-heh-MOO khah-KAH-lehv vee-SOH-ve-voo EER

They return at evening, they howl like a dog, and go round about the city.

### 59:8

הִנֵּה יַבִּיעוּן בְּפִיהֶם חֲרָבוֹת בְּשִׂפְתוֹתֵיהֶם כִּי־מִי שֹׁמֵעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH  yah-bee-OON be-fee-HEHM khah-rah-VOHT be-seef-toh-tey-HEHM kee MEE shoh-MEH-ah

Behold, they belch out with their mouth; swords are in their lips: 'For who doth hear?'

### 59:9

וְאַתָּה יְהוָה תִּשְׂחַק־לָמוֹ תִּלְעַג לְכָל־גּוֹיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI tees-khahk LAH-moh teel-AHG le-khohl goh-YEEM

But Thou, O LORD, shalt laugh at them; Thou shalt have all the nations in derision.

### 59:10

עֻזּוֹ אֵלֶיךָ אֶשְׁמֹרָה כִּי־אֱלֹהִים מִשְׂגַּבִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-ZOH eh-LEY-khah ehsh-MOH-rah kee eh-loh-HEEM mees-gah-BEE

### 59:11

אֱלֹהֵי חסדו [חַסְדִּי] יְקַדְּמֵנִי אֱלֹהִים יַרְאֵנִי בְשֹׁרְרָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEY khahs-DEE ye-kah-de-MEH-nee eh-loh-HEEM yahr-EH-nee ve-shoh-re-RAHY

### 59:12

אַל־תַּהַרְגֵם פֶּן־יִשְׁכְּחוּ עַמִּי הֲנִיעֵמוֹ בְחֵילְךָ וְהוֹרִידֵמוֹ מָגִנֵּנוּ אֲדֹנָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tah-hahr-GEHM  pehn yeesh-ke-KHOO ah-MEE hah-nee-EH-moh ve-khey-le-KHAH ve-hoh-ree-DEH-moh mah-gee-NEH-noo ah-doh-NAHY

Slay them not, lest my people forget, make them wander to and fro by Thy power, and bring them down, O Lord our shield.

### 59:13

חַטַּאת־פִּימוֹ דְּבַר־שְׂפָתֵימוֹ וְיִלָּכְדוּ בִגְאוֹנָם וּמֵאָלָה וּמִכַּחַשׁ יְסַפֵּרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-taht PEE-moh de-vahr se-fah-TEY-moh ve-yee-lah-khe-DOO veeg-oh-NAHM oo-meh-ah-LAH oo-mee-KAH-khahsh ye-sah-PEH-roo

For the sin of their mouth, and the words of their lips, let them even be taken in their pride, and for cursing and lying which they speak.

### 59:14

כַּלֵּה בְחֵמָה כַּלֵּה וְאֵינֵמוֹ וְיֵדְעוּ כִּי־אֱלֹהִים מֹשֵׁל בְּיַעֲקֹב לְאַפְסֵי הָאָרֶץ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-LEH ve-kheh-MAH kah-LEH ve-ey-NEH-moh ve-yeh-de-OO kee eh-loh-HEEM moh-SHEHL be-yah-ah-KOHV le-ahf-SEY hah-AH-rehts SEH-lah

Consume them in wrath, consume them, that they be no more; and let them know that God ruleth in Jacob, unto the ends of the earth. Selah

### 59:15

וְיָשׁוּבוּ לָעֶרֶב יֶהֱמוּ כַכָּלֶב וִיסוֹבְבוּ עִיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yah-SHOO-voo lah-eh-REHV yeh-heh-MOO khah-KAH-lehv vee-SOH-ve-voo EER

And they return at evening, they howl like a dog, and go round about the city;

### 59:16

הֵמָּה ינועון [יְנִיעוּן] לֶאֱכֹל אִם־לֹא יִשְׂבְּעוּ וַיָּלִינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heh-MAH ye-nee-OON leh-eh-KHOHL eem LOH yees-be-OO vah-yah-LEE-noo

### 59:17

וַאֲנִי אָשִׁיר עֻזֶּךָ וַאֲרַנֵּן לַבֹּקֶר חַסְדֶּךָ כִּי־הָיִיתָ מִשְׂגָּב לִי וּמָנוֹס בְּיוֹם צַר־לִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  ah-SHEER oo-zeh-KHAH vah-ah-rah-NEHN lah-BOH-kehr khahs-DEH-khah kee hah-YEE-tah mees-GAHV LEE oo-mah-NOHS be-YOHM tsahr LEE

But as for me, I will sing of Thy strength; yea, I will sing aloud of Thy mercy in the morning; for Thou hast been my high tower, and a refuge in the day of my distress.

### 59:18

## Psalm 60 — Communal defeat and renewed hope

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.60) · Buckets: community, hope

### 60:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־שׁוּשַׁן עֵדוּת מִכְתָּם לְדָוִד לְלַמֵּד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-tseh-AHKH ahl shoo-SHAHN eh-DOOT meekh-TAHM le-dah-VEED le-lah-MEHD

For the Leader; upon Shushan Eduth; Michtam of David, to teach;

### 60:2

בְּהַצּוֹתוֹ אֶת אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם וְאֶת־אֲרַם צוֹבָה וַיָּשָׁב יוֹאָב וַיַּךְ אֶת־אֱדוֹם בְּגֵיא־מֶלַח שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר אָלֶף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-hah-tsoh-TOH  EHT ah-RAHM nah-hah-rah-YEEM ve-eht ah-RAHM tsoh-VAH vah-YAH-shohv yoh-AHV vah-YAHKH eht eh-DOHM be-gey MEH-lahkh she-NEYM ah-SAHR AH-lehf

when he strove with Aram-naharaim and with Aram-zobah, and Joab returned, and smote of Edom in the Valley of Salt twelve thousand.

### 60:3

אֱלֹהִים זְנַחְתָּנוּ פְרַצְתָּנוּ אָנַפְתָּ תְּשׁוֹבֵב לָנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM ze-nahkh-TAH-noo fe-rahts-TAH-noo ah-NAHF-tah te-SHOH-VEHV LAH-noo

O God, Thou hast cast us off, Thou hast broken us down; Thou hast been angry; O restore us.

### 60:4

הִרְעַשְׁתָּה אֶרֶץ פְּצַמְתָּהּ רְפָה שְׁבָרֶיהָ כִי־מָטָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heer-AHSH-tah EH-rehts pe-tsahm-TAHH re-FAH she-vah-REY-hah khee MAH-tah

Thou hast made the land to shake, Thou hast cleft it; Heal the breaches thereof; for it tottereth.

### 60:5

הִרְאִיתָה עַמְּךָ קָשָׁה הִשְׁקִיתָנוּ יַיִן תַּרְעֵלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heer-EE-tah ah-me-KHAH kah-SHAH heesh-kee-TAH-noo YAH-yeen tahr-eh-LAH

Thou hast made Thy people to see hard things; Thou hast made us to drink the wine of staggering.

### 60:6

נָתַתָּה לִּירֵאֶיךָ נֵּס לְהִתְנוֹסֵס מִפְּנֵי קֹשֶׁט סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-TAH-tah lee-reh-EY-khah NEHS le-heet-noh-SEHS mee-pe-NEY KOH-sheht SEH-lah

Thou hast given a banner to them that fear Thee, That it may be displayed because of the truth. Selah

### 60:7

לְמַעַן יֵחָלְצוּן יְדִידֶיךָ הוֹשִׁיעָה יְמִינְךָ ועננו [וַעֲנֵנִי׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** le-mah-AHN yeh-khah-le-TSOON ye-dee-DEY-khah hoh-SHEE-ah ye-mee-ne-KHAH vah-ah-NEH-nee

That Thy beloved may be delivered, Save with Thy right hand, and answer me.

Reading note: written ועננו → read וַעֲנֵֽנִי׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 60:8

אֱלֹהִים דִּבֶּר בְּקָדְשׁוֹ אֶעְלֹזָה אֲחַלְּקָה שְׁכֶם וְעֵמֶק סֻכּוֹת אֲמַדֵּד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM  dee-BEHR be-kohd-SHOH eh-LOH-zah ah-khah-le-KAH she-KHEHM ve-EH-mehk soo-KOHT ah-mah-DEHD

God spoke in His holiness, that I would exult; That I would divide Shechem, and mete out the valley of Succoth.

### 60:9

לִי גִלְעָד וְלִי מְנַשֶּׁה וְאֶפְרַיִם מָעוֹז רֹאשִׁי יְהוּדָה מְחֹקְקִי׃

### 60:10

מוֹאָב סִיר רַחְצִי עַל־אֱדוֹם אַשְׁלִיךְ נַעֲלִי עָלַי פְּלֶשֶׁת הִתְרֹעָעִי׃

### 60:11

**Pronunciation draft:** MEE yoh-vee-leh-NEE EER mah-TSOHR MEE nah-KHAH-nee ahd eh-DOHM

Who will bring me into the fortified city? Who will lead me unto Edom?

### 60:12

הֲלֹא־אַתָּה אֱלֹהִים זְנַחְתָּנוּ וְלֹא־תֵצֵא אֱלֹהִים בְּצִבְאוֹתֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-loh ah-TAH eh-loh-HEEM ze-nahkh-TAH-noo ve-loh teh-TSEH eh-loh-HEEM be-tseev-oh-TEY-noo

Hast not Thou, O God, cast us off? And Thou goest not forth, O God, with our hosts.

### 60:13

הָבָה־לָּנוּ עֶזְרָת מִצָּר וְשָׁוְא תְּשׁוּעַת אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-vah LAH-noo ehz-RAHT mee-TSAHR ve-SHAHV te-shoo-AHT ah-dahm

Give us help against the adversary; For vain is the help of man.

### 60:14

בֵּאלֹהִים נַעֲשֶׂה־חָיִל וְהוּא יָבוּס צָרֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** beh-loh-HEEM nah-ah-seh KHAH-yeel ve-HOO yah-VOOS tsah-REY-noo

Through God we shall do valiantly; For He it is that will tread down our adversaries.

## Psalm 61 — Refuge and an enduring shelter

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.61) · Buckets: protection, wellness

### 61:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־נְגִינַת לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh  ahl ne-gee-NAHT le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; with string-music. [A Psalm] of David.

### 61:2

שִׁמְעָה אֱלֹהִים רִנָּתִי הַקְשִׁיבָה תְּפִלָּתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheem-AH eh-loh-HEEM ree-nah-TEE hahk-SHEE-vah te-fee-lah-TEE

Hear my cry, O God; Attend unto my prayer.

### 61:3

מִקְצֵה הָאָרֶץ אֵלֶיךָ אֶקְרָא בַּעֲטֹף לִבִּי בְּצוּר־יָרוּם מִמֶּנִּי תַנְחֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meek-TSEH hah-AH-rehts  eh-LEY-khah ehk-RAH bah-ah-TOHF lee-BEE be-tsoor yah-ROOM mee-MEH-nee tahn-KHEH-nee

From the end of the earth will I call unto Thee, when my heart fainteth; Lead me to a rock that is too high for me.

### 61:4

כִּי־הָיִיתָ מַחְסֶה לִי מִגְדַּל־עֹז מִפְּנֵי אוֹיֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hah-YEE-tah mahkh-SEH LEE meeg-dahl OHZ mee-pe-NEY oh-YEHV

For Thou hast been a refuge for me, A tower of strength in the face of the enemy.

### 61:5

אָגוּרָה בְאָהָלְךָ עוֹלָמִים אֶחֱסֶה בְסֵתֶר כְּנָפֶיךָ סֶּלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-GOO-rah ve-ah-hah-le-KHAH oh-lah-MEEM eh-kheh-SEH ve-SEH-tehr ke-nah-FEY-khah SEH-lah

I will dwell in Thy Tent for ever; I will take refuge in the covert of Thy wings. Selah

### 61:6

כִּי־אַתָּה אֱלֹהִים שָׁמַעְתָּ לִנְדָרָי נָתַתָּ יְרֻשַּׁת יִרְאֵי שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-TAH eh-loh-HEEM shah-MAH-tah leen-dah-RAHY nah-TAH-tah ye-roo-SHAHT yeer-EY she-MEH-khah

For Thou, O God, hast heard my vows; Thou hast granted the heritage of those that fear Thy name. .

### 61:7

יָמִים עַל־יְמֵי־מֶלֶךְ תּוֹסִיף שְׁנוֹתָיו כְּמוֹ־דֹר וָדֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-MEEM ahl ye-mey MEH-lehkh toh-SEEF she-noh-TAHV ke-moh DOHR vah-DOHR

Mayest Thou add days unto the king's days! May his years be as many generations!

### 61:8

יֵשֵׁב עוֹלָם לִפְנֵי אֱלֹהִים חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת מַן יִנְצְרֻהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-SHEHV oh-LAHM leef-NEY eh-loh-HEEM KHEH-sehd veh-eh-MEHT MAHN yeen-tse-ROO-hoo

May he be enthroned before God for ever! Appoint mercy and truth, that they may preserve him.

### 61:9

כֵּן אֲזַמְּרָה שִׁמְךָ לָעַד לְשַׁלְּמִי נְדָרַי יוֹם יוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEHN ah-zah-me-RAH sheem-KHAH lah-AHD le-shah-le-MEE ne-dah-RAHY YOHM  YOHM

So will I sing praise unto Thy name for ever, That I may daily perform my vows.

## Psalm 62 — Quiet trust and the limits of wealth

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.62) · Buckets: wellness, provision

### 62:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־יְדוּתוּן מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl ye-doo-TOON meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; for Jeduthun. A Psalm of David.

### 62:2

אַךְ אֶל־אֱלֹהִים דּוּמִיָּה נַפְשִׁי מִמֶּנּוּ יְשׁוּעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH ehl eh-loh-HEEM doo-mee-YAH nahf-SHEE mee-MEH-noo ye-shoo-ah-TEE

Only for God doth my soul wait in stillness; From Him cometh my salvation.

### 62:3

אַךְ־הוּא צוּרִי וִישׁוּעָתִי מִשְׂגַּבִּי לֹא־אֶמּוֹט רַבָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahkh HOO tsoo-REE vee-shoo-ah-TEE mees-gah-BEE loh eh-MOHT rah-BAH

He only is my rock and my salvation, My high tower, I shall not be greatly moved.

### 62:4

עַד־אָנָה תְּהוֹתְתוּ עַל אִישׁ תְּרָצְּחוּ כֻלְּכֶם כְּקִיר נָטוּי גָּדֵר הַדְּחוּיָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd AH-nah  te-hoh-te-TOO ahl EESH te-rah-tse-KHOO khoo-le-KHEHM ke-KEER nah-TOOY gah-DEHR hah-de-khoo-YAH

How long will ye set upon a man, That ye may slay him, all of you, As a leaning wall, a tottering fence?

### 62:5

אַךְ מִשְּׂאֵתוֹ יָעֲצוּ לְהַדִּיחַ יִרְצוּ כָזָב בְּפִיו יְבָרֵכוּ וּבְקִרְבָּם יְקַלְלוּ־סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH mee-se-eh-TOH  yah-ah-TSOO le-hah-dee-AHKH yeer-TSOO khah-ZAHV be-FEEV ye-vah-REH-khoo oo-ve-keer-BAHM ye-kahl-loo SEH-lah

They only devise to thrust him down from his height, delighting in lies; They bless with their mouth, but they curse inwardly. Selah

### 62:6

אַךְ לֵאלֹהִים דּוֹמִּי נַפְשִׁי כִּי־מִמֶּנּוּ תִּקְוָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH leh-loh-HEEM DOH-mee nahf-SHEE kee mee-MEH-noo teek-vah-TEE

Only for God wait thou in stillness, my soul; For from Him cometh my hope.

### 62:7

אַךְ־הוּא צוּרִי וִישׁוּעָתִי מִשְׂגַּבִּי לֹא אֶמּוֹט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahkh HOO tsoo-REE vee-shoo-ah-TEE mees-gah-BEE LOH eh-MOHT

He only is my rock and my salvation, My high tower, I shall not be moved.

### 62:8

עַל־אֱלֹהִים יִשְׁעִי וּכְבוֹדִי צוּר־עֻזִּי מַחְסִי בֵּאלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl eh-loh-HEEM yeesh-EE oo-khe-voh-DEE tsoor oo-ZEE mahkh-SEE beh-loh-HEEM

Upon God resteth my salvation and my glory; The rock of my strength, and my refuge, is in God.

### 62:9

בִּטְחוּ בוֹ בְכָל־עֵת עָם שִׁפְכוּ־לְפָנָיו לְבַבְכֶם אֱלֹהִים מַחֲסֶה־לָּנוּ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** beet-KHOO VOH ve-khohl EHT  AHM sheef-khoo le-fah-NAHV le-vahv-KHEHM eh-loh-HEEM mah-khah-seh LAH-noo SEH-lah

Trust in Him at all times, ye people; Pour out your heart before Him; God is a refuge for us. Selah

### 62:10

אַךְ הֶבֶל בְּנֵי־אָדָם כָּזָב בְּנֵי אִישׁ בְּמֹאזְנַיִם לַעֲלוֹת הֵמָּה מֵהֶבֶל יָחַד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH  HEH-vehl be-ney ah-DAHM kah-ZAHV be-NEY EESH be-moh-ze-NAH-yeem lah-ah-LOHT heh-MAH meh-HEH-vehl YAH-khahd

Men of low degree are vanity, and men of high degree are a lie; If they be laid in the balances, they are together lighter than vanity.

### 62:11

אַל־תִּבְטְחוּ בְעֹשֶׁק וּבְגָזֵל אַל־תֶּהְבָּלוּ חַיִל כִּי־יָנוּב אַל־תָּשִׁיתוּ לֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl teev-te-KHOO ve-oh-SHEHK oo-ve-gah-ZEHL ahl teh-BAH-loo KHAH-yeel  kee yah-NOOV ahl tah-SHEE-too LEHV

Trust not in oppression, And put not vain hope in robbery; If riches increase, set not your heart thereon.

### 62:12

אַחַת דִּבֶּר אֱלֹהִים שְׁתַּיִם־זוּ שָׁמָעְתִּי כִּי עֹז לֵאלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-KHAHT  dee-BEHR eh-loh-HEEM she-tah-yeem ZOO shah-MAH-e-tee KEE OHZ leh-loh-HEEM

God hath spoken once, Twice have I heard this: That strength belongeth unto God;

### 62:13

וּלְךָ־אֲדֹנָי חָסֶד כִּי־אַתָּה תְשַׁלֵּם לְאִישׁ כְּמַעֲשֵׂהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-le-khah ah-doh-NAHY KHAH-sehd kee ah-TAH te-shah-LEHM le-EESH ke-mah-ah-SEH-hoo

Also unto Thee, O Lord, belongeth mercy; For Thou renderest to every man according to his work.

## Psalm 63 — Longing for God and nighttime remembrance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.63) · Buckets: devotion, sleep

### 63:1

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד בִּהְיוֹתוֹ בְּמִדְבַּר יְהוּדָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED beeh-yoh-TOH be-meed-BAHR ye-hoo-DAH

A Psalm of David, when he was in the wilderness of Judah.

### 63:2

אֱלֹהִים אֵלִי אַתָּה אֲ‍שַׁחֲרֶךָּ צָמְאָה לְךָ נַפְשִׁי כָּמַהּ לְךָ בְשָׂרִי בְּאֶרֶץ־צִיָּה וְעָיֵף בְּלִי־מָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM  eh-LEE ah-TAH ah-shah-khah-REH-kah‍ tsah-me-AH le-KHAH  nahf-SHEE kah-MAHH le-KHAH ve-sah-REE be-eh-rehts tsee-YAH ve-ah-YEHF be-lee MAH-yeem

O God, Thou art my God, earnestly will I seek Thee; My soul thirsteth for Thee, my flesh longeth for Thee, In a dry and weary land, where no water is.

### 63:3

כֵּן בַּקֹּדֶשׁ חֲזִיתִיךָ לִרְאוֹת עֻזְּךָ וּכְבוֹדֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEHN bah-KOH-dehsh khah-zee-TEE-khah leer-OHT oo-ze-KHAH oo-khe-voh-DEH-khah

So have I looked for Thee in the sanctuary, To see Thy power and Thy glory. .

### 63:4

כִּי־טוֹב חַסְדְּךָ מֵחַיִּים שְׂפָתַי יְשַׁבְּחוּנְךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee TOHV khahs-de-KHAH meh-khah-YEEM se-fah-TAHY ye-shah-be-KHOO-ne-khah

For Thy lovingkindness is better than life; My lips shall praise Thee.

### 63:5

כֵּן אֲבָרֶכְךָ בְחַיָּי בְּשִׁמְךָ אֶשָּׂא כַפָּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEHN ah-vah-rehkh-KHAH ve-khah-YAHY be-sheem-KHAH eh-SAH khah-PAHY

So will I bless Thee as long as I live; In Thy name will I lift up my hands.

### 63:6

כְּמוֹ חֵלֶב וָדֶשֶׁן תִּשְׂבַּע נַפְשִׁי וְשִׂפְתֵי רְנָנוֹת יְהַלֶּל־פִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-MOH KHEH-lehv vah-deh-SHEHN tees-BAH nahf-SHEE ve-seef-TEY re-nah-NOHT ye-hah-lehl PEE

My soul is satisfied as with marrow and fatness; And my mouth doth praise Thee with joyful lips;

### 63:7

אִם־זְכַרְתִּיךָ עַל־יְצוּעָי בְּאַשְׁמֻרוֹת אֶהְגֶּה־בָּךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eem ze-khahr-TEE-khah ahl ye-tsoo-AHY be-ahsh-moo-ROHT eh-geh BAHKH

When I remember Thee upon my couch, And meditate on Thee in the night-watches.

### 63:8

כִּי־הָיִיתָ עֶזְרָתָה לִּי וּבְצֵל כְּנָפֶיךָ אֲרַנֵּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hah-YEE-tah ehz-RAH-tah LEE oo-ve-TSEHL ke-nah-FEY-khah ah-rah-NEHN

For Thou hast been my help, And in the shadow of Thy wings do I rejoice.

### 63:9

דָּבְקָה נַפְשִׁי אַחֲרֶיךָ בִּי תָּמְכָה יְמִינֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-ve-KAH nahf-SHEE ah-khah-REY-khah BEE tah-me-KHAH ye-mee-NEH-khah

My soul cleaveth unto Thee; Thy right hand holdeth me fast.

### 63:10

וְהֵמָּה לְשׁוֹאָה יְבַקְשׁוּ נַפְשִׁי יָבֹאוּ בְּתַחְתִּיּוֹת הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-HEH-mah le-shoh-AH ye-vahk-SHOO nahf-SHEE yah-VOH-oo be-tahkh-tee-YOHT hah-AH-rehts

But those that seek my soul, to destroy it, Shall go into the nethermost parts of the earth.

### 63:11

יַגִּירֻהוּ עַל־יְדֵי־חָרֶב מְנָת שֻׁעָלִים יִהְיוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-gee-ROO-hoo ahl ye-dey KHAH-rehv me-NAHT shoo-ah-LEEM yeeh-YOO

They shall be hurled to the power of the sword; They shall be a portion for foxes.

### 63:12

וְהַמֶּלֶךְ יִשְׂמַח בֵּאלֹהִים יִתְהַלֵּל כָּל־הַנִּשְׁבָּע בּוֹ כִּי יִסָּכֵר פִּי דוֹבְרֵי־שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hah-meh-LEHKH yees-MAHKH beh-loh-HEEM yeet-hah-LEHL kohl hah-neesh-BAH BOH KEE yee-sah-KHEHR PEE doh-ve-rey SHAH-kehr

But the king shall rejoice in God; Every one that sweareth by Him shall glory; For the mouth of them that speak lies shall be stopped.

## Psalm 64 — Protection from harmful speech

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.64) · Buckets: protection, relationships

### 64:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

### 64:2

שְׁמַע־אֱלֹהִים קוֹלִי בְשִׂיחִי מִפַּחַד אוֹיֵב תִּצֹּר חַיָּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-mah eh-loh-HEEM koh-LEE ve-see-KHEE mee-PAH-khahd oh-YEHV tee-TSOHR khah-YAHY

Hear my voice, O God, in my complaint; preserve my life from the terror of the enemy.

### 64:3

תַּסְתִּירֵנִי מִסּוֹד מְרֵעִים מֵרִגְשַׁת פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tahs-tee-reh-NEE mee-SOHD me-reh-EEM meh-reeg-SHAHT POH-ah-ley AH-vehn

Hide me from the council of evil-doers; from the tumult of the workers of iniquity;

### 64:4

אֲשֶׁר שָׁנְנוּ כַחֶרֶב לְשׁוֹנָם דָּרְכוּ חִצָּם דָּבָר מָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR shah-ne-NOO khah-KHEH-rehv le-shoh-NAHM dah-re-KHOO khee-TSAHM dah-VAHR MAHR

Who have whet their tongue like a sword, and have aimed their arrow, a poisoned word;

### 64:5

לִירוֹת בַּמִּסְתָּרִים תָּם פִּתְאֹם יֹרֻהוּ וְלֹא יִירָאוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lee-ROHT bah-mees-tah-REEM TAHM peet-OHM yoh-ROO-hoo ve-LOH yee-RAH-oo

That they may shoot in secret places at the blameless; suddenly do they shoot at him, and fear not.

### 64:6

יְחַזְּקוּ־לָמוֹ דָּבָר רָע יְסַפְּרוּ לִטְמוֹן מוֹקְשִׁים אָמְרוּ מִי יִרְאֶה־לָּמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-khah-ze-koo LAH-moh  dah-VAHR RAH ye-sah-pe-ROO leet-MOHN moh-ke-SHEEM ah-me-ROO MEE yeer-eh LAH-moh

They encourage one another in an evil matter; they converse of laying snares secretly; they ask, who would see them.

### 64:7

יַחְפְּשׂוּ־עוֹלֹת תַּמְנוּ חֵפֶשׂ מְחֻפָּשׂ וְקֶרֶב אִישׁ וְלֵב עָמֹק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-khe-pe-soo oh-LOHT tahm-NOO KHEH-fehs me-khoo-PAHS ve-KEH-rehv EESH ve-LEHV ah-MOHK

They search out iniquities, they have accomplished a diligent search; even in the inward thought of every one, and the deep heart.

### 64:8

וַיֹּרֵם אֱלֹהִים חֵץ פִּתְאוֹם הָיוּ מַכּוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-REHM eh-loh-HEEM KHEHTS peet-OHM hah-YOO mah-koh-TAHM

But God doth shoot at them with an arrow suddenly; thence are their wounds.

### 64:9

וַיַּכְשִׁילוּהוּ עָלֵימוֹ לְשׁוֹנָם יִתְנֹדֲדוּ כָּל־רֹאֵה בָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahkh-shee-LOO-hoo ah-LEY-moh le-shoh-NAHM yeet-noh-dah-DOO kohl ROH-eh VAHM

So they make their own tongue a stumbling unto themselves; all that see them shake the head.

### 64:10

וַיִּירְאוּ כָּל־אָדָם וַיַּגִּידוּ פֹּעַל אֱלֹהִים וּמַעֲשֵׂהוּ הִשְׂכִּילוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-re-OO kohl ah-DAHM vah-yah-gee-DOO POH-ahl eh-loh-HEEM oo-mah-ah-SEH-hoo hees-KEE-loo

And all men fear; and they declare the work of God, and understand His doing.

### 64:11

For the Leader. A Psalm. A Song of David.

**Pronunciation draft:** meh-KHEEN hah-REEM be-khoh-KHOH neh-ZAHR beeg-voo-RAH

**Pronunciation draft:** mahsh-BEE-ahkh  she-OHN yah-MEEM she-OHN gah-ley-HEHM vah-hah-MOHN le-oo-MEEM

### 65:9

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-KAHD-tah hah-AH-rehts  vah-te-SHOH-ke-KEH-hah rah-BAHT tah-she-REH-nah PEH-lehg eh-loh-HEEM MAH-leh MAH-yeem tah-KHEEN de-gah-NAHM kee KHEHN te-khee-NEH-hah

### 65:11

תְּלָמֶיהָ רַוֵּה נַחֵת גְּדוּדֶיהָ בִּרְבִיבִים תְּמֹגְגֶנָּה צִמְחָהּ תְּבָרֵךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-lah-MEY-hah rah-VEH nah-KHEHT ge-doo-DEY-hah beer-vee-VEEM te-moh-ge-GEH-nah tseem-KHAHH te-vah-REHKH

**Pronunciation draft:** ee-tahr-TAH she-NAHT toh-vah-TEH-khah oo-mah-gah-LEY-khah yeer-ah-FOON DAH-shehn

**Pronunciation draft:** yeer-ah-FOO ne-OHT meed-BAHR ve-GEEL ge-vah-OHT tahkh-GOH-re-nah

## Psalm 66 — Collective and personal thanksgiving

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.66) · Buckets: gratitude, community

### 66:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ שִׁיר מִזְמוֹר הָרִיעוּ לֵאלֹהִים כָּל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-tseh-AHKH SHEER meez-MOHR hah-REE-oo leh-loh-HEEM kohl hah-AH-rehts

For the Leader. A Song, a Psalm. Shout unto God, all the earth;

### 66:2

זַמְּרוּ כְבוֹד־שְׁמוֹ שִׂימוּ כָבוֹד תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-me-ROO khe-vohd she-MOH SEE-moo khah-VOHD te-hee-lah-TOH

Sing praises unto the glory of His name; Make His praise glorious.

### 66:3

אִמְרוּ לֵאלֹהִים מַה־נּוֹרָא מַעֲשֶׂיךָ בְּרֹב עֻזְּךָ יְכַחֲשׁוּ לְךָ אֹיְבֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eem-ROO leh-loh-HEEM mahh noh-RAH mah-ah-SEY-khah be-ROHV oo-ze-KHAH ye-khah-khah-SHOO le-KHAH oh-ye-VEY-khah

Say unto God: 'How tremendous is Thy work! Through the greatness of Thy power shall Thine enemies dwindle away before Thee.

### 66:4

כָּל־הָאָרֶץ יִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לְךָ וִיזַמְּרוּ־לָךְ יְזַמְּרוּ שִׁמְךָ סֶלָה׃

### 66:5

לְכוּ וּרְאוּ מִפְעֲלוֹת אֱלֹהִים נוֹרָא עֲלִילָה עַל־בְּנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-KHOO oo-re-OO meef-ah-LOHT eh-loh-HEEM noh-RAH ah-lee-LAH ahl be-NEY ah-DAHM

### 66:6

**Pronunciation draft:** HAH-fahkh YAHM  le-yah-bah-SHAH bah-nah-HAHR yah-ahv-ROO ve-RAH-gehl SHAHM nees-me-khah BOH

He turned the sea into dry land; They went through the river on foot; There let us rejoice in Him!

### 66:7

מֹשֵׁל בִּגְבוּרָתוֹ עוֹלָם עֵינָיו בַּגּוֹיִם תִּצְפֶּינָה הַסּוֹרְרִים אַל־ירימו [יָרוּמוּ] לָמוֹ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** moh-SHEHL beeg-voo-rah-TOH  oh-LAHM ey-NAHV bah-goh-YEEM teets-PEY-nah hah-soh-re-REEM  ah-yah-ROO-moo LAH-moh SEH-lah

### 66:8

בָּרְכוּ עַמִּים אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְהַשְׁמִיעוּ קוֹל תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-re-KHOO ah-MEEM  eh-loh-HEY-noo ve-hahsh-MEE-oo KOHL te-hee-lah-TOH

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-SAHM nahf-sheh-NOO bah-khah-YEEM ve-loh nah-TAHN lah-MOHT rahg-LEH-noo

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ve-khahn-TAH-noo eh-loh-HEEM tse-rahf-TAH-noo keets-rahf KAH-sehf

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-veh-TAH-noo vah-me-tsoo-DAH SAHM-tah moo-ah-KAH ve-moht-NEY-noo

### 66:12

### 66:13

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-VOH vey-te-KHAH ve-oh-LOHT ah-shah-LEHM le-KHAH ne-dah-RAHY

### 66:14

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shehr pah-TSOO se-fah-TAHY ve-dee-behr PEE bah-tsahr LEE

יְבָרְכֵנוּ אֱלֹהִים וְיִירְאוּ אֹתוֹ כָּל־אַפְסֵי־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-vah-re-KHEH-noo eh-loh-HEEM ve-yee-re-OO oh-TOH kohl ahf-sey AH-rehts

May God bless us; And let all the ends of the earth fear Him.

## Psalm 68 — Protection of the vulnerable and collective praise

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.68) · Buckets: justice, community

### 68:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר שִׁיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh le-dah-VEED meez-MOHR SHEER

For the Leader. A Psalm of David, a Song.

### 68:2

יָקוּם אֱלֹהִים יָפוּצוּ אוֹיְבָיו וְיָנוּסוּ מְשַׂנְאָיו מִפָּנָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-KOOM eh-loh-HEEM yah-FOO-tsoo oh-ye-VAHV ve-yah-NOO-soo me-sahn-AHV mee-pah-NAHV

Let God arise, let His enemies be scattered; And let them that hate Him flee before Him.

### 68:3

כְּהִנְדֹּף עָשָׁן תִּנְדֹּף כְּהִמֵּס דּוֹנַג מִפְּנֵי־אֵשׁ יֹאבְדוּ רְשָׁעִים מִפְּנֵי אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-heen-DOHF ah-SHAHN teen-DOHF ke-hee-MEHS doh-NAHG mee-pe-ney EHSH yoh-ve-DOO re-shah-EEM mee-pe-NEY eh-loh-HEEM

As smoke is driven away, so drive them away; As wax melteth before the fire, So let the wicked perish at the presence of God.

### 68:4

וְצַדִּיקִים יִשְׂמְחוּ יַעַלְצוּ לִפְנֵי אֱלֹהִים וְיָשִׂישׂוּ בְשִׂמְחָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-tsah-dee-KEEM yees-me-KHOO yah-ahl-TSOO leef-NEY eh-loh-HEEM ve-yah-SEE-soo ve-seem-KHAH

But let the righteous be glad, let them exult before God; Yea, let them rejoice with gladness.

### 68:5

שִׁירוּ לֵאלֹהִים זַמְּרוּ שְׁמוֹ סֹלּוּ לָרֹכֵב בָּעֲרָבוֹת בְּיָהּ שְׁמוֹ וְעִלְזוּ לְפָנָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEE-roo  leh-loh-HEEM zah-me-ROO she-MOH SOH-loo lah-roh-KHEHV bah-ah-rah-VOHT be-YAHH she-MOH ve-eel-ZOO le-fah-NAHV

Sing unto God, sing praises to His name; Extol Him that rideth upon the skies, whose name is the LORD; And exult ye before Him.

### 68:6

אֲבִי יְתוֹמִים וְדַיַּן אַלְמָנוֹת אֱלֹהִים בִּמְעוֹן קָדְשׁוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-VEE ye-toh-MEEM ve-dah-YAHN ahl-mah-NOHT eh-loh-HEEM beem-OHN kohd-SHOH

A father of the fatherless, and a judge of the widows, Is God in His holy habitation.

### 68:7

אֱלֹהִים מוֹשִׁיב יְחִידִים בַּיְתָה מוֹצִיא אֲסִירִים בַּכּוֹשָׁרוֹת אַךְ סוֹרֲרִים שָׁכְנוּ צְחִיחָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM  moh-SHEEV ye-khee-DEEM  BAHY-tah moh-TSEE ah-see-REEM bah-koh-shah-ROHT AHKH soh-rah-REEM shah-khe-NOO tse-khee-KHAH

God maketh the solitary to dwell in a house; He bringeth out the prisoners into prosperity; The rebellious dwell but in a parched land.

### 68:8

אֱ‍לֹהִים בְּצֵאתְךָ לִפְנֵי עַמֶּךָ בְּצַעְדְּךָ בִישִׁימוֹן סֶלָה׃

### 68:10

גֶּשֶׁם נְדָבוֹת תָּנִיף אֱלֹהִים נַחֲלָתְךָ וְנִלְאָה אַתָּה כוֹנַנְתָּהּ׃

The Lord giveth the word; The women that proclaim the tidings are a great host.

### 68:13

Kings of armies flee, they flee; And she that tarrieth at home divideth the spoil.

### 68:14

### 68:15

**Pronunciation draft:** be-fah-REHS shah-DAHY me-lah-KHEEM BAHH tahsh-LEHG be-tsahl-MOHN

When the Almighty scattereth kings therein, It snoweth in Zalmon.

### 68:16

הַר־אֱלֹהִים הַר־בָּשָׁן הַר גַּבְנֻנִּים הַר־בָּשָׁן׃

### 68:18

The Lord said: 'I will bring back from Bashan, I will bring them back from the depths of the sea;

## Psalm 69 — Deep distress and an appeal for rescue

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.69) · Buckets: wellness, protection, health

### 69:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־שׁוֹשַׁנִּים לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl shoh-shah-NEEM le-dah-VEED

For the Leader; upon Shoshannim. [A Psalm] of David.

### 69:2

הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי אֱלֹהִים כִּי בָאוּ מַיִם עַד־נָפֶשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-shee-EH-nee eh-loh-HEEM KEE VAH-oo MAH-yeem ahd NAH-fehsh

Save me, O God; For the waters are come in even unto the soul.

### 69:3

טָבַעְתִּי בִּיוֵן מְצוּלָה וְאֵין מָעֳמָד בָּאתִי בְמַעֲמַקֵּי־מַיִם וְשִׁבֹּלֶת שְׁטָפָתְנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-VAH-tee  bee-VEHN me-tsoo-LAH ve-EYN moh-oh-MAHD BAH-tee ve-mah-ah-mah-key mah-YEEM ve-shee-BOH-leht she-tah-FAH-te-nee

I am sunk in deep mire, where there is no standing; I am come into deep waters, and the flood overwhelmeth me.

### 69:4

יָגַעְתִּי בְקָרְאִי נִחַר גְּרוֹנִי כָּלוּ עֵינַי מְיַחֵל לֵאלֹהָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-GAH-tee ve-kah-re-EE nee-KHAHR ge-roh-NEE kah-LOO ey-NAHY me-yah-KHEHL leh-loh-HAHY

I am weary of my crying; my throat is dried; Mine eyes fail while I wait for my God.

### 69:5

רַבּוּ מִשַּׂעֲרוֹת רֹאשִׁי שֹׂנְאַי חִנָּם עָצְמוּ מַצְמִיתַי אֹיְבַי שֶׁקֶר אֲשֶׁר לֹא־גָזַלְתִּי אָז אָשִׁיב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BOO  mee-sah-ah-ROHT roh-SHEE soh-ne-AHY khee-NAHM ah-tse-MOO mahts-mee-TAHY oh-ye-VAHY SHEH-kehr ah-SHEHR loh gah-ZAHL-tee AHZ ah-SHEEV

They that hate me without a cause are more than the hairs of my head; They that would cut me off, being mine enemies wrongfully, are many; Should I restore that which I took not away?

### 69:6

אֱ‍לֹהִים אַתָּה יָדַעְתָּ לְאִוַּלְתִּי וְאַשְׁמוֹתַי מִמְּךָ לֹא־נִכְחָדוּ׃

O God, Thou knowest my folly; And my trespasses are not hid from Thee.

### 69:7

אַל־יֵבֹשׁוּ בִי קֹוֶיךָ אֲדֹנָי יְהוִה צְבָאוֹת אַל־יִכָּלְמוּ בִי מְבַקְשֶׁיךָ אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl yeh-VOH-shoo VEE  koh-vey-KHAH ah-doh-NAHY eh-loh-HEEM tse-vah-OHT ahl yee-KAH-le-moo VEE me-vahk-SHEY-khah eh-loh-HEY yees-rah-EHL

Let not them that wait for Thee be ashamed through me, O Lord GOD of hosts; Let not those that seek Thee be brought to confusion through me, O God of Israel.

### 69:8

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-ley-KHAH nah-SAH-tee khehr-PAH kee-se-TAH khe-lee-MAH fah-NAHY

Because for Thy sake I have borne reproach; Confusion hath covered my face.

### 69:9

**Pronunciation draft:** moo-ZAHR hah-YEE-tee le-eh-KHAHY ve-nah-khe-REE leev-NEY ee-MEE

### 69:10

Because zeal for Thy house hath eaten me up, And the reproaches of them that reproach Thee are fallen upon me.

### 69:11

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ehv-KEH vah-TSOHM nahf-SHEE vah-te-HEE lah-khah-rah-FOHT LEE

### 69:12

### 69:14

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE te-fee-lah-tee le-KHAH  ah-doh-NAI EHT rah-TSOHN eh-loh-HEEM be-rohv khahs-DEH-khah ah-NEH-nee beh-eh-MEHT yeesh-EH-khah

### 69:15

הַצִּילֵנִי מִטִּיט וְאַל־אֶטְבָּעָה אִנָּצְלָה מִשֹּׂנְאַי וּמִמַּעֲמַקֵּי־מָיִם׃

### 69:16

### 69:17

### 69:20

**Pronunciation draft:** khehr-PAH  shah-ve-RAH lee-BEE vah-ah-NOO-shah vah-ah-kah-VEH lah-NOOD vah-AH-yeen ve-lahm-nah-khah-MEEM ve-LOH mah-TSAH-tee

### 69:22

וַיִּתְּנוּ בְּבָרוּתִי רֹאשׁ וְלִצְמָאִי יַשְׁקוּנִי חֹמֶץ׃

### 69:23

### 69:25

### 69:26

**Pronunciation draft:** te-hee tee-rah-TAHM ne-shah-MAH be-oh-hoh-ley-HEHM ahl ye-HEE yoh-SHEHV

Let their encampment be desolate; Let none dwell in their tents.

### 69:27

כִּי־אַתָּה אֲשֶׁר־הִכִּיתָ רָדָפוּ וְאֶל־מַכְאוֹב חֲלָלֶיךָ יְסַפֵּרוּ׃

### 69:28

### 69:29

### 69:30

### 69:31

### 69:32

### 69:33

### 69:34

**Pronunciation draft:** kee shoh-MEH-ah ehl ehv-yoh-NEEM ah-doh-NAI ve-eht ah-see-RAHV LOH vah-ZAH

## Psalm 70 — An urgent request for help

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.70) · Buckets: protection, hope

### 70:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד לְהַזְכִּיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh le-dah-VEED le-hahz-KEER

For the Leader. [A Psalm] of David; to make memorial.

### 70:2

אֱלֹהִים לְהַצִּילֵנִי יְהוָה לְעֶזְרָתִי חוּשָׁה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM le-hah-tsee-LEH-nee ah-doh-NAI le-ehz-RAH-tee khoo-SHAH

O God, to deliver me, O LORD, to help me, make haste.

### 70:3

יֵבֹשׁוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי נַפְשִׁי יִסֹּגוּ אָחוֹר וְיִכָּלְמוּ חֲפֵצֵי רָעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo ve-yahkh-pe-ROO me-vahk-SHEY nahf-SHEE yee-SOH-goo ah-KHOHR ve-yee-kah-le-MOO khah-feh-TSEY rah-ah-TEE

Let them be ashamed and abashed That seek after my soul; Let them be turned backward and brought to confusion That delight in my hurt.

### 70:4

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-shoo-VOO ahl EH-kehv bohsh-TAHM hah-oh-me-REEM heh-AHKH  heh-AHKH

Let them be turned back by reason of their shame That say: 'Aha, aha.'

### 70:5

יָשִׂישׂוּ וְיִשְׂמְחוּ בְּךָ כָּל־מְבַקְשֶׁיךָ וְיֹאמְרוּ תָמִיד יִגְדַּל אֱלֹהִים אֹהֲבֵי יְשׁוּעָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SEE-soo ve-yees-me-KHOO  be-KHAH kahl me-vahk-SHEY-khah ve-yoh-me-ROO tah-MEED yeeg-DAHL eh-loh-HEEM oh-hah-VEY ye-shoo-ah-TEH-khah

Let all those that seek Thee rejoice and be glad in Thee; And let such as love Thy salvation say continually: 'Let God be magnified.'

### 70:6

וַאֲנִי עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן אֱלֹהִים חוּשָׁה־לִּי עֶזְרִי וּמְפַלְטִי אַתָּה יְהוָה אַל־תְּאַחַר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN eh-loh-HEEM khoo-SHAH LEE ehz-REE oo-me-fahl-TEE AH-tah ah-doh-NAI ahl te-ah-KHAHR

But I am poor and needy; O God, make haste unto me; Thou art my help and my deliverer; O LORD, tarry not.

## Psalm 71 — Trust and dignity in old age

### 71:1

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khah ah-doh-NAI khah-SEE-tee ahl eh-VOH-shah le-oh-LAHM

In Thee, O LORD, have I taken refuge; Let me never be ashamed.

### 71:2

בְּצִדְקָתְךָ תַּצִּילֵנִי וּתְפַלְּטֵנִי הַטֵּה־אֵלַי אָזְנְךָ וְהוֹשִׁיעֵנִי׃
