### 71:4

אֱ‍לֹהַי פַּלְּטֵנִי מִיַּד רָשָׁע מִכַּף מְעַוֵּל וְחוֹמֵץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HAHY‍ pah-le-teh-NEE mee-YAHD rah-SHAH mee-KAHF me-ah-VEHL ve-khoh-mehts

O my God, rescue me out of the hand of the wicked, Out of the grasp of the unrighteous and ruthless man.

### 71:5

כִּי־אַתָּה תִקְוָתִי אֲדֹנָי יְהוִה מִבְטַחִי מִנְּעוּרָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-TAH teek-vah-TEE ah-doh-NAHY eh-loh-HEEM meev-tah-KHEE mee-ne-oo-RAHY

For Thou art my hope; O Lord GOD, my trust from my youth.

### 71:6

עָלֶיךָ נִסְמַכְתִּי מִבֶּטֶן מִמְּעֵי אִמִּי אַתָּה גוֹזִי בְּךָ תְהִלָּתִי תָמִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-LEY-khah  nees-MAHKH-tee mee-BEH-tehn mee-me-EY ee-MEE ah-TAH goh-ZEE be-KHAH te-hee-lah-TEE tah-MEED

Upon Thee have I stayed myself from birth; Thou art he that took me out of my mother's womb; My praise is continually of Thee.

### 71:7

כְּמוֹפֵת הָיִיתִי לְרַבִּים וְאַתָּה מַחֲסִי־עֹז׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-moh-FEHT hah-YEE-tee le-rah-BEEM ve-ah-TAH mah-khah-see OHZ

I am as a wonder unto many; But Thou art my strong refuge.

### 71:8

יִמָּלֵא פִי תְּהִלָּתֶךָ כָּל־הַיּוֹם תִּפְאַרְתֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-MAH-leh FEE te-hee-lah-TEH-khah kohl hah-YOHM teef-ahr-TEH-khah

My mouth shall be filled with Thy praise, And with Thy glory all the day. .

### 71:9

אַל־תַּשְׁלִיכֵנִי לְעֵת זִקְנָה כִּכְלוֹת כֹּחִי אַל־תַּעַזְבֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tahsh-lee-kheh-NEE le-EHT zeek-NAH keekh-LOHT koh-KHEE ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee

Cast me not off in the time of old age; When my strength faileth, forsake me not.

### 71:10

כִּי־אָמְרוּ אוֹיְבַי לִי וְשֹׁמְרֵי נַפְשִׁי נוֹעֲצוּ יַחְדָּו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-me-ROO oh-ye-VAHY LEE ve-shoh-me-REY nahf-SHEE noh-ah-TSOO yahkh-DAHV

For mine enemies speak concerning me, And they that watch for my soul take counsel together,

### 71:11

לֵאמֹר אֱלֹהִים עֲזָבוֹ רִדְפוּ וְתִפְשׂוּהוּ כִּי־אֵין מַצִּיל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leh-MOHR eh-loh-HEEM ah-zah-VOH ree-de-FOO ve-teef-SOO-hoo kee EYN mah-TSEEL

Saying: 'God hath forsaken him; Pursue and take him; for there is none to deliver.'

### 71:12

אֱלֹהִים אַל־תִּרְחַק מִמֶּנִּי אֱלֹהַי לְעֶזְרָתִי חישה [חוּשָׁה׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM ahl teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-nee eh-loh-HAHY le-ehz-RAH-tee KHOO-shah

O God, be not far from me; O my God, make haste to help me.

Reading note: written חישה → read חֽוּשָׁה׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 71:13

יֵבֹשׁוּ יִכְלוּ שֹׂטְנֵי נַפְשִׁי יַעֲטוּ חֶרְפָּה וּכְלִמָּה מְבַקְשֵׁי רָעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo yeekh-LOO soh-te-NEY nahf-SHEE yah-ah-TOO khehr-PAH oo-khe-lee-MAH me-vahk-SHEY rah-ah-TEE

Let them be ashamed and consumed that are adversaries to my soul; Let them be covered with reproach and confusion that seek my hurt.

### 71:14

וַאֲנִי תָּמִיד אֲיַחֵל וְהוֹסַפְתִּי עַל־כָּל־תְּהִלָּתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE tah-MEED ah-yah-KHEHL ve-hoh-sahf-TEE ahl kohl te-hee-lah-TEH-khah

But as for me, I will hope continually, And will praise Thee yet more and more.

### 71:15

פִּי יְסַפֵּר צִדְקָתֶךָ כָּל־הַיּוֹם תְּשׁוּעָתֶךָ כִּי לֹא יָדַעְתִּי סְפֹרוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** PEE  ye-sah-PEHR tseed-kah-TEH-khah kohl hah-YOHM te-shoo-ah-TEH-khah KEE LOH yah-DAH-tee se-foh-ROHT

My mouth shall tell of Thy righteousness, And of Thy salvation all the day; For I know not the numbers thereof.

### 71:16

אָבוֹא בִּגְבֻרוֹת אֲדֹנָי יְהוִה אַזְכִּיר צִדְקָתְךָ לְבַדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-VOH beeg-voo-ROHT ah-doh-NAHY eh-loh-HEEM ahz-KEER tseed-kah-te-KHAH le-vah-DEH-khah

I will come with Thy mighty acts, O Lord GOD; I will make mention of Thy righteousness, even of Thine only.

### 71:17

אֱ‍לֹהִים לִמַּדְתַּנִי מִנְּעוּרָי וְעַד־הֵנָּה אַגִּיד נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM‍ lee-mahd-TAH-nee mee-ne-oo-RAHY ve-ahd heh-NAH ah-GEED neef-le-oh-TEY-khah

O God, Thou hast taught me from my youth; And until now do I declare Thy wondrous works.

### 71:18

וְגַם עַד־זִקְנָה וְשֵׂיבָה אֱלֹהִים אַל־תַּעַזְבֵנִי עַד־אַגִּיד זְרוֹעֲךָ לְדוֹר לְכָל־יָבוֹא גְּבוּרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-GAHM ahd zeek-NAH  ve-sey-VAH eh-loh-HEEM ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee ahd ah-GEED ze-roh-ah-KHAH le-DOHR le-khohl yah-VOH ge-voo-rah-TEH-khah

And even unto old age and hoary hairs, O God, forsake me not; Until I have declared Thy strength unto the next generation, Thy might to every one that is to come.

### 71:19

וְצִדְקָתְךָ אֱלֹהִים עַד־מָרוֹם אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂיתָ גְדֹלוֹת אֱלֹהִים מִי כָמוֹךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-tseed-kah-te-KHAH eh-loh-HEEM ahd mah-ROHM ah-shehr ah-SEE-tah ge-doh-LOHT eh-loh-HEEM MEE khah-MOH-khah

Thy righteousness also, O God, which reacheth unto high heaven; Thou who hast done great things, O God, who is like unto Thee?

### 71:20

אֲשֶׁר הראיתנו [הִרְאִיתַנִי ] צָרוֹת רַבּוֹת וְרָעוֹת תָּשׁוּב תחיינו [תְּחַיֵּינִי] וּמִתְּהֹמוֹת הָאָרֶץ תָּשׁוּב תַּעֲלֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR heer-ee-TAH-nee  tsah-ROHT rah-BOHT ve-rah-OHT tah-SHOOV te-khah-YEY-nee oo-mee-te-hoh-MOHT hah-AH-rehts tah-SHOOV tah-ah-LEH-nee

Thou, who hast made me to see many and sore troubles, Wilt quicken me again, and bring me up again from the depths of the earth.

Reading note: written הראיתנו → read הִרְאִיתַ֨נִי ׀; written תחיינו → read תְּחַיֵּ֑ינִי. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 71:21

תֶּרֶב גְּדֻלָּתִי וְתִסֹּב תְּנַחֲמֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TEH-rehv  ge-doo-lah-TEE ve-tee-SOHV te-nah-khah-MEH-nee

Thou wilt increase my greatness, And turn and comfort me.

### 71:22

גַּם־אֲנִי אוֹדְךָ בִכְלִי־נֶבֶל אֲמִתְּךָ אֱלֹהָי אֲזַמְּרָה לְךָ בְכִנּוֹר קְדוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gahm ah-NEE  oh-de-KHAH veekh-lee neh-VEHL ah-mee-te-KHAH eh-loh-HAHY ah-zah-me-RAH le-KHAH ve-khee-NOHR ke-DOHSH yees-rah-EHL

I also will give thanks unto Thee with the psaltery, Even unto Thy truth, O my God; I will sing praises unto Thee with the harp, O Thou Holy One of Israel.

### 71:23

תְּרַנֵּנָּה שְׂפָתַי כִּי אֲזַמְּרָה־לָּךְ וְנַפְשִׁי אֲשֶׁר פָּדִיתָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-rah-NEH-NAH se-fah-TAHY KEE ah-zah-me-rah LAHKH ve-nahf-SHEE ah-SHEHR pah-DEE-tah

My lips shall greatly rejoice when I sing praises unto Thee; And my soul, which Thou hast redeemed.

### 71:24

גַּם־לְשׁוֹנִי כָּל־הַיּוֹם תֶּהְגֶּה צִדְקָתֶךָ כִּי־בֹשׁוּ כִי־חָפְרוּ מְבַקְשֵׁי רָעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gahm le-shoh-NEE kohl hah-YOHM teh-GEH tseed-kah-TEH-khah kee VOH-shoo khee khah-fe-ROO me-vahk-SHEY rah-ah-TEE

My tongue also shall tell of Thy righteousness all the day; For they are ashamed, for they are abashed, that seek my hurt.

## Psalm 72 — Just leadership and care for the poor

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.72) · Buckets: justice, community, provision

### 72:1

לִשְׁלֹמֹה אֱ‍לֹהִים מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לְמֶלֶךְ תֵּן וְצִדְקָתְךָ לְבֶן־מֶלֶךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leesh-loh-MOHH  eh-loh-HEEM‍ meesh-pah-tey-KHAH le-MEH-lehkh TEHN ve-tseed-kah-te-KHAH le-vehn MEH-lehkh

[A Psalm] of Solomon. Give the king Thy judgments, O God, and Thy righteousness unto the king's son;

### 72:2

יָדִין עַמְּךָ בְצֶדֶק וַעֲנִיֶּיךָ בְמִשְׁפָּט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-DEEN ah-me-KHAH ve-TSEH-dehk vah-ah-nee-YEY-khah ve-meesh-PAHT

That he may judge Thy people with righteousness, and Thy poor with justice.

### 72:3

יִשְׂאוּ הָרִים שָׁלוֹם לָעָם וּגְבָעוֹת בִּצְדָקָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yees-OO hah-REEM shah-LOHM lah-AHM oo-ge-vah-OHT beets-dah-KAH

Let the mountains bear peace to the people, and the hills, through righteousness.

### 72:4

יִשְׁפֹּט עֲ‍נִיֵּי־עָם יוֹשִׁיעַ לִבְנֵי אֶבְיוֹן וִידַכֵּא עוֹשֵׁק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeesh-POHT  ah-nee-yey ‍AHM yoh-shee-AH leev-NEY ehv-YOHN vee-dah-KEH oh-SHEHK

May he judge the poor of the people, and save the children of the needy, And crush the oppressor.

### 72:5

יִירָאוּךָ עִם־שָׁמֶשׁ וְלִפְנֵי יָרֵחַ דּוֹר דּוֹרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-rah-OO-khah eem SHAH-mehsh ve-leef-NEY yah-REH-ahkh DOHR doh-REEM

They shall fear Thee while the sun endureth, and so long as the moon, throughout all generations.

### 72:6

יֵרֵד כְּמָטָר עַל־גֵּז כִּרְבִיבִים זַרְזִיף אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-REHD ke-mah-TAHR ahl GEHZ keer-vee-VEEM zahr-ZEEF AH-rehts

May he come down like rain upon the mown grass, as showers that water the earth.

### 72:7

יִפְרַח־בְּיָמָיו צַדִּיק וְרֹב שָׁלוֹם עַד־בְּלִי יָרֵחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-fe-rahkh be-yah-MAHV tsah-DEEK ve-ROHV shah-LOHM ahd be-LEE yah-REH-ahkh

In his days let the righteous flourish, and abundance of peace, till the moon be no more.

### 72:8

וְיֵרְדְּ מִיָּם עַד־יָם וּמִנָּהָר עַד־אַפְסֵי־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-YEHRD mee-YAHM ahd YAHM oo-mee-nah-HAHR ahd ahf-sey AH-rehts

May he have dominion also from sea to sea, and from the River unto the ends of the earth.

### 72:9

לְפָנָיו יִכְרְעוּ צִיִּים וְאֹיְבָיו עָפָר יְלַחֵכוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-fah-NAHV yeekh-re-OO tsee-YEEM ve-oh-ye-VAHV ah-FAHR ye-lah-KHEH-khoo

Let them that dwell in the wilderness bow before him; and his enemies lick the dust.

### 72:10

מַלְכֵי תַרְשִׁישׁ וְאִיִּים מִנְחָה יָשִׁיבוּ מַלְכֵי שְׁבָא וּסְבָא אֶשְׁכָּר יַקְרִיבוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahl-KHEY tahr-SHEESH ve-ee-YEEM meen-KHAH yah-SHEE-voo mahl-KHEY she-VAH oo-se-VAH ehsh-KAHR yahk-REE-voo

The kings of Tarshish and of the isles shall render tribute; the kings of Sheba and Seba shall offer gifts.

### 72:11

וְיִשְׁתַּחֲווּ־לוֹ כָל־מְלָכִים כָּל־גּוֹיִם יַעַבְדוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yeesh-tah-khah-voo LOH khohl me-lah-KHEEM kohl goh-YEEM yah-ahv-DOO-hoo

Yea, all kings shall prostrate themselves before him; All nations shall serve him.

### 72:12

כִּי־יַצִּיל אֶבְיוֹן מְשַׁוֵּעַ וְעָנִי וְאֵין־עֹזֵר לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee yah-TSEEL ehv-YOHN me-shah-VEH-ah ve-ah-NEE ve-eyn oh-ZEHR LOH

For he will deliver the needy when he crieth; the poor also, and him that hath no helper.

### 72:13

יָחֹס עַל־דַּל וְאֶבְיוֹן וְנַפְשׁוֹת אֶבְיוֹנִים יוֹשִׁיעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-KHOHS ahl DAHL ve-ehv-YOHN ve-nahf-SHOHT ehv-yoh-NEEM yoh-SHEE-ah

He will have pity on the poor and needy, And the souls of the needy he will save.

### 72:14

מִתּוֹךְ וּמֵחָמָס יִגְאַל נַפְשָׁם וְיֵיקַר דָּמָם בְּעֵינָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-TOHKH oo-meh-khah-MAHS yeeg-AHL nahf-SHAHM ve-yey-KAHR dah-MAHM be-ey-NAHV

He will redeem their soul from oppression and violence, And precious will their blood be in his sight.

### 72:15

וִיחִי וְיִתֶּן־לוֹ מִזְּהַב שְׁבָא וְיִתְפַּלֵּל בַּעֲדוֹ תָמִיד כָּל־הַיּוֹם יְבָרֲכֶנְהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vee-KHEE ve-yee-tehn LOH mee-ze-HAHV she-VAH ve-yeet-pah-LEHL bah-ah-DOH tah-MEED kohl hah-YOHM ye-vah-rahkheh-ne-HOO

That they may live, and that he may give them of the gold of Sheba, That they may pray for him continually, Yea, bless him all the day.

### 72:16

יְהִי פִסַּת־בַּר בָּאָרֶץ בְּרֹאשׁ הָרִים יִרְעַשׁ כַּלְּבָנוֹן פִּרְיוֹ וְיָצִיצוּ מֵעִיר כְּעֵשֶׂב הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-HEE fee-saht BAHR  bah-ah-REHTS be-ROHSH hah-REEM yeer-AHSH kah-le-vah-NOHN peer-YOH ve-yah-TSEE-tsoo meh-EER ke-EH-sehv hah-AH-rehts

May he be as a rich cornfield in the land upon the top of the mountains; May his fruit rustle like Lebanon; And may they blossom out of the city like grass of the earth.

### 72:17

יְהִי שְׁמוֹ לְעוֹלָם לִפְנֵי־שֶׁמֶשׁ ינין [יִנּוֹן] שְׁמוֹ וְיִתְבָּרְכוּ בוֹ כָּל־גּוֹיִם יְאַשְּׁרוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-HEE she-MOH le-oh-LAHM leef-ney sheh-MEHSH yee-NOHN she-MOH ve-yeet-BAH-re-khoo VOH kohl goh-YEEM ye-ah-she-ROO-hoo

May his name endure for ever; May his name be continued as long as the sun; May men also bless themselves by him; May all nations call him happy.

Reading note: written ינין → read יִנּ֪וֹן. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 72:18

בָּרוּךְ יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עֹשֵׂה נִפְלָאוֹת לְבַדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH  ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEEM eh-loh-HEY yees-rah-EHL oh-SEH neef-lah-OHT le-vah-DOH

Blessed be the LORD God, the God of Israel, Who only doeth wondrous things;

### 72:19

וּבָרוּךְ שֵׁם כְּבוֹדוֹ לְעוֹלָם וְיִמָּלֵא כְבוֹדוֹ אֶת־כֹּל הָאָרֶץ אָמֵן וְאָמֵן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-vah-ROOKH  SHEHM ke-voh-DOH le-oh-LAHM ve-yee-mah-LEH khe-voh-DOH eht KOHL hah-AH-rehts ah-MEHN  ve-ah-MEHN

And blessed be His glorious name for ever; And let the whole earth be filled with His glory. Amen, and Amen.

### 72:20

כָּלּוּ תְפִלּוֹת דָּוִד בֶּן־יִשָׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-LOO te-fee-LOHT dah-VEED behn yee-SHAHY

The prayers of David the son of Jesse are ended.

## Psalm 73 — Envy, prosperity, and regained perspective

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.73) · Buckets: provision, wisdom

### 73:1

מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף אַךְ טוֹב לְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֱלֹהִים לְבָרֵי לֵבָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-ah-SAHF AHKH TOHV le-yees-rah-EHL eh-loh-HEEM le-vah-REY leh-VAHV

A Psalm of Asaph. Surely God is good to Israel, even to such as are pure in heart.

### 73:2

וַאֲנִי כִּמְעַט נטוי [נָטָיוּ] רַגְלָי כְּאַיִן שפכה [שֻׁפְּכוּ] אֲשֻׁרָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE keem-AHT nah-TAH-yoo rahg-LAHY ke-AH-yeen shoo-pe-KHOO ah-shoo-RAHY

But as for me, my feet were almost gone; my steps had well nigh slipped.

Reading note: written נטוי → read נָטָ֣יוּ; written שפכה → read שֻׁפְּכ֥וּ. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 73:3

כִּי־קִנֵּאתִי בַּהוֹלְלִים שְׁלוֹם רְשָׁעִים אֶרְאֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee kee-neh-TEE bah-hoh-le-LEEM she-LOHM re-shah-EEM ehr-EH

For I was envious at the arrogant, when I saw the prosperity of the wicked.

### 73:4

כִּי אֵין חַרְצֻבּוֹת לְמוֹתָם וּבָרִיא אוּלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE EYN khahr-tsoo-BOHT le-moh-TAHM oo-vah-REE oo-LAHM

For there are no pangs at their death, and their body is sound.

### 73:5

בַּעֲמַל אֱנוֹשׁ אֵינֵמוֹ וְעִם־אָדָם לֹא יְנֻגָּעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ah-MAHL eh-NOHSH ey-NEH-moh ve-eem ah-DAHM LOH ye-noo-GAH-oo

In the trouble of man they are not; neither are they plagued like men.

### 73:6

לָכֵן עֲנָקַתְמוֹ גַאֲוָה יַעֲטָף־שִׁית חָמָס לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-KHEHN ah-nah-KAHT-moh gah-ah-VAH yah-ah-tahf SHEET khah-MAHS LAH-moh

Therefore pride is as a chain about their neck; violence covereth them as a garment.

### 73:7

יָצָא מֵחֵלֶב עֵינֵמוֹ עָבְרוּ מַשְׂכִּיּוֹת לֵבָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-TSAH meh-KHEH-lehv ey-NEH-moh ah-ve-ROO mahs-kee-YOHT leh-VAHV

Their eyes stand forth from fatness; they are gone beyond the imaginations of their heart.

### 73:8

יָמִיקוּ וִידַבְּרוּ בְרָע עֹשֶׁק מִמָּרוֹם יְדַבֵּרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-MEE-koo  vee-dah-be-ROO ve-RAH OH-shehk mee-mah-ROHM ye-dah-BEH-roo

They scoff, and in wickedness utter oppression; they speak as if there were none on high.

### 73:9

שַׁתּוּ בַשָּׁמַיִם פִּיהֶם וּלְשׁוֹנָם תִּהֲלַךְ בָּאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-TOO vah-shah-MAH-yeem pee-HEHM oo-le-shoh-NAHM tee-hah-LAHKH bah-AH-rehts

They have set their mouth against the heavens, And their tongue walketh through the earth.

### 73:10

לָכֵן ישיב [יָשׁוּב] עַמּוֹ הֲלֹם וּמֵי מָלֵא יִמָּצוּ לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-KHEHN  yah-SHOOV ah-MOH hah-LOHM oo-MEY mah-LEH yee-MAH-tsoo LAH-moh

Therefore His people return hither; And waters of fullness are drained out by them.

Reading note: written ישיב → read יָשׁ֣וּב. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 73:11

וְאָמְרוּ אֵיכָה יָדַע־אֵל וְיֵשׁ דֵּעָה בְעֶלְיוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-me-ROO ey-KHAH yah-dah EHL ve-YEHSH deh-AH ve-ehl-YOHN

And they say: 'How doth God know? And is there knowledge in the Most High?'

### 73:12

הִנֵּה־אֵלֶּה רְשָׁעִים וְשַׁלְוֵי עוֹלָם הִשְׂגּוּ־חָיִל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-neh EH-leh re-shah-EEM ve-shahl-VEY oh-LAHM hees-goo KHAH-yeel

Behold, such are the wicked; And they that are always at ease increase riches.

### 73:13

אַךְ־רִיק זִכִּיתִי לְבָבִי וָאֶרְחַץ בְּנִקָּיוֹן כַּפָּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahkh REEK zee-KEE-tee le-vah-VEE vah-ehr-KHAHTS be-nee-kah-YOHN kah-PAHY

Surely in vain have I cleansed my heart, And washed my hands in innocency;

### 73:14

וָאֱהִי נָגוּעַ כָּל־הַיּוֹם וְתוֹכַחְתִּי לַבְּקָרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-eh-HEE nah-goo-AH kohl hah-YOHM ve-toh-khahkh-TEE lah-be-kah-REEM

For all the day have I been plagued, And my chastisement came every morning.

### 73:15

אִם־אָמַרְתִּי אֲסַפְּרָה כְמוֹ הִנֵּה דוֹר בָּנֶיךָ בָגָדְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eem ah-mahr-TEE ah-sah-pe-RAH khe-MOH hee-NEH DOHR bah-NEY-khah vah-GAH-de-tee

If I had said: 'I will speak thus', Behold, I had been faithless to the generation of Thy children.

### 73:16

וָאֲחַשְּׁבָה לָדַעַת זֹאת עָמָל היא [הוּא] בְעֵינָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-khah-she-VAH lah-DAH-aht ZOHT ah-MAHL HOO ve-ey-NAHY

And when I pondered how I might know this, It was wearisome in mine eyes;

Reading note: written היא → read ה֣וּא. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 73:17

עַד־אָבוֹא אֶל־מִקְדְּשֵׁי־אֵל אָבִינָה לְאַחֲרִיתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd ah-VOH ehl meek-de-shey EHL ah-VEE-nah le-ah-khah-ree-TAHM

Until I entered into the sanctuary of God, And considered their end.

### 73:18

אַךְ בַּחֲלָקוֹת תָּשִׁית לָמוֹ הִפַּלְתָּם לְמַשּׁוּאוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH bah-khah-lah-KOHT tah-SHEET LAH-moh hee-pahl-TAHM le-mah-shoo-OHT

Surely Thou settest them in slippery places; Thou hurlest them down to utter ruin.

### 73:19

אֵיךְ הָיוּ לְשַׁמָּה כְרָגַע סָפוּ תַמּוּ מִן־בַּלָּהוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EYKH hah-YOO le-shah-MAH khe-RAH-gah SAH-foo tah-MOO meen bah-lah-HOHT

How are they become a desolation in a moment! They are wholly consumed by terrors.

### 73:20

כַּחֲלוֹם מֵהָקִיץ אֲדֹנָי בָּעִיר צַלְמָם תִּבְזֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-khah-LOHM meh-hah-KEETS ah-doh-NAHY bah-EER  tsahl-MAHM teev-ZEH

As a dream when one awaketh, So, O Lord, when Thou arousest Thyself, Thou wilt despise their semblance.

### 73:21

כִּי יִתְחַמֵּץ לְבָבִי וְכִלְיוֹתַי אֶשְׁתּוֹנָן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE yeet-khah-MEHTS le-vah-VEE ve-kheel-yoh-TAHY ehsh-toh-NAHN

For my heart was in a ferment, And I was pricked in my reins. .

### 73:22

וַאֲנִי־בַעַר וְלֹא אֵדָע בְּהֵמוֹת הָיִיתִי עִמָּךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-nee vah-AHR ve-LOH eh-DAH be-heh-MOHT hah-YEE-tee ee-MAHKH

But I was brutish, and ignorant; I was as a beast before Thee.

### 73:23

וַאֲנִי תָמִיד עִמָּךְ אָחַזְתָּ בְּיַד־יְמִינִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE tah-MEED ee-MAHKH ah-KHAHZ-tah be-yahd ye-mee-NEE

Nevertheless I am continually with Thee; Thou holdest my right hand.

### 73:24

בַּעֲצָתְךָ תַנְחֵנִי וְאַחַר כָּבוֹד תִּקָּחֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ah-tsah-te-KHAH tahn-KHEH-nee ve-ah-KHAHR kah-VOHD tee-kah-KHEH-nee

Thou wilt guide me with Thy counsel, And afterward receive me with glory.

### 73:25

מִי־לִי בַשָּׁמָיִם וְעִמְּךָ לֹא־חָפַצְתִּי בָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee LEE vah-shah-MAH-yeem ve-ee-me-KHAH loh khah-FAHTS-tee vah-AH-rehts

Whom have I in heaven but Thee? And beside Thee I desire none upon earth.

### 73:26

כָּלָה שְׁאֵרִי וּלְבָבִי צוּר־לְבָבִי וְחֶלְקִי אֱלֹהִים לְעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-LAH she-eh-REE oo-le-vah-VEE tsoor le-vah-VEE ve-khehl-KEE eh-loh-HEEM le-oh-LAHM

My flesh and my heart faileth; But God is the rock of my heart and my portion for ever.

### 73:27

כִּי־הִנֵּה רְחֵקֶיךָ יֹאבֵדוּ הִצְמַתָּה כָּל־זוֹנֶה מִמֶּךָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hee-NEH re-kheh-KEY-khah yoh-VEH-doo heets-MAH-tah kohl zoh-NEH mee-MEH-kah

For, lo, they that go far from Thee shall perish; Thou dost destroy all them that go astray from Thee.

### 73:28

וַאֲנִי קִרֲבַת אֱלֹהִים לִי־טוֹב שַׁתִּי בַּאדֹנָי יְהֹוִה מַחְסִי לְסַפֵּר כָּל־מַלְאֲכוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  kee-rah-VAHT eh-loh-HEEM LEE TOHV shah-TEE  bah-doh-NAHY eh-loh-HEEM mahkh-SEE le-sah-PEHR kohl mahl-ah-khoh-TEY-khah

But as for me, the nearness of God is my good; I have made the Lord GOD my refuge, That I may tell of all Thy works.

## Psalm 74 — Communal devastation and remembrance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.74) · Buckets: grief, community

### 74:1

מַשְׂכִּיל לְאָסָף לָמָה אֱלֹהִים זָנַחְתָּ לָנֶצַח יֶעְשַׁן אַפְּךָ בְּצֹאן מַרְעִיתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahs-KEEL le-ah-SAHF lah-MAH eh-loh-HEEM zah-NAHKH-tah lah-NEH-tsahkh yeh-SHAHN ah-pe-KHAH be-TSOHN mahr-ee-TEH-khah

Maschil of Asaph. Why, O God, hast Thou cast us off for ever? Why doth Thine anger smoke against the flock of Thy pasture?

### 74:2

זְכֹר עֲדָתְךָ קָנִיתָ קֶּדֶם גָּאַלְתָּ שֵׁבֶט נַחֲלָתֶךָ הַר־צִיּוֹן זֶה שָׁכַנְתָּ בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ze-KHOHR ah-dah-te-KHAH  kah-NEE-tah KEH-dehm gah-ahl-TAH SHEH-veht nah-khah-lah-TEH-khah hahr tsee-YOHN ZEH  shah-KHAHN-tah BOH

Remember Thy congregation, which Thou hast gotten of old, Which Thou hast redeemed to be the tribe of Thine inheritance; And mount Zion, wherein Thou hast dwelt.

### 74:3

הָרִימָה פְעָמֶיךָ לְמַשֻּׁאוֹת נֶצַח כָּל־הֵרַע אוֹיֵב בַּקֹּדֶשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-REE-mah fe-ah-mey-KHAH le-mah-shoo-OHT NEH-tsahkh kohl heh-RAH oh-YEHV bah-KOH-dehsh

Lift up Thy steps because of the perpetual ruins, Even all the evil that the enemy hath done in the sanctuary.

### 74:4

שָׁאֲגוּ צֹרְרֶיךָ בְּקֶרֶב מוֹעֲדֶךָ שָׂמוּ אוֹתֹתָם אֹתוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-ah-GOO tsoh-re-rey-KHAH be-KEH-rehv moh-ah-DEH-khah SAH-moo oh-toh-TAHM oh-TOHT

Thine adversaries have roared in the midst of Thy meeting-place; They have set up their own signs for signs.

### 74:5

יִוָּדַע כְּמֵבִיא לְמָעְלָה בִּסֲבָךְ־עֵץ קַרְדֻּמּוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-vah-DAH ke-meh-VEE le-MAH-e-lah bee-sah-vahkh EHTS kahr-doo-MOHT

It seemed as when men wield upwards Axes in a thicket of trees.

### 74:6

ועת [וְעַתָּה] פִּתּוּחֶיהָ יָּחַד בְּכַשִּׁיל וְכֵילַפֹּת יַהֲלֹמוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH pee-too-KHEY-hah YAH-khahd be-khah-SHEEL ve-khey-lah-POHT yah-hah-loh-MOON

And now all the carved work thereof together They strike down with hatchet and hammers.

Reading note: written ועת → read וְ֭עַתָּה. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 74:7

שִׁלְחוּ בָאֵשׁ מִקְדָּשֶׁךָ לָאָרֶץ חִלְּלוּ מִשְׁכַּן־שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheel-KHOO vah-EHSH meek-dah-SHEH-khah lah-AH-rehts khee-le-LOO mee-she-kahn she-MEH-khah

They have set Thy sanctuary on fire; They have profaned the dwelling-place of Thy name even to the ground.

### 74:8

אָמְרוּ בְלִבָּם נִינָם יָחַד שָׂרְפוּ כָל־מוֹעֲדֵי־אֵל בָּאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-me-ROO ve-lee-BAHM nee-NAHM YAH-khahd sah-re-FOO khohl moh-ah-dey EHL bah-AH-rehts

They said in their heart: 'Let us make havoc of them altogether'; They have burned up all the meeting-places of God in the land.

### 74:9

אוֹתֹתֵינוּ לֹא רָאִינוּ אֵין־עוֹד נָבִיא וְלֹא־אִתָּנוּ יֹדֵעַ עַד־מָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-toh-TEY-noo LOH rah-EE-noo eyn OHD nah-VEE ve-loh ee-TAH-noo yoh-DEH-ah ahd MAH

We see not our signs; There is no more any prophet; Neither is there among us any that knoweth how long.

### 74:10

עַד־מָתַי אֱלֹהִים יְחָרֶף צָר יְנָאֵץ אוֹיֵב שִׁמְךָ לָנֶצַח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd mah-TAHY eh-loh-HEEM ye-KHAH-rehf TSAHR ye-nah-EHTS oh-YEHV sheem-KHAH lah-NEH-tsahkh

How long, O God, shall the adversary reproach? Shall the enemy blaspheme Thy name for ever?

### 74:11

לָמָּה תָשִׁיב יָדְךָ וִימִינֶךָ מִקֶּרֶב חוקך [חֵיקְךָ] כַלֵּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** LAH-mah tah-SHEEV yah-de-KHAH vee-mee-NEH-khah mee-KEH-rehv khey-ke-KHAH khah-LEH

Why withdrawest Thou Thy hand, even Thy right hand? Draw it out of Thy bosom and consume them.

Reading note: written חוקך → read חֵֽיקְךָ֣. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 74:12

וֵאלֹהִים מַלְכִּי מִקֶּדֶם פֹּעֵל יְשׁוּעוֹת בְּקֶרֶב הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** veh-loh-HEEM mahl-KEE mee-KEH-dehm poh-EHL ye-shoo-OHT be-KEH-rehv hah-AH-rehts

Yet God is my King of old, Working salvation in the midst of the earth.

### 74:13

אַתָּה פוֹרַרְתָּ בְעָזְּךָ יָם שִׁבַּרְתָּ רָאשֵׁי תַנִּינִים עַל־הַמָּיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH foh-RAHR-tah ve-oh-ze-KHAH YAHM shee-BAHR-tah rah-SHEY tah-nee-NEEM ahl hah-MAH-yeem

Thou didst break the sea in pieces by Thy strength; Thou didst shatter the heads of the sea-monsters in the waters.

### 74:14

אַתָּה רִצַּצְתָּ רָאשֵׁי לִוְיָתָן תִּתְּנֶנּוּ מַאֲכָל לְעָם לְצִיִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH ree-tsahts-TAH rah-SHEY leev-yah-TAHN tee-te-NEH-noo mah-ah-KHAHL le-AHM le-tsee-YEEM

Thou didst crush the heads of leviathan, Thou gavest him to be food to the folk inhabiting the wilderness.

### 74:15

אַתָּה בָקַעְתָּ מַעְיָן וָנָחַל אַתָּה הוֹבַשְׁתָּ נַהֲרוֹת אֵיתָן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH vah-kah-TAH mah-YAHN vah-NAH-khahl ah-TAH hoh-VAHSH-tah nah-hah-ROHT ey-TAHN

Thou didst cleave fountain and brook; Thou driedst up ever-flowing rivers.

### 74:16

לְךָ יוֹם אַף־לְךָ לָיְלָה אַתָּה הֲכִינוֹתָ מָאוֹר וָשָׁמֶשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-KHAH YOHM ahf le-KHAH LAH-ye-lah ah-TAH hah-khee-NOH-tah mah-OHR vah-SHAH-mehsh

Thine is the day, Thine also the night; Thou hast established luminary and sun.

### 74:17

אַתָּה הִצַּבְתָּ כָּל־גְּבוּלוֹת אָרֶץ קַיִץ וָחֹרֶף אַתָּה יְצַרְתָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH hee-tsahv-TAH kohl ge-voo-LOHT AH-rehts KAH-yeets vah-KHOH-rehf ah-TAH ye-tsahr-tahm

Thou hast set all the borders of the earth; Thou hast made summer and winter.

### 74:18

זְכָר־זֹאת אוֹיֵב חֵרֵף יְהוָה וְעַם נָבָל נִאֲצוּ שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ze-khahr ZOHT oh-YEHV kheh-REHF  ah-doh-NAI ve-AHM nah-VAHL nee-ah-TSOO she-MEH-khah

Remember this, how the enemy hath reproached the LORD, And how a base people have blasphemed Thy name.

### 74:19

אַל־תִּתֵּן לְחַיַּת נֶפֶשׁ תּוֹרֶךָ חַיַּת עֲנִיֶּיךָ אַל־תִּשְׁכַּח לָנֶצַח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tee-TEHN le-khah-YAHT NEH-fehsh toh-REH-khah khah-YAHT ah-nee-YEY-khah ahl teesh-KAHKH lah-NEH-tsahkh

O deliver not the soul of Thy turtle-dove unto the wild beast; Forget not the life of Thy poor for ever.

### 74:20

הַבֵּט לַבְּרִית כִּי מָלְאוּ מַחֲשַׁכֵּי־אֶרֶץ נְאוֹת חָמָס׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-BEHT lah-be-REET KEE mah-le-OO mah-khah-shah-key eh-REHTS ne-OHT khah-MAHS

Look upon the covenant; For the dark places of the land are full of the habitations of violence.

### 74:21

אַל־יָשֹׁב דַּךְ נִכְלָם עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן יְהַלְלוּ שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl yah-SHOHV DAHKH neekh-LAHM ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN ye-hah-le-LOO she-MEH-khah

O let not the oppressed turn back in confusion; Let the poor and needy praise Thy name.

### 74:22

קוּמָה אֱלֹהִים רִיבָה רִיבֶךָ זְכֹר חֶרְפָּתְךָ מִנִּי־נָבָל כָּל־הַיּוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH eh-loh-HEEM ree-VAH ree-VEH-khah ze-KHOHR khehr-pah-te-KHAH mee-nee nah-VAHL kohl hah-YOHM

Arise, O God, plead Thine own cause; Remember Thy reproach all the day at the hand of the base man.

### 74:23

אַל־תִּשְׁכַּח קוֹל צֹרְרֶיךָ שְׁאוֹן קָמֶיךָ עֹלֶה תָמִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl teesh-KAHKH KOHL tsoh-re-REY-khah she-OHN kah-MEY-khah oh-LEH tah-MEED

Forget not the voice of Thine adversaries, The tumult of those that rise up against Thee which ascendeth continually.

## Psalm 75 — Gratitude and just judgment

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.75) · Buckets: gratitude, justice

### 75:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ אַל־תַּשְׁחֵת מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף שִׁיר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ahl tahsh-KHEHT meez-MOHR le-ah-SAHF SHEER

For the Leader; Al-tashheth. A Psalm of Asaph, a Song.

### 75:2

הוֹדִינוּ לְּךָ אֱ‍לֹהִים הוֹדִינוּ וְקָרוֹב שְׁמֶךָ סִפְּרוּ נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DEE-noo le-KHAH  eh-loh-HEEM‍ hoh-dee-NOO ve-kah-ROHV she-MEH-khah see-pe-ROO neef-le-oh-TEY-khah

We give thanks unto Thee, O God, We give thanks, and Thy name is near; Men tell of Thy wondrous works.

### 75:3

כִּי אֶקַּח מוֹעֵד אֲנִי מֵישָׁרִים אֶשְׁפֹּט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE eh-KAHKH moh-EHD ah-NEE mey-shah-REEM ehsh-POHT

'When I take the appointed time, I Myself will judge with equity.

### 75:4

נְמֹגִים אֶרֶץ וְכָל־יֹשְׁבֶיהָ אָנֹכִי תִכַּנְתִּי עַמּוּדֶיהָ סֶּלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-moh-GEEM EH-rehts ve-khohl yoh-she-VEY-hah ah-noh-KHEE tee-KAHN-tee ah-moo-DEY-hah SEH-lah

When the earth and all the inhabitants thereof are dissolved, I Myself establish the pillars of it.' Selah

### 75:5

אָמַרְתִּי לַהוֹלְלִים אַל־תָּהֹלּוּ וְלָרְשָׁעִים אַל־תָּרִימוּ קָרֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR-tee lah-hoh-le-LEEM ahl tah-HOH-loo ve-lah-re-shah-EEM ahl tah-REE-moo KAH-rehn

I say unto the arrogant 'Deal not arrogantly'; And to the wicked 'Lift not up the horn.'

### 75:6

אַל־תָּרִימוּ לַמָּרוֹם קַרְנְכֶם תְּדַבְּרוּ בְצַוָּאר עָתָק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tah-REE-moo lah-mah-ROHM kahr-ne-KHEHM te-dah-be-ROO ve-tsah-VAHR ah-TAHK

Lift not up your horn on high; Speak not insolence with a haughty neck.

### 75:7

כִּי לֹא מִמּוֹצָא וּמִמַּעֲרָב וְלֹא מִמִּדְבַּר הָרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE LOH mee-moh-TSAH oo-mee-mah-ah-RAHV ve-LOH mee-meed-BAHR hah-REEM

For neither from the east, nor from the west, Nor yet from the wilderness, cometh lifting up.

### 75:8

כִּי־אֱלֹהִים שֹׁפֵט זֶה יַשְׁפִּיל וְזֶה יָרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee eh-loh-HEEM shoh-FEHT ZEH yahsh-PEEL ve-ZEH yah-REEM

For God is judge; He putteth down one, and lifteth up another.

### 75:9

כִּי כוֹס בְּיַד־יְהוָה וְיַיִן חָמַר מָלֵא מֶסֶךְ וַיַּגֵּר מִזֶּה אַךְ־שְׁמָרֶיהָ יִמְצוּ יִשְׁתּוּ כֹּל רִשְׁעֵי־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE KHOHS be-yahd ah-doh-NAI ve-YAH-yeen khah-MAHR  MAH-leh meh-SEHKH vah-yah-GEHR mee-ZEH ahkh she-mah-rey-HAH yeem-TSOO yeesh-TOO KOHL reesh-ey AH-rehts

For in the hand of the LORD there is a cup, with foaming wine, full of mixture, And He poureth out of the same; Surely the dregs thereof, all the wicked of the earth shall drain them, and drink them.

### 75:10

וַאֲנִי אַגִּיד לְעֹלָם אֲזַמְּרָה לֵאלֹהֵי יַעֲקֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE ah-GEED le-oh-LAHM ah-zah-me-RAH leh-loh-HEY yah-ah-KOHV

But as for me, I will declare for ever, I will sing praises to the God of Jacob.

### 75:11

וְכָל־קַרְנֵי רְשָׁעִים אֲגַדֵּעַ תְּרוֹמַמְנָה קַרְנוֹת צַדִּיק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-khohl kahr-NEY re-shah-EEM ah-gah-DEH-ah te-roh-MAHM-nah kah-re-NOHT tsah-DEEK

All the horns of the wicked also will I cut off; But the horns of the righteous shall be lifted up.

## Psalm 76 — Awe, judgment, and the ending of warfare

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.76) · Buckets: peace, justice

### 76:1

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh been-gee-NOHT meez-MOHR le-ah-SAHF SHEER

For the Leader; with string-music. A Psalm of Asaph, a Song.

### 76:2

נוֹדָע בִּיהוּדָה אֱלֹהִים בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל גָּדוֹל שְׁמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** noh-DAH bee-hoo-DAH eh-loh-HEEM be-yees-rah-EHL gah-DOHL she-MOH

In Judah is God known; His name is great in Israel.

### 76:3

וַיְהִי בְשָׁלֵם סֻכּוֹ וּמְעוֹנָתוֹ בְצִיּוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-HEE ve-shah-LEHM soo-KOH oo-me-OH-nah-TOH ve-tsee-YOHN

### 76:4

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-MAH shee-BAHR reesh-fey KAH-sheht mah-GEHN ve-KHEH-rehv oo-meel-khah-MAH SEH-lah

Glorious art Thou and excellent, coming down from the mountains of prey.

### 76:6

אֶשְׁתּוֹלְלוּ אַבִּירֵי לֵב נָמוּ שְׁנָתָם וְלֹא־מָצְאוּ כָל־אַנְשֵׁי־חַיִל יְדֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehsh-toh-le-LOO  ah-BEE-rey LEHV nah-MOO she-nah-TAHM ve-loh mah-tse-OO khohl ahn-shey KHAH-yeel ye-dey-HEHM

### 76:7

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-gah-ah-RAH-te-KHAH eh-loh-HEY yah-ah-KOHV neer-DAHM ve-REH-khehv vah-SOOS

### 76:8

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH  NOH-rah AH-tah oo-mee yah-ah-MOHD le-fah-NEY-khah meh-AHZ ah-PEH-khah

Thou, even Thou, art terrible; And who may stand in Thy sight when once Thou art angry?

### 76:9

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-shah-mah-YEEM heesh-MAH-tah DEEN EH-rehts yah-re-AH ve-shah-KAH-tah

Thou didst cause sentence to be heard from heaven; The earth feared, and was still,

### 76:10

בְּקוּם־לַמִּשְׁפָּט אֱלֹהִים לְהוֹשִׁיעַ כָּל־עַנְוֵי־אֶרֶץ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-koom lah-meesh-PAHT eh-loh-HEEM le-hoh-SHEE-ah kohl ahn-vey EH-rehts SEH-lah

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khah-MAHT ah-DAHM toh-DEH-kah she-eh-REET kheh-MOHT tahkh-GOHR

**Pronunciation draft:** nee-dah-ROO ve-shah-le-MOO lah-ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-hey-KHEHM‍ kohl se-vee-VAHV yoh-VEE-loo SHAHY lah-moh-RAH

### 76:13

**Pronunciation draft:** yeev-TSOHR ROO-ahkh ne-gee-DEEM noh-RAH le-mahl-khey AH-rehts

## Psalm 77 — Sleepless distress and recalling deliverance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.77) · Buckets: sleep, wellness

### 77:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ עַל־ידיתון [יְדוּתוּן] לְאָסָף מִזְמוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh ah-ye-doo-TOON le-ah-SAHF meez-MOHR

For the Leader; for Jeduthun. A Psalm of Asaph.

### 77:2

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-LEE ehl eh-loh-HEEM ve-ehts-AH-kah koh-LEE ehl eh-loh-HEEM ve-hah-ah-ZEEN eh-LAHY

I will lift up my voice unto God, an cry; I will lift up my voice unto God, that He may give ear unto me.

### 77:3

בְּיוֹם צָרָתִי אֲדֹנָי דָּרָשְׁתִּי יָדִי לַיְלָה נִגְּרָה וְלֹא תָפוּג מֵאֲנָה הִנָּחֵם נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-YOHM tsah-rah-TEE ah-doh-NAHY dah-RAH-she-tee yah-DEE  LAHY-lah nee-ge-RAH ve-LOH tah-FOOG meh-ah-NAH hee-nah-KHEHM nahf-SHEE

In the day of my trouble I seek the Lord; With my hand uplifted, [mine eye] streameth in the night without ceasing; My soul refuseth to be comforted.

### 77:4

אֶזְכְּרָה אֱלֹהִים וְאֶהֱמָיָה אָשִׂיחָה וְתִתְעַטֵּף רוּחִי סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehz-ke-RAH eh-loh-HEEM ve-eh-heh-mah-YAH ah-SEE-khah  ve-teet-ah-TEHF roo-KHEE SEH-lah

When I think thereon, O God, I must moan; When I muse thereon, my spirit fainteth. Selah .

### 77:5

אָחַזְתָּ שְׁמֻרוֹת עֵינָי נִפְעַמְתִּי וְלֹא אֲדַבֵּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-khahz-TAH she-moo-ROHT ey-NAHY neef-AHM-tee ve-LOH ah-dah-BEHR

Thou holdest fast the lids of mine eyes; I am troubled, and cannot speak.

### 77:6

**Pronunciation draft:** khee-SHAHV-tee yah-MEEM mee-KEH-dehm she-NOHT oh-lah-MEEM

I have pondered the days of old, the years of ancient times.

### 77:7

אֶזְכְּרָה נְגִינָתִי בַּלָּיְלָה עִם־לְבָבִי אָשִׂיחָה וַיְחַפֵּשׂ רוּחִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-ze-ke-RAH ne-gee-nah-TEE bah-LAH-ye-lah eem le-vah-VEE ah-SEE-khah vah-ye-khah-PEHS roo-KHEE

In the night I will call to remembrance my song; I will commune with mine own heart; And my spirit maketh diligent search

### 77:8

הַלְעוֹלָמִים יִזְנַח אֲדֹנָי וְלֹא־יֹסִיף לִרְצוֹת עוֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-oh-lah-MEEM yeez-NAHKH  ah-doh-NAHY ve-loh yoh-SEEF leer-TSOHT OHD

'Will the Lord cast off for ever? And will He be favourable no more?

### 77:9

הֶאָפֵס לָנֶצַח חַסְדּוֹ גָּמַר אֹמֶר לְדֹר וָדֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heh-ah-FEHS lah-NEH-tsahkh khahs-DOH GAH-mahr oh-MEHR le-DOHR vah-DOHR

Is His mercy clean gone for ever? Is His promise come to an end for evermore?

### 77:10

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-shah-KHAHKH khah-NOHT EHL eem kah-FAHTS be-AHF rah-khah-MAHV SEH-lah

Hath God forgotten to be gracious? Hath He in anger shut up his compassions?' Selah

### 77:11

וָאֹמַר חַלּוֹתִי הִיא שְׁנוֹת יְמִין עֶלְיוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-oh-MAHR khah-LOH-tee HEE she-NOHT ye-MEEN ehl-YOHN

### 77:12

אזכיר [אֶזְכּוֹר] מַעַלְלֵי־יָהּ כִּי־אֶזְכְּרָה מִקֶּדֶם פִּלְאֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehz-KOHR mah-ahl-ley YAHH kee ehz-ke-RAH mee-KEH-dehm peel-EH-khah

### 77:13

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hah-GEE-tee ve-khohl poh-oh-LEH-khah oo-vah-ah-LEE-loh-TEY-khah ah-SEE-khah

### 77:14

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM bah-KOH-dehsh dahr-KEH-khah mee EHL gah-DOHL keh-loh-HEEM

### 77:15

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH hah-EHL OH-seh FEH-leh hoh-DAH-tah vah-ah-MEEM oo-ZEH-khah

### 77:16

גָּאַלְתָּ בִּזְרוֹעַ עַמֶּךָ בְּנֵי־יַעֲקֹב וְיוֹסֵף סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-AHL-tah beez-ROH-ah ah-MEH-khah be-ney yah-ah-KOHV ve-yoh-SEHF SEH-lah

Thou hast with Thine arm redeemed Thy people, The sons of Jacob and Joseph. Selah

### 77:17

רָאוּךָ מַּיִם אֱ‍לֹהִים רָאוּךָ מַּיִם יָחִילוּ אַף יִרְגְּזוּ תְהֹמוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem  eh-loh-HEEM‍ rah-OO-khah MAH-yeem yah-KHEE-loo AHF yeer-ge-ZOO te-hoh-MOHT

The waters saw Thee, O God; The waters saw Thee, they were in pain; The depths also trembled.

### 77:18

זֹרְמוּ מַיִם עָבוֹת קוֹל נָתְנוּ שְׁחָקִים אַף־חֲצָצֶיךָ יִתְהַלָּכוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZOH-re-moo MAH-yeem  ah-VOHT KOHL nah-te-NOO she-khah-KEEM ahf khah-tsah-TSEY-khah yeet-hah-LAH-khoo

The clouds flooded forth waters; The skies sent out a sound; Thine arrows also went abroad.

### 77:19

קוֹל רַעַמְךָ בַּגַּלְגַּל הֵאִירוּ בְרָקִים תֵּבֵל רָגְזָה וַתִּרְעַשׁ הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOHL rah-ahm-KHAH  bah-gahl-GAHL heh-EE-roo ve-rah-KEEM teh-VEHL rohg-ZAH vah-teer-AHSH hah-AH-rehts

The voice of Thy thunder was in the whirlwind; The lightnings lighted up the world; The earth trembled and shook.

### 77:20

בַּיָּם דַּרְכֶּךָ ושביליך [וּשְׁבִילְךָ] בְּמַיִם רַבִּים וְעִקְּבוֹתֶיךָ לֹא נֹדָעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-YAHM dahr-KEH-khah oo-she-vee-le-KHAH be-MAH-yeem rah-BEEM ve-ee-ke-voh-TEY-khah LOH noh-DAH-oo

Thy way was in the sea, And Thy path in the great waters, And Thy footsteps were not known.

Reading note: written ושביליך → read וּֽ֭שְׁבִֽילְךָ. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 77:21

נָחִיתָ כַצֹּאן עַמֶּךָ בְּיַד־מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-KHEE-tah khah-TSOHN ah-MEH-khah be-yahd moh-SHEH ve-ah-hah-ROHN

## Psalm 78 — Teaching history to the next generation

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.78) · Buckets: learning, children, community

### 78:1

מַשְׂכִּיל לְאָסָף הַאֲזִינָה עַמִּי תּוֹרָתִי הַטּוּ אָזְנְכֶם לְאִמְרֵי־פִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahs-KEEL le-ah-SAHF hah-ah-ZEE-nah ah-MEE toh-rah-TEE hah-TOO ohz-ne-KHEHM le-eem-rey FEE

Maschil of Asaph. Give ear, O my people, to my teaching; Incline your ears to the words of my mouth.

### 78:2

אֶפְתְּחָה בְמָשָׁל פִּי אַבִּיעָה חִידוֹת מִנִּי־קֶדֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehf-te-KHAH ve-mah-SHAHL PEE ah-BEE-ah khee-DOHT mee-nee KEH-dehm

I will open my mouth with a parable; I will utter dark sayings concerning days of old;

### 78:3

אֲשֶׁר שָׁמַעְנוּ וַנֵּדָעֵם וַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ סִפְּרוּ־לָנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR shah-mah-NOO vah-neh-dah-EHM vah-ah-voh-TEY-noo see-pe-roo LAH-noo

That which we have heard and known, And our fathers have told us,

### 78:4

לֹא נְכַחֵד מִבְּנֵיהֶם לְדוֹר אַחֲרוֹן מְסַפְּרִים תְּהִלּוֹת יְהוָה וֶעֱזוּזוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** LOH ne-khah-KHEHD  mee-be-ney-HEHM le-DOHR ah-khah-ROHN me-sah-pe-REEM te-hee-LOHT ah-doh-NAI veh-eh-zoo-ZOH ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV ah-SHEHR ah-SAH

We will not hide from their children, Telling to the generation to come the praises of the LORD, And His strength, and His wondrous works that He hath done. .

### 78:5

וַיָּקֶם עֵדוּת בְּיַעֲקֹב וְתוֹרָה שָׂם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר צִוָּה אֶת־אֲבוֹתֵינוּ לְהוֹדִיעָם לִבְנֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YAH-kehm eh-DOOT  be-yah-ah-KOHV ve-toh-RAH SAHM be-yees-rah-EHL ah-SHEHR tsee-VAH eht ah-voh-TEY-noo le-hoh-dee-AHM leev-ney-HEHM

For He established a testimony in Jacob, And appointed a law in Israel, Which He commanded our fathers, That they should make them known to their children;

### 78:6

לְמַעַן יֵדְעוּ דּוֹר אַחֲרוֹן בָּנִים יִוָּלֵדוּ יָקֻמוּ וִיסַפְּרוּ לִבְנֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-MAH-ahn yeh-de-OO  DOHR ah-khah-ROHN bah-NEEM yee-vah-LEH-doo yah-KOO-moo vee-sah-pe-ROO leev-ney-HEHM

That the generation to come might know them, even the children that should be born; Who should arise and tell them to their children,

### 78:7

וְיָשִׂימוּ בֵאלֹהִים כִּסְלָם וְלֹא יִשְׁכְּחוּ מַעַלְלֵי־אֵל וּמִצְוֺתָיו יִנְצֹרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yah-SEE-moo veh-loh-HEEM kees-LAHM ve-LOH yeesh-ke-KHOO mah-ahl-ley EHL oo-meets-voh-TAHV yeen-TSOH-roo

That they might put their confidence in God, And not forget the works of God, But keep His commandments;

### 78:8

וְלֹא יִהְיוּ כַּאֲבוֹתָם דּוֹר סוֹרֵר וּמֹרֶה דּוֹר לֹא־הֵכִין לִבּוֹ וְלֹא־נֶאֶמְנָה אֶת־אֵל רוּחוֹ׃

### 78:9

בְּנֵי־אֶפְרַיִם נוֹשְׁקֵי רוֹמֵי־קָשֶׁת הָפְכוּ בְּיוֹם קְרָב׃

The children of Ephraim were as archers handling the bow, That turned back in the day of battle.

### 78:10

**Pronunciation draft:** LOH shah-me-ROO be-REET eh-loh-HEEM oo-ve-toh-rah-TOH meh-ah-NOO lah-LEH-kheht

### 78:11

### 78:16

וַיִּזְכֹּר כִּי־בָשָׂר הֵמָּה רוּחַ הוֹלֵךְ וְלֹא יָשׁוּב׃

So He remembered that they were but flesh, A wind that passeth away, and cometh not again.

### 78:40

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-MAH yahm-ROO-hoo vah-meed-BAHR yah-ah-tsee-VOO-hoo bee-shee-MOHN

How oft did they rebel against Him in the wilderness, And grieve Him in the desert!

### 78:41

וַיָּשׁוּבוּ וַיְנַסּוּ אֵל וּקְדוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל הִתְווּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-SHOO-voo vah-ye-nah-SOO EHL oo-ke-DOHSH yees-rah-EHL heet-voo

And still again they tried God, And set bounds to the Holy One of Israel.

### 78:42

**Pronunciation draft:** loh zah-khe-ROO eht yah-DOH YOHM ah-shehr ‍pah-DAHM mee-nee TSAHR

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shehr SAHM be-meets-rah-YEEM oh-toh-TAHV oo-moh-fe-TAHV bees-deh TSOH-ahn

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-hah-FOHKH le-DAHM ye-oh-rey-HEHM ve-noh-ze-ley-HEHM bahl yeesh-tah-YOON

### 78:45

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN leh-khah-SEEL ye-voo-LAHM vee-gee-AHM lah-ahr-BEH

### 78:47

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-hah-ROHG bah-bah-RAHD gahf-NAHM ve-sheek-moh-TAHM bah-khah-nah-MAHL

### 78:48

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahs-GEHR lah-bah-RAHD be-ee-RAHM oo-meek-ney-HEHM lah-re-shah-FEEM

### 78:49

יְשַׁלַּח־בָּם חֲרוֹן אַפּוֹ עֶבְרָה וָזַעַם וְצָרָה מִשְׁלַחַת מַלְאֲכֵי רָעִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-shah-lahkh BAHM  khah-ROHN ah-POH ehv-RAH vah-ZAH-ahm ve-tsah-RAH meesh-LAH-khaht mahl-ah-KHEY rah-EEM

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-fah-LEHS nah-TEEV le-ah-POH loh khah-SAHKH mee-MAH-veht nahf-SHAHM ve-khah-yah-TAHM lah-DEH-vehr hees-GEER

### 78:51

וַיַּךְ כָּל־בְּכוֹר בְּמִצְרָיִם רֵאשִׁית אוֹנִים בְּאָהֳלֵי־חָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YAHKH kohl be-KHOHR be-meets-RAH-yeem reh-SHEET oh-NEEM be-oh-hoh-ley KHAHM

### 78:52

וַיַּסַּע כַּצֹּאן עַמּוֹ וַיְנַהֲגֵם כַּעֵדֶר בַּמִּדְבָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-SAH kah-TSOHN ah-MOH vah-ye-nah-hah-GEHM kah-EH-dehr bah-meed-BAHR

### 78:53

וַיַּנְחֵם לָבֶטַח וְלֹא פָחָדוּ וְאֶת־אוֹיְבֵיהֶם כִּסָּה הַיָּם׃

וַיְבִיאֵם אֶל־גְּבוּל קָדְשׁוֹ הַר־זֶה קָנְתָה יְמִינוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-vee-EHM ehl ge-VOOL kohd-SHOH hahr ZEH kah-ne-TAH ye-mee-NOH

And He brought them to His holy border, To the mountain, which His right hand had gotten.

### 78:55

וַיְגָרֶשׁ מִפְּנֵיהֶם גּוֹיִם וַיַּפִּילֵם בְּחֶבֶל נַחֲלָה וַיַּשְׁכֵּן בְּאָהֳלֵיהֶם שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-GAH-rehsh mee-pe-ney-HEHM  goh-YEEM vah-yah-pee-LEHM be-KHEH-vehl nah-khah-LAH vah-yahsh-KEHN be-oh-hoh-ley-HEHM sheev-TEY yees-rah-EHL

### 78:56

וַיְנַסּוּ וַיַּמְרוּ אֶת־אֱלֹהִים עֶלְיוֹן וְעֵדוֹתָיו לֹא שָׁמָרוּ׃

### 78:57

וַיִּסֹּגוּ וַיִּבְגְּדוּ כַּאֲבוֹתָם נֶהְפְּכוּ כְּקֶשֶׁת רְמִיָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-SOH-goo vah-yeev-ge-DOO kah-ah-voh-TAHM neh-pe-KHOO ke-KEH-sheht re-mee-YAH

### 78:58

וַיַּכְעִיסוּהוּ בְּבָמוֹתָם וּבִפְסִילֵיהֶם יַקְנִיאוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahkh-ee-SOO-hoo be-vah-moh-TAHM oo-veef-see-ley-HEHM yahk-nee-OO-hoo

For they provoked Him with their high places, And moved Him to jealousy with their graven images.

### 78:59

שָׁמַע אֱלֹהִים וַיִּתְעַבָּר וַיִּמְאַס מְאֹד בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-MAH eh-loh-HEEM vah-yeet-ah-BAHR vah-yeem-AHS me-OHD be-yees-rah-EHL

God heard, and was wroth, And He greatly abhorred Israel;

### 78:60

וַיִּטֹּשׁ מִשְׁכַּן שִׁלוֹ אֹהֶל שִׁכֵּן בָּאָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TOHSH meesh-KAHN shee-LOH oh-HEHL shee-KEHN bah-ah-DAHM

And He forsook the tabernacle of Shiloh, The tent which He had made to dwell among men;

### 78:61

וַיִּתֵּן לַשְּׁבִי עֻזּוֹ וְתִפְאַרְתּוֹ בְיַד־צָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN lah-she-VEE oo-ZOH ve-teef-ahr-TOH ve-yahd TSAHR

And delivered His strength into captivity, And His glory into the adversary's hand.

### 78:62

וַיַּסְגֵּר לַחֶרֶב עַמּוֹ וּבְנַחֲלָתוֹ הִתְעַבָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahs-GEHR lah-KHEH-rehv ah-MOH oo-ve-nah-khah-lah-TOH heet-ah-BAHR

### 78:63

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-khoo-RAHV ah-khe-lah EHSH oo-ve-too-loh-TAHV LOH hoo-LAH-loo

Fire devoured their young men; And their virgins had no marriage-song.

### 78:64

כֹּהֲנָיו בַּחֶרֶב נָפָלוּ וְאַלְמְנֹתָיו לֹא תִבְכֶּינָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-hah-NAHV bah-KHEH-rehv nah-FAH-loo ve-ahl-me-noh-TAHV LOH teev-KEY-nah

Their priests fell by the sword; And their widows made no lamentation. .

### 78:65

וַיִּקַץ כְּיָשֵׁן אֲדֹנָי כְּגִבּוֹר מִתְרוֹנֵן מִיָּיִן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-KAHTS ke-yah-SHEHN  ah-doh-NAHY ke-gee-BOHR meet-roh-NEHN mee-YAH-yeen

Then the Lord awaked as one asleep, Like a mighty man recovering from wine.

### 78:66

וַיַּךְ־צָרָיו אָחוֹר חֶרְפַּת עוֹלָם נָתַן לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahkh tsah-RAHV ah-KHOHR khehr-PAHT oh-LAHM NAH-tahn LAH-moh

And He smote His adversaries backward; He put upon them a perpetual reproach.

### 78:67

וַיִּמְאַס בְּאֹהֶל יוֹסֵף וּבְשֵׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם לֹא בָחָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeem-AHS be-OH-hehl yoh-SEHF oo-ve-SHEH-veht ehf-RAH-yeem LOH vah-KHAHR

### 78:68

וַיִּבְחַר אֶת־שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה אֶת־הַר צִיּוֹן אֲשֶׁר אָהֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeev-KHAHR eht SHEH-veht ye-hoo-DAH eht HAHR tsee-YOHN ah-SHEHR ah-HEHV

But chose the tribe of Judah, The mount Zion which He loved.

### 78:69

וַיִּבֶן כְּמוֹ־רָמִים מִקְדָּשׁוֹ כְּאֶרֶץ יְסָדָהּ לְעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YEE-vehn ke-moh rah-MEEM meek-dah-SHOH ke-EH-rehts ye-sah-DAHH le-oh-LAHM

### 78:70

וַיִּבְחַר בְּדָוִד עַבְדּוֹ וַיִּקָּחֵהוּ מִמִּכְלְאֹת צֹאן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeev-KHAHR be-dah-VEED ahv-DOH vah-yee-kah-KHEH-hoo mee-meekh-le-OHT TSOHN

He chose David also His servant, And took him from the sheepfolds;

### 78:71

מֵאַחַר עָלוֹת הֱבִיאוֹ לִרְעוֹת בְּיַעֲקֹב עַמּוֹ וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל נַחֲלָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meh-ah-KHAHR ah-LOHT heh-vee-OH leer-OHT be-yah-ah-KOHV ah-MOH oo-ve-yees-rah-EHL nah-khah-lah-TOH

From following the ewes that give suck He brought him, To be shepherd over Jacob His people, and Israel His inheritance.

### 78:72

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeer-EHM ke-TOHM le-vah-VOH oo-veet-voo-NOHT kah-PAHV yahn-KHEHM

So he shepherded them according to the integrity of his heart; And lead them by the skilfulness of his hands.

## Psalm 79 — Destruction, mourning, and appeal for mercy

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.79) · Buckets: grief, community

### 79:1

מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף אֱ‍לֹהִים בָּאוּ גוֹיִם בְּנַחֲלָתֶךָ טִמְּאוּ אֶת־הֵיכַל קָדְשֶׁךָ שָׂמוּ אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלִַם לְעִיִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-ah-SAHF eh-loh-HEEM‍ BAH-oo goh-YEEM  be-nah-khah-lah-TEH-khah tee-me-OO eht hey-KHAHL kohd-SHEH-khah SAH-moo eht ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem le-ee-YEEM

A Psalm of Asaph. O God, the heathen are come into Thine inheritance; They have defiled Thy holy temple; They have made Jerusalem into heaps.

### 79:2

נָתְנוּ אֶת־נִבְלַת עֲבָדֶיךָ מַאֲכָל לְעוֹף הַשָּׁמָיִם בְּשַׂר חֲסִידֶיךָ לְחַיְתוֹ־אָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-te-NOO eht neev-LAHT ah-vah-DEY-khah mah-ah-KHAHL le-OHF hah-shah-MAH-yeem be-SAHR khah-see-DEY-khah le-khahy-toh AH-rehts

They have given the dead bodies of Thy servants to be food unto the fowls of the heaven, The flesh of Thy saints unto the beasts of the earth.

### 79:3

שָׁפְכוּ דָמָם כַּמַּיִם סְבִיבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִָם וְאֵין קוֹבֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-fe-KHOO dah-MAHM  kah-MAH-yeem se-vee-VOHT ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem ve-EYN koh-VEHR

They have shed their blood like water Round about Jerusalem, with none to bury them.

### 79:4

הָיִינוּ חֶרְפָּה לִשְׁכֵנֵינוּ לַעַג וָקֶלֶס לִסְבִיבוֹתֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-YEE-noo khehr-PAH leesh-kheh-NEY-noo LAH-ahg vah-KEH-lehs lees-vee-voh-TEY-noo

We are become a taunt to our neighbours, A scorn and derision to them that are round about us.

### 79:5

עַד־מָה יְהוָה תֶּאֱנַף לָנֶצַח תִּבְעַר כְּמוֹ־אֵשׁ קִנְאָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd MAH ah-doh-NAI teh-eh-NAHF lah-NEH-tsahkh teev-AHR ke-moh EHSH keen-ah-TEH-khah

How long, O LORD, wilt Thou be angry for ever? How long will Thy jealousy burn like fire?

### 79:6

שְׁפֹךְ חֲמָתְךָ אֶל־הַגּוֹיִם אֲשֶׁר לֹא־יְדָעוּךָ וְעַל מַמְלָכוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּשִׁמְךָ לֹא קָרָאוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-FOHKH khah-mah-te-KHAH ehl hah-goh-YEEM ah-SHEHR loh ye-dah-OO-khah ve-AHL mahm-lah-KHOHT ah-SHEHR be-sheem-KHAH LOH kah-RAH-oo

Pour out Thy wrath upon the nations that know Thee not, And upon the kingdoms that call not upon Thy name.

### 79:7

כִּי אָכַל אֶת־יַעֲקֹב וְאֶת־נָוֵהוּ הֵשַׁמּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ah-KHAHL eht yah-ah-KOHV ve-eht nah-VEH-hoo heh-SHAH-moo

For they have devoured Jacob, And laid waste his habitation.

### 79:8

אַל־תִּזְכָּר־לָנוּ עֲוֺנֹת רִאשֹׁנִים מַהֵר יְקַדְּמוּנוּ רַחֲמֶיךָ כִּי דַלּוֹנוּ מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl teez-kahr lah-NOO ah-voh-NOHT ree-shoh-NEEM mah-HEHR ye-kah-de-MOO-noo rah-khah-MEY-khah KEE dah-LOH-noo me-OHD

Remember not against us the iniquities of our forefathers; Let Thy compassions speedily come to meet us; For we are brought very low.

### 79:9

עָזְרֵנוּ אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׁעֵנוּ עַל־דְּבַר כְּבוֹד־שְׁמֶךָ וְהַצִּילֵנוּ וְכַפֵּר עַל־חַטֹּאתֵינוּ לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ze-REH-noo  eh-loh-HEY yeesh-EH-noo ahl de-VAHR ke-vohd she-MEH-khah ve-hah-tsee-LEH-noo ve-khah-PEHR ahl khah-toh-TEY-noo le-MAH-ahn she-MEH-khah

Help us, O God of our salvation, for the sake of the glory of Thy name; And deliver us, and forgive our sins, for Thy name's sake.

### 79:10

לָמָּה יֹאמְרוּ הַגּוֹיִם אַיֵּה אֱ‍לֹהֵיהֶם יִוָּדַע בגיים [בַּגּוֹיִם] לְעֵינֵינוּ נִקְמַת דַּם־עֲבָדֶיךָ הַשָּׁפוּךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** LAH-mah  yoh-me-ROO hah-goh-YEEM ah-YEH eh-loh-hey-HEHM‍ yee-vah-DAH bah-goh-YEEM le-ey-NEY-noo neek-MAHT dahm ah-vah-DEY-khah hah-shah-FOOKH

Wherefore should the nations say: 'Where is their God?' Let the avenging of Thy servants' blood that is shed Be made known among the nations in our sight. .

Reading note: written בגיים → read בַּגּוֹיִ֣ם. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 79:11

## Psalm 80 — Restore us; the image of a devastated vine

### 80:1

### 80:2

Thou hast fed them with the bread of tears, And given them tears to drink in large measure.

### 80:7

תְּשִׂימֵנוּ מָדוֹן לִשְׁכֵנֵינוּ וְאֹיְבֵינוּ יִלְעֲגוּ־לָמוֹ׃

### 80:8

אֱלֹהִים צְבָאוֹת הֲשִׁיבֵנוּ וְהָאֵר פָּנֶיךָ וְנִוָּשֵׁעָה׃

O God of hosts, restore us; And cause Thy face to shine, and we shall be saved.

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-SOO hah-REEM tsee-LAHH vah-ah-nah-FEY-hah ah-re-zey EHL

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-MAH pah-RAHTS-tah ge-deh-REY-hah ve-ah-ROO-hah kohl OH-ve-rey DAH-rehkh

### 80:14

יְכַרְסְמֶנָּה חֲזִיר מִיָּעַר וְזִיז שָׂדַי יִרְעֶנָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-khahr-se-MEH-nah khah-ZEER mee-YAH-ahr ve-ZEEZ sah-DAHY yeer-EH-nah

### 80:15

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM tse-vah-OHT SHOOV NAH hah-BEHT mee-shah-MAH-yeem oo-re-EH oo-fe-KOHD GEH-fehn ZOHT

O God of hosts, return, we beseech Thee; Look from heaven, and behold, and be mindful of this vine,

### 80:16

וְכַנָּה אֲשֶׁר־נָטְעָה יְמִינֶךָ וְעַל־בֵּן אִמַּצְתָּה לָּךְ׃

## Psalm 81 — Festival praise, listening, and provision

### 81:1

For the Leader; upon the Gittith. [A Psalm] of Asaph.

**Pronunciation draft:** hahr-nee-NOO leh-loh-HEEM oo-ZEH-noo hah-REE-oo leh-loh-HEY yah-ah-KOHV

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE KHOHK le-yees-rah-EHL HOO meesh-PAHT leh-loh-HEY yah-ah-KOHV

### 81:6

עֵדוּת בִּיהוֹסֵף שָׂמוֹ בְּצֵאתוֹ עַל־אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם שְׂפַת לֹא־יָדַעְתִּי אֶשְׁמָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-see-ROH-tee mee-SEH-vehl sheekh-MOH kah-PAHV mee-DOOD tah-ah-VOH-re-nah

בַּצָּרָה קָרָאתָ וָאֲחַלְּצֶךָּ אֶעֶנְךָ בְּסֵתֶר רַעַם אֶבְחָנְךָ עַל־מֵי מְרִיבָה סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-MAH ah-MEE ve-ah-EE-dah BAHKH yees-rah-EHL eem tee-she-mah LEE

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-noh-KHEE  ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-khah hah-mah-ahl-KHAH meh-EH-rehts meets-RAH-yeem hahr-khehv pee-KHAH vah-ah-mahl-EH-hoo

**Pronunciation draft:** LOO ah-MEE shoh-MEH-ah LEE yees-rah-EHL beed-rah-KHAHY ye-hah-LEH-khoo

### 81:15

כִּמְעַט אוֹיְבֵיהֶם אַכְנִיעַ וְעַל צָרֵיהֶם אָשִׁיב יָדִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** keem-AHT oh-ye-vey-HEHM ahkh-NEE-ah ve-AHL tsah-rey-HEHM ah-SHEEV yah-DEE

### 81:16

מְשַׂנְאֵי יְהוָה יְכַחֲשׁוּ־לוֹ וִיהִי עִתָּם לְעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** me-sahn-EY ah-doh-NAI ye-khah-khah-shoo LOH vee-HEE ee-TAHM le-oh-LAHM

### 81:17

## Psalm 82 — Judgment and defense of the powerless

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.82) · Buckets: justice, caregiving

### 82:1

מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף אֱ‍לֹהִים נִצָּב בַּעֲדַת־אֵל בְּקֶרֶב אֱלֹהִים יִשְׁפֹּט׃

A Psalm of Asaph. God standeth in the congregation of God; in the midst of the judges He judgeth:

### 82:2

עַד־מָתַי תִּשְׁפְּטוּ־עָוֶל וּפְנֵי רְשָׁעִים תִּשְׂאוּ־סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd mah-TAHY teesh-pe-too AH-vehl oo-fe-NEY re-shah-EEM tees-oo SEH-lah

**Pronunciation draft:** sheef-too DAHL ve-yah-TOHM ah-NEE vah-RAHSH hahts-DEE-koo

Judge the poor and fatherless; do justice to the afflicted and destitute.

### 82:4

פַּלְּטוּ־דַל וְאֶבְיוֹן מִיַּד רְשָׁעִים הַצִּילוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-le-too DAHL ve-ehv-YOHN mee-YAHD re-shah-EEM hah-TSEE-loo

Rescue the poor and needy; deliver them out of the hand of the wicked.

### 82:5

**Pronunciation draft:** LOH yah-de-OO  ve-LOH yah-VEE-noo bah-khah-sheh-KHAH yeet-hah-LAH-khoo yee-MOH-too kohl MOH-se-dey AH-rehts

They know not, neither do they understand; they go about in darkness; all the foundations of the earth are moved.

### 82:6

אֲ‍נִי־אָמַרְתִּי אֱלֹהִים אַתֶּם וּבְנֵי עֶלְיוֹן כֻּלְּכֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-nee ‍ah-mahr-TEE eh-loh-HEEM ah-TEHM oo-ve-NEY ehl-YOHN koo-le-KHEHM

I said: Ye are godlike beings, and all of you sons of the Most High.

### 82:7

אָכֵן כְּאָדָם תְּמוּתוּן וּכְאַחַד הַשָּׂרִים תִּפֹּלוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-KHEHN ke-ah-DAHM te-moo-TOON oo-khe-ah-KHAHD hah-sah-REEM tee-POH-loo

### 82:8

## Psalm 83 — An appeal amid collective danger

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.83) · Buckets: protection, community

### 83:1

שִׁיר מִזְמוֹר לְאָסָף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER meez-MOHR le-ah-SAHF

A Song, a Psalm of Asaph.

### 83:2

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM ahl doh-mee LAHKH ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH ve-ahl teesh-KOHT EHL

O God, keep not Thou silence; Hold not Thy peace, and be not still, O God.

### 83:3

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hee-NEH oh-ye-vey-KHAH yeh-heh-mah-YOON oo-me-sahn-EY-khah NAH-se-oo ROHSH

### 83:4

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl ah-me-KHAH yah-ah-REE-moo SOHD ve-yeet-yah-ah-TSOO ahl tse-foo-NEY-khah

They hold crafty converse against Thy people, And take counsel against Thy treasured ones.

### 83:5

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-me-ROO le-KHOO ve-nahkh-khee-DEHM mee-GOHY ve-loh yee-zah-KHEHR shehm yees-rah-EHL OHD

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE noh-ah-TSOO LEHV yahkh-DAHV ah-LEY-khah be-REET yeekh-ROH-too

For they have consulted together with one consent; Against Thee do they make a covenant;

### 83:7

אָהֳלֵי אֱדוֹם וְיִשְׁמְעֵאלִים מוֹאָב וְהַגְרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-hoh-LEY eh-DOHM ve-yeesh-me-eh-LEEM moh-AHV ve-hahg-REEM

The tents of Edom and the Ishmaelites; Moab, and the Hagrites;

### 83:8

גְּבָל וְעַמּוֹן וַעֲמָלֵק פְּלֶשֶׁת עִם־יֹשְׁבֵי צוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ge-VAHL ve-ah-MOHN vah-ah-mah-LEHK pe-LEH-sheht eem YOH-she-vey TSOHR

Gebal, and Ammon, and Amalek; Philistia with the inhabitants of Tyre;

### 83:9

גַּם־אַשּׁוּר נִלְוָה עִמָּם הָיוּ זְרוֹעַ לִבְנֵי־לוֹט סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gahm ah-SHOOR neel-VAH ee-MAHM HAH-YOO ze-ROH-ah leev-ney LOHT SEH-lah

### 83:10

עֲשֵׂה־לָהֶם כְּמִדְיָן כְּסִיסְרָא כְיָבִין בְּנַחַל קִישׁוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-seh lah-HEHM ke-meed-YAHN ke-see-se-RAH khe-yah-VEEN be-NAH-khahl kee-SHOHN

### 83:11

**Pronunciation draft:** neesh-me-DOO ve-eyn DOHR HAH-yoo doh-MEHN lah-ah-dah-MAH

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-TEH-moh ne-dee-veh-MOH ke-oh-REHV ve-kheez-EHV oo-khe-ZEH-vahkh oo-khe-tsahl-moo-NAH kohl ne-see-KHEH-moh

Make their nobles like Oreb and Zeeb, And like Zebah and Zalmunna all their princes;

### 83:13

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HAHY‍ shee-TEH-moh khah-gahl-GAHL ke-KAHSH leef-ney ROO-ahkh

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-EHSH teev-ahr YAH-ahr oo-khe-leh-hah-VAH te-lah-HEHT hah-REEM

**Pronunciation draft:** KEHN teer-de-FEHM be-sah-ah-REH-khah oo-ve-soo-fah-te-KHAH te-vah-hah-LEHM

### 83:17

**Pronunciation draft:** mah-LEH fe-ney-HEHM kah-LOHN vee-vahk-SHOO sheem-KHAH ah-doh-NAI

### 83:18

### 83:19

## Psalm 84 — Longing for God's dwelling

### 84:1

### 84:6

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY ah-DAHM ohz LOH VAHKH me-see-LOHT beel-vah-VAHM

## Psalm 85 — Restoration, mercy, truth, and peace

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.85) · Buckets: peace, repentance

### 85:1

### 85:3

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-oh-LAHM teh-eh-nahf BAH-noo teem-SHOHKH ah-pe-KHAH le-DOHR vah-DOHR

### 85:8

I will hear what God the LORD will speak; For He will speak peace unto His people, and to His saints; But let them not turn back to folly.

### 85:10

אַךְ קָרוֹב לִירֵאָיו יִשְׁעוֹ לִשְׁכֹּן כָּבוֹד בְּאַרְצֵנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH  kah-ROHV lee-reh-AHV yeesh-OH leesh-KOHN kah-VOHD be-ahr-TSEH-noo

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-MEHT meh-EH-rehts teets-MAHKH ve-TSEH-dehk mee-shah-MAH-yeem neesh-KAHF

**Pronunciation draft:** gahm ah-doh-NAI yee-TEHN hah-TOHV ve-ahr-TSEH-noo tee-TEHN ye-voo-LAHH

### 85:14

### 86:1

**Pronunciation draft:** be-YOHM tsah-rah-TEE ehk-rah-EH-kah KEE tah-ah-NEH-nee

### 87:1

### 87:5

Shall Thy wonders be known in the dark? And Thy righteousness in the land of forgetfulness?

### 88:14

וַאֲנִי אֵלֶיךָ יְהוָה שִׁוַּעְתִּי וּבַבֹּקֶר תְּפִלָּתִי תְקַדְּמֶךָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  eh-LEY-khah ah-doh-NAI shee-VAH-tee oo-vah-BOH-kehr te-fee-lah-TEE te-kah-de-MEH-kah

But as for me, unto Thee, O LORD, do I cry, And in the morning doth my prayer come to meet Thee.

### 88:15

לָמָה יְהוָה תִּזְנַח נַפְשִׁי תַּסְתִּיר פָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-MAH ah-doh-NAI teez-NAHKH nahf-SHEE tahs-TEER pah-NEY-khah mee-MEH-nee

LORD, why castest Thou off my soul? Why hidest Thou Thy face from me?

### 88:16

עָנִי אֲנִי וְגֹוֵעַ מִנֹּעַר נָשָׂאתִי אֵמֶיךָ אָפוּנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-NEE ah-NEE ve-goh-VEH-ah mee-NOH-ahr nah-SAH-tee eh-MEY-khah ah-FOO-nah

I am afflicted and at the point of death from my youth up; I have borne Thy terrors, I am distracted.

### 88:17

עָלַי עָבְרוּ חֲרוֹנֶיךָ בִּעוּתֶיךָ צִמְּתוּתֻנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-LAHY ah-ve-ROO khah-roh-NEY-khah bee-oo-TEY-khah tsee-me-too-TOO-nee

Thy fierce wrath is gone over me; Thy terrors have cut me off.

### 88:18

סַבּוּנִי כַמַּיִם כָּל־הַיּוֹם הִקִּיפוּ עָלַי יָחַד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-BOO-nee khah-mah-YEEM kohl hah-YOHM hee-KEE-foo ah-LAHY YAH-khahd

They came round about me like water all the day; They compassed me about together.

### 88:19

הִרְחַקְתָּ מִמֶּנִּי אֹהֵב וָרֵעַ מְיֻדָּעַי מַחְשָׁךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heer-KHAHK-tah mee-meh-NEE oh-HEHV vah-REH-ah me-yoo-dah-AHY mahkh-SHAHKH

Friend and companion hast Thou put far from me, And mine acquaintance into darkness.

## Psalm 89 — Covenant, praise, and disappointment

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.89) · Buckets: community, hope, health

### 89:1

מַשְׂכִּיל לְאֵיתָן הָאֶזְרָחִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahs-KEEL le-ey-TAHN hah-ehz-rah-KHEE

Maschil of Ethan the Ezrahite.

### 89:2

חַסְדֵי יְהוָה עוֹלָם אָשִׁירָה לְדֹר וָדֹר אוֹדִיעַ אֱמוּנָתְךָ בְּפִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-se-DEY ah-doh-NAI oh-LAHM ah-SHEE-rah le-DOHR vah-DOHR  oh-DEE-ah eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH be-FEE

I will sing of the mercies of the LORD for ever; To all generations will I make known Thy faithfulness with my mouth.

### 89:3

כִּי־אָמַרְתִּי עוֹלָם חֶסֶד יִבָּנֶה שָׁמַיִם תָּכִן אֱמוּנָתְךָ בָהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-MAHR-tee oh-LAHM KHEH-sehd yee-bah-NEH shah-MAH-yeem  tah-KHEEN eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH vah-HEHM

For I have said: 'For ever is mercy built; In the very heavens Thou dost establish Thy faithfulness.

### 89:4

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-RAH-tee ve-REET leev-khee-REE neesh-BAH-tee le-dah-VEED ahv-DEE

I have made a covenant with My chosen, I have sworn unto David My servant:

### 89:5

עַד־עוֹלָם אָכִין זַרְעֶךָ וּבָנִיתִי לְדֹר־וָדוֹר כִּסְאֲךָ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd oh-LAHM ah-KHEEN zahr-EH-khah oo-vah-NEE-tee le-dohr vah-DOHR kees-ah-KHAH SEH-lah

### 89:6

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yoh-DOO shah-MAH-yeem peel-ah-KHAH ah-doh-NAI ahf eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH beek-HAHL ke-doh-SHEEM

### 89:7

כִּי מִי בַשַּׁחַק יַעֲרֹךְ לַיהוָה יִדְמֶה לַיהוָה בִּבְנֵי אֵלִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE MEE vah-shah-KHAHK yah-ah-ROHKH lah-ah-doh-NAI yeed-MEH lah-ah-doh-NAI beev-NEY eh-leem

### 89:8

**Pronunciation draft:** EHL nah-ah-RAHTS be-sohd ke-doh-SHEEM rah-BAH ve-noh-RAH ahl kohl se-vee-VAHV

A God dreaded in the great council of the holy ones, And feared of all them that are about Him?

### 89:9

יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵי צְבָאוֹת מִי־כָמוֹךָ חֲסִין יָהּ וֶאֱמוּנָתְךָ סְבִיבוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-ree-moh-TAH ye-MEEN tsah-RAHV hees-MAHKH-tah kohl oh-ye-VAHV

**Pronunciation draft:** heek-tsahr-TAH ye-MEY ah-loo-MAHV heh-eh-TEE-tah ah-LAHV boo-SHAH SEH-lah

### 89:48

**Pronunciation draft:** ze-khahr ah-NEE meh KHAH-lehd ahl mahh SHAHV bah-RAH-tah khohl be-ney ah-DAHM

### 89:49

## Psalm 90 — Mortality, wisdom, and the work of our hands

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.90) · Buckets: opportunity, wisdom, aging, health

### 90:1

### 90:2

### 90:3

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-SHEHV eh-NOHSH ahd dah-KAH vah-TOH-mehr SHOO-voo ve-ney ah-DAHM

### 90:4

כִּי אֶלֶף שָׁנִים בְּעֵינֶיךָ כְּיוֹם אֶתְמוֹל כִּי יַעֲבֹר וְאַשְׁמוּרָה בַלָּיְלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE EH-lehf shah-NEEM be-ey-NEY-khah ke-YOHM eht-MOHL KEE yah-ah-VOHR ve-ahsh-moo-RAH vah-LAH-ye-lah

For a thousand years in Thy sight Are but as yesterday when it is past, And as a watch in the night.

### 90:5

**Pronunciation draft:** ze-rahm-TAHM sheh-NAH yeeh-YOO bah-BOH-kehr keh-khah-TSEER yah-khah-LOHF

### 90:6

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-boh-KEHR yah-TSEETS ve-khah-LAHF lah-EH-rehv ye-moh-LEHL ve-yah-VEHSH

### 90:7

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khah-LEE-noo ve-ah-PEH-khah oo-vah-khah-mah-te-KHAH neev-HAH-le-noo

For we are consumed in Thine anger, And by Thy wrath are we hurried away.

### 90:8

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-TAH ah-voh-noh-TEY-noo le-nehg-DEH-khah ah-loo-MEH-noo leem-OHR pah-NEY-khah

### 90:9

### 90:11

**Pronunciation draft:** shoo-VAH ah-doh-NAI ahd mah-TAHY ve-hee-nah-KHEHM ahl ah-vah-DEY-khah

### 90:14

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-be-EH-noo vah-BOH-kehr khahs-DEH-khah oo-ne-rah-ne-NAH ve-nees-me-KHAH be-khohl yah-MEY-noo

### 90:15

## Psalm 91 — Shelter and protection

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.91) · Buckets: protection, wellness, health

### 91:1

O thou that dwellest in the covert of the Most High, And abidest in the shadow of the Almighty;

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-MAHR lah-ah-doh-NAI mahkh-SEE oo-me-tsoo-dah-TEE eh-loh-HAHY ehv-tahkh BOH

### 91:3

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE HOO yah-tsee-le-KHAH mee-PAHKH yah-KOOSH mee-DEH-vehr hah-VOHT

### 91:4

He will cover thee with His pinions, And under His wings shalt thou take refuge; His truth is a shield and a buckler.

### 91:5

לֹא־תִירָא מִפַּחַד לָיְלָה מֵחֵץ יָעוּף יוֹמָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh tee-RAH mee-PAH-khahd LAH-ye-lah meh-KHEHTS yah-OOF yoh-MAHM

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-deh-VEHR bah-OH-fehl yah-hah-LOHKH mee-KEH-tehv yah-SHOOD tsoh-hoh-RAH-yeem

### 91:7

### 91:8

### 91:9

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI mahkh-SEE ehl-YOHN SAHM-tah me-oh-NEH-khah

### 91:16

אֹרֶךְ יָמִים אַשְׂבִּיעֵהוּ וְאַרְאֵהוּ בִּישׁוּעָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** OH-rehkh yah-MEEM ahs-bee-EH-hoo ve-ahr-EH-hoo bee-shoo-ah-TEE

With long life will I satisfy him, And make Him to behold My salvation.'

## Psalm 92 — A Sabbath song of thanksgiving

### 92:1

A Psalm, a Song. For the sabbath day.

**Pronunciation draft:** TOHV le-hoh-DOHT lah-ah-doh-NAI oo-le-zah-MEHR le-sheem-KHAH ehl-YOHN

**Pronunciation draft:** le-hah-GEED bah-BOH-kehr khahs-DEH-khah veh-eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH bah-ley-LOHT

### 92:5

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH mah-ROHM le-oh-LAHM ah-doh-NAI

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE hee-NEH oh-ye-VEY-khah  ah-doh-NAI kee hee-NEH oh-ye-VEY-khah yoh-VEH-doo yeet-pah-re-DOO kohl POH-ah-ley AH-vehn

### 93:1

### 95:1
