כִּי אֵל גָּדוֹל יְהוָה וּמֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל עַל־כָּל־אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE EHL gah-DOHL ah-doh-NAI oo-MEH-lehkh gah-DOHL ahl kohl eh-loh-HEEM

For the LORD is a great God, And a great King above all gods;

### 95:4

אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדוֹ מֶחְקְרֵי־אָרֶץ וְתוֹעֲפוֹת הָרִים לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR be-yah-DOH mehkh-ke-rey AH-rehts ve-toh-ah-FOHT hah-REEM LOH

In whose hand are the depths of the earth; The heights of the mountains are His also.

### 95:5

אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ הַיָּם וְהוּא עָשָׂהוּ וְיַבֶּשֶׁת יָדָיו יָצָרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shehr LOH hah-YAHM ve-HOO ah-SAH-hoo ve-yah-BEH-sheht yah-DAHV yah-TSAH-roo

The sea is His, and He made it; And His hands formed the dry land.

### 95:6

בֹּאוּ נִשְׁתַּחֲוֶה וְנִכְרָעָה נִבְרְכָה לִפְנֵי־יְהוָה עֹשֵׂנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** boh-OO neesh-tah-khah-VEH ve-neekh-RAH-ah neev-re-KHAH lee-fe-ney ah-doh-NAI oh-SEH-noo

O come, let us bow down and bend the knee; Let us kneel before the LORD our Maker;

### 95:7

כִּי הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וַאֲנַחְנוּ עַם מַרְעִיתוֹ וְצֹאן יָדוֹ הַיּוֹם אִם־בְּקֹלוֹ תִשְׁמָעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE HOO eh-loh-HEY-noo vah-ah-NAHKH-noo AHM mahr-ee-TOH ve-TSOHN yah-DOH hah-YOHM eem be-koh-LOH teesh-MAH-oo

For He is our God, And we are the people of His pasture, and the flock of His hand. To-day, if ye would but hearken to His voice!

### 95:8

אַל־תַּקְשׁוּ לְבַבְכֶם כִּמְרִיבָה כְּיוֹם מַסָּה בַּמִּדְבָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tahk-SHOO le-vahv-KHEHM keem-ree-VAH ke-YOHM mah-SAH bah-meed-BAHR

'Harden not your heart, as at Meribah, As in the day of Massah in the wilderness;

### 95:9

אֲשֶׁר נִסּוּנִי אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם בְּחָנוּנִי גַּם־רָאוּ פָעֳלִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR nee-soo-NEE ah-voh-tey-KHEHM be-khah-NOO-nee gahm rah-OO foh-oh-LEE

When your fathers tried Me, Proved Me, even though they saw My work.

### 95:10

אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה אָקוּט בְּדוֹר וָאֹמַר עַם תֹּעֵי לֵבָב הֵם וְהֵם לֹא־יָדְעוּ דְרָכָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahr-bah-EEM shah-NAH  ah-KOOT be-DOHR vah-oh-MAHR AHM toh-EY leh-VAHV HEHM ve-HEHM loh yah-de-OO de-rah-KHAHY

For forty years was I wearied with that generation, And said: It is a people that do err in their heart, And they have not known My ways;

### 95:11

אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּעְתִּי בְאַפִּי אִם־יְבֹאוּן אֶל־מְנוּחָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shehr neesh-BAH-tee ve-ah-PEE eem ye-voh-OON ehl me-noo-khah-TEE

Wherefore I swore in My wrath, That they should not enter into My rest.'

## Psalm 96 — A new song and creation's rejoicing

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.96) · Buckets: nature, gratitude

### 96:1

שִׁירוּ לַיהוָה שִׁיר חָדָשׁ שִׁירוּ לַיהוָה כָּל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEE-roo lah-ah-doh-NAI SHEER khah-DAHSH SHEE-roo lah-ah-doh-NAI kohl hah-AH-rehts

O sing unto the LORD a new song; Sing unto the LORD, all the earth.

### 96:2

שִׁירוּ לַיהוָה בָּרֲכוּ שְׁמוֹ בַּשְּׂרוּ מִיּוֹם־לְיוֹם יְשׁוּעָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEE-roo lah-ah-doh-NAI bah-rah-KHOO she-MOH bah-se-ROO mee-yohm le-YOHM ye-shoo-ah-TOH

Sing unto the LORD, bless His name; Proclaim His salvation from day to day.

### 96:3

סַפְּרוּ בַגּוֹיִם כְּבוֹדוֹ בְּכָל־הָעַמִּים נִפְלְאוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-pe-ROO vah-goh-YEEM ke-voh-DOH be-khohl hah-ah-MEEM neef-le-oh-TAHV

Declare His glory among the nations, His marvellous works among all the peoples.

### 96:4

כִּי גָדוֹל יְהוָה וּמְהֻלָּל מְאֹד נוֹרָא הוּא עַל־כָּל־אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE gah-DOHL ah-doh-NAI oo-me-hoo-LAHL me-OHD noh-RAH HOO ahl kohl eh-loh-HEEM

For great is the LORD, and highly to be praised; He is to be feared above all gods. .

### 96:5

כִּי כָּל־אֱלֹהֵי הָעַמִּים אֱלִילִים וַיהוָה שָׁמַיִם עָשָׂה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE  kohl eh-loh-HEY hah-ah-MEEM eh-lee-LEEM vah-ah-doh-NAI shah-MAH-yeem ah-SAH

For all the gods of the peoples are things of nought; But the LORD made the heavens.

### 96:6

הוֹד־וְהָדָר לְפָנָיו עֹז וְתִפְאֶרֶת בְּמִקְדָּשׁוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hohd ve-hah-DAHR le-fah-NAHV OHZ ve-teef-EH-reht be-meek-dah-SHOH

Honour and majesty are before Him; Strength and beauty are in His sanctuary.

### 96:7

הָבוּ לַיהוָה מִשְׁפְּחוֹת עַמִּים הָבוּ לַיהוָה כָּבוֹד וָעֹז׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-VOO lah-ah-doh-NAI meesh-pe-KHOHT ah-MEEM hah-VOO lah-ah-doh-NAI kah-VOHD vah-OHZ

Ascribe unto the LORD, ye kindreds of the peoples, Ascribe unto the LORD glory and strength.

### 96:8

הָבוּ לַיהוָה כְּבוֹד שְׁמוֹ שְׂאוּ־מִנְחָה וּבֹאוּ לְחַצְרוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-VOO lah-ah-doh-NAI ke-VOHD she-MOH se-oo meen-KHAH oo-VOH-oo le-khahts-roh-TAHV

Ascribe unto the LORD the glory due unto His name; Bring an offering, and come into His courts.

### 96:9

הִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לַיהוָה בְּהַדְרַת־קֹדֶשׁ חִילוּ מִפָּנָיו כָּל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heesh-tah-khah-VOO lah-ah-doh-NAI be-hahd-raht KOH-dehsh KHEE-loo mee-pah-NAHV kohl hah-AH-rehts

O worship the LORD in the beauty of holiness; Tremble before Him, all the earth.

### 96:10

אִמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם יְהוָה מָלָךְ אַף־תִּכּוֹן תֵּבֵל בַּל־תִּמּוֹט יָדִין עַמִּים בְּמֵישָׁרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eem-ROO vah-goh-YEEM  ah-doh-NAI mah-LAHKH ahf tee-KOHN teh-VEHL bahl tee-MOHT yah-DEEN ah-MEEM be-mey-shah-REEM

Say among the nations: 'The LORD reigneth.' The world also is established that it cannot be moved; He will judge the peoples with equity.

### 96:11

יִשְׂמְחוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וְתָגֵל הָאָרֶץ יִרְעַם הַיָּם וּמְלֹאוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yees-me-KHOO hah-shah-mah-YEEM ve-tah-GEHL hah-AH-rehts yee-re-AHM hah-YAHM oo-me-loh-OH

Let the heavens be glad, and let the earth rejoice; Let the sea roar, and the fulness thereof;

### 96:12

יַעֲלֹז שָׂדַי וְכָל־אֲשֶׁר־בּוֹ אָז יְרַנְּנוּ כָּל־עֲצֵי־יָעַר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-ah-LOHZ sah-DAHY ve-khohl ah-shehr BOH AHZ ye-rah-ne-NOO kohl ah-tsey YAH-ahr

Let the field exult; and all that is therein; Then shall all the trees of the wood sing for joy;

### 96:13

לִפְנֵי יְהוָה כִּי בָא כִּי בָא לִשְׁפֹּט הָאָרֶץ יִשְׁפֹּט־תֵּבֵל בְּצֶדֶק וְעַמִּים בֶּאֱמוּנָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leef-NEY ah-doh-NAI  KEE VAH KEE VAH leesh-POHT hah-AH-rehts yeesh-poht teh-VEHL be-TSEH-dehk ve-ah-MEEM beh-eh-moo-nah-TOH

Before the LORD, for He is come; For He is come to judge the earth; He will judge the world with righteousness, And the peoples in His faithfulness.

## Psalm 97 — Divine rule, justice, and joy

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.97) · Buckets: justice, gratitude

### 97:1

יְהוָה מָלָךְ תָּגֵל הָאָרֶץ יִשְׂמְחוּ אִיִּים רַבִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI mah-LAHKH tah-GEHL hah-AH-rehts yees-me-KHOO ee-YEEM rah-BEEM

The LORD reigneth; let the earth rejoice; Let the multitude of isles be glad.

### 97:2

עָנָן וַעֲרָפֶל סְבִיבָיו צֶדֶק וּמִשְׁפָּט מְכוֹן כִּסְאוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-NAHN vah-ah-rah-FEHL se-vee-VAHV TSEH-dehk oo-meesh-PAHT me-KHOHN kees-OH

Clouds and darkness are round about Him; Righteousness and justice are the foundation of His throne.

### 97:3

אֵשׁ לְפָנָיו תֵּלֵךְ וּתְלַהֵט סָבִיב צָרָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EHSH le-fah-NAHV teh-LEHKH oo-te-lah-HEHT sah-VEEV tsah-RAHV

A fire goeth before Him, And burneth up His adversaries round about.

### 97:4

הֵאִירוּ בְרָקָיו תֵּבֵל רָאֲתָה וַתָּחֵל הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heh-EE-roo ve-rah-KAHV teh-VEHL rah-ah-TAH vah-tah-KHEHL hah-AH-rehts

His lightnings lighted up the world; The earth saw, and trembled.

### 97:5

הָרִים כַּדּוֹנַג נָמַסּוּ מִלִּפְנֵי יְהוָה מִלִּפְנֵי אֲדוֹן כָּל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-REEM kah-doh-NAHG nah-mah-SOO mee-leef-NEY ah-doh-NAI mee-leef-NEY ah-DOHN kohl hah-AH-rehts

The mountains melted like wax at the presence of the LORD, At the presence of the Lord of the whole earth.

### 97:6

הִגִּידוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם צִדְקוֹ וְרָאוּ כָל־הָעַמִּים כְּבוֹדוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-GEE-doo hah-shah-MAH-yeem tseed-KOH ve-rah-OO khohl hah-ah-MEEM ke-voh-DOH

The heavens declared His righteousness, And all the peoples saw His glory.

### 97:7

יֵבֹשׁוּ כָּל־עֹבְדֵי פֶסֶל הַמִּתְהַלְלִים בָּאֱלִילִים הִשְׁתַּחֲווּ־לוֹ כָּל־אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-VOH-shoo  kohl OH-ve-dey FEH-sehl hah-mee-te-hah-le-LEEM bah-eh-lee-LEEM heesh-tah-khah-voo LOH kohl eh-loh-HEEM

Ashamed be all they that serve graven images, That boast themselves of things of nought; Bow down to Him, all ye gods.

### 97:8

שָׁמְעָה וַתִּשְׂמַח צִיּוֹן וַתָּגֵלְנָה בְּנוֹת יְהוּדָה לְמַעַן מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-me-AH vah-tees-MAHKH  tsee-YOHN vah-tah-geh-le-NAH be-NOHT ye-hoo-DAH le-MAH-ahn meesh-pah-TEY-khah ah-doh-NAI

Zion heard and was glad, And the daughters of Judah rejoiced; Because of Thy judgments, O LORD.

### 97:9

כִּי־אַתָּה יְהוָה עֶלְיוֹן עַל־כָּל־הָאָרֶץ מְאֹד נַעֲלֵיתָ עַל־כָּל־אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI ehl-YOHN ahl kohl hah-AH-rehts me-OHD nah-ah-LEY-tah ahl kohl eh-loh-HEEM

For Thou, LORD, art most high above all the earth; Thou art exalted far above all gods.

### 97:10

אֹהֲבֵי יְהוָה שִׂנְאוּ רָע שֹׁמֵר נַפְשׁוֹת חֲסִידָיו מִיַּד רְשָׁעִים יַצִּילֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-hah-VEY ah-doh-NAI seen-OO RAH shoh-MEHR nahf-SHOHT khah-see-DAHV mee-YAHD re-shah-EEM yah-tsee-LEHM

O ye that love the LORD, hate evil; He preserveth the souls of His saints; He delivered them out of the hand of the wicked.

### 97:11

אוֹר זָרֻעַ לַצַּדִּיק וּלְיִשְׁרֵי־לֵב שִׂמְחָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** OHR zah-ROO-ah lah-tsah-DEEK oo-le-yeesh-rey LEHV seem-KHAH

Light is sown for the righteous, And gladness for the upright in heart.

### 97:12

שִׂמְחוּ צַדִּיקִים בַּיהוָה וְהוֹדוּ לְזֵכֶר קָדְשׁוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** seem-KHOO tsah-dee-KEEM bah-ah-doh-NAI ve-hoh-DOO le-ZEH-khehr kohd-SHOH

Be glad in the LORD, ye righteous; And give thanks to His holy name.

## Psalm 98 — A new song of deliverance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.98) · Buckets: gratitude, community

### 98:1

מִזְמוֹר שִׁירוּ לַיהוָה שִׁיר חָדָשׁ כִּי־נִפְלָאוֹת עָשָׂה הוֹשִׁיעָה־לּוֹ יְמִינוֹ וּזְרוֹעַ קָדְשׁוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR SHEE-roo lah-ah-doh-NAI  SHEER khah-DAHSH kee neef-lah-OHT ah-SAH hoh-shee-ah LOH ye-mee-NOH oo-ze-ROH-ah kohd-SHOH

A Psalm. O sing unto the LORD a new song; For He hath done marvellous things; His right hand, and His holy arm, hath wrought salvation for Him.

### 98:2

הוֹדִיעַ יְהוָה יְשׁוּעָתוֹ לְעֵינֵי הַגּוֹיִם גִּלָּה צִדְקָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DEE-ah ah-doh-NAI ye-shoo-ah-TOH le-ey-NEY hah-goh-YEEM gee-LAH tseed-kah-TOH

The LORD hath made known His salvation; His righteousness hath He revealed in the sight of the nations.

### 98:3

זָכַר חַסְדּוֹ וֶאֱמוּנָתוֹ לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל רָאוּ כָל־אַפְסֵי־אָרֶץ אֵת יְשׁוּעַת אֱלֹהֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-KHAHR khahs-DOH  veh-eh-moo-nah-TOH le-VEYT yees-rah-EHL rah-OO khohl ahf-sey AH-rehts EHT ye-shoo-AHT eh-loh-HEY-noo

He hath remembered His mercy and His faithfulness toward the house of Israel; All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.

### 98:4

הָרִיעוּ לַיהוָה כָּל־הָאָרֶץ פִּצְחוּ וְרַנְּנוּ וְזַמֵּרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-REE-oo lah-ah-doh-NAI kohl hah-AH-rehts peets-KHOO ve-rah-ne-NOO ve-zah-MEH-roo

Shout unto the LORD, all the earth; Break forth and sing for joy, yea, sing praises.

### 98:5

זַמְּרוּ לַיהוָה בְּכִנּוֹר בְּכִנּוֹר וְקוֹל זִמְרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-me-ROO lah-ah-doh-NAI be-khee-NOHR be-khee-NOHR ve-KOHL zeem-RAH

Sing praises unto the LORD with the harp; With the harp and the voice of melody.

### 98:6

בַּחֲצֹצְרוֹת וְקוֹל שׁוֹפָר הָרִיעוּ לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-khah-TSOH-tse-ROHT ve-KOHL shoh-FAHR hah-REE-oo leef-NEY  hah-MEH-lehkh ah-doh-NAI

With trumpets and sound of the horn Shout ye before the King, the LORD.

### 98:7

יִרְעַם הַיָּם וּמְלֹאוֹ תֵּבֵל וְיֹשְׁבֵי בָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeer-AHM hah-YAHM oo-me-loh-OH teh-VEHL ve-YOH-she-vey VAHH

Let the sea roar, and the fulness thereof; The world, and they that dwell therein;

### 98:8

נְהָרוֹת יִמְחֲאוּ־כָף יַחַד הָרִים יְרַנֵּנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ne-hah-ROHT yeem-khah-oo KHAHF yah-KHAHD hah-REEM ye-rah-NEH-noo

Let the floods clap their hands; Let the mountains sing for joy together;

### 98:9

לִפְנֵי־יְהוָה כִּי בָא לִשְׁפֹּט הָאָרֶץ יִשְׁפֹּט־תֵּבֵל בְּצֶדֶק וְעַמִּים בְּמֵישָׁרִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lee-fe-ney ah-doh-NAI KEE VAH leesh-POHT hah-AH-rehts yeesh-poht teh-VEHL be-TSEH-dehk ve-ah-MEEM be-mey-shah-REEM

Before the LORD, for He is come to judge the earth; He will judge the world with righteousness, And the peoples with equity.

## Psalm 99 — Holiness and just kingship

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.99) · Buckets: justice, devotion

### 99:1

יְהוָה מָלָךְ יִרְגְּזוּ עַמִּים יֹשֵׁב כְּרוּבִים תָּנוּט הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI mah-LAHKH yeer-ge-ZOO ah-MEEM yoh-SHEHV ke-roo-VEEM tah-NOOT hah-AH-rehts

The LORD reigneth; let the peoples tremble; He is enthroned upon the cherubim; let the earth quake.

### 99:2

יְהוָה בְּצִיּוֹן גָּדוֹל וְרָם הוּא עַל־כָּל־הָעַמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI be-tsee-YOHN gah-DOHL ve-RAHM HOO ahl kohl hah-ah-MEEM

The LORD is great in Zion; And He is high above all the peoples.

### 99:3

יוֹדוּ שִׁמְךָ גָּדוֹל וְנוֹרָא קָדוֹשׁ הוּא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO sheem-KHAH gah-DOHL ve-noh-RAH kah-DOHSH HOO

Let them praise Thy name as great and awful; Holy is He.

### 99:4

וְעֹז מֶלֶךְ מִשְׁפָּט אָהֵב אַתָּה כּוֹנַנְתָּ מֵישָׁרִים מִשְׁפָּט וּצְדָקָה בְּיַעֲקֹב אַתָּה עָשִׂיתָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-OHZ meh-LEHKH meesh-PAHT ah-HEHV ah-TAH koh-NAHN-tah mey-shah-REEM meesh-PAHT oo-tse-dah-KAH be-yah-ah-KOHV  ah-TAH ah-SEE-tah

The strength also of the king who loveth justice— Thou hast established equity, Thou hast executed justice and righteousness in Jacob.

### 99:5

רוֹמְמוּ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לַהֲדֹם רַגְלָיו קָדוֹשׁ הוּא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** roh-me-MOO ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo ve-heesh-tah-khah-VOO lah-hah-DOHM rahg-LAHV kah-DOHSH HOO

Exalt ye the LORD our God, And prostrate yourselves at His footstool; Holy is He.

### 99:6

מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן בְּכֹהֲנָיו וּשְׁמוּאֵל בְּקֹרְאֵי שְׁמוֹ קֹרִאים אֶל־יְהוָה וְהוּא יַעֲנֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** moh-SHEH ve-ah-hah-ROHN  be-khoh-hah-NAHV oo-she-moo-EHL be-koh-re-EY she-MOH koh-REEYM ehl ah-doh-NAI ve-HOO yah-ah-NEHM

Moses and Aaron among His priests, And Samuel among them that call upon His name, Did call upon the LORD, and He answered them.

### 99:7

בְּעַמּוּד עָנָן יְדַבֵּר אֲלֵיהֶם שָׁמְרוּ עֵדֹתָיו וְחֹק נָתַן־לָמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-ah-MOOD ah-NAHN ye-dah-BEHR ah-ley-HEHM shah-me-ROO eh-doh-TAHV ve-KHOHK nah-tahn LAH-moh

He spoke unto them in the pillar of cloud; They kept His testimonies, and the statute that He gave them.

### 99:8

יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ אַתָּה עֲנִיתָם אֵל נֹשֵׂא הָיִיתָ לָהֶם וְנֹקֵם עַל־עֲלִילוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-hey-NOO ah-TAH ah-nee-TAHM EHL noh-SEH hah-YEE-tah lah-HEHM ve-noh-KEHM ahl ah-lee-loh-TAHM

O LORD our God, Thou didst answer them; A forgiving God wast Thou unto them, Though Thou tookest vengeance of their misdeeds.

### 99:9

רוֹמְמוּ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לְהַר קָדְשׁוֹ כִּי־קָדוֹשׁ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** roh-me-MOO ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo ve-hee-she-tah-khah-VOO le-HAHR kohd-SHOH kee kah-DOHSH ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo

Exalt ye the LORD our God, And worship at His holy hill; For the LORD our God is holy.

## Psalm 100 — A song of thanksgiving

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.100) · Buckets: gratitude

### 100:1

מִזְמוֹר לְתוֹדָה הָרִיעוּ לַיהוָה כָּל־הָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-toh-DAH hah-REE-oo lah-ah-doh-NAI kohl hah-AH-rehts

A Psalm of thanksgiving. Shout unto the LORD, all the earth.

### 100:2

עִבְדוּ אֶת־יְהוָה בְּשִׂמְחָה בֹּאוּ לְפָנָיו בִּרְנָנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eev-DOO eht ah-doh-NAI be-seem-KHAH BOH-oo le-fah-NAHV beer-nah-NAH

Serve the LORD with gladness; come before His presence with singing.

### 100:3

דְּעוּ כִּי־יְהוָה הוּא אֱלֹהִים הוּא־עָשָׂנוּ ולא [וְלוֹ] אֲנַחְנוּ עַמּוֹ וְצֹאן מַרְעִיתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** de-OO kee ah-doh-NAI HOO eh-loh-HEEM hoo ah-sah-NOO ve-LOH ah-NAHKH-noo ah-MOH ve-TSOHN mahr-ee-TOH

Know ye that the LORD He is God; it is He that hath made us, and we are His, His people, and the flock of His pasture.

Reading note: written ולא → read וְל֣וֹ. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 100:4

בֹּאוּ שְׁעָרָיו בְּתוֹדָה חֲצֵרֹתָיו בִּתְהִלָּה הוֹדוּ־לוֹ בָּרֲכוּ שְׁמוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** BOH-oo she-ah-RAHV  be-toh-DAH khah-tseh-roh-TAHV beet-hee-LAH hoh-doo LOH bah-rah-KHOO she-MOH

Enter into His gates with thanksgiving, and into His courts with praise; give thanks unto Him, and bless His name.

### 100:5

כִּי־טוֹב יְהֹוָה לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ וְעַד־דֹּר וָדֹר אֱמוּנָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee TOHV ah-doh-NAI le-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH ve-ahd DOHR vah-DOHR eh-moo-nah-TOH

For the LORD is good; His mercy endureth for ever; and His faithfulness unto all generations.

## Psalm 101 — Integrity in leadership and one's household

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.101) · Buckets: wisdom, relationships

### 101:1

לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר חֶסֶד־וּמִשְׁפָּט אָשִׁירָה לְךָ יְהוָה אֲזַמֵּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED meez-MOHR kheh-sehd oo-meesh-PAHT ah-SHEE-rah le-KHAH ah-doh-NAI ah-zah-MEH-rah

A Psalm of David. I will sing of mercy and justice; unto Thee, O LORD, will I sing praises.

### 101:2

אַשְׂכִּילָה בְּדֶרֶךְ תָּמִים מָתַי תָּבוֹא אֵלָי אֶתְהַלֵּךְ בְּתָם־לְבָבִי בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahs-KEE-lah  be-DEH-rehkh tah-MEEM mah-TAHY tah-VOH eh-LAHY eht-hah-LEHKH be-tohm le-vah-VEE be-KEH-rehv bey-TEE

I will give heed unto the way of integrity; oh when wilt Thou come unto me? I will walk within my house in the integrity of my heart.

### 101:3

לֹא־אָשִׁית לְנֶגֶד עֵינַי דְּבַר־בְּלִיָּעַל עֲשֹׂה־סֵטִים שָׂנֵאתִי לֹא יִדְבַּק בִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh ah-SHEET  le-NEH-gehd ey-NAHY de-vahr be-lee-YAH-ahl ah-sohh seh-TEEM sah-NEH-tee LOH yeed-BAHK BEE

I will set no base thing before mine eyes; I hate the doing of things crooked; it shall not cleave unto me.

### 101:4

לֵבָב עִקֵּשׁ יָסוּר מִמֶּנִּי רָע לֹא אֵדָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leh-VAHV ee-KEHSH yah-SOOR mee-MEH-nee RAH LOH eh-DAH

A perverse heart shall depart from me; I will know no evil thing.

### 101:5

מלושני [מְלָשְׁנִי] בַסֵּתֶר רֵעֵהוּ אוֹתוֹ אַצְמִית גְּבַהּ־עֵינַיִם וּרְחַב לֵבָב אֹתוֹ לֹא אוּכָל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** me-lah-she-NEE vah-SEH-tehr  reh-eh-HOO oh-TOH ahts-MEET ge-vahh ey-nah-YEEM oo-re-KHAHV leh-VAHV oh-TOH LOH oo-KHAHL

Whoso slandereth his neighbour in secret, him will I destroy; Whoso is haughty of eye and proud of heart, him will I not suffer.

Reading note: written מלושני → read מְלָשְׁנִ֬י. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 101:6

עֵינַי בְּנֶאֶמְנֵי־אֶרֶץ לָשֶׁבֶת עִמָּדִי הֹלֵךְ בְּדֶרֶךְ תָּמִים הוּא יְשָׁרְתֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ey-NAHY  be-neh-ehm-ney eh-REHTS lah-SHEH-veht ee-mah-DEE hoh-LEHKH be-DEH-rehkh tah-MEEM HOO ye-shah-re-TEH-nee

Mine eyes are upon the faithful of the land, that they may dwell with me; he that walketh in a way of integrity, he shall minister unto me.

### 101:7

לֹא־יֵשֵׁב בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִי עֹשֵׂה רְמִיָּה דֹּבֵר שְׁקָרִים לֹא־יִכּוֹן לְנֶגֶד עֵינָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh yeh-SHEHV  be-KEH-rehv bey-TEE oh-SEH re-mee-YAH doh-VEHR she-kah-REEM loh yee-KOHN le-NEH-gehd ey-NAHY

He that worketh deceit shall not dwell within my house; he that speaketh falsehood shall not be established before mine eyes.

### 101:8

לַבְּקָרִים אַצְמִית כָּל־רִשְׁעֵי־אָרֶץ לְהַכְרִית מֵעִיר־יְהוָה כָּל־פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-be-kah-REEM ahts-MEET kohl reesh-ey AH-rehts le-hahkh-REET meh-eer ah-doh-NAI kohl POH-ah-ley AH-vehn

Morning by morning will I destroy all the wicked of the land; to cut off all the workers of iniquity from the city of the LORD.

## Psalm 102 — The prayer of one overwhelmed by affliction

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.102) · Buckets: health, wellness, grief

### 102:1

תְּפִלָּה לְעָנִי כִי־יַעֲטֹף וְלִפְנֵי יְהוָה יִשְׁפֹּךְ שִׂיחוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-fee-LAH le-ah-NEE khee yah-ah-TOHF ve-leef-NEY ah-doh-NAI yeesh-POHKH see-KHOH

A Prayer of the afflicted, when he fainteth, and poureth out his complaint before the LORD.

### 102:2

יְהוָה שִׁמְעָה תְפִלָּתִי וְשַׁוְעָתִי אֵלֶיךָ תָבוֹא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI sheem-AH te-fee-lah-TEE ve-shahv-ah-TEE eh-LEY-khah tah-VOH

O LORD, hear my prayer, and let my cry come unto Thee.

### 102:3

אַל־תַּסְתֵּר פָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי בְּיוֹם צַר לִי הַטֵּה־אֵלַי אָזְנֶךָ בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא מַהֵר עֲנֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tahs-TEHR pah-NEY-khah  mee-meh-NEE be-YOHM TSAHR LEE hah-teh eh-LAHY ohz-NEH-khah be-YOHM ehk-RAH mah-HEHR ah-NEH-nee

Hide not Thy face from me in the day of my distress; incline Thine ear unto me; in the day when I call answer me speedily.

### 102:4

כִּי־כָלוּ בְעָשָׁן יָמָי וְעַצְמוֹתַי כְּמוֹ־קֵד נִחָרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee khah-LOO ve-ah-SHAHN yah-MAHY ve-ahts-moh-TAHY ke-moh KEHD nee-KHAH-roo

For my days are consumed like smoke, and my bones are burned as a hearth.

### 102:5

הוּכָּה־כָעֵשֶׂב וַיִּבַשׁ לִבִּי כִּי־שָׁכַחְתִּי מֵאֲכֹל לַחְמִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoo-kah khah-eh-SEHV vah-yee-VAHSH lee-BEE kee shah-KHAHKH-tee meh-ah-KHOHL lahkh-MEE

My heart is smitten like grass, and withered; for I forget to eat my bread.

### 102:6

מִקּוֹל אַנְחָתִי דָּבְקָה עַצְמִי לִבְשָׂרִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-KOHL ahn-khah-TEE dah-ve-KAH ahts-MEE leev-sah-REE

By reason of the voice of my sighing My bones cleave to my flesh.

### 102:7

דָּמִיתִי לִקְאַת מִדְבָּר הָיִיתִי כְּכוֹס חֳרָבוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-mee-TEE leek-AHT meed-BAHR hah-YEE-tee ke-KHOHS khoh-rah-VOHT

I am like a pelican of the wilderness; I am become as an owl of the waste places.

### 102:8

שָׁקַדְתִּי וָאֶהְיֶה כְּצִפּוֹר בּוֹדֵד עַל־גָּג׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-KAHD-tee vah-eh-he-YEH ke-tsee-POHR boh-DEHD ahl GAHG

I watch, and am become like a sparrow that is alone upon the housetop.

### 102:9

כָּל־הַיּוֹם חֵרְפוּנִי אוֹיְבָי מְהוֹלָלַי בִּי נִשְׁבָּעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl hah-YOHM kheh-re-FOO-nee oh-ye-VAHY me-hoh-lah-LAHY BEE neesh-BAH-oo

Mine enemies taunt me all the day; they that are mad against me do curse by me.

### 102:10

כִּי־אֵפֶר כַּלֶּחֶם אָכָלְתִּי וְשִׁקֻּוַי בִּבְכִי מָסָכְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee eh-FEHR kah-LEH-khehm ah-KHAH-le-tee ve-shee-koo-VAHY beev-KHEE mah-SAH-khe-tee

For I have eaten ashes like bread, and mingled my drink with weeping,

### 102:11

מִפְּנֵי־זַעַמְךָ וְקִצְפֶּךָ כִּי נְשָׂאתַנִי וַתַּשְׁלִיכֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-pe-ney zah-ahm-KHAH ve-keets-PEH-khah KEE ne-sah-TAH-nee vah-tahsh-lee-KHEH-nee

Because of Thine indignation and Thy wrath; for Thou hast taken me up, and cast me away.

### 102:12

יָמַי כְּצֵל נָטוּי וַאֲנִי כָּעֵשֶׂב אִיבָשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-MAHY ke-TSEHL nah-TOOY vah-ah-NEE kah-EH-sehv ee-VAHSH

My days are like a lengthening shadow; and I am withered like grass.

### 102:13

וְאַתָּה יְהוָה לְעוֹלָם תֵּשֵׁב וְזִכְרְךָ לְדֹר וָדֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI le-oh-LAHM teh-SHEHV ve-zeekh-re-KHAH le-DOHR vah-DOHR

But Thou, O LORD, sittest enthroned for ever; and Thy name is unto all generations.

### 102:14

אַתָּה תָקוּם תְּרַחֵם צִיּוֹן כִּי־עֵת לְחֶנְנָהּ כִּי־בָא מוֹעֵד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH tah-KOOM te-rah-KHEHM tsee-YOHN kee EHT le-kheh-ne-NAHH kee VAH moh-EHD

Thou wilt arise, and have compassion upon Zion; for it is time to be gracious unto her, for the appointed time is come.

### 102:15

כִּי־רָצוּ עֲבָדֶיךָ אֶת־אֲבָנֶיהָ וְאֶת־עֲפָרָהּ יְחֹנֵנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee rah-TSOO ah-vah-dey-KHAH eht ah-vah-NEY-hah ve-eht ah-fah-RAHH ye-khoh-NEH-noo

For Thy servants take pleasure in her stones, and love her dust.

### 102:16

וְיִירְאוּ גוֹיִם אֶת־שֵׁם יְהוָה וְכָל־מַלְכֵי הָאָרֶץ אֶת־כְּבוֹדֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yee-re-OO goh-YEEM eht SHEHM ah-doh-NAI ve-khohl mahl-KHEY hah-AH-rehts eht ke-voh-DEH-khah

So the nations will fear the name of the LORD, and all the kings of the earth Thy glory;

### 102:17

כִּי־בָנָה יְהוָה צִיּוֹן נִרְאָה בִּכְבוֹדוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee vah-NAH ah-doh-NAI tsee-YOHN neer-AH beekh-voh-DOH

When the LORD hath built up Zion, when He hath appeared in His glory;

### 102:18

פָּנָה אֶל־תְּפִלַּת הָעַרְעָר וְלֹא־בָזָה אֶת־תְּפִלָּתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-NAH ehl te-fee-LAHT hah-ahr-AHR ve-loh vah-ZAH eht te-fee-lah-TAHM

When He hath regarded the prayer of the destitute, And hath not despised their prayer.

### 102:19

תִּכָּתֶב זֹאת לְדוֹר אַחֲרוֹן וְעַם נִבְרָא יְהַלֶּל־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tee-KAH-tehv ZOHT le-DOHR ah-khah-ROHN ve-AHM neev-RAH ye-hah-lehl YAHH

This shall be written for the generation to come; And a people which shall be created shall praise the LORD.

### 102:20

כִּי־הִשְׁקִיף מִמְּרוֹם קָדְשׁוֹ יְהוָה מִשָּׁמַיִם אֶל־אֶרֶץ הִבִּיט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee heesh-KEEF mee-me-ROHM kohd-SHOH ah-doh-NAI mee-shah-MAH-yeem  ehl EH-rehts hee-BEET

For He hath looked down from the height of His sanctuary; From heaven did the LORD behold the earth;

### 102:21

לִשְׁמֹעַ אֶנְקַת אָסִיר לְפַתֵּחַ בְּנֵי תְמוּתָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leesh-moh-AH ehn-KAHT ah-SEER le-fah-TEH-ahkh be-NEY te-moo-TAH

To hear the groaning of the prisoner; To loose those that are appointed to death;

### 102:22

**Pronunciation draft:** le-sah-PEHR be-tsee-YOHN SHEHM ah-doh-NAI oo-te-hee-lah-TOH bee-roo-shah-LAH-eem

That men may tell of the name of the LORD in Zion, And His praise in Jerusalem; .

### 102:23

**Pronunciation draft:** be-hee-kah-VEHTS ah-MEEM yahkh-DAHV oo-mahm-lah-KHOHT lah-ah-VOHD eht ah-doh-NAI

They shall perish, but Thou shalt endure; Yea, all of them shall wax old like a garment; As a vesture shalt Thou change them, and they shall pass away;

## Psalm 103 — Compassion, forgiveness, and healing

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.103) · Buckets: health, repentance, gratitude

### 103:1

לְדָוִד בָּרֲכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת־יְהוָה וְכָל־קְרָבַי אֶת־שֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ׃

[A Psalm] of David. Bless the LORD, O my soul; And all that is within me, bless His holy name.

### 103:2

בָּרֲכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת־יְהוָה וְאַל־תִּשְׁכְּחִי כָּל־גְּמוּלָיו׃

### 103:5

### 103:6

The LORD executeth righteousness, And acts of justice for all that are oppressed.

### 103:7

He made known His ways unto Moses, His doings unto the children of Israel.

### 103:8

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-KHOOM ve-khah-NOON ah-doh-NAI EH-rehkh ah-PAH-yeem ve-rahv KHAH-sehd

The LORD is full of compassion and gracious, Slow to anger, and plenteous in mercy.

### 103:9

לֹא־לָנֶצַח יָרִיב וְלֹא לְעוֹלָם יִטּוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh lah-NEH-tsahkh yah-REEV ve-LOH le-oh-LAHM yee-TOHR

He will not always contend; Neither will He keep His anger for ever.

### 103:10

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE kheeg-VOH-ahh shah-mah-YEEM ahl hah-AH-rehts gah-VAHR khahs-DOH ahl ye-reh-AHV

As far as the east is from the west, So far hath He removed our transgressions from us.

### 103:13

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-rah-KHEHM AHV ahl bah-NEEM ree-KHAHM ah-doh-NAI ahl ye-reh-AHV

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ROO-ahkh ah-ve-rah BOH ve-ey-NEH-noo ve-loh yah-kee-REH-noo OHD me-koh-MOH

### 103:17

But the mercy of the LORD is from everlasting to everlasting upon them that fear Him, And His righteousness unto children's children;

### 103:18

לְשֹׁמְרֵי בְרִיתוֹ וּלְזֹכְרֵי פִקֻּדָיו לַעֲשׂוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-shoh-me-REY ve-ree-TOH oo-le-zoh-khe-REY fee-koo-DAHV lah-ah-soh-TAHM

To such as keep His covenant, And to those that remember His precepts to do them.

### 103:19

## Psalm 104 — Creation, living things, and sustenance

### 104:1

בָּרֲכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת־יְהוָה יְהוָה אֱלֹהַי גָּדַלְתָּ מְּאֹד הוֹד וְהָדָר לָבָשְׁתָּ׃

Bless the LORD, O my soul. O LORD my God, Thou art very great; Thou art clothed with glory and majesty.

### 104:2

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-teh OHR kah-sahl-MAH noh-TEH shah-MAH-yeem kahy-ree-AH

Who coverest Thyself with light as with a garment, who stretchest out the heavens like a curtain;

### 104:3

הַמְקָרֶה בַמַּיִם עֲ‍לִיּוֹתָיו הַשָּׂם־עָבִים רְכוּבוֹ הַמְהַלֵּךְ עַל־כַּנְפֵי־רוּחַ׃

Who layest the beams of Thine upper chambers in the waters, who makest the clouds Thy chariot, who walkest upon the wings of the wind;

### 104:4

עֹשֶׂה מַלְאָכָיו רוּחוֹת מְשָׁרְתָיו אֵשׁ לֹהֵט׃

### 104:5

### 104:6

**Pronunciation draft:** te-HOHM kah-le-VOOSH kee-see-TOH ahl hah-REEM yah-ahm-doo MAH-yeem

### 104:7

**Pronunciation draft:** meen gah-ah-RAH-te-KHAH ye-noo-SOON meen KOHL rah-ahm-KHAH yeh-khah-feh-ZOON

### 104:8

יַעֲלוּ הָרִים יֵרְדוּ בְקָעוֹת אֶל־מְקוֹם זֶה יָסַדְתָּ לָהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-ah-LOO hah-REEM yeh-re-DOO ve-kah-OHT ehl me-KOHM ZEH  yah-SAHD-tah lah-HEHM

### 104:9

### 104:11

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ley-HEHM ohf hah-shah-MAH-yeem yeesh-KOHN mee-BEYN oh-fah-YEEM yee-te-noo KOHL

### 104:14

מַצְמִיחַ חָצִיר לַבְּהֵמָה וְעֵשֶׂב לַעֲבֹדַת הָאָדָם לְהוֹצִיא לֶחֶם מִן־הָאָרֶץ׃

Who causeth the grass to spring up for the cattle, And herb for the service of man; To bring forth bread out of the earth,

### 104:15

וְיַיִן יְשַׂמַּח לְבַב־אֱנוֹשׁ לְהַצְהִיל פָּנִים מִשָּׁמֶן וְלֶחֶם לְבַב־אֱנוֹשׁ יִסְעָד׃
