And wine that maketh glad the heart of man, Making the face brighter than oil, And bread that stayeth man's heart.

### 104:16

יִשְׂבְּעוּ עֲצֵי יְהוָה אַרְזֵי לְבָנוֹן אֲשֶׁר נָטָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yees-be-OO ah-TSEY ah-doh-NAI ah-re-ZEY le-vah-NOHN ah-SHEHR nah-TAH

The trees of the LORD have their fill, The cedars of Lebanon, which He hath planted;

### 104:17

אֲשֶׁר־שָׁם צִפֳּרִים יְקַנֵּנוּ חֲסִידָה בְּרוֹשִׁים בֵּיתָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-shehr SHAHM tsee-poh-REEM ye-kah-NEH-noo khah-see-DAH be-roh-SHEEM bey-TAHH

Wherein the birds make their nests; As for the stork, the fir-trees are her house.

### 104:18

הָרִים הַגְּבֹהִים לַיְּעֵלִים סְלָעִים מַחְסֶה לַשְׁפַנִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-REEM hah-ge-voh-HEEM lah-ye-eh-LEEM se-lah-EEM mahkh-SEH lah-she-fah-NEEM

The high mountains are for the wild goats; The rocks are a refuge for the conies.

### 104:19

עָשָׂה יָרֵחַ לְמוֹעֲדִים שֶׁמֶשׁ יָדַע מְבוֹאוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SAH yah-reh-AHKH le-moh-ah-DEEM sheh-MEHSH yah-DAH me-voh-OH

Who appointedst the moon for seasons; The sun knoweth his going down.

### 104:20

תָּשֶׁת־חֹשֶׁךְ וִיהִי לָיְלָה בּוֹ־תִרְמֹשׂ כָּל־חַיְתוֹ־יָעַר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-sheht khoh-SHEHKH VEE-hee LAH-ye-lah boh teer-MOHS kohl khahy-toh YAH-ahr

Thou makest darkness, and it is night, Wherein all the beasts of the forest do creep forth.

### 104:21

הַכְּפִירִים שֹׁאֲגִים לַטָּרֶף וּלְבַקֵּשׁ מֵאֵל אָכְלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-ke-fee-REEM shoh-ah-GEEM lah-TAH-rehf oo-le-vah-KEHSH meh-EHL ohkh-LAHM

The young lions roar after their prey, And seek their food from God.

### 104:22

תִּזְרַח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ יֵאָסֵפוּן וְאֶל־מְעוֹנֹתָם יִרְבָּצוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** teez-RAHKH hah-sheh-MEHSH yeh-ah-seh-FOON ve-ehl me-oh-noh-TAHM yeer-bah-TSOON

The sun ariseth, they slink away, And couch in their dens.

### 104:23

יֵצֵא אָדָם לְפָעֳלוֹ וְלַעֲבֹדָתוֹ עֲדֵי־עָרֶב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-TSEH ah-DAHM le-foh-oh-LOH ve-lah-ah-VOH-dah-TOH ah-dey AH-rehv

Man goeth forth unto his work And to his labour until the evening.

### 104:24

מָה־רַבּוּ מַעֲשֶׂיךָ יְהוָה כֻּלָּם בְּחָכְמָה עָשִׂיתָ מָלְאָה הָאָרֶץ קִנְיָנֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mah rah-BOO mah-ah-SEY-khah  ah-doh-NAI koo-LAHM be-khohkh-MAH ah-SEE-tah mah-le-AH hah-AH-rehts keen-yah-NEH-khah

How manifold are Thy works, O LORD! In wisdom hast Thou made them all; The earth is full of Thy creatures.

### 104:25

זֶה הַיָּם גָּדוֹל וּרְחַב יָדָיִם שָׁם־רֶמֶשׂ וְאֵין מִסְפָּר חַיּוֹת קְטַנּוֹת עִם־גְּדֹלוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZEH  hah-YAHM gah-DOHL oo-re-KHAHV yah-DAH-yeem shahm reh-MEHS ve-EYN mees-PAHR khah-YOHT ke-tah-NOHT eem ge-doh-LOHT

Yonder sea, great and wide, Therein are creeping things innumerable, Living creatures, both small and great.

### 104:26

שָׁם אֳנִיּוֹת יְהַלֵּכוּן לִוְיָתָן זֶה־יָצַרְתָּ לְשַׂחֶק־בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHAHM oh-nee-YOHT ye-hah-leh-KHOON leev-yah-TAHN zeh yah-TSAHR-tah le-sah-khehk BOH

There go the ships; There is leviathan, whom Thou hast formed to sport therein.

### 104:27

כֻּלָּם אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּן לָתֵת אָכְלָם בְּעִתּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-LAHM eh-LEY-khah ye-sah-beh-ROON lah-TEHT ohkh-LAHM be-ee-TOH

All of them wait for Thee, That Thou mayest give them their food in due season.

### 104:28

תִּתֵּן לָהֶם יִלְקֹטוּן תִּפְתַּח יָדְךָ יִשְׂבְּעוּן טוֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tee-TEHN lah-HEHM yeel-koh-TOON teef-TAHKH yah-de-KHAH yees-be-OON TOHV

Thou givest it unto them, they gather it; Thou openest Thy hand, they are satisfied with good.

### 104:29

תַּסְתִּיר פָּנֶיךָ יִבָּהֵלוּן תֹּסֵף רוּחָם יִגְוָעוּן וְאֶל־עֲפָרָם יְשׁוּבוּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tahs-TEER pah-ney-KHAH yee-bah-heh-LOON toh-SEHF roo-KHAHM yeeg-vah-OON ve-ehl ah-fah-RAHM ye-shoo-VOON

Thou hidest Thy face, they vanish; Thou withdrawest their breath, they perish, And return to their dust.

### 104:30

תְּשַׁלַּח רוּחֲךָ יִבָּרֵאוּן וּתְחַדֵּשׁ פְּנֵי אֲדָמָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-shah-LAHKH roo-khah-KHAH yee-bah-reh-OON oo-te-khah-DEHSH pe-NEY ah-dah-MAH

Thou sendest forth Thy spirit, they are created; and Thou renewest the face of the earth.

### 104:31

יְהִי כְבוֹד יְהוָה לְעוֹלָם יִשְׂמַח יְהוָה בְּמַעֲשָׂיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-HEE khe-VOHD ah-doh-NAI le-oh-LAHM yees-MAHKH ah-doh-NAI be-mah-ah-SAHV

May the glory of the LORD endure for ever; let the LORD rejoice in His works!

### 104:32

הַמַּבִּיט לָאָרֶץ וַתִּרְעָד יִגַּע בֶּהָרִים וְיֶעֱשָׁנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-mah-BEET lah-ah-REHTS vah-teer-AHD yee-GAH beh-hah-REEM ve-yeh-eh-SHAH-noo

Who looketh on the earth, and it trembleth; He toucheth the mountains, and they smoke.

### 104:33

אָשִׁירָה לַיהוָה בְּחַיָּי אֲזַמְּרָה לֵאלֹהַי בְּעוֹדִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEE-rah lah-ah-doh-NAI be-khah-YAHY ah-zah-me-RAH leh-loh-HAHY be-oh-DEE

I will sing unto the LORD as long as I live; I will sing praise to my God while I have any being.

### 104:34

יֶעֱרַב עָלָיו שִׂיחִי אָנֹכִי אֶשְׂמַח בַּיהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-eh-RAHV ah-LAHV see-KHEE ah-noh-KHEE ehs-MAHKH bah-ah-doh-NAI

Let my musing be sweet unto Him; as for me, I will rejoice in the LORD.

### 104:35

יִתַּמּוּ חַטָּאִים מִן־הָאָרֶץ וּרְשָׁעִים עוֹד אֵינָם בָּרֲכִי נַפְשִׁי אֶת־יְהוָה הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-TAH-moo khah-tah-EEM  meen hah-AH-rehts oo-re-shah-EEM  OHD ey-NAHM bah-rah-KHEE nahf-SHEE eht ah-doh-NAI hah-le-loo YAHH

Let sinners cease out of the earth, and let the wicked be no more. Bless the LORD, O my soul. Hallelujah.

## Psalm 105 — Remembering the covenant and history

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.105) · Buckets: community, gratitude

### 105:1

הוֹדוּ לַיהוָה קִרְאוּ בִּשְׁמוֹ הוֹדִיעוּ בָעַמִּים עֲלִילוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI keer-OO beesh-MOH hoh-DEE-oo vah-ah-MEEM ah-lee-loh-TAHV

O give thanks unto the LORD, call upon His name; Make known His doings among the peoples.

### 105:2

שִׁירוּ־לוֹ זַמְּרוּ־לוֹ שִׂיחוּ בְּכָל־נִפְלְאוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-roo LOH zah-me-roo LOH see-KHOO be-khohl neef-le-oh-TAHV

Sing unto Him, sing praises unto Him; Speak ye of all His marvellous works.

### 105:3

הִתְהַלְלוּ בְּשֵׁם קָדְשׁוֹ יִשְׂמַח לֵב מְבַקְשֵׁי יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-te-hah-le-LOO be-SHEHM kohd-SHOH yees-MAHKH LEHV  me-vahk-SHEY ah-doh-NAI

Glory ye in His holy name; Let the heart of them rejoice that seek the LORD.

### 105:4

דִּרְשׁוּ יְהוָה וְעֻזּוֹ בַּקְּשׁוּ פָנָיו תָּמִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deer-SHOO ah-doh-NAI ve-oo-ZOH bah-ke-SHOO fah-NAHV tah-MEED

Seek ye the LORD and His strength; Seek His face continually.

### 105:5

זִכְרוּ נִפְלְאוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂה מֹפְתָיו וּמִשְׁפְּטֵי־פִיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zeekh-ROO neef-le-oh-TAHV ah-shehr ah-SAH moh-fe-TAHV oo-meesh-pe-tey FEEV

Remember His marvellous works that He hath done, His wonders, and the judgments of His mouth;

### 105:6

זֶרַע אַבְרָהָם עַבְדּוֹ בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב בְּחִירָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zeh-RAH ahv-rah-HAHM ahv-DOH be-NEY yah-ah-KOHV be-khee-RAHV

O ye seed of Abraham His servant, Ye children of Jacob, His chosen ones.

### 105:7

הוּא יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּכָל־הָאָרֶץ מִשְׁפָּטָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HOO ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo be-khohl hah-AH-rehts meesh-pah-TAHV

He is the LORD our God; His judgments are in all the earth.

### 105:8

זָכַר לְעוֹלָם בְּרִיתוֹ דָּבָר צִוָּה לְאֶלֶף דּוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-KHAHR le-oh-LAHM be-ree-TOH dah-VAHR tsee-VAH le-EH-lehf DOHR

He hath remembered His covenant for ever, The word which He commanded to a thousand generations;

### 105:9

אֲשֶׁר כָּרַת אֶת־אַבְרָהָם וּשְׁבוּעָתוֹ לְיִשְׂחָק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR kah-RAHT eht ahv-rah-HAHM oo-she-VOO-ah-TOH le-yees-KHAHK

[The covenant] which He made with Abraham, And His oath unto Isaac;

### 105:10

וַיַּעֲמִידֶהָ לְיַעֲקֹב לְחֹק לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרִית עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ah-mee-DEH-hah le-yah-ah-KOHV le-KHOHK le-yees-rah-EHL be-REET oh-LAHM

And He established it unto Jacob for a statute, To Israel for an everlasting covenant;

### 105:11

לֵאמֹר לְךָ אֶתֵּן אֶת־אֶרֶץ־כְּנָעַן חֶבֶל נַחֲלַתְכֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leh-MOHR le-KHAH eh-TEHN eht eh-rehts ke-NAH-ahn kheh-VEHL nah-khah-laht-KHEHM

Saying: 'Unto thee will I give the land of Canaan, The lot of your inheritance.'

### 105:12

בִּהְיוֹתָם מְתֵי מִסְפָּר כִּמְעַט וְגָרִים בָּהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bee-he-yoh-TAHM me-TEY mees-PAHR keem-AHT ve-gah-REEM BAHH

When they were but a few men in number. Yea, very few, and sojourners in it,

### 105:13

וַיִּתְהַלְּכוּ מִגּוֹי אֶל־גּוֹי מִמַּמְלָכָה אֶל־עַם אַחֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeet-hah-le-KHOO mee-GOHY ehl GOHY mee-mahm-lah-KHAH ehl AHM ah-KHEHR

And when they went about from nation to nation, From one kingdom to another people,

### 105:14

לֹא־הִנִּיחַ אָדָם לְעָשְׁקָם וַיּוֹכַח עֲלֵיהֶם מְלָכִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh hee-NEE-ahkh ah-DAHM le-ah-she-KAHM vah-YOH-khahkh ah-ley-HEHM me-lah-KHEEM

He suffered no man to do them wrong, Yea, for their sake He reproved kings:

### 105:15

אַל־תִּגְּעוּ בִמְשִׁיחָי וְלִנְבִיאַי אַל־תָּרֵעוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tee-ge-OO veem-shee-KHAHY ve-leen-vee-AHY ahl tah-REH-oo

'Touch not Mine anointed ones, And do My prophets no harm.'

### 105:16

וַיִּקְרָא רָעָב עַל־הָאָרֶץ כָּל־מַטֵּה־לֶחֶם שָׁבָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeek-RAH rah-AHV ahl hah-AH-rehts kahl mah-teh LEH-khehm shah-VAHR

And He called a famine upon the land; He broke the whole staff of bread.

### 105:17

שָׁלַח לִפְנֵיהֶם אִישׁ לְעֶבֶד נִמְכַּר יוֹסֵף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-LAHKH leef-ney-HEHM EESH le-EH-vehd neem-KAHR yoh-SEHF

He sent a man before them; Joseph was sold for a servant;

### 105:18

עִנּוּ בַכֶּבֶל רגליו [רַגְלוֹ] בַּרְזֶל בָּאָה נַפְשׁוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ee-NOO vah-KEH-vehl rahg-LOH bahr-ZEHL BAH-ah nahf-SHOH

His feet they hurt with fetters, His person was laid in iron;

Reading note: written רגליו → read רַגְל֑וֹ. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 105:19

עַד־עֵת בֹּא־דְבָרוֹ אִמְרַת יְהוָה צְרָפָתְהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahd EHT boh de-vah-ROH eem-RAHT ah-doh-NAI tse-rah-FAH-te-hoo

Until the time that his word came to pass, The word of the LORD tested him.

### 105:20

שָׁלַח מֶלֶךְ וַיַּתִּירֵהוּ מֹשֵׁל עַמִּים וַיְפַתְּחֵהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHAH-lahkh meh-LEHKH vah-yah-tee-REH-hoo moh-SHEHL ah-MEEM vah-ye-fah-te-KHEH-hoo

The king sent and loosed him; Even the ruler of the peoples, and set him free.

### 105:21

שָׂמוֹ אָדוֹן לְבֵיתוֹ וּמֹשֵׁל בְּכָל־קִנְיָנוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-MOH ah-DOHN le-vey-TOH oo-moh-SHEHL be-khohl keen-yah-NOH

He made him lord of his house, And ruler of all his possessions;

### 105:22

לֶאְסֹר שָׂרָיו בְּנַפְשׁוֹ וּזְקֵנָיו יְחַכֵּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leh-SOHR sah-RAHV be-nahf-SHOH oo-ze-keh-NAHV ye-khah-KEHM

To bind his princes at his pleasure, And teach his elders wisdom.

### 105:23

וַיָּבֹא יִשְׂרָאֵל מִצְרָיִם וְיַעֲקֹב גָּר בְּאֶרֶץ־חָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-VOH yees-rah-EHL meets-RAH-yeem ve-yah-ah-KOHV GAHR be-eh-rehts KHAHM

Israel also came into Egypt; And Jacob sojourned in the land of Ham.

### 105:24

וַיֶּפֶר אֶת־עַמּוֹ מְאֹד וַיַּעֲצִמֵהוּ מִצָּרָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YEH-fehr eht ah-MOH me-OHD vah-yah-ah-tsee-meh-HOO mee-tsah-RAHV

And He increased His people greatly, And made them too mighty for their adversaries.

### 105:25

הָפַךְ לִבָּם לִשְׂנֹא עַמּוֹ לְהִתְנַכֵּל בַּעֲבָדָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-FAHKH lee-BAHM lees-NOH ah-MOH le-heet-nah-KEHL bah-ah-vah-DAHV

He turned their heart to hate His people, To deal craftily with His servants.

### 105:26

שָׁלַח מֹשֶׁה עַבְדּוֹ אַהֲרֹן אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר־בּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-LAHKH moh-SHEH ahv-DOH ah-hah-ROHN ah-SHEHR bah-khahr BOH

He sent Moses His servant, And Aaron whom He had chosen.

### 105:27

שָׂמוּ־בָם דִּבְרֵי אֹתוֹתָיו וּמֹפְתִים בְּאֶרֶץ חָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-moo VAHM deev-REY oh-toh-TAHV oo-moh-fe-TEEM be-EH-rehts KHAHM

They wrought among them His manifold signs, And wonders in the land of Ham.

### 105:28

שָׁלַח חֹשֶׁךְ וַיַּחְשִׁךְ וְלֹא־מָרוּ אֶת־דברוו [דְּבָרוֹ׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** SHAH-lahkh khoh-SHEHKH vah-yahkh-SHEEKH ve-loh mah-ROO eh-de-vah-ROH

He sent darkness, and it was dark; And they rebelled not against His word.

Reading note: written ת־דברוו → read דְּבָרֽוֹ׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 105:29

הָפַךְ אֶת־מֵימֵיהֶם לְדָם וַיָּמֶת אֶת־דְּגָתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-FAHKH eht mey-mey-HEHM le-DAHM vah-YAH-meht eht de-gah-TAHM

He turned their waters into blood, And slew their fish.

### 105:30

שָׁרַץ אַרְצָם צְפַרְדְּעִים בְּחַדְרֵי מַלְכֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-RAHTS ahr-TSAHM tse-fahr-de-EEM be-khahd-REY mahl-khey-HEHM

Their land swarmed with frogs, In the chambers of their kings.

### 105:31

אָמַר וַיָּבֹא עָרֹב כִּנִּים בְּכָל־גְּבוּלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR vah-yah-VOH ah-ROHV kee-NEEM be-khohl ge-voo-LAHM

He spoke, and there came swarms of flies, And gnats in all their borders.

### 105:32

נָתַן גִּשְׁמֵיהֶם בָּרָד אֵשׁ לֶהָבוֹת בְּאַרְצָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-TAHN geesh-mey-HEHM bah-RAHD EHSH leh-hah-VOHT be-ahr-TSAHM

He gave them hail for rain, And flaming fire in their land.

### 105:33

וַיַּךְ גַּפְנָם וּתְאֵנָתָם וַיְשַׁבֵּר עֵץ גְּבוּלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YAHKH gahf-NAHM oo-te-eh-nah-TAHM vah-ye-shah-BEHR EHTS ge-voo-LAHM

He smote their vines also and their fig-trees; And broke the trees of their borders.

### 105:34

אָמַר וַיָּבֹא אַרְבֶּה וְיֶלֶק וְאֵין מִסְפָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR vah-yah-VOH ahr-BEH ve-YEH-lehk ve-EYN mees-PAHR

He spoke, and the locust came, And the canker-worm without number,

### 105:35

וַיֹּאכַל כָּל־עֵשֶׂב בְּאַרְצָם וַיֹּאכַל פְּרִי אַדְמָתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YOH-khahl kohl EH-sehv be-ahr-TSAHM vah-YOH-khahl pe-REE ahd-mah-TAHM

And did eat up every herb in their land, And did eat up the fruit of their ground.

### 105:36

וַיַּךְ כָּל־בְּכוֹר בְּאַרְצָם רֵאשִׁית לְכָל־אוֹנָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YAHKH kohl be-KHOHR be-ahr-TSAHM reh-SHEET le-khohl oh-NAHM

He smote also all the first-born in their land, The first-fruits of all their strength.

### 105:37

וַיּוֹצִיאֵם בְּכֶסֶף וְזָהָב וְאֵין בִּשְׁבָטָיו כּוֹשֵׁל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-tsee-EHM be-KHEH-sehf ve-zah-HAHV ve-EYN beesh-vah-TAHV koh-SHEHL

And He brought them forth with silver and gold; And there was none that stumbled among His tribes.

### 105:38

שָׂמַח מִצְרַיִם בְּצֵאתָם כִּי־נָפַל פַּחְדָּם עֲלֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-MAHKH meets-RAH-yeem be-tseh-TAHM kee nah-FAHL pahkh-DAHM ah-ley-HEHM

Egypt was glad when they departed; For the fear of them had fallen upon them.

### 105:39

פָּרַשׂ עָנָן לְמָסָךְ וְאֵשׁ לְהָאִיר לָיְלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-RAHS ah-NAHN le-mah-SAHKH ve-EHSH le-hah-EER LAH-ye-lah

He spread a cloud for a screen; And fire to give light in the night.

### 105:40

שָׁאַל וַיָּבֵא שְׂלָו וְלֶחֶם שָׁמַיִם יַשְׂבִּיעֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-AHL vah-yah-VEH se-LAHV ve-LEH-khehm shah-MAH-yeem yahs-bee-EHM

They asked, and He brought quails, And gave them in plenty the bread of heaven.

### 105:41

פָּתַח צוּר וַיָּזוּבוּ מָיִם הָלְכוּ בַּצִּיּוֹת נָהָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** PAH-tahkh TSOOR vah-yah-ZOO-voo MAH-yeem hah-le-KHOO bah-tsee-YOHT nah-HAHR

He opened the rock, and waters gushed out; They ran, a river in the dry places.

### 105:42

כִּי־זָכַר אֶת־דְּבַר קָדְשׁוֹ אֶת־אַבְרָהָם עַבְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee zah-KHAHR eht de-VAHR kohd-SHOH eht ahv-rah-HAHM ahv-DOH

For He remembered His holy word Unto Abraham His servant;

### 105:43

וַיּוֹצִא עַמּוֹ בְשָׂשׂוֹן בְּרִנָּה אֶת־בְּחִירָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-TSEE ah-MOH ve-sah-SOHN be-ree-NAH eht be-khee-RAHV

And He brought forth His people with joy, His chosen ones with singing.

### 105:44

וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם אַרְצוֹת גּוֹיִם וַעֲמַל לְאֻמִּים יִירָשׁוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN lah-HEHM ahr-TSOHT goh-YEEM vah-ah-MAHL le-oo-MEEM yee-RAH-shoo

And He gave them the lands of the nations, And they took the labour of the peoples in possession;

### 105:45

בַּעֲבוּר יִשְׁמְרוּ חֻקָּיו וְתוֹרֹתָיו יִנְצֹרוּ הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ah-VOOR  yeesh-me-ROO khoo-KAHV ve-toh-roh-TAHV yeen-TSOH-roo hah-le-loo YAHH

That they might keep His statutes, And observe His laws. Hallelujah.

## Psalm 106 — Collective confession and mercy

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.106) · Buckets: repentance, community

### 106:1

הַלְלוּיָהּ הוֹדוּ לַיהוָה כִּי־טוֹב כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-loo-YAHH  hoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI kee TOHV KEE le-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH

Hallelujah. O give thanks unto the LORD; for He is good; for His mercy endureth for ever.

### 106:2

מִי יְמַלֵּל גְּבוּרוֹת יְהוָה יַשְׁמִיעַ כָּל־תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** MEE ye-mah-LEHL ge-voo-ROHT ah-doh-NAI yahsh-MEE-ah kohl te-hee-lah-TOH

Who can express the mighty acts of the LORD, or make all His praise to be heard?

### 106:3

אַשְׁרֵי שֹׁמְרֵי מִשְׁפָּט עֹשֵׂה צְדָקָה בְכָל־עֵת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY shoh-me-REY meesh-PAHT oh-SEH tse-dah-KAH ve-khohl EHT

Happy are they that keep justice, that do righteousness at all times.

### 106:4

זָכְרֵנִי יְהוָה בִּרְצוֹן עַמֶּךָ פָּקְדֵנִי בִּישׁוּעָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-khe-REH-nee ah-doh-NAI beer-TSOHN ah-MEH-khah pah-ke-DEH-nee bee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah

Remember me, O LORD, when Thou favourest Thy people; O think of me at Thy salvation;

### 106:5

לִרְאוֹת בְּטוֹבַת בְּחִירֶיךָ לִשְׂמֹחַ בְּשִׂמְחַת גּוֹיֶךָ לְהִתְהַלֵּל עִם־נַחֲלָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leer-OHT  be-toh-VAHT be-khee-REY-khah lees-moh-AHKH be-seem-KHAHT goh-YEH-khah le-heet-hah-LEHL eem nah-khah-lah-TEH-khah

That I may behold the prosperity of Thy chosen, that I may rejoice in the gladness of Thy nation, that I may glory with Thine inheritance.

### 106:6

חָטָאנוּ עִם־אֲבוֹתֵינוּ הֶעֱוִינוּ הִרְשָׁעְנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-TAH-noo eem ah-voh-TEY-noo heh-eh-VEE-noo heer-SHAH-e-noo

We have sinned with our fathers, we have done iniquitously, we have dealt wickedly.

### 106:7

אֲבוֹתֵינוּ בְמִצְרַיִם לֹא־הִשְׂכִּילוּ נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ לֹא זָכְרוּ אֶת־רֹב חֲסָדֶיךָ וַיַּמְרוּ עַל־יָם בְּיַם־סוּף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-voh-TEY-noo ve-meets-RAH-yeem  loh hees-KEE-loo neef-le-oh-TEY-khah LOH zah-khe-ROO eht ROHV khah-sah-DEY-khah vah-yahm-ROO ahl YAHM be-yahm SOOF

Our fathers in Egypt gave no heed unto Thy wonders; they remembered not the multitude of Thy mercies; but were rebellious at the sea, even at the Red Sea.

### 106:8

וַיּוֹשִׁיעֵם לְמַעַן שְׁמוֹ לְהוֹדִיעַ אֶת־גְּבוּרָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-shee-EHM le-MAH-ahn she-MOH le-hoh-DEE-ah eht ge-voo-rah-TOH

Nevertheless He saved them for His name's sake, that He might make His mighty power to be known.

### 106:9

וַיִּגְעַר בְּיַם־סוּף וַיֶּחֱרָב וַיּוֹלִיכֵם בַּתְּהֹמוֹת כַּמִּדְבָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeeg-AHR be-yahm SOOF vah-yeh-kheh-RAHV vah-yoh-lee-KHEHM bah-te-hoh-MOHT kah-meed-BAHR

And He rebuked the Red Sea, and it was dried up; and He led them through the depths, as through a wilderness.

### 106:10

וַיּוֹשִׁיעֵם מִיַּד שׂוֹנֵא וַיִּגְאָלֵם מִיַּד אוֹיֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yoh-shee-EHM mee-YAHD soh-NEH vah-yeeg-ah-LEHM mee-YAHD oh-YEHV

And He saved them from the hand of him that hated them, And redeemed them from the hand of the enemy.

### 106:11

וַיְכַסּוּ־מַיִם צָרֵיהֶם אֶחָד מֵהֶם לֹא נוֹתָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-khah-soo MAH-yeem tsah-rey-HEHM eh-KHAHD meh-HEHM LOH noh-TAHR

And the waters covered their adversaries; There was not one of them left.

### 106:12

וַיַּאֲמִינוּ בִדְבָרָיו יָשִׁירוּ תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ah-MEE-noo veed-vah-RAHV yah-SHEE-roo te-hee-lah-TOH

Then believed they His words; They sang His praise.

### 106:13

מִהֲרוּ שָׁכְחוּ מַעֲשָׂיו לֹא־חִכּוּ לַעֲצָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-hah-ROO shah-khe-KHOO mah-ah-SAHV loh khee-KOO lah-ah-tsah-TOH

They soon forgot His works; They waited not for His counsel;

### 106:14

וַיִּתְאַוּוּ תַאֲוָה בַּמִּדְבָּר וַיְנַסּוּ־אֵל בִּישִׁימוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeet-ah-VOO tah-ah-VAH bah-meed-BAHR vah-ye-nah-soo EHL bee-shee-MOHN

But lusted exceedingly in the wilderness, And tried God in the desert.

### 106:15

וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם שֶׁאֱלָתָם וַיְשַׁלַּח רָזוֹן בְּנַפְשָׁם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN lah-HEHM sheh-eh-lah-TAHM vah-ye-shah-LAHKH rah-ZOHN be-nahf-SHAHM

And He gave them their request; But sent leanness into their soul.

### 106:16

וַיְקַנְאוּ לְמֹשֶׁה בַּמַּחֲנֶה לְאַהֲרֹן קְדוֹשׁ יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-kahn-OO le-moh-SHEH bah-mah-khah-NEH le-ah-hah-ROHN ke-DOHSH ah-doh-NAI

They were jealous also of Moses in the camp, And of Aaron the holy one of the LORD.

### 106:17

תִּפְתַּח־אֶרֶץ וַתִּבְלַע דָּתָן וַתְּכַס עַל־עֲדַת אֲבִירָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** teef-tahkh eh-REHTS vah-teev-LAH dah-TAHN vah-te-KHAHS ahl ah-DAHT ah-vee-RAHM

The earth opened and swallowed up Dathan, And covered the company of Abiram.

### 106:18

וַתִּבְעַר־אֵשׁ בַּעֲדָתָם לֶהָבָה תְּלַהֵט רְשָׁעִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-teev-ahr EHSH bah-ah-dah-TAHM leh-hah-VAH te-lah-HEHT re-shah-EEM

And a fire was kindled in their company; The flame burned up the wicked.

### 106:19

יַעֲשׂוּ־עֵגֶל בְּחֹרֵב וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲווּ לְמַסֵּכָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-ah-soo EH-gehl be-khoh-REHV vah-yeesh-tah-khah-VOO le-mah-seh-KHAH

They made a calf in Horeb, And worshipped a molten image.

### 106:20

וַיָּמִירוּ אֶת־כְּבוֹדָם בְּתַבְנִית שׁוֹר אֹכֵל עֵשֶׂב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-MEE-roo eht ke-voh-DAHM be-tahv-NEET SHOHR oh-KHEHL EH-sehv

Thus they exchanged their glory For the likeness of an ox that eateth grass.

### 106:21

שָׁכְחוּ אֵל מוֹשִׁיעָם עֹשֶׂה גְדֹלוֹת בְּמִצְרָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-khe-KHOO EHL moh-shee-AHM oh-SEH ge-doh-LOHT be-meets-RAH-yeem

They forgot God their saviour, Who had done great things in Egypt;

### 106:22

נִפְלָאוֹת בְּאֶרֶץ חָם נוֹרָאוֹת עַל־יַם־סוּף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neef-lah-OHT be-EH-rehts KHAHM noh-rah-OHT ahl yahm SOOF

Wondrous works in the land of Ham, Terrible things by the Red Sea.

### 106:23

וַיֹּאמֶר לְהַשְׁמִידָם לוּלֵי מֹשֶׁה בְחִירוֹ עָמַד בַּפֶּרֶץ לְפָנָיו לְהָשִׁיב חֲמָתוֹ מֵהַשְׁחִית׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YOH-mehr le-hahsh-mee-DAHM loo-LEY moh-SHEH ve-khee-ROH ah-MAHD bah-PEH-rehts le-fah-NAHV le-hah-SHEEV khah-mah-TOH meh-hahsh-KHEET

Therefore He said that He would destroy them, Had not Moses His chosen stood before Him in the breach, To turn back His wrath, lest He should destroy them.

### 106:24

וַיִּמְאֲסוּ בְּאֶרֶץ חֶמְדָּה לֹא־הֶאֱמִינוּ לִדְבָרוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeem-ah-SOO be-EH-rehts khehm-DAH loh heh-eh-MEE-noo leed-vah-ROH

Moreover, they scorned the desirable land, They believed not His word;

### 106:25

וַיֵּרָגְנוּ בְאָהֳלֵיהֶם לֹא שָׁמְעוּ בְּקוֹל יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeh-rah-ge-NOO ve-oh-hoh-ley-HEHM LOH shah-me-OO be-KOHL ah-doh-NAI

And they murmured in their tents, They hearkened not unto the voice of the LORD.

### 106:26

וַיִּשָּׂא יָדוֹ לָהֶם לְהַפִּיל אוֹתָם בַּמִּדְבָּר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-SAH yah-DOH lah-HEHM le-hah-PEEL oh-TAHM bah-meed-BAHR

Therefore He swore concerning them, That He would overthrow them in the wilderness;

### 106:27

וּלְהַפִּיל זַרְעָם בַּגּוֹיִם וּלְזָרוֹתָם בָּאֲרָצוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-le-hah-PEEL zahr-AHM bah-goh-YEEM oo-le-zah-roh-TAHM bah-ah-rah-TSOHT

And that He would cast out their seed among the nations, And scatter them in the lands.

### 106:28

וַיִּצָּמְדוּ לְבַעַל פְּעוֹר וַיֹּאכְלוּ זִבְחֵי מֵתִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TSAH-me-DOO le-VAH-ahl pe-OHR vah-yoh-khe-LOO zeev-KHEY meh-TEEM

They joined themselves also unto Baal of Peor, And ate the sacrifices of the dead.

### 106:29

וַיַּכְעִיסוּ בְּמַעַלְלֵיהֶם וַתִּפְרָץ־בָּם מַגֵּפָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahkh-ee-SOO be-mah-ahl-ley-HEHM vah-teef-rahts BAHM mah-geh-FAH

Thus they provoked Him with their doings, And the plague broke in upon them.

### 106:30

וַיַּעֲמֹד פִּינְחָס וַיְפַלֵּל וַתֵּעָצַר הַמַּגֵּפָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ah-MOHD pee-ne-KHAHS vah-ye-fah-LEHL vah-teh-ah-TSAHR hah-mah-geh-FAH

Then stood up Phinehas, and wrought judgment, And so the plague was stayed.

### 106:31

וַתֵּחָשֶׁב לוֹ לִצְדָקָה לְדֹר וָדֹר עַד־עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-teh-KHAH-shehv LOH leets-dah-KAH le-DOHR vah-DOHR ahd oh-LAHM

And that was counted unto him for righteousness, Unto all generations for ever.

### 106:32

וַיַּקְצִיפוּ עַל־מֵי מְרִיבָה וַיֵּרַע לְמֹשֶׁה בַּעֲבוּרָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahk-tsee-FOO ahl MEY me-ree-VAH vah-YEH-rah le-moh-SHEH bah-ah-voo-RAHM

They angered Him also at the waters of Meribah, And it went ill with Moses because of them;

### 106:33

כִּי־הִמְרוּ אֶת־רוּחוֹ וַיְבַטֵּא בִּשְׂפָתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee heem-ROO eht roo-KHOH vah-ye-vah-TEH bees-fah-TAHV

For they embittered his spirit, And he spoke rashly with his lips.

### 106:34

לֹא־הִשְׁמִידוּ אֶת־הָעַמִּים אֲשֶׁר אָמַר יְהוָה לָהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh heesh-mee-DOO eht hah-ah-MEEM ah-SHEHR ah-MAHR ah-doh-NAI lah-HEHM

They did not destroy the peoples, As the LORD commanded them;

### 106:35

וַיִּתְעָרְבוּ בַגּוֹיִם וַיִּלְמְדוּ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeet-ah-re-VOO vah-goh-YEEM vah-yeel-me-DOO mah-ah-sey-HEHM

But mingled themselves with the nations, And learned their works;

### 106:36

וַיַּעַבְדוּ אֶת־עֲצַבֵּיהֶם וַיִּהְיוּ לָהֶם לְמוֹקֵשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-ahv-DOO eht ah-tsah-bey-HEHM vah-yeeh-YOO lah-HEHM le-moh-KEHSH

And they served their idols, Which became a snare unto them;

### 106:37

וַיִּזְבְּחוּ אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶם וְאֶת־בְּנוֹתֵיהֶם לַשֵּׁדִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeez-be-KHOO eht be-ney-HEHM ve-eht be-noh-tey-HEHM lah-SHEH-deem

Yea, they sacrificed their sons and their daughters unto demons,

### 106:38

וַיִּשְׁפְּכוּ דָם נָקִי דַּם־בְּנֵיהֶם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר זִבְּחוּ לַעֲצַבֵּי כְנָעַן וַתֶּחֱנַף הָאָרֶץ בַּדָּמִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-she-pe-KHOO DAHM nah-KEE dahm be-ney-HEHM oo-ve-noh-tey-HEHM ah-SHEHR zee-be-KHOO lah-ah-tsah-BEY khe-NAH-ahn vah-teh-kheh-NAHF hah-AH-rehts bah-dah-MEEM

And shed innocent blood, even the blood of their sons and of their daughters, Whom they sacrificed unto the idols of Canaan; And the land was polluted with blood.

### 106:39

וַיִּטְמְאוּ בְמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם וַיִּזְנוּ בְּמַעַלְלֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeet-me-OO ve-mah-ah-sey-HEHM vah-yeez-NOO be-mah-ahl-ley-HEHM

Thus were they defiled with their works, And went astray in their doings.

### 106:40

וַיִּחַר־אַף יְהוָה בְּעַמּוֹ וַיְתָעֵב אֶת־נַחֲלָתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-khahr AHF ah-doh-NAI be-ah-MOH vah-ye-tah-EHV eht nah-khah-lah-TOH

Therefore was the wrath of the LORD kindled against His people, And He abhorred His inheritance.

### 106:41

וַיִּתְּנֵם בְּיַד־גּוֹיִם וַיִּמְשְׁלוּ בָהֶם שֹׂנְאֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-te-NEHM be-yahd goh-YEEM vah-yeem-she-LOO vah-HEHM soh-ne-ey-HEHM

And He gave them into the hand of the nations; And they that hated them ruled over them.

### 106:42

וַיִּלְחָצוּם אוֹיְבֵיהֶם וַיִּכָּנְעוּ תַּחַת יָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeel-khah-TSOOM oh-ye-vey-HEHM vah-yee-kah-ne-OO TAH-khaht yah-DAHM

Their enemies also oppressed them, And they were subdued under their hand.

### 106:43

פְּעָמִים רַבּוֹת יַצִּילֵם וְהֵמָּה יַמְרוּ בַעֲצָתָם וַיָּמֹכּוּ בַּעֲוֺנָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-ah-MEEM rah-BOHT yah-tsee-LEHM ve-heh-MAH yahm-ROO vah-ah-tsah-TAHM vah-yah-MOH-koo bah-ah-voh-NAHM

Many times did He deliver them; But they were rebellious in their counsel, And sank low through their iniquity.

### 106:44

וַיַּרְא בַּצַּר לָהֶם בְּשָׁמְעוֹ אֶת־רִנָּתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YAHR bah-TSAHR lah-HEHM be-shah-me-OH eht ree-nah-TAHM

Nevertheless He looked upon their distress, When He heard their cry;

### 106:45

וַיִּזְכֹּר לָהֶם בְּרִיתוֹ וַיִּנָּחֵם כְּרֹב חסדו [חֲסָדָיו׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeez-KOHR lah-HEHM be-ree-TOH vah-yee-nah-KHEHM ke-ROHV khah-sah-DAHV

And He remembered for them His covenant, And repented according to the multitude of His mercies.

Reading note: written חסדו → read חֲסָדָֽיו׃. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 106:46

וַיִּתֵּן אוֹתָם לְרַחֲמִים לִפְנֵי כָּל־שׁוֹבֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yee-TEHN oh-TAHM le-rah-khah-MEEM leef-NEY kohl shoh-vey-HEHM

He made them also to be pitied Of all those that carried them captive.

### 106:47

הוֹשִׁיעֵנוּ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְקַבְּצֵנוּ מִן־הַגּוֹיִם לְהֹדוֹת לְשֵׁם קָדְשֶׁךָ לְהִשְׁתַּבֵּחַ בִּתְהִלָּתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-shee-EH-noo  ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo ve-kah-be-tseh-NOO meen hah-goh-YEEM le-hoh-DOHT le-SHEHM kohd-SHEH-khah le-heesh-tah-BEH-ahkh beet-hee-lah-TEH-khah

Save us, O LORD our God, And gather us from among the nations, That we may give thanks unto Thy holy name, That we may triumph in Thy praise.

### 106:48

בָּרוּךְ־יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן־הָעוֹלָם וְעַד הָעוֹלָם וְאָמַר כָּל־הָעָם אָמֵן הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY yees-rah-EHL meen HAH-oh-LAHM  ve-AHD hah-oh-LAHM ve-ah-MAHR kohl hah-AHM ah-MEHN hah-le-loo YAHH

Blessed be the LORD, the God of Israel, from everlasting even to everlasting, and let all the people say: 'Amen.' Hallelujah.

## Psalm 107 — Thanksgiving after distress and rescue

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.107) · Buckets: gratitude, travel, health

### 107:1

הֹדוּ לַיהוָה כִּי־טוֹב כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI kee TOHV KEE le-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH

'O give thanks unto the LORD, for He is good, For His mercy endureth for ever.'

### 107:2

יֹאמְרוּ גְּאוּלֵי יְהוָה אֲשֶׁר גְּאָלָם מִיַּד־צָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-me-ROO ge-oo-LEY ah-doh-NAI ah-SHEHR ge-ah-LAHM mee-yahd TSAHR

So let the redeemed of the LORD say, Whom He hath redeemed from the hand of the adversary;

### 107:3

וּמֵאֲרָצוֹת קִבְּצָם מִמִּזְרָח וּמִמַּעֲרָב מִצָּפוֹן וּמִיָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-meh-ah-rah-TSOHT kee-be-TSAHM mee-meez-RAHKH oo-mee-mah-ah-RAHV mee-tsah-FOHN oo-mee-YAHM

And gathered them out of the lands, From the east and from the west, From the north and from the sea.

### 107:4

תָּעוּ בַמִּדְבָּר בִּישִׁימוֹן דָּרֶךְ עִיר מוֹשָׁב לֹא מָצָאוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-OO vah-meed-BAHR bee-shee-MOHN DAH-rehkh EER moh-SHAHV LOH mah-TSAH-oo

They wandered in the wilderness in a desert way; They found no city of habitation.

### 107:5

רְעֵבִים גַּם־צְמֵאִים נַפְשָׁם בָּהֶם תִּתְעַטָּף׃

**Pronunciation draft:** re-eh-VEEM gahm tse-meh-EEM nahf-SHAHM bah-HEHM teet-ah-TAHF

Hungry and thirsty, Their soul fainted in them.

### 107:6

וַיִּצְעֲקוּ אֶל־יְהוָה בַּצַּר לָהֶם מִמְּצוּקוֹתֵיהֶם יַצִּילֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeets-ah-KOO ehl ah-doh-NAI bah-TSAHR lah-HEHM mee-me-tsoo-koh-tey-HEHM yah-tsee-LEHM

Then they cried unto the LORD in their trouble, And He delivered them out of their distresses.

### 107:7

וַיַּדְרִיכֵם בְּדֶרֶךְ יְשָׁרָה לָלֶכֶת אֶל־עִיר מוֹשָׁב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-de-ree-KHEHM be-DEH-rehkh ye-shah-RAH lah-LEH-kheht ehl EER moh-SHAHV

And He led them by a straight way, That they might go to a city of habitation.

### 107:8

יוֹדוּ לַיהוָה חַסְדּוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו לִבְנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI khahs-DOH ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV leev-NEY ah-DAHM

Let them give thanks unto the LORD for His mercy, And for His wonderful works to the children of men!

### 107:9

כִּי־הִשְׂבִּיעַ נֶפֶשׁ שֹׁקֵקָה וְנֶפֶשׁ רְעֵבָה מִלֵּא־טוֹב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hees-bee-AH NEH-fehsh shoh-keh-KAH ve-NEH-fehsh re-eh-VAH mee-leh TOHV

For He hath satisfied the longing soul, And the hungry soul He hath filled with good.

### 107:10

יֹשְׁבֵי חֹשֶׁךְ וְצַלְמָוֶת אֲסִירֵי עֳנִי וּבַרְזֶל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-she-VEY KHOH-shehkh ve-tsahl-MAH-veht ah-see-REY oh-NEE oo-vahr-ZEHL

Such as sat in darkness and in the shadow of death, Being bound in affliction and iron—

### 107:11

כִּי־הִמְרוּ אִמְרֵי־אֵל וַעֲצַת עֶלְיוֹן נָאָצוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee heem-ROO eem-rey EHL vah-ah-TSAHT ehl-YOHN nah-AH-tsoo

Because they rebelled against the words of God, And contemned the counsel of the Most High.

### 107:12

וַיַּכְנַע בֶּעָמָל לִבָּם כָּשְׁלוּ וְאֵין עֹזֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yahkh-NAH beh-ah-MAHL lee-BAHM kah-she-LOO ve-EYN oh-ZEHR

Therefore He humbled their heart with travail, They stumbled, and there was none to help—

### 107:13

וַיִּזְעֲקוּ אֶל־יְהוָה בַּצַּר לָהֶם מִמְּצֻקוֹתֵיהֶם יוֹשִׁיעֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeez-ah-KOO ehl ah-doh-NAI bah-TSAHR lah-HEHM mee-me-tsoo-koh-tey-HEHM yoh-shee-EHM

They cried unto the LORD in their trouble, And He saved them out of their distresses.

### 107:14

יוֹצִיאֵם מֵחֹשֶׁךְ וְצַלְמָוֶת וּמוֹסְרוֹתֵיהֶם יְנַתֵּק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-tsee-EHM meh-KHOH-shehkh ve-tsahl-MAH-veht oo-moh-se-ROH-tey-HEHM ye-nah-TEHK

He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death, And broke their bands in sunder. .

### 107:15

יוֹדוּ לַיהוָה חַסְדּוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו לִבְנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI khahs-DOH ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV leev-NEY ah-DAHM

Let them give thanks unto the LORD for His mercy, And for His wonderful works to the children of men!

### 107:16

כִּי־שִׁבַּר דַּלְתוֹת נְחֹשֶׁת וּבְרִיחֵי בַרְזֶל גִּדֵּעַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee shee-BAHR dahl-TOHT ne-KHOH-sheht oo-ve-ree-KHEY vahr-ZEHL gee-DEH-ah

For He hath broken the gates of brass, And cut the bars of iron in sunder.

### 107:17

אֱוִלִים מִדֶּרֶךְ פִּשְׁעָם וּמֵעֲוֺנֹתֵיהֶם יִתְעַנּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-vee-LEEM mee-DEH-rehkh peesh-AHM oo-meh-ah-voh-noh-tey-HEHM yeet-ah-NOO

Crazed because of the way of their transgression, And afflicted because of their iniquities—

### 107:18

כָּל־אֹכֶל תְּתַעֵב נַפְשָׁם וַיַּגִּיעוּ עַד־שַׁעֲרֵי מָוֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl oh-KHEHL te-tah-EHV nahf-SHAHM vah-yah-GEE-oo ahd SHAH-ah-rey MAH-veht

Their soul abhorred all manner of food, And they drew near unto the gates of death—

### 107:19

וַיִּזְעֲקוּ אֶל־יְהוָה בַּצַּר לָהֶם מִמְּצֻקוֹתֵיהֶם יוֹשִׁיעֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeez-ah-KOO ehl ah-doh-NAI bah-TSAHR lah-HEHM mee-me-tsoo-koh-tey-HEHM yoh-shee-EHM

They cried unto the LORD in their trouble, And He saved them out of their distresses;

### 107:20

יִשְׁלַח דְּבָרוֹ וְיִרְפָּאֵם וִימַלֵּט מִשְּׁחִיתוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeesh-LAHKH de-vah-ROH ve-yeer-pah-EHM vee-mah-LEHT mee-she-khee-toh-TAHM

He sent His word, and healed them, And delivered them from their graves.

### 107:21

יוֹדוּ לַיהוָה חַסְדּוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו לִבְנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI khahs-DOH ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV leev-NEY ah-DAHM

Let them give thanks unto the LORD for His mercy, And for His wonderful works to the children of men!

### 107:22

וְיִזְבְּחוּ זִבְחֵי תוֹדָה וִיסַפְּרוּ מַעֲשָׂיו בְּרִנָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yeez-be-KHOO zeev-KHEY toh-DAH vee-sah-pe-ROO mah-ah-SAHV be-ree-NAH

And let them offer the sacrifices of thanksgiving, And declare His works with singing.

### 107:23

יוֹרְדֵי הַיָּם בָּאֳנִיּוֹת עֹשֵׂי מְלָאכָה בְּמַיִם רַבִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-re-DEY hah-YAHM boh-oh-nee-YOHT oh-SEY me-lah-KHAH be-MAH-yeem rah-BEEM

They that go down to the sea in ships, That do business in great waters—

### 107:24

הֵמָּה רָאוּ מַעֲשֵׂי יְהוָה וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו בִּמְצוּלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HEH-mah rah-OO mah-ah-SEY ah-doh-NAI ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV beem-tsoo-LAH

These saw the works of the LORD, And His wonders in the deep;

### 107:25

וַיֹּאמֶר וַיַּעֲמֵד רוּחַ סְעָרָה וַתְּרוֹמֵם גַּלָּיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YOH-mehr vah-yah-ah-MEHD ROO-ahkh se-ah-RAH vah-te-roh-MEHM gah-LAHV

For He commanded, and raised the stormy wind, Which lifted up the waves thereof;

### 107:26

יַעֲלוּ שָׁמַיִם יֵרְדוּ תְהוֹמוֹת נַפְשָׁם בְּרָעָה תִתְמוֹגָג׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-ah-LOO shah-mah-YEEM yeh-re-DOO te-hoh-MOHT nahf-SHAHM be-rah-AH teet-moh-GAHG

They mounted up to the heaven, they went down to the deeps; Their soul melted away because of trouble;

### 107:27

יָחוֹגּוּ וְיָנוּעוּ כַּשִּׁכּוֹר וְכָל־חָכְמָתָם תִּתְבַּלָּע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-KHOH-goo ve-yah-noo-OO kah-shee-KOHR ve-khohl khohkh-mah-TAHM teet-bah-LAH

They reeled to and fro, and staggered like a drunken man, And all their wisdom was swallowed up—

### 107:28

וַיִּצְעֲקוּ אֶל־יְהוָה בַּצַּר לָהֶם וּמִמְּצוּקֹתֵיהֶם יוֹצִיאֵם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeets-ah-KOO ehl ah-doh-NAI bah-TSAHR lah-HEHM oo-mee-me-tsoo-koh-tey-HEHM yoh-tsee-EHM

They cried unto the LORD in their trouble, And He brought them out of their distresses.

### 107:29

יָקֵם סְעָרָה לִדְמָמָה וַיֶּחֱשׁוּ גַּלֵּיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-KEHM se-ah-RAH leed-mah-MAH vah-yeh-kheh-SHOO gah-ley-HEHM

He made the storm a calm, So that the waves thereof were still.

### 107:30

וַיִּשְׂמְחוּ כִי־יִשְׁתֹּקוּ וַיַּנְחֵם אֶל־מְחוֹז חֶפְצָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yees-me-KHOO khee yeesh-TOH-koo vah-yahn-KHEHM ehl me-KHOHZ khehf-TSAHM

Then were they glad because they were quiet, And He led them unto their desired haven.

### 107:31

יוֹדוּ לַיהוָה חַסְדּוֹ וְנִפְלְאוֹתָיו לִבְנֵי אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI khahs-DOH ve-neef-le-oh-TAHV leev-NEY ah-DAHM

Let them give thanks unto the LORD for His mercy, And for His wonderful works to the children of men!

### 107:32

וִירֹמְמוּהוּ בִּקְהַל־עָם וּבְמוֹשַׁב זְקֵנִים יְהַלְלוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vee-roh-me-moo-HOO beek-hahl AHM oo-ve-moh-SHAHV ze-keh-NEEM ye-hah-le-LOO-hoo

Let them exalt Him also in the assembly of the people, And praise Him in the seat of the elders.

### 107:33

יָשֵׂם נְהָרוֹת לְמִדְבָּר וּמֹצָאֵי מַיִם לְצִמָּאוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SEHM ne-hah-ROHT le-meed-BAHR oo-moh-TSAH-ey mah-YEEM le-tsee-mah-OHN

He turneth rivers into a wilderness, And watersprings into a thirsty ground;

### 107:34

אֶרֶץ פְּרִי לִמְלֵחָה מֵרָעַת יֹשְׁבֵי בָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EH-rehts pe-REE leem-leh-KHAH meh-rah-AHT YOH-she-vey VAHH

A fruitful land into a salt waste, For the wickedness of them that dwell therein.

### 107:35

יָשֵׂם מִדְבָּר לַאֲגַם־מַיִם וְאֶרֶץ צִיָּה לְמֹצָאֵי מָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SEHM meed-BAHR lah-ah-gahm MAH-yeem ve-EH-rehts tsee-YAH le-moh-TSAH-ey MAH-yeem

He turneth a wilderness into a pool of water, And a dry land into watersprings.

### 107:36

וַיּוֹשֶׁב שָׁם רְעֵבִים וַיְכוֹנְנוּ עִיר מוֹשָׁב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-YOH-shehv SHAHM re-eh-VEEM vah-ye-khoh-ne-NOO EER moh-SHAHV

And there He maketh the hungry to dwell, And they establish a city of habitation;

### 107:37

וַיִּזְרְעוּ שָׂדוֹת וַיִּטְּעוּ כְרָמִים וַיַּעֲשׂוּ פְּרִי תְבוּאָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeez-re-OO sah-DOHT vah-yee-te-OO khe-rah-MEEM vah-yah-ah-SOO pe-REE te-VOO-ah

And sow fields, and plant vineyards, Which yield fruits of increase.

### 107:38

וַיְבָרֲכֵם וַיִּרְבּוּ מְאֹד וּבְהֶמְתָּם לֹא יַמְעִיט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-vah-rah-KHEHM vah-yeer-BOO me-OHD oo-ve-hehm-TAHM LOH yahm-EET

He blesseth them also, so that they are multiplied greatly, And suffereth not their cattle to decrease.

### 107:39

וַיִּמְעֲטוּ וַיָּשֹׁחוּ מֵעֹצֶר רָעָה וְיָגוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeem-ah-TOO vah-yah-SHOH-khoo meh-OH-tsehr rah-AH ve-yah-GOHN

Again, they are minished and dwindle away Through oppression of evil and sorrow.

### 107:40

שֹׁפֵךְ בּוּז עַל־נְדִיבִים וַיַּתְעֵם בְּתֹהוּ לֹא־דָרֶךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shoh-FEHKH BOOZ ahl ne-dee-VEEM vah-yaht-EHM be-TOH-hoo loh DAH-rehkh

He poureth contempt upon princes, And causeth them to wander in the waste, where there is no way.

### 107:41

וַיְשַׂגֵּב אֶבְיוֹן מֵעוֹנִי וַיָּשֶׂם כַּצֹּאן מִשְׁפָּחוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ye-sah-GEHV ehv-YOHN meh-OH-nee vah-YAH-sehm kah-TSOHN meesh-pah-KHOHT

Yet setteth He the needy on high from affliction, And maketh his families like a flock.

### 107:42

יִרְאוּ יְשָׁרִים וְיִשְׂמָחוּ וְכָל־עַוְלָה קָפְצָה פִּיהָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeer-OO ye-shah-REEM ve-yees-MAH-khoo ve-khohl ahv-LAH KAH-fe-tsah PEE-hah

The upright see it, and are glad; And all iniquity stoppeth her mouth.

### 107:43

מִי־חָכָם וְיִשְׁמָר־אֵלֶּה וְיִתְבּוֹנְנוּ חַסְדֵי יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee khah-KHAHM ve-yeesh-mohr EH-leh ve-yeet-boh-ne-NOO khah-se-DEY ah-doh-NAI

Whoso is wise, let him observe these things, And let them consider the mercies of the LORD.

## Psalm 108 — Praise and communal hope

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.108) · Buckets: gratitude, community

### 108:1

שִׁיר מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

A Song, a Psalm of David.

### 108:2

נָכוֹן לִבִּי אֱלֹהִים אָשִׁירָה וַאֲזַמְּרָה אַף־כְּבוֹדִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-KHOHN lee-BEE eh-loh-HEEM ah-SHEE-rah vah-ah-zah-me-RAH ahf ke-voh-DEE

My heart is steadfast, O God; I will sing, yea, I will sing praises, even with my glory.

### 108:3

עוּרָה הַנֵּבֶל וְכִנּוֹר אָעִירָה שָּׁחַר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-RAH hah-NEH-vehl ve-khee-NOHR ah-EE-rah SHAH-khahr

Awake, psaltery and harp; I will awake the dawn.

### 108:4

אוֹדְךָ בָעַמִּים יְהוָה וַאֲזַמֶּרְךָ בַּל־אֻמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-de-KHAH vah-ah-MEEM  ah-doh-NAI vah-ah-zah-mehr-KHAH bahl oo-MEEM

I will give thanks unto Thee, O LORD, among the peoples; And I will sing praises unto Thee among the nations.

### 108:5

כִּי־גָדוֹל מֵעַל־שָׁמַיִם חַסְדֶּךָ וְעַד־שְׁחָקִים אֲמִתֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee gah-DOHL meh-ahl shah-MAH-yeem khahs-DEH-khah ve-ahd she-khah-KEEM ah-mee-TEH-khah

For Thy mercy is great above the heavens, And Thy truth reacheth unto the skies.

### 108:6

רוּמָה עַל־שָׁמַיִם אֱלֹהִים וְעַל כָּל־הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ROO-mah ahl shah-MAH-yeem eh-loh-HEEM ve-AHL kohl hah-AH-rehts ke-voh-DEH-khah

Be Thou exalted, O God, above the heavens; And Thy glory be above all the earth.

### 108:7

לְמַעַן יֵחָלְצוּן יְדִידֶיךָ הוֹשִׁיעָה יְמִינְךָ וַעֲנֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-mah-AHN yeh-khah-le-TSOON ye-dee-DEY-khah hoh-SHEE-ah ye-mee-ne-KHAH vah-ah-NEH-nee

That Thy beloved may be delivered, Save with Thy right hand, and answer me.

### 108:8

אֱלֹהִים דִּבֶּר בְּקָדְשׁוֹ אֶעְלֹזָה אֲחַלְּקָה שְׁכֶם וְעֵמֶק סֻכּוֹת אֲמַדֵּד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM  dee-BEHR be-kohd-SHOH eh-LOH-zah ah-khah-le-KAH she-KHEHM ve-EH-mehk soo-KOHT ah-mah-DEHD

God spoke in His holiness, that I would exult; That I would divide Shechem, and mete out the valley of Succoth.

### 108:9

לִי גִלְעָד לִי מְנַשֶּׁה וְאֶפְרַיִם מָעוֹז רֹאשִׁי יְהוּדָה מְחֹקְקִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** LEE geel-AHD  LEE me-nah-SHEH ve-ehf-rah-YEEM mah-OHZ roh-SHEE ye-hoo-DAH me-khoh-ke-KEE

Gilead is mine, Manasseh is mine; Ephraim also is the defence of my head; Judah is my sceptre.

### 108:10

מוֹאָב סִיר רַחְצִי עַל־אֱדוֹם אַשְׁלִיךְ נַעֲלִי עֲלֵי־פְלֶשֶׁת אֶתְרוֹעָע׃

**Pronunciation draft:** moh-AHV  SEER rahkh-TSEE ahl eh-DOHM ahsh-LEEKH nah-ah-LEE ah-ley fe-LEH-sheht eht-roh-AH

Moab is my washpot; Upon Edom do I cast my shoe; Over Philistia do I cry aloud.

### 108:11

מִי יֹבִלֵנִי עִיר מִבְצָר מִי נָחַנִי עַד־אֱדוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** MEE yoh-vee-leh-NEE EER meev-TSAHR MEE nah-KHAH-nee ahd eh-DOHM

Who will bring me into the fortified city? Who will lead me unto Edom?

### 108:12

הֲלֹא־אֱלֹהִים זְנַחְתָּנוּ וְלֹא־תֵצֵא אֱלֹהִים בְּצִבְאֹתֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-loh eh-loh-HEEM ze-nahkh-TAH-noo ve-loh teh-TSEH eh-loh-HEEM be-tseev-oh-TEY-noo

Hast not Thou cast us off, O God? And Thou goest not forth, O God, with our hosts?

### 108:13

הָבָה־לָּנוּ עֶזְרָת מִצָּר וְשָׁוְא תְּשׁוּעַת אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-vah LAH-noo ehz-RAHT mee-TSAHR ve-SHAHV te-shoo-AHT ah-DAHM

Give us help against the adversary; For vain is the help of man.

### 108:14

בֵּאלֹהִים נַעֲשֶׂה־חָיִל וְהוּא יָבוּס צָרֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** beh-loh-HEEM nah-ah-seh KHAH-yeel ve-HOO yah-VOOS tsah-REY-noo

Through God we shall do valiantly; For He it is that will tread down our adversaries.

## Psalm 109 — Accusation and a plea for vindication

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.109) · Buckets: justice, protection

### 109:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר אֱלֹהֵי תְהִלָּתִי אַל־תֶּחֱרַשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-tseh-AHKH le-dah-VEED meez-MOHR eh-loh-HEY te-hee-lah-TEE ahl teh-kheh-RAHSH

For the Leader. A Psalm of David. O God of my praise, keep not silence;

### 109:2

כִּי פִי רָשָׁע וּפִי־מִרְמָה עָלַי פָּתָחוּ דִּבְּרוּ אִתִּי לְשׁוֹן שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE FEE rah-SHAH oo-fee meer-MAH ah-LAHY pah-TAH-khoo dee-be-ROO ee-TEE le-SHOHN SHAH-kehr

For the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of deceit have they opened against me; They have spoken unto me with a lying tongue.

### 109:3

וְדִבְרֵי שִׂנְאָה סְבָבוּנִי וַיִּלָּחֲמוּנִי חִנָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-deev-REY seen-AH se-vah-VOO-nee vah-yee-lah-khah-MOO-nee khee-NAHM

They compassed me about also with words of hatred, And fought against me without a cause.

### 109:4

תַּחַת־אַהֲבָתִי יִשְׂטְנוּנִי וַאֲנִי תְפִלָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-khaht ah-hah-vah-TEE yees-te-NOO-nee vah-ah-NEE te-fee-LAH

In return for my love they are my adversaries; But I am all prayer.

### 109:5

וַיָּשִׂימוּ עָלַי רָעָה תַּחַת טוֹבָה וְשִׂנְאָה תַּחַת אַהֲבָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yah-SEE-moo ah-LAHY rah-AH TAH-khaht toh-VAH ve-seen-AH TAH-khaht ah-hah-vah-TEE

And they have laid upon me evil for good, And hatred for my love:

### 109:6

הַפְקֵד עָלָיו רָשָׁע וְשָׂטָן יַעֲמֹד עַל־יְמִינוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hahf-KEHD ah-LAHV rah-SHAH ve-sah-TAHN yah-ah-MOHD ahl ye-mee-NOH

’Set Thou a wicked man over him; And let an adversary stand at his right hand.

### 109:7

בְּהִשָּׁפְטוֹ יֵצֵא רָשָׁע וּתְפִלָּתוֹ תִּהְיֶה לַחֲטָאָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-hee-SHAH-fe-TOH yeh-TSEH rah-SHAH oo-te-fee-lah-TOH teeh-YEH lah-khah-tah-AH

When he is judged, let him go forth condemned; And let his prayer be turned into sin.

### 109:8

יִהְיוּ־יָמָיו מְעַטִּים פְּקֻדָּתוֹ יִקַּח אַחֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-he-yoo yah-MAHV me-ah-TEEM pe-koo-dah-TOH yee-KAHKH ah-KHEHR

Let his days be few; Let another take his charge.

### 109:9

יִהְיוּ־בָנָיו יְתוֹמִים וְאִשְׁתּוֹ אַלְמָנָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-he-yoo vah-NAHV ye-toh-MEEM ve-eesh-TOH ahl-mah-NAH

Let his children be fatherless, And his wife a widow.

### 109:10

וְנוֹעַ יָנוּעוּ בָנָיו וְשִׁאֵלוּ וְדָרְשׁוּ מֵחָרְבוֹתֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-NOH-ah yah-NOO-oo vah-NAHV ve-shee-EH-loo ve-dah-re-SHOO meh-khohr-voh-tey-HEHM

Let his children be vagabonds, and beg; And let them seek their bread out of their desolate places.

### 109:11

יְנַקֵּשׁ נוֹשֶׁה לְכָל־אֲשֶׁר־לוֹ וְיָבֹזּוּ זָרִים יְגִיעוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-nah-KEHSH noh-SHEH le-khohl ah-shehr LOH ve-yah-VOH-zoo zah-REEM ye-gee-OH

Let the creditor distrain all that he hath; And let strangers make spoil of his labour.

### 109:12

אַל־יְהִי־לוֹ מֹשֵׁךְ חָסֶד וְאַל־יְהִי חוֹנֵן לִיתוֹמָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl ye-hee LOH moh-SHEHKH KHAH-sehd ve-ahl ye-HEE khoh-NEHN lee-toh-MAHV

Let there be none to extend kindness unto him; Neither let there be any to be gracious unto his fatherless children.

### 109:13

יְהִי־אַחֲרִיתוֹ לְהַכְרִית בְּדוֹר אַחֵר יִמַּח שְׁמָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-hee ah-khah-ree-TOH le-hahkh-REET be-DOHR ah-KHEHR yee-MAHKH she-MAHM

Let his posterity be cut off; In the generation following let their name be blotted out.

### 109:14

יִזָּכֵר עֲוֺן אֲבֹתָיו אֶל־יְהוָה וְחַטַּאת אִמּוֹ אַל־תִּמָּח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-zah-KHEHR  ah-VOHN ah-voh-TAHV ehl ah-doh-NAI ve-khah-TAHT ee-MOH ahl tee-MAHKH

Let the iniquity of his fathers be brought to remembrance unto the LORD; And let not the sin of his mother be blotted out.

### 109:15

יִהְיוּ נֶגֶד־יְהוָה תָּמִיד וְיַכְרֵת מֵאֶרֶץ זִכְרָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeeh-YOO neh-gehd ah-doh-NAI tah-MEED ve-yahkh-REHT meh-EH-rehts zeekh-RAHM

Let them be before the LORD continually, That He may cut off the memory of them from the earth.

### 109:16

יַעַן אֲשֶׁר לֹא זָכַר עֲשׂוֹת חָסֶד וַיִּרְדֹּף אִישׁ־עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן וְנִכְאֵה לֵבָב לְמוֹתֵת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** YAH-ahn ah-SHEHR  LOH zah-KHAHR ah-SOHT KHAH-sehd vah-yeer-DOHF eesh ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN ve-neekh-EH leh-VAHV le-moh-TEHT

Because that he remembered not to do kindness, But persecuted the poor and needy man, And the broken in heart he was ready to slay.

### 109:17

וַיֶּאֱהַב קְלָלָה וַתְּבוֹאֵהוּ וְלֹא־חָפֵץ בִּבְרָכָה וַתִּרְחַק מִמֶּנּוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeh-eh-HAHV ke-lah-LAH vah-te-voh-EH-hoo ve-loh khah-FEHTS beev-rah-KHAH vah-teer-KHAHK mee-MEH-noo

Yea, he loved cursing, and it came unto him; And he delighted not in blessing, and it is far from him.

### 109:18

וַיִּלְבַּשׁ קְלָלָה כְּמַדּוֹ וַתָּבֹא כַמַּיִם בְּקִרְבּוֹ וְכַשֶּׁמֶן בְּעַצְמוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-yeel-BAHSH ke-lah-LAH ke-mah-DOH vah-tah-VOH khah-MAH-yeem be-keer-BOH ve-khah-SHEH-mehn be-ahts-moh-TAHV

He clothed himself also with cursing as with his raiment, And it is come into his inward parts like water, And like oil into his bones.

### 109:19

תְּהִי־לוֹ כְּבֶגֶד יַעְטֶה וּלְמֵזַח תָּמִיד יַחְגְּרֶהָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-hee LOH ke-VEH-gehd yah-TEH oo-le-MEH-zahkh tah-MEED yahkh-ge-REH-hah

Let it be unto him as the garment which he putteth on, And for the girdle wherewith he is girded continually.'

### 109:20

זֹאת פְּעֻלַּת שֹׂטְנַי מֵאֵת יְהוָה וְהַדֹּבְרִים רָע עַל־נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZOHT pe-oo-LAHT soh-te-NAHY meh-EHT ah-doh-NAI ve-hah-doh-ve-REEM RAH ahl nahf-SHEE

This would mine adversaries effect from the LORD, And they that speak evil against my soul.

### 109:21

וְאַתָּה יְהוִה אֲדֹנָי עֲ‍שֵׂה־אִתִּי לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ כִּי־טוֹב חַסְדְּךָ הַצִּילֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ah-TAH  eh-loh-HEEM ah-doh-NAHY ah-seh ‍ee-TEE le-MAH-ahn she-MEH-khah kee TOHV khahs-de-KHAH hah-tsee-LEH-nee

But Thou, O GOD the Lord, deal with me for Thy name's sake; Because Thy mercy is good, deliver Thou me.

### 109:22

כִּי־עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן אָנֹכִי וְלִבִּי חָלַל בְּקִרְבִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee ah-NEE ve-ehv-YOHN ah-NOH-khee ve-lee-BEE khah-LAHL be-keer-BEE

For I am poor and needy, And my heart is wounded within me.

### 109:23

כְּצֵל־כִּנְטוֹתוֹ נֶהֱלָכְתִּי נִנְעַרְתִּי כָּאַרְבֶּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-tsehl keen-toh-TOH neh-heh-LAH-khe-tee neen-AHR-tee kah-ahr-BEH

I am gone like the shadow when it lengtheneth; I am shaken off as the locust.

### 109:24

בִּרְכַּי כָּשְׁלוּ מִצּוֹם וּבְשָׂרִי כָּחַשׁ מִשָּׁמֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** beer-KAHY kah-she-LOO mee-TSOHM oo-ve-sah-REE kah-KHAHSH mee-SHAH-mehn

My knees totter through fasting; And my flesh is lean, and hath no fatness.

### 109:25

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-NEE  hah-YEE-tee khehr-PAH lah-HEHM yeer-OO-nee ye-nee-OON roh-SHAHM

I am become also a taunt unto them; When they see me, they shake their head.

### 109:26

עָזְרֵנִי יְהוָה אֱלֹהָי הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי כְחַסְדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ze-reh-NEE ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHY hoh-shee-EH-NEE khe-khahs-DEH-khah

Help me, O LORD my God; O save me according to Thy mercy;

### 109:27

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-yeh-de-OO kee YAH-de-khah ZOHT ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI ah-see-TAHH

That they may know that this is Thy hand; That Thou, LORD, hast done it.

### 109:28

יְקַלְלוּ־הֵמָּה וְאַתָּה תְבָרֵךְ קָמוּ וַיֵּבֹשׁוּ וְעַבְדְּךָ יִשְׂמָח׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-kah-le-loo heh-MAH ve-ah-TAH te-vah-REHKH KAH-moo  vah-yeh-VOH-shoo ve-ahv-de-KHAH yees-MAHKH

### 109:29

**Pronunciation draft:** yeel-be-SHOO soh-te-NAHY ke-lee-MAH ve-yah-ah-TOO khahm-EEL bohsh-TAHM

Mine adversaries shall be clothed with confusion, And shall put on their own shame as a robe.

### 109:30

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-DEH ah-doh-NAI me-OHD be-FEE oo-ve-TOHKH rah-BEEM ahhah-le-LEH-noo

### 109:31

## Psalm 110 — Kingship and victory

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.110) · Buckets: community

### 110:1

לְדָוִד מִזְמוֹר נְאֻם יְהוָה לַאדֹנִי שֵׁב לִימִינִי עַד־אָשִׁית אֹיְבֶיךָ הֲדֹם לְרַגְלֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-dah-VEED meez-MOHR ne-OOM ah-doh-NAI  lah-doh-NEE SHEHV lee-mee-NEE ahd ah-SHEET oh-ye-VEY-khah hah-DOHM le-rahg-LEY-khah

A Psalm of David. The LORD saith unto my lord: ‘Sit thou at My right hand, until I make thine enemies thy footstool.'

### 110:2

מַטֵּה־עֻזְּךָ יִשְׁלַח יְהוָה מִצִּיּוֹן רְדֵה בְּקֶרֶב אֹיְבֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mah-teh oo-ze-KHAH yeesh-LAHKH ah-doh-NAI mee-tsee-YOHN re-DEH be-KEH-rehv oh-ye-VEY-khah

The rod of Thy strength the LORD will send out of Zion: 'Rule thou in the midst of thine enemies.'

### 110:3

עַמְּךָ נְדָבֹת בְּיוֹם חֵילֶךָ בְּהַדְרֵי־קֹדֶשׁ מֵרֶחֶם מִשְׁחָר לְךָ טַל יַלְדֻתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-me-KHAH ne-dah-VOHT be-YOHM khey-LEH-khah be-hahd-rey koh-DEHSH meh-REH-khehm meesh-KHAHR le-KHAH TAHL yahl-doo-TEY-khah

Thy people offer themselves willingly in the day of thy warfare; in adornments of holiness, from the womb of the dawn, Thine is the dew of thy youth.

### 110:4

נִשְׁבַּע יְהוָה וְלֹא יִנָּחֵם אַתָּה־כֹהֵן לְעוֹלָם עַל־דִּבְרָתִי מַלְכִּי־צֶדֶק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neesh-BAH ah-doh-NAI  ve-LOH yee-nah-KHEHM ah-tah khoh-HEHN le-oh-LAHM ahl deev-rah-TEE mahl-kee TSEH-dehk

The LORD hath sworn, and will not repent: 'Thou art a priest for ever After the manner of Melchizedek.'

### 110:5

אֲדֹנָי עַל־יְמִינְךָ מָחַץ בְּיוֹם־אַפּוֹ מְלָכִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAHY ahl ye-mee-ne-KHAH mah-KHAHTS be-yohm ah-POH me-lah-KHEEM

The Lord at thy right hand Doth crush kings in the day of His wrath.

### 110:6

יָדִין בַּגּוֹיִם מָלֵא גְוִיּוֹת מָחַץ רֹאשׁ עַל־אֶרֶץ רַבָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-DEEN bah-goh-YEEM mah-LEH ge-vee-YOHT MAH-khahts ROHSH ahl EH-rehts rah-BAH

He will judge among the nations; He filleth it with the dead bodies, He crusheth the head over a wide land.

### 110:7

מִנַּחַל בַּדֶּרֶךְ יִשְׁתֶּה עַל־כֵּן יָרִים רֹאשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-nah-KHAHL bah-DEH-rehkh yeesh-TEH ahl KEHN yah-REEM ROHSH

## Psalm 111 — Praise for divine works and wisdom

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.111) · Buckets: gratitude, wisdom

### 111:1

הַלְלוּ יָהּ אוֹדֶה יְהוָה בְּכָל־לֵבָב בְּסוֹד יְשָׁרִים וְעֵדָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HAH-le-loo YAHH  oh-DEH ah-doh-NAI be-khohl leh-VAHV be-SOHD ye-shah-REEM ve-eh-DAH

Hallelujah. I will give thanks unto the LORD with my whole heart, . In the council of the upright, and in the congregation.

### 111:2

**Pronunciation draft:** ge-doh-LEEM mah-ah-SEY ah-doh-NAI de-roo-SHEEM le-khohl khehf-tsey-HEHM

The works of the LORD are great, Sought out of all them that have delight therein.

His work is glory and majesty; And His righteousness endureth for ever.

### 111:4

זֵכֶר עָשָׂה לְנִפְלְאֹתָיו חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZEH-khehr ah-SAH le-neef-le-oh-TAHV khah-NOON ve-rah-KHOOM ah-doh-NAI

He hath made a memorial for His wonderful works; The LORD is gracious and full of compassion.

### 111:5

טֶרֶף נָתַן לִירֵאָיו יִזְכֹּר לְעוֹלָם בְּרִיתוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** teh-REHF nah-TAHN lee-reh-AHV yeez-KOHR le-oh-LAHM be-ree-TOH

He hath given food unto them that fear Him; He will ever be mindful of His covenant.

### 111:6

כֹּחַ מַעֲשָׂיו הִגִּיד לְעַמּוֹ לָתֵת לָהֶם נַחֲלַת גּוֹיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOH-ahkh mah-ah-SAHV hee-GEED le-ah-MOH lah-TEHT lah-HEHM nah-khah-LAHT goh-YEEM

He hath declared to His people the power of His works, In giving them the heritage of the nations.

### 111:7

**Pronunciation draft:** mah-ah-SEY yah-DAHV eh-MEHT oo-meesh-PAHT neh-eh-mah-NEEM kohl pee-koo-DAHV

The works of His hands are truth and justice; All His precepts are sure.

### 111:8

סְמוּכִים לָעַד לְעוֹלָם עֲשׂוּיִם בֶּאֱמֶת וְיָשָׁר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** se-moo-KHEEM lah-AHD le-oh-LAHM ah-soo-YEEM beh-eh-MEHT ve-yah-SHAHR

They are established for ever and ever, They are done in truth and uprightness.

### 111:9

פְּדוּת שָׁלַח לְעַמּוֹ צִוָּה־לְעוֹלָם בְּרִיתוֹ קָדוֹשׁ וְנוֹרָא שְׁמוֹ׃

### 111:10

רֵאשִׁית חָכְמָה יִרְאַת יְהוָה שֵׂכֶל טוֹב לְכָל־עֹשֵׂיהֶם תְּהִלָּתוֹ עֹמֶדֶת לָעַד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** reh-SHEET khohkh-MAH  yeer-AHT ah-doh-NAI SEH-khehl TOHV le-khohl oh-sey-HEHM te-hee-lah-TOH oh-MEH-deht lah-AHD

### 112:1

## Psalm 114 — The Exodus and transformed nature

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.114) · Buckets: community, nature

### 114:1

בְּצֵאת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִּצְרָיִם בֵּית יַעֲקֹב מֵעַם לֹעֵז׃

### 114:3

The sea saw it, and fled; The Jordan turned backward.

**Pronunciation draft:** heh-hah-REEM rah-ke-DOO khe-ey-LEEM ge-vah-OHT keev-ney TSOHN

**Pronunciation draft:** mahh le-KHAH hah-YAHM KEE tah-NOOS hah-yahr-DEHN tee-SOHV le-ah-KHOHR

### 114:6

**Pronunciation draft:** heh-hah-REEM teer-ke-DOO khe-ey-LEEM ge-vah-OHT keev-ney TSOHN

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-leef-NEY ah-DOHN KHOO-lee AH-rehts mee-leef-NEY eh-LOH-ahh yah-ah-KOHV

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-hoh-fe-KHEE hah-TSOOR ah-gahm MAH-yeem khah-lah-MEESH le-mah-ye-noh MAH-yeem

## Psalm 115 — Trust and blessing for the community

### 115:1

## Psalm 116 — Deliverance, gratitude, and vows

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.116) · Buckets: gratitude, health

### 116:1

I love that the LORD should hear my voice and my supplications.

**Pronunciation draft:** shoh-MEHR pe-tah-YEEM ah-doh-NAI dah-loh-TEE ve-LEE ye-hoh-SHEE-ah

### 116:16

## Psalm 117 — Universal praise

### 117:1

## Psalm 118 — Thanksgiving, rescue, and rejoicing

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.118) · Buckets: gratitude, hope, health

### 118:1

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI LEE be-oh-ze-RAHY vah-ah-NEE ehr-EH ve-soh-ne-AHY

The LORD is for me as my helper; And I shall gaze upon them that hate me.

### 118:8

טוֹב לַחֲסוֹת בַּיהוָה מִבְּטֹחַ בָּאָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TOHV lah-khah-SOHT bah-ah-doh-NAI mee-be-TOH-ahkh bah-ah-DAHM

It is better to take refuge in the LORD Than to trust in man.

### 118:9

טוֹב לַחֲסוֹת בַּיהוָה מִבְּטֹחַ בִּנְדִיבִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TOHV lah-khah-SOHT bah-ah-doh-NAI mee-be-TOH-ahkh been-dee-VEEM

It is better to take refuge in the LORD Than to trust in princes.

### 118:10

כָּל־גּוֹיִם סְבָבוּנִי בְּשֵׁם יְהוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl goh-YEEM se-vah-VOO-nee be-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI KEE ah-mee-LAHM

All nations compass me about; Verily, in the name of the LORD I will cut them off.

### 118:11

סַבּוּנִי גַם־סְבָבוּנִי בְּשֵׁם יְהוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-BOO-nee gahm se-vah-VOO-nee be-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI KEE ah-mee-LAHM

They compass me about, yea, they compass me about; Verily, in the name of the LORD I will cut them off.

### 118:12

סַבּוּנִי כִדְבוֹרִים דֹּעֲכוּ כְּאֵשׁ קוֹצִים בְּשֵׁם יְהוָה כִּי אֲמִילַם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-BOO-nee kheed-voh-REEM doh-ah-KHOO ke-EHSH koh-TSEEM be-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI KEE ah-mee-LAHM

They compass me about like bees; They are quenched as the fire of thorns; Verily, in the name of the LORD I will cut them off.

### 118:13

דַּחֹה דְחִיתַנִי לִנְפֹּל וַיהוָה עֲזָרָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-KHOHH de-khee-TAH-nee leen-POHL VAH-ah-doh-NAI ah-zah-RAH-nee

Thou didst thrust sore at me that I might fall; But the LORD helped me.

### 118:14

עָזִּי וְזִמְרָת יָהּ וַיְהִי־לִי לִישׁוּעָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-ZEE ve-zeem-RAHT YAHH vah-ye-hee LEE lee-shoo-AH

The LORD is my strength and song; And He is become my salvation.

### 118:15

קוֹל רִנָּה וִישׁוּעָה בְּאָהֳלֵי צַדִּיקִים יְמִין יְהוָה עֹשָׂה חָיִל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOHL  ree-NAH vee-shoo-AH be-oh-hoh-LEY tsah-dee-KEEM ye-MEEN ah-doh-NAI OH-sah KHAH-yeel

The voice of rejoicing and salvation is in the tents of the righteous; The right hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.

### 118:16

יְמִין יְהוָה רוֹמֵמָה יְמִין יְהוָה עֹשָׂה חָיִל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-MEEN ah-doh-NAI roh-meh-MAH ye-MEEN ah-doh-NAI OH-sah KHAH-yeel

The right hand of the LORD is exalted; The right hand of the LORD doeth valiantly.

### 118:17

לֹא אָמוּת כִּי־אֶחְיֶה וַאֲסַפֵּר מַעֲשֵׂי יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loh ah-MOOT kee eh-khe-YEH vah-ah-sah-PEHR mah-ah-SEY YAHH

I shall not die, but live, And declare the works of the LORD.

### 118:18

יַסֹּר יִסְּרַנִּי יָּהּ וְלַמָּוֶת לֹא נְתָנָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-SOHR yee-se-RAH-nee YAHH ve-lah-MAH-veht LOH ne-tah-NAH-nee

The LORD hath chastened me sore; But He hath not given me over unto death.

### 118:19

פִּתְחוּ־לִי שַׁעֲרֵי־צֶדֶק אָבֹא־בָם אוֹדֶה יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** peet-khoo LEE shah-ah-rey TSEH-dehk ah-voh VAHM oh-DEH YAHH

Open to me the gates of righteousness; I will enter into them, I will give thanks unto the LORD.

### 118:20

זֶה־הַשַּׁעַר לַיהוָה צַדִּיקִים יָבֹאוּ בוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zeh hah-SHAH-ahr lah-ah-doh-NAI tsah-dee-KEEM yah-VOH-oo VOH

This is the gate of the LORD; The righteous shall enter into it.

### 118:21

אוֹדְךָ כִּי עֲנִיתָנִי וַתְּהִי־לִי לִישׁוּעָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-de-KHAH KEE ah-nee-TAH-nee vah-te-hee LEE lee-shoo-AH

I will give thanks unto Thee, for Thou hast answered me, And art become my salvation.

### 118:22

אֶבֶן מָאֲסוּ הַבּוֹנִים הָיְתָה לְרֹאשׁ פִּנָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-VEHN mah-ah-SOO hah-boh-NEEM hah-ye-TAH le-ROHSH pee-NAH

The stone which the builders rejected Is become the chief corner-stone.

### 118:23

מֵאֵת יְהוָה הָיְתָה זֹּאת הִיא נִפְלָאת בְּעֵינֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meh-EHT ah-doh-NAI HAH-ye-tah ZOHT HEE neef-LAHT be-ey-NEY-noo

This is the LORD’S doing; It is marvellous in our eyes.

### 118:24

זֶה־הַיּוֹם עָשָׂה יְהוָה נָגִילָה וְנִשְׂמְחָה בוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zeh hah-YOHM ah-SAH ah-doh-NAI nah-GEE-lah ve-nees-me-KHAH VOH

This is the day which the LORD hath made; We will rejoice and be glad in it.

### 118:25

אָנָּא יְהוָה הוֹשִׁיעָה נָּא אָנָּא יְהוָה הַצְלִיחָה נָּא׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-NAH ah-doh-NAI hoh-shee-AH NAH ah-NAH ah-doh-NAI hahts-lee-KHAH NAH

We beseech Thee, O LORD, save now! We beseech Thee, O LORD, make us now to prosper!

### 118:26

בָּרוּךְ הַבָּא בְּשֵׁם יְהוָה בֵּרַכְנוּכֶם מִבֵּית יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH hah-BAH be-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI beh-rah-khe-noo-KHEHM mee-BEYT ah-doh-NAI

Blessed be he that cometh in the name of the LORD; We bless you out of the house of the LORD.

### 118:27

אֵל יְהוָה וַיָּאֶר לָנוּ אִסְרוּ־חַג בַּעֲבֹתִים עַד־קַרְנוֹת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EHL  ah-doh-NAI vah-YAH-ehr LAH-noo ees-roo KHAHG bah-ah-voh-TEEM ahd kahr-NOHT hah-meez-BEH-ahkh

The LORD is God, and hath given us light; Order the festival procession with boughs, even unto the horns of the altar.

### 118:28

אֵלִי אַתָּה וְאוֹדֶךָּ אֱלֹהַי אֲרוֹמְמֶךָּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-LEE ah-TAH ve-oh-DEH-kah eh-loh-HAHY ah-roh-me-MEH-kah

Thou art my God, and I will give thanks unto Thee; Thou art my God, I will exalt Thee.

### 118:29

הוֹדוּ לַיהוָה כִּי־טוֹב כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-DOO lah-ah-doh-NAI kee TOHV KEE le-oh-LAHM khahs-DOH

O give thanks unto the LORD, for He is good, For His mercy endureth for ever.

## Psalm 119 — Torah, longing, affliction, and guidance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.119) · Buckets: learning, wisdom, hope

### 119:1

אַשְׁרֵי תְמִימֵי־דָרֶךְ הַהֹלְכִים בְּתוֹרַת יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY te-mee-mey DAH-rehkh hah-hoh-le-KHEEM be-toh-RAHT ah-doh-NAI

ALEPH. Happy are they that are upright in the way, who walk in the law of the LORD.

### 119:2

אַשְׁרֵי נֹצְרֵי עֵדֹתָיו בְּכָל־לֵב יִדְרְשׁוּהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY noh-tse-REY eh-doh-TAHV be-khohl LEHV yeed-re-SHOO-hoo

Happy are they that keep His testimonies, that seek Him with the whole heart.

### 119:3

אַף לֹא־פָעֲלוּ עַוְלָה בִּדְרָכָיו הָלָכוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHF loh fah-ah-LOO ahv-LAH beed-rah-KHAHV hah-LAH-khoo

Yea, they do no unrighteousness; they walk in His ways.

### 119:4

אַתָּה צִוִּיתָה פִקֻּדֶיךָ לִשְׁמֹר מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-TAH tsee-VEE-tah fee-koo-DEY-khah leesh-MOHR me-OHD

Thou hast ordained Thy precepts, that we should observe them diligently.

### 119:5

אַחֲלַי יִכֹּנוּ דְרָכָי לִשְׁמֹר חֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-khah-LAHY yee-KOH-noo de-rah-KHAHY leesh-MOHR khoo-KEY-khah

Oh that my ways were directed to observe Thy statutes!

### 119:6

אָז לֹא־אֵבוֹשׁ בְּהַבִּיטִי אֶל־כָּל־מִצְוֺתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHZ loh eh-VOHSH be-hah-bee-TEE ehl kohl meets-voh-TEY-khah

Then should I not be ashamed, when I have regard unto all Thy commandments.

### 119:7

אוֹדְךָ בְּיֹשֶׁר לֵבָב בְּלָמְדִי מִשְׁפְּטֵי צִדְקֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-de-KHAH be-YOH-shehr leh-VAHV be-lah-me-DEE meesh-pe-TEY tseed-KEH-khah

I will give thanks unto Thee with uprightness of heart, when I learn Thy righteous ordinances. .

### 119:8

אֶת־חֻקֶּיךָ אֶשְׁמֹר אַל־תַּעַזְבֵנִי עַד־מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eht khoo-KEY-khah ehsh-MOHR ahl tah-ahz-VEH-nee ahd me-OHD

I will observe Thy statutes; O forsake me not utterly.

### 119:9

בַּמֶּה יְזַכֶּה־נַּעַר אֶת־אָרְחוֹ לִשְׁמֹר כִּדְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-MEH ye-zah-keh nah-AHR eht ohr-KHOH leesh-MOHR keed-vah-REH-khah

BETH. Wherewithal shall a young man keep his way pure? By taking heed thereto according to Thy word.

### 119:10

בְּכָל־לִבִּי דְרַשְׁתִּיךָ אַל־תַּשְׁגֵּנִי מִמִּצְוֺתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khohl lee-BEE de-rahsh-TEE-khah ahl tahsh-GEH-nee mee-meets-voh-TEY-khah

With my whole heart have I sought Thee; O let me not err from Thy commandments.

### 119:11

בְּלִבִּי צָפַנְתִּי אִמְרָתֶךָ לְמַעַן לֹא אֶחֱטָא־לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-lee-BEE tsah-FAHN-tee eem-rah-TEH-khah le-MAH-ahn LOH eh-kheh-tah LAHKH

Thy word have I laid up in my heart, that I might not sin against Thee.

### 119:12

בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה יְהוָה לַמְּדֵנִי חֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI lah-me-DEH-nee khoo-KEY-khah

Blessed art Thou, O LORD; Teach me Thy statutes.

### 119:13

בִּשְׂפָתַי סִפַּרְתִּי כֹּל מִשְׁפְּטֵי־פִיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bees-fah-TAHY see-PAHR-tee KOHL meesh-pe-tey FEE-khah

With my lips have I told All the ordinances of Thy mouth.

### 119:14

בְּדֶרֶךְ עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ שַׂשְׂתִּי כְּעַל כָּל־הוֹן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-DEH-rehkh eh-de-voh-TEY-khah SAHS-tee ke-AHL kohl HOHN

I have rejoiced in the way of Thy testimonies, As much as in all riches.

### 119:15

בְּפִקֻּדֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה וְאַבִּיטָה אֹרְחֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-fee-koo-DEY-khah ah-SEE-khah ve-ah-BEE-tah oh-re-khoh-TEY-khah

I will meditate in Thy precepts, And have respect unto Thy ways.

### 119:16

בְּחֻקֹּתֶיךָ אֶשְׁתַּעֲשָׁע לֹא אֶשְׁכַּח דְּבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khoo-koh-TEY-khah eh-she-tah-ah-SHAH LOH ehsh-KAHKH de-vah-REH-khah

I will delight myself in Thy statutes; I will not forget Thy word.

### 119:17

גְּמֹל עַל־עַבְדְּךָ אֶחְיֶה וְאֶשְׁמְרָה דְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ge-MOHL ahl ahv-de-KHAH eh-khe-YEH ve-ehsh-me-RAH de-vah-REH-khah

GIMEL. Deal bountifully with Thy servant that I may live, and I will observe Thy word.

### 119:18

גַּל־עֵינַי וְאַבִּיטָה נִפְלָאוֹת מִתּוֹרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gahl ey-NAHY ve-ah-BEE-tah neef-lah-OHT mee-toh-rah-TEH-khah

Open Thou mine eyes, that I may behold wondrous things out of Thy law.

### 119:19

גֵּר אָנֹכִי בָאָרֶץ אַל־תַּסְתֵּר מִמֶּנִּי מִצְוֺתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** GEHR ah-noh-KHEE vah-AH-rehts ahl tahs-TEHR mee-MEH-nee meets-voh-TEY-khah

I am a sojourner in the earth; hide not Thy commandments from me.

### 119:20

גָּרְסָה נַפְשִׁי לְתַאֲבָה אֶל־מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ בְכָל־עֵת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-re-SAH nahf-SHEE le-tah-ah-VAH ehl meesh-pah-TEY-khah ve-khohl EHT

My soul breaketh for the longing that it hath unto Thine ordinances at all times.

### 119:21

גָּעַרְתָּ זֵדִים אֲרוּרִים הַשֹּׁגִים מִמִּצְוֺתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-ahr-TAH zeh-DEEM ah-roo-REEM hah-shoh-GEEM mee-meets-voh-TEY-khah

Thou hast rebuked the proud that are cursed, that do err from Thy commandments.

### 119:22

גַּל מֵעָלַי חֶרְפָּה וָבוּז כִּי עֵדֹתֶיךָ נָצָרְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** GAHL meh-ah-LAHY khehr-PAH vah-VOOZ KEE eh-doh-TEY-khah nah-TSAH-re-tee

Take away from me reproach and contempt; for I have kept Thy testimonies.

### 119:23

גַּם יָשְׁבוּ שָׂרִים בִּי נִדְבָּרוּ עַבְדְּךָ יָשִׂיחַ בְּחֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** GAHM yah-she-VOO sah-REEM BEE need-BAH-roo ahv-de-KHAH yah-SEE-ahkh be-khoo-KEY-khah

Even though princes sit and talk against me, Thy servant doth meditate in Thy statutes.

### 119:24

גַּם־עֵדֹתֶיךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי אַנְשֵׁי עֲצָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gahm eh-doh-tey-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-AHY ahn-SHEY ah-tsah-TEE

Yea, Thy testimonies are my delight, they are my counsellors. DALETH.

### 119:25

דָּבְקָה לֶעָפָר נַפְשִׁי חַיֵּנִי כִּדְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-ve-KAH leh-ah-FAHR nahf-SHEE khah-YEH-nee keed-vah-REH-khah

My soul cleaveth unto the dust; quicken Thou me according to Thy word.

### 119:26

דְּרָכַי סִפַּרְתִּי וַתַּעֲנֵנִי לַמְּדֵנִי חֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** de-rah-KHAHY see-pahr-TEE vah-tah-ah-NEH-nee lah-me-DEH-nee khoo-KEY-khah

I told of my ways, and Thou didst answer me; teach me Thy statutes.

### 119:27

דֶּרֶךְ־פִּקּוּדֶיךָ הֲבִינֵנִי וְאָשִׂיחָה בְּנִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deh-rehkh pee-koo-DEY-khah hah-vee-NEH-nee ve-ah-SEE-khah be-neef-le-oh-TEY-khah

Make me to understand the way of Thy precepts, that I may talk of Thy wondrous works.

### 119:28

דָּלְפָה נַפְשִׁי מִתּוּגָה קַיְּמֵנִי כִּדְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-le-FAH nahf-SHEE mee-too-GAH kah-ye-MEH-nee keed-vah-REH-khah

My soul melteth away for heaviness; sustain me according unto Thy word.

### 119:29

דֶּרֶךְ־שֶׁקֶר הָסֵר מִמֶּנִּי וְתוֹרָתְךָ חָנֵּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deh-rehkh sheh-KEHR hah-SEHR mee-MEH-nee ve-toh-rah-te-KHAH khah-NEH-nee

Remove from me the way of falsehood; and grant me Thy law graciously.

### 119:30

דֶּרֶךְ־אֱמוּנָה בָחָרְתִּי מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ שִׁוִּיתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deh-rehkh eh-moo-NAH vah-KHAH-re-tee meesh-pah-TEY-khah shee-VEE-tee

I have chosen the way of faithfulness; Thine ordinances have I set [before me].

### 119:31

דָּבַקְתִּי בְעֵדְוֺתֶיךָ יְהוָה אַל־תְּבִישֵׁנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** dah-VAHK-tee ve-eh-de-voh-TEY-khah ah-doh-NAI ahl te-vee-SHEH-nee

I cleave unto Thy testimonies; O LORD, put me not to shame.

### 119:32

דֶּרֶךְ־מִצְוֺתֶיךָ אָרוּץ כִּי תַרְחִיב לִבִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** deh-rehkh meets-voh-TEY-khah ah-ROOTS KEE tahr-KHEEV lee-BEE

I will run the way of Thy commandments, For Thou dost enlarge my heart.

### 119:33

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-REH-nee ah-doh-NAI DEH-rehkh khoo-KEY-khah ve-eh-tse-REH-nah EH-kehv

### 119:34

הֲבִינֵנִי וְאֶצְּרָה תוֹרָתֶךָ וְאֶשְׁמְרֶנָּה בְכָל־לֵב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-vee-neh-NEE ve-eh-tse-RAH toh-rah-TEH-khah ve-ehsh-me-REH-nah ve-khohl LEHV

Give me understanding, that I keep Thy law and observe it with my whole heart.

### 119:35

**Pronunciation draft:** hahd-ree-kheh-NEE been-TEEV meets-voh-TEY-khah kee VOH khah-FAH-tse-tee

**Pronunciation draft:** haht lee-BEE ehl eh-de-voh-TEY-khah ve-AHL ehl BAH-tsah

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-ah-VEHR ey-NAHY meh-re-OHT SHAHV beed-rah-KHEH-khah khah-YEH-nee

### 119:38

### 119:39

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-ah-VEHR khehr-pah-TEE ah-SHEHR yah-GOH-re-tee KEE meesh-pah-TEY-khah toh-VEEM

### 119:40

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH tah-AHV-tee le-fee-koo-DEY-khah be-tseed-kah-te-KHAH khah-YEH-nee

### 119:41

וִיבֹאֻנִי חֲסָדֶךָ יְהוָה תְּשׁוּעָתְךָ כְּאִמְרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vee-voh-OO-nee khah-sah-DEH-khah ah-doh-NAI te-shoo-ah-te-KHAH ke-eem-rah-TEH-khah

### 119:42

וְאֶעֱנֶה חֹרְפִי דָבָר כִּי־בָטַחְתִּי בִּדְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-eh-eh-NEH khoh-re-FEE dah-VAHR kee vah-tahkh-TEE beed-vah-REH-khah

### 119:43

### 119:44

### 119:45

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-eht-hah-le-KHAH vah-re-khah-VAH KEE fee-koo-DEY-khah dah-RAH-she-tee

### 119:46

וַאֲדַבְּרָה בְעֵדֹתֶיךָ נֶגֶד מְלָכִים וְלֹא אֵבוֹשׁ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-ah-dah-be-RAH ve-eh-doh-tey-KHAH NEH-gehd me-lah-KHEEM ve-LOH eh-VOHSH

**Pronunciation draft:** ze-khohr dah-VAHR le-ahv-DEH-khah AHL ah-SHEHR yee-khahl-TAH-nee

**Pronunciation draft:** ZOHT neh-khah-mah-TEE ve-ohn-YEE KEE eem-rah-te-KHAH khee-YAH-te-nee

**Pronunciation draft:** zeh-DEEM heh-lee-TSOO-nee ahd me-OHD mee-toh-rah-te-KHAH LOH nah-TEE-tee

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-KHAHR-tee meesh-pah-TEY-khah meh-oh-LAHM  ah-doh-NAI vah-eht-neh-KHAHM

**Pronunciation draft:** TOHV ah-SEE-tah eem ahv-de-KHAH ah-doh-NAI keed-vah-REH-khah

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-FAHSH kah-KHEH-lehv lee-BAHM ah-NEE toh-rah-te-KHAH shee-ah-SHAH-e-tee

**Pronunciation draft:** tohv LEE toh-raht PEE-khah meh-ahl-FEY zah-HAHV vah-KHAH-sehf

### 119:74

### 119:75

### 119:76

### 119:77

### 119:78

### 119:79
