Mine eyes fail for Thy word, Saying: 'When wilt Thou comfort me?'

### 119:83

כִּי־הָיִיתִי כְּנֹאד בְּקִיטוֹר חֻקֶּיךָ לֹא שָׁכָחְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee hah-yee-TEE ke-NOHD be-kee-TOHR khoo-KEY-khah LOH shah-KHAH-khe-tee

For I am become like a wine-skin in the smoke; yet do I not forget Thy statutes.

### 119:84

כַּמָּה יְמֵי־עַבְדֶּךָ מָתַי תַּעֲשֶׂה בְרֹדְפַי מִשְׁפָּט׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-MAH ye-mey ahv-DEH-khah mah-TAHY tah-ah-SEH ve-roh-de-FAHY meesh-PAHT

How many are the days of Thy servant? When wilt Thou execute judgment on them that persecute me?

### 119:85

כָּרוּ־לִי זֵדִים שִׁיחוֹת אֲשֶׁר לֹא כְתוֹרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-roo LEE zeh-DEEM shee-KHOHT ah-SHEHR LOH khe-toh-rah-TEH-khah

The proud have digged pits for me, which is not according to Thy law.

### 119:86

כָּל־מִצְוֺתֶיךָ אֱמוּנָה שֶׁקֶר רְדָפוּנִי עָזְרֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kohl meets-voh-TEY-khah eh-moo-NAH SHEH-kehr re-dah-FOO-nee ah-ze-REH-nee

All Thy commandments are faithful; they persecute me for nought; help Thou me.

### 119:87

כִּמְעַט כִּלּוּנִי בָאָרֶץ וַאֲנִי לֹא־עָזַבְתִּי פִקֻּודֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** keem-AHT kee-LOO-nee vah-AH-rehts vah-ah-NEE loh ah-ZAHV-tee fee-koo-v-DEY-khah

They had almost consumed me upon earth; but as for me, I forsook not Thy precepts.

### 119:88

כְּחַסְדְּךָ חַיֵּנִי וְאֶשְׁמְרָה עֵדוּת פִּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-khahs-de-KHAH khah-YEH-nee ve-ehsh-me-RAH eh-DOOT PEE-khah

Quicken me after Thy lovingkindness, and I will observe the testimony of Thy mouth.

### 119:89

לְעוֹלָם יְהוָה דְּבָרְךָ נִצָּב בַּשָּׁמָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-oh-LAHM ah-doh-NAI de-vah-re-KHAH nee-TSAHV bah-shah-MAH-yeem

LAMED. For ever, O LORD, Thy word standeth fast in heaven.

### 119:90

לְדֹר וָדֹר אֱמוּנָתֶךָ כּוֹנַנְתָּ אֶרֶץ וַתַּעֲמֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-DOHR vah-DOHR eh-moo-nah-TEH-khah koh-NAHN-tah eh-REHTS vah-tah-ah-MOHD

Thy faithfulness is unto all generations; Thou hast established the earth, and it standeth.

### 119:91

לְמִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ עָמְדוּ הַיּוֹם כִּי הַכֹּל עֲבָדֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-meesh-pah-tey-KHAH ah-me-DOO hah-YOHM KEE hah-KOHL ah-vah-DEY-khah

They stand this day according to Thine ordinances; for all things are Thy servants.

### 119:92

לוּלֵי תוֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי אָז אָבַדְתִּי בְעָנְיִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loo-LEY toh-rah-te-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-AHY AHZ ah-VAHD-tee ve-ohn-YEE

Unless Thy law had been my delight, I should then have perished in mine affliction.

### 119:93

לְעוֹלָם לֹא־אֶשְׁכַּח פִּקּוּדֶיךָ כִּי בָם חִיִּיתָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-oh-LAHM loh ehsh-KAHKH pee-koo-DEY-khah KEE VAHM khee-yee-TAH-nee

I will never forget Thy precepts; for with them Thou hast quickened me.

### 119:94

לְךָ־אֲנִי הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ דָרָשְׁתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-khah ah-NEE hoh-shee-EH-nee KEE fee-koo-DEY-khah dah-RAH-she-tee

I am Thine, save me; for I have sought Thy precepts.

### 119:95

לִי קִוּוּ רְשָׁעִים לְאַבְּדֵנִי עֵדֹתֶיךָ אֶתְבּוֹנָן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** LEE kee-VOO re-shah-EEM le-ah-be-DEH-nee eh-doh-TEY-khah eht-boh-NAHN

The wicked have waited for me to destroy me; but I will consider Thy testimonies.

### 119:96

לְכָל תִּכְלָה רָאִיתִי קֵץ רְחָבָה מִצְוָתְךָ מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-khahl teekh-LAH rah-EE-tee KEHTS re-khah-VAH meets-vah-te-KHAH me-OHD

I have seen an end to every purpose; but Thy commandment is exceeding broad.

### 119:97

מָה־אָהַבְתִּי תוֹרָתֶךָ כָּל־הַיּוֹם הִיא שִׂיחָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mah ah-HAHV-tee toh-rah-TEH-khah kohl hah-YOHM HEE see-khah-TEE

MEM. O how love I Thy law! It is my meditation all the day.

### 119:98

מֵאֹיְבַי תְּחַכְּמֵנִי מִצְוֺתֶךָ כִּי לְעוֹלָם הִיא־לִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meh-OH-ye-VAHY te-khah-ke-MEH-nee meets-voh-TEH-khah KEE le-oh-LAHM hee LEE

Thy commandments make me wiser than mine enemies: For they are ever with me.

### 119:99

מִכָּל־מְלַמְּדַי הִשְׂכַּלְתִּי כִּי עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ שִׂיחָה לִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-kohl me-lah-me-DAHY hees-KAHL-tee KEE eh-de-voh-TEY-khah SEE-khah LEE

I have more understanding than all my teachers; For Thy testimonies are my meditation.

### 119:100

מִזְּקֵנִים אֶתְבּוֹנָן כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ נָצָרְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-ze-keh-NEEM eht-boh-NAHN KEE fee-koo-DEY-khah nah-TSAH-re-tee

I understand more than mine elders, because I have keep Thy precepts.

### 119:101

מִכָּל־אֹרַח רָע כָּלִאתִי רַגְלָי לְמַעַן אֶשְׁמֹר דְּבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-kohl OH-rahkh RAH kah-LEE-tee rahg-LAHY le-MAH-ahn ehsh-MOHR de-vah-REH-khah

I have refrained my feet from every evil way, in order that I might observe Thy word.

### 119:102

מִמִּשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לֹא־סָרְתִּי כִּי־אַתָּה הוֹרֵתָנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-meesh-pah-TEY-khah loh SAH-re-tee kee ah-TAH hoh-reh-TAH-nee

I have not turned aside from Thine ordinances; For Thou hast instructed me.

### 119:103

מַה־נִּמְלְצוּ לְחִכִּי אִמְרָתֶךָ מִדְּבַשׁ לְפִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahh neem-le-TSOO le-khee-KEE eem-rah-TEH-khah mee-de-VAHSH le-FEE

How sweet are Thy words unto my palate! Yea, sweeter than honey to my mouth!

### 119:104

מִפִּקּוּדֶיךָ אֶתְבּוֹנָן עַל־כֵּן שָׂנֵאתִי כָּל־אֹרַח שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mee-pee-koo-DEY-khah eht-boh-NAHN ahl KEHN sah-NEH-tee  kohl OH-rahkh SHAH-kehr

From Thy precepts I get understanding; Therefore I hate every false way.

### 119:105

נֵר־לְרַגְלִי דְבָרֶךָ וְאוֹר לִנְתִיבָתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nehr le-rahg-LEE de-vah-REH-khah ve-OHR leen-tee-vah-TEE

NUN. Thy word is a lamp unto my feet, And a light unto my path.

### 119:106

נִשְׁבַּעְתִּי וָאֲקַיֵּמָה לִשְׁמֹר מִשְׁפְּטֵי צִדְקֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neesh-BAH-tee vah-ah-kah-YEH-mah leesh-MOHR meesh-pe-TEY tseed-KEH-khah

I have sworn, and have confirmed it, To observe Thy righteous ordinances.

### 119:107

נַעֲנֵיתִי עַד־מְאֹד יְהוָה חַיֵּנִי כִדְבָרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-ah-NEY-tee ahd me-OHD ah-doh-NAI khah-YEH-nee kheed-vah-REH-khah

I am afflicted very much; Quicken me, O LORD, according unto Thy word.

### 119:108

נִדְבוֹת פִּי רְצֵה־נָא יְהוָה וּמִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לַמְּדֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** need-VOHT PEE re-tseh NAH ah-doh-NAI oo-meesh-pah-TEY-khah lah-me-DEH-nee

Accept, I beseech Thee, the freewill-offerings of my mouth, O LORD, And teach me Thine ordinances.

### 119:109

נַפְשִׁי בְכַפִּי תָמִיד וְתוֹרָתְךָ לֹא שָׁכָחְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nahf-SHEE ve-khah-PEE tah-MEED ve-toh-rah-te-KHAH LOH shah-KHAH-khe-tee

My soul is continually in my hand; yet have I not forgotten Thy law.

### 119:110

נָתְנוּ רְשָׁעִים פַּח לִי וּמִפִּקּוּדֶיךָ לֹא תָעִיתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-te-NOO re-shah-EEM PAHKH LEE oo-mee-pee-koo-DEY-khah LOH tah-EE-tee

The wicked have laid a snare for me; yet went I not astray from Thy precepts.

### 119:111

נָחַלְתִּי עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ לְעוֹלָם כִּי־שְׂשׂוֹן לִבִּי הֵמָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-KHAHL-tee eh-de-voh-TEY-khah le-oh-LAHM kee se-SOHN lee-BEE HEH-mah

Thy testimonies have I taken as a heritage for ever; for they are the rejoicing of my heart.

### 119:112

נָטִיתִי לִבִּי לַעֲשׂוֹת חֻקֶּיךָ לְעוֹלָם עֵקֶב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nah-TEE-tee lee-BEE lah-ah-SOHT khoo-KEY-khah le-oh-LAHM EH-kehv

I have inclined my heart to perform Thy statutes, for ever, at every step.

### 119:113

סֵעֲפִים שָׂנֵאתִי וְתוֹרָתְךָ אָהָבְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** seh-ah-FEEM sah-NEH-tee ve-toh-rah-te-KHAH ah-HAH-ve-tee

SAMECH. I hate them that are of a double mind; but Thy law do I love.

### 119:114

סִתְרִי וּמָגִנִּי אָתָּה לִדְבָרְךָ יִחָלְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** seet-REE oo-mah-gee-NEE AH-tah leed-vah-re-KHAH yee-KHAH-le-tee

Thou art my covert and my shield; in Thy word do I hope.

### 119:115

סוּרוּ־מִמֶּנִּי מְרֵעִים וְאֶצְּרָה מִצְוֺת אֱלֹהָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** soo-roo mee-MEH-nee me-reh-EEM ve-eh-tse-RAH meets-VOHT eh-loh-HAHY

Depart from me, ye evildoers; that I may keep the commandments of my God.

### 119:116

סָמְכֵנִי כְאִמְרָתְךָ וְאֶחְיֶה וְאַל־תְּבִישֵׁנִי מִשִּׂבְרִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-me-KHEH-nee khe-eem-rah-te-KHAH ve-eh-khe-YEH ve-ahl te-vee-SHEH-nee mee-seev-REE

Uphold me according unto Thy word, that I may live; and put me not to shame in my hope.

### 119:117

סְעָדֵנִי וְאִוָּשֵׁעָה וְאֶשְׁעָה בְחֻקֶּיךָ תָמִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** se-ah-DEH-nee ve-ee-vah-SHEH-ah ve-ehsh-AH ve-khoo-KEY-khah tah-MEED

Support Thou me, and I shall be saved; and I will occupy myself with Thy statutes continually.

### 119:118

סָלִיתָ כָּל־שׁוֹגִים מֵחֻקֶּיךָ כִּי־שֶׁקֶר תַּרְמִיתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-lee-TAH kohl shoh-GEEM meh-khoo-KEY-khah kee sheh-KEHR tahr-mee-TAHM

Thou hast made light of all them that err from Thy statutes; for their deceit is vain.

### 119:119

סִגִים הִשְׁבַּתָּ כָל־רִשְׁעֵי־אָרֶץ לָכֵן אָהַבְתִּי עֵדֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** see-GEEM heesh-BAH-tah khohl reesh-ey AH-rehts lah-KHEHN ah-HAHV-tee eh-doh-TEY-khah

Thou puttest away all the wicked of the earth like dross; therefore I love Thy testimonies.

### 119:120

סָמַר מִפַּחְדְּךָ בְשָׂרִי וּמִמִּשְׁפָּטֶיךָ יָרֵאתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sah-MAHR mee-pahkh-de-KHAH ve-sah-REE oo-mee-meesh-pah-TEY-khah yah-REH-tee

My flesh shuddereth for fear of Thee; And I am afraid of Thy judgments.

### 119:121

עָשִׂיתִי מִשְׁפָּט וָצֶדֶק בַּל־תַּנִּיחֵנִי לְעֹשְׁקָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-see-TEE meesh-PAHT vah-TSEH-dehk bahl tah-nee-KHEH-nee le-oh-she-KAHY

AIN. I have done justice and righteousness; Leave me not to mine oppressors.

### 119:122

עֲרֹב עַבְדְּךָ לְטוֹב אַל־יַעַשְׁקֻנִי זֵדִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ROHV ahv-de-KHAH le-TOHV ahl yah-ahsh-KOO-nee zeh-DEEM

Be surety for Thy servant for good; Let not the proud oppress me.

### 119:123

עֵינַי כָּלוּ לִישׁוּעָתֶךָ וּלְאִמְרַת צִדְקֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ey-NAHY kah-LOO lee-shoo-ah-TEH-khah oo-le-eem-RAHT tseed-KEH-khah

Mine eyes fail for Thy salvation, and for Thy righteous word.

### 119:124

עֲשֵׂה עִם־עַבְדְּךָ כְחַסְדֶּךָ וְחֻקֶּיךָ לַמְּדֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SEH eem ahv-de-KHAH khe-khahs-DEH-khah ve-khoo-KEY-khah lah-me-DEH-nee

Deal with Thy servant according unto Thy mercy, and teach me Thy statutes.

### 119:125

עַבְדְּךָ־אָנִי הֲבִינֵנִי וְאֵדְעָה עֵדֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahv-de-khah AH-nee hah-vee-NEH-nee ve-eh-de-AH eh-doh-TEY-khah

I am Thy servant, give me understanding, that I may know Thy testimonies.

### 119:126

עֵת לַעֲשׂוֹת לַיהוָה הֵפֵרוּ תּוֹרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EHT lah-ah-SOHT lah-ah-doh-NAI heh-FEH-roo toh-rah-TEH-khah

It is time for the LORD to work; They have made void Thy law.

### 119:127

עַל־כֵּן אָהַבְתִּי מִצְוֺתֶיךָ מִזָּהָב וּמִפָּז׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl KEHN ah-HAHV-tee meets-voh-TEY-khah mee-zah-HAHV oo-mee-PAHZ

Therefore I love Thy commandments Above gold, yea, above fine gold.

### 119:128

עַל־כֵּן כָּל־פִּקּוּדֵי כֹל יִשָּׁרְתִּי כָּל־אֹרַח שֶׁקֶר שָׂנֵאתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl KEHN  kohl pee-KOO-dey KHOHL yee-SHAH-re-tee kohl OH-rahkh SHEH-kehr sah-NEH-tee

Therefore I esteem all [Thy] precepts concerning all things to be right; Every false way I hate.

### 119:129

פְּלָאוֹת עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ עַל־כֵּן נְצָרָתַם נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-lah-OHT eh-de-voh-TEY-khah ahl KEHN ne-tsah-RAH-tahm nahf-SHEE

PE. Thy testimonies are wonderful; Therefore doth my soul keep them.

### 119:130

פֵּתַח דְּבָרֶיךָ יָאִיר מֵבִין פְּתָיִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** PEH-tahkh de-vah-REY-khah yah-EER meh-VEEN pe-tah-YEEM

The opening of Thy words giveth light; it giveth understanding unto the simple.

### 119:131

פִּי־פָעַרְתִּי וָאֶשְׁאָפָה כִּי לְמִצְוֺתֶיךָ יָאָבְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pee fah-ahr-TEE vah-ehsh-AH-fah KEE le-meets-voh-TEY-khah yah-AH-ve-tee

I opened wide my mouth, and panted; For I longed for Thy commandments.

### 119:132

פְּנֵה־אֵלַי וְחָנֵּנִי כְּמִשְׁפָּט לְאֹהֲבֵי שְׁמֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-neh eh-LAHY ve-khoh-NEH-nee ke-meesh-PAHT le-oh-hah-VEY she-MEH-khah

Turn Thee towards me, and be gracious unto me, As is Thy wont to do unto those that love Thy name.

### 119:133

פְּעָמַי הָכֵן בְּאִמְרָתֶךָ וְאַל־תַּשְׁלֶט־בִּי כָל־אָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-ah-MAHY hah-KHEHN be-eem-rah-TEH-khah ve-ahl tahsh-leht BEE khohl AH-vehn

Order my footsteps by Thy word; And let not any iniquity have dominion over me.

### 119:134

פְּדֵנִי מֵעֹשֶׁק אָדָם וְאֶשְׁמְרָה פִּקּוּדֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-deh-NEE meh-OH-shehk ah-DAHM ve-ehsh-me-RAH pee-koo-DEY-khah

Redeem me from the oppression of man, And I will observe Thy precepts.

### 119:135

פָּנֶיךָ הָאֵר בְּעַבְדֶּךָ וְלַמְּדֵנִי אֶת־חֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pah-ney-KHAH hah-EHR be-ahv-DEH-khah ve-lah-me-DEH-nee eht khoo-KEY-khah

Make Thy face to shine upon Thy servant; And teach me Thy statutes.

### 119:136

פַּלְגֵי־מַיִם יָרְדוּ עֵינָי עַל לֹא־שָׁמְרוּ תוֹרָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pahl-gey mah-YEEM yah-re-DOO ey-NAHY AHL loh shah-me-ROO toh-rah-TEH-khah

Mine eyes run down with rivers of water, Because they observe not Thy law.

### 119:137

צַדִּיק אַתָּה יְהוָה וְיָשָׁר מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsah-DEEK ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI ve-yah-SHAHR meesh-pah-TEY-khah

TZADE. Righteous art Thou, O LORD, And upright are Thy judgments.

### 119:138

צִוִּיתָ צֶדֶק עֵדֹתֶיךָ וֶאֱמוּנָה מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsee-vee-TAH TSEH-dehk eh-doh-TEY-khah veh-eh-moo-NAH me-OHD

Thou hast commanded Thy testimonies in righteousness and exceeding faithfulness.

### 119:139

צִמְּתַתְנִי קִנְאָתִי כִּי־שָׁכְחוּ דְבָרֶיךָ צָרָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsee-me-TAHT-nee keen-ah-TEE kee shah-khe-KHOO de-vah-REY-khah tsah-RAHY

My zeal hath undone me, because mine adversaries have forgotten Thy words.

### 119:140

צְרוּפָה אִמְרָתְךָ מְאֹד וְעַבְדְּךָ אֲהֵבָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tse-roo-FAH eem-rah-te-KHAH me-OHD ve-ahv-de-KHAH ah-heh-VAHH

Thy word is tried to the uttermost, and Thy servant loveth it.

### 119:141

צָעִיר אָנֹכִי וְנִבְזֶה פִּקֻּדֶיךָ לֹא שָׁכָחְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsah-EER ah-noh-KHEE ve-neev-ZEH pee-koo-DEY-khah LOH shah-KHAH-khe-tee

I am small and despised; yet have I not forgotten Thy precepts.

### 119:142

צִדְקָתְךָ צֶדֶק לְעוֹלָם וְתוֹרָתְךָ אֱמֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tseed-kah-te-KHAH TSEH-dehk le-oh-LAHM ve-toh-rah-te-KHAH eh-MEHT

Thy righteousness is an everlasting righteousness, and Thy law is truth.

### 119:143

צַר־וּמָצוֹק מְצָאוּנִי מִצְוֺתֶיךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tsahr oo-mah-TSOHK me-tsah-OO-nee meets-voh-TEY-khah shah-ah-shoo-AHY

Trouble and anguish have overtaken me; yet Thy commandments are my delight.

### 119:144

צֶדֶק עֵדְוֺתֶיךָ לְעוֹלָם הֲבִינֵנִי וְאֶחְיֶה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TSEH-dehk eh-de-voh-TEY-khah le-oh-LAHM hah-vee-NEH-nee ve-ehkh-YEH

Thy testimonies are righteous for ever; give me understanding, and I shall live.

### 119:145

קָרָאתִי בְכָל־לֵב עֲנֵנִי יְהוָה חֻקֶּיךָ אֶצֹּרָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-RAH-tee ve-khohl LEHV ah-NEH-nee ah-doh-NAI khoo-KEY-khah eh-TSOH-rah

### 119:146

קְרָאתִיךָ הוֹשִׁיעֵנִי וְאֶשְׁמְרָה עֵדֹתֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-rah-TEE-khah hoh-shee-EH-nee ve-ehsh-me-RAH eh-doh-TEY-khah

I have called Thee, save me, and I will observe Thy testimonies.

### 119:147

קִדַּמְתִּי בַנֶּשֶׁף וָאֲשַׁוֵּעָה לדבריך [לִדְבָרְךָ] יִחָלְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee-DAHM-tee vah-neh-SHEHF vah-ah-shah-VEH-ah leed-vah-re-KHAH yee-KHAH-le-tee

### 119:148

**Pronunciation draft:** kee-de-MOO ey-NAHY ahsh-moo-ROHT lah-SEE-ahkh be-eem-rah-TEH-khah

Mine eyes forestalled the night-watches, that I might meditate in Thy word.

### 119:149

קוֹלִי שִׁמְעָה כְחַסְדֶּךָ יְהוָה כְּמִשְׁפָּטֶךָ חַיֵּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-LEE sheem-AH khe-khahs-DEH-khah ah-doh-NAI ke-meesh-pah-TEH-khah khah-YEH-nee

Hear my voice according unto Thy lovingkindness; Quicken me, O LORD, as Thou art wont.

### 119:150

קָרְבוּ רֹדְפֵי זִמָּה מִתּוֹרָתְךָ רָחָקוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-re-VOO roh-de-FEY zee-MAH mee-toh-rah-te-KHAH rah-KHAH-koo

They draw nigh that follow after wickedness; they are far from Thy law.

### 119:151

קָרוֹב אַתָּה יְהוָה וְכָל־מִצְוֺתֶיךָ אֱמֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-ROHV ah-TAH ah-doh-NAI ve-khohl meets-voh-TEY-khah eh-MEHT

Thou art nigh, O LORD; and all Thy commandments are truth.

### 119:152

קֶדֶם יָדַעְתִּי מֵעֵדֹתֶיךָ כִּי לְעוֹלָם יְסַדְתָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEH-dehm yah-dah-TEE meh-eh-doh-TEY-khah KEE le-oh-LAHM ye-sahd-TAHM

Of old have I known from Thy testimonies that Thou hast founded them for ever.

### 119:153

רְאֵה־עָנְיִי וְחַלְּצֵנִי כִּי־תוֹרָתְךָ לֹא שָׁכָחְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** re-eh ohn-YEE ve-khah-le-TSEH-nee kee toh-rah-te-KHAH LOH shah-KHAH-khe-tee

RESH. O see mine affliction, and rescue me; For I do not forget Thy law.

### 119:154

רִיבָה רִיבִי וּגְאָלֵנִי לְאִמְרָתְךָ חַיֵּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ree-VAH ree-VEE oo-ge-ah-LEH-nee le-eem-rah-te-KHAH khah-YEH-nee

Plead Thou my cause, and redeem me; Quicken me according to Thy word.

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-KHOHK meh-re-shah-EEM ye-shoo-AH kee khoo-key-KHAH LOH dah-RAH-shoo

### 119:156

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-khah-MEY-khah rah-BEEM  ah-doh-NAI ke-meesh-pah-TEY-khah khah-YEH-nee

Great are Thy compassions, O LORD; Quicken me as Thou art wont.

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BEEM roh-de-FAHY ve-tsah-RAHY meh-eh-de-voh-TEY-khah LOH nah-TEE-tee

### 119:158

### 119:159

רְאֵה כִּי־פִקּוּדֶיךָ אָהָבְתִּי יְהוָה כְּחַסְדְּךָ חַיֵּנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** re-EH kee fee-koo-DEY-khah ah-HAH-ve-tee ah-doh-NAI ke-khahs-de-KHAH khah-YEH-nee

### 119:160

**Pronunciation draft:** rohsh de-vah-re-KHAH eh-MEHT oo-le-oh-LAHM kohl meesh-PAHT tseed-KEH-khah

### 119:161

שָׂרִים רְדָפוּנִי חִנָּם ומדבריך [וּמִדְּבָרְךָ] פָּחַד לִבִּי׃

SCHIN. Princes have persecuted me without a cause; But my heart standeth in awe of Thy words.

**Pronunciation draft:** SAHS ah-noh-KHEE ahl eem-rah-TEH-khah ke-moh-TSEH shah-LAHL RAHV

### 119:163

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEH-kehr sah-neh-TEE vah-ah-tah-EH-vah toh-rah-te-KHAH ah-HAH-ve-tee

I hate and abhor falsehood; Thy law do I love. .

### 119:164

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEH-vah bah-YOHM hee-lahl-TEE-khah AHL meesh-pe-TEY tseed-KEH-khah

Seven times a day do I praise Thee, because of Thy righteous ordinances.

שָׁלוֹם רָב לְאֹהֲבֵי תוֹרָתֶךָ וְאֵין־לָמוֹ מִכְשׁוֹל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-LOHM RAHV le-oh-hah-VEY toh-rah-TEH-khah ve-eyn LAH-moh meekh-SHOHL

Great peace have they that love Thy law; And there is no stumbling for them.

### 119:166

שִׂבַּרְתִּי לִישׁוּעָתְךָ יְהוָה וּמִצְוֺתֶיךָ עָשִׂיתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** see-BAHR-tee lee-shoo-ah-te-KHAH ah-doh-NAI oo-meets-voh-TEY-khah ah-SEE-tee

I have hoped for Thy salvation, O LORD, And have done Thy commandments.

### 119:167

שָׁמְרָה נַפְשִׁי עֵדֹתֶיךָ וָאֹהֲבֵם מְאֹד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-me-RAH nahf-SHEE eh-doh-TEY-khah vah-oh-hah-VEHM me-OHD

My soul hath observed Thy testimonies; And I love them exceedingly.

### 119:168

שָׁמַרְתִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ וְעֵדֹתֶיךָ כִּי כָל־דְּרָכַי נֶגְדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-MAHR-tee fee-koo-dey-KHAH ve-eh-doh-TEY-khah KEE khohl de-rah-KHAHY nehg-DEH-khah

I have observed Thy precepts and Thy testimonies; For all my ways are before Thee.

### 119:169

תִּקְרַב רִנָּתִי לְפָנֶיךָ יְהוָה כִּדְבָרְךָ הֲבִינֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** teek-RAHV ree-nah-TEE le-fah-NEY-khah ah-doh-NAI keed-vah-re-KHAH hah-vee-NEH-nee

TAV. Let my cry come near before Thee, O LORD; Give me understanding according to Thy word.

### 119:170

תָּבוֹא תְּחִנָּתִי לְפָנֶיךָ כְּאִמְרָתְךָ הַצִּילֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-VOH te-khee-nah-TEE le-fah-NEY-khah ke-eem-rah-te-KHAH hah-tsee-LEH-nee

Let my supplication come before Thee; deliver me according to Thy word.

### 119:171

תַּבַּעְנָה שְׂפָתַי תְּהִלָּה כִּי תְלַמְּדֵנִי חֻקֶּיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-BAH-nah se-fah-TAHY te-hee-LAH KEE te-lah-me-DEH-nee khoo-KEY-khah

Let my lips utter praise: Because Thou teachest me Thy statutes.

### 119:172

תַּעַן לְשׁוֹנִי אִמְרָתֶךָ כִּי כָל־מִצְוֺתֶיךָ צֶּדֶק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** TAH-ahn le-shoh-NEE eem-rah-TEH-khah KEE khohl meets-voh-TEY-khah TSEH-dehk

Let my tongue sing of Thy word; For all Thy commandments are righteousness.

### 119:173

תְּהִי־יָדְךָ לְעָזְרֵנִי כִּי פִקּוּדֶיךָ בָחָרְתִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-hee yah-de-KHAH le-ah-ze-REH-nee KEE fee-koo-DEY-khah vah-KHAH-re-tee

Let Thy hand be ready to help me; For I have chosen Thy precepts.

### 119:174

תָּאַבְתִּי לִישׁוּעָתְךָ יְהוָה וְתוֹרָתְךָ שַׁעֲשֻׁעָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-AHV-tee lee-shoo-ah-te-KHAH ah-doh-NAI ve-toh-rah-te-KHAH shah-ah-shoo-AHY

I have longed for Thy salvation, O LORD; And Thy law is my delight.

### 119:175

תְּחִי־נַפְשִׁי וּתְהַלְלֶךָּ וּמִשְׁפָּטֶךָ יַעֲזְרֻנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-khee nahf-SHEE oo-te-hah-le-LEH-kah oo-meesh-pah-TEH-khah yah-ahz-ROO-nee

### 119:176

## Psalm 120 — Distress over deception and conflict

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.120) · Buckets: relationships, peace

### 120:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת אֶל־יְהוָה בַּצָּרָתָה לִּי קָרָאתִי וַיַּעֲנֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT ehl ah-doh-NAI bah-tsah-RAH-tah LEE kah-RAH-tee vah-yah-ah-NEH-nee

A Song of Ascents. In my distress I called unto the LORD, And He answered me.

### 120:2

יְהוָה הַצִּילָה נַפְשִׁי מִשְּׂפַת־שֶׁקֶר מִלָּשׁוֹן רְמִיָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI hah-TSEE-lah nahf-SHEE mee-se-faht SHEH-kehr mee-lah-SHOHN re-mee-YAH

O LORD, deliver my soul from lying lips, From a deceitful tongue.

### 120:3

מַה־יִּתֵּן לְךָ וּמַה־יֹּסִיף לָךְ לָשׁוֹן רְמִיָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahh yee-TEHN le-KHAH oo-mahh yoh-SEEF LAHKH lah-SHOHN re-mee-YAH

What shall be given unto thee, and what shall be done more unto thee, Thou deceitful tongue?

### 120:4

חִצֵּי גִבּוֹר שְׁנוּנִים עִם גַּחֲלֵי רְתָמִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khee-TSEH gee-BOHR she-noo-NEEM EEM gah-khah-LEY re-tah-MEEM

Sharp arrows of the mighty, With coals of broom.

### 120:5

אוֹיָה־לִי כִּי־גַרְתִּי מֶשֶׁךְ שָׁכַנְתִּי עִם־אָהֳלֵי קֵדָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-yah LEE kee GAHR-tee MEH-shehkh shah-KHAHN-tee eem oh-hoh-LEY keh-DAHR

Woe is me, that I sojourn with Meshech, That I dwell beside the tents of Kedar!

### 120:6

רַבַּת שָׁכְנָה־לָּהּ נַפְשִׁי עִם שׂוֹנֵא שָׁלוֹם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BAHT shah-khe-nah LAHH nahf-SHEE EEM soh-NEH shah-LOHM

My soul hath full long had her dwelling With him that hateth peace.

### 120:7

אֲ‍נִי־שָׁלוֹם וְכִי אֲדַבֵּר הֵמָּה לַמִּלְחָמָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-nee ‍shah-LOHM ve-KHEE ah-dah-BEHR heh-MAH lah-meel-khah-MAH

I am all peace; But when I speak, they are for war.

## Psalm 121 — Help and guarding on the way

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.121) · Buckets: travel, protection, health

### 121:1

שִׁיר לַמַּעֲלוֹת אֶשָּׂא עֵינַי אֶל־הֶהָרִים מֵאַיִן יָבֹא עֶזְרִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER lah-mah-ah-LOHT eh-SAH ey-NAHY ehl heh-hah-REEM meh-AH-yeen yah-VOH ehz-REE

A Song of Ascents. I will lift up mine eyes unto the mountains: From whence shall my help come?

### 121:2

עֶזְרִי מֵעִם יְהוָה עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehz-REE meh-EEM ah-doh-NAI oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-rehts

My help cometh from the LORD, Who made heaven and earth.

### 121:3

אַל־יִתֵּן לַמּוֹט רַגְלֶךָ אַל־יָנוּם שֹׁמְרֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl yee-TEHN lah-MOHT rahg-LEH-khah ahl yah-NOOM shoh-me-REH-khah

He will not suffer thy foot to be moved; He that keepeth thee will not slumber.

### 121:4

הִנֵּה לֹא־יָנוּם וְלֹא יִישָׁן שׁוֹמֵר יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH loh yah-NOOM ve-LOH yee-SHAHN shoh-MEHR yees-rah-EHL

Behold, He that keepeth Israel Doth neither slumber nor sleep.

### 121:5

יְהוָה שֹׁמְרֶךָ יְהוָה צִלְּךָ עַל־יַד יְמִינֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI shoh-me-REH-khah ah-doh-NAI tsee-le-KHAH ahl YAHD ye-mee-NEH-khah

The LORD is thy keeper; The LORD is thy shade upon thy right hand.

### 121:6

יוֹמָם הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ לֹא־יַכֶּכָּה וְיָרֵחַ בַּלָּיְלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-MAHM hah-SHEH-mehsh loh yah-KEH-kah ve-yah-REH-ahkh bah-LAH-ye-lah

The sun shall not smite thee by day, Nor the moon by night.

### 121:7

יְהוָה יִשְׁמָרְךָ מִכָּל־רָע יִשְׁמֹר אֶת־נַפְשֶׁךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI yeesh-mah-re-KHAH mee-kohl RAH yeesh-MOHR eht nahf-SHEH-khah

The LORD shall keep thee from all evil; He shall keep thy soul.

### 121:8

יְהוָה יִשְׁמָר־צֵאתְךָ וּבוֹאֶךָ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד־עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI yeesh-mohr tseh-te-KHAH oo-voh-EH-khah meh-ah-TAH ve-ahd oh-LAHM

The LORD shall guard thy going out and thy coming in, From this time forth and for ever.

## Psalm 122 — Jerusalem, pilgrimage, and peace

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.122) · Buckets: jerusalem, peace

### 122:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת לְדָוִד שָׂמַחְתִּי בְּאֹמְרִים לִי בֵּית יְהוָה נֵלֵךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT le-dah-VEED sah-mahkh-TEE be-oh-me-REEM LEE BEYT ah-doh-NAI neh-LEHKH

A Song of Ascents; of David. I rejoiced when they said unto me: 'Let us go unto the house of the LORD.'

### 122:2

עֹמְדוֹת הָיוּ רַגְלֵינוּ בִּשְׁעָרַיִךְ יְרוּשָׁלִָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oh-me-DOHT hah-YOO rahg-LEY-noo beesh-ah-RAH-yeekh ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem

Our feet are standing within thy gates, O Jerusalem;

### 122:3

יְרוּשָׁלִַם הַבְּנוּיָה כְּעִיר שֶׁחֻבְּרָה־לָּהּ יַחְדָּו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem hah-be-noo-YAH ke-EER sheh-khoo-be-rah LAHH yahkh-DAHV

Jerusalem, that art builded as a city that is compact together;

### 122:4

שֶׁשָּׁם עָלוּ שְׁבָטִים שִׁבְטֵי־יָהּ עֵדוּת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְהֹדוֹת לְשֵׁם יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheh-SHAHM ah-LOO she-vah-TEEM sheev-tey YAHH eh-DOOT le-yees-rah-EHL le-hoh-DOHT le-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI

Whither the tribes went up, even the tribes of the LORD, as a testimony unto Israel, to give thanks unto the name of the LORD.

### 122:5

כִּי שָׁמָּה יָשְׁבוּ כִסְאוֹת לְמִשְׁפָּט כִּסְאוֹת לְבֵית דָּוִיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE SHAH-mah  yah-she-VOO khees-OHT le-meesh-PAHT kees-OHT le-VEYT dah-VEED

For there were set thrones for judgment, the thrones of the house of David.

### 122:6

שַׁאֲלוּ שְׁלוֹם יְרוּשָׁלִָם יִשְׁלָיוּ אֹהֲבָיִךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-ah-LOO she-LOHM ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem yeesh-LAH-yoo oh-hah-VAH-yeekh

Pray for the peace of Jerusalem; May they prosper that love thee.

### 122:7

יְהִי־שָׁלוֹם בְּחֵילֵךְ שַׁלְוָה בְּאַרְמְנוֹתָיִךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-hee shah-LOHM be-khey-LEHKH shahl-VAH be-ahr-me-noh-TAH-yeekh

Peace be within thy walls, and prosperity within thy palaces.

### 122:8

לְמַעַן אַחַי וְרֵעָי אֲדַבְּרָה־נָּא שָׁלוֹם בָּךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-mah-AHN ah-KHAHY ve-reh-AHY ah-dah-be-rah NAH shah-LOHM BAHKH

For my brethren and companions' sakes, I will now say: 'Peace be within thee.'

### 122:9

לְמַעַן בֵּית־יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲבַקְשָׁה טוֹב לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-mah-AHN beyt ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo ah-vahk-SHAH TOHV LAHKH

## Psalm 123 — Mercy in the face of contempt

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.123) · Buckets: wellness, justice

### 123:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת אֵלֶיךָ נָשָׂאתִי אֶת־עֵינַי הַיֹּשְׁבִי בַּשָּׁמָיִם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT eh-ley-KHAH nah-SAH-tee eht ey-NAHY hah-yoh-she-VEE bah-shah-MAH-yeem

A Song of Ascents. Unto Thee I lift up mine eyes, O Thou that art enthroned in the heavens.

### 123:2

הִנֵּה כְעֵינֵי עֲבָדִים אֶל־יַד אֲ‍דוֹנֵיהֶם כְּעֵינֵי שִׁפְחָה אֶל־יַד גְּבִרְתָּהּ כֵּן עֵינֵינוּ אֶל־יְהוָה אֱלֹהֵינוּ עַד שֶׁיְּחָנֵּנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hee-NEH khe-ey-NEY ah-vah-DEEM ehl YAHD ah-doh-ney-HEHM‍ ke-ey-NEY sheef-KHAH ehl YAHD ge-veer-TAHH KEHN ey-ney-NOO ehl ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HEY-noo AHD sheh-ye-khah-NEH-noo

Behold, as the eyes of servants unto the hand of their master, As the eyes of a maiden unto the hand of her mistress; So our eyes look unto the LORD our God, Until He be gracious unto us.

### 123:3

חָנֵּנוּ יְהוָה חָנֵּנוּ כִּי־רַב שָׂבַעְנוּ בוּז׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-NEH-noo ah-doh-NAI khah-NEH-noo kee RAHV sah-VAH-noo VOOZ

Be gracious unto us, O LORD, be gracious unto us; For we are full sated with contempt.

### 123:4

רַבַּת שָׂבְעָה־לָּהּ נַפְשֵׁנוּ הַלַּעַג הַשַּׁאֲנַנִּים הַבּוּז לִגְאֵיוֹנִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** rah-BAHT sah-ve-ah LAHH nahf-SHEH-noo hah-LAH-ahg hah-shah-ah-nah-NEEM hah-BOOZ leeg-EH-yoh-NEEM

Our soul is full sated With the scorning of those that are at ease, And with the contempt of the proud oppressors.

## Psalm 124 — Collective rescue

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.124) · Buckets: protection, gratitude

### 124:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת לְדָוִד לוּלֵי יְהוָה שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ יֹאמַר־נָא יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT le-dah-VEED loo-LEY ah-doh-NAI sheh-HAH-yah LAH-noo yoh-mahr NAH yees-rah-EHL

A Song of Ascents; of David. 'If it had not been the LORD who was for us', Let Israel now say;

### 124:2

לוּלֵי יְהוָה שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ בְּקוּם עָלֵינוּ אָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** loo-LEY ah-doh-NAI sheh-HAH-yah LAH-noo be-KOOM ah-LEY-noo ah-DAHM

'If it had not been the LORD who was for us, When men rose up against us,

### 124:3

אֲזַי חַיִּים בְּלָעוּנוּ בַּחֲרוֹת אַפָּם בָּנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ZAHY khah-YEEM be-lah-OO-noo bah-khah-ROHT ah-PAHM BAH-noo

Then they had swallowed us up alive, when their wrath was kindled against us;

### 124:4

אֲזַי הַמַּיִם שְׁטָפוּנוּ נַחְלָה עָבַר עַל־נַפְשֵׁנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ZAHY hah-MAH-yeem she-tah-FOO-noo NAHKH-lah ah-VAHR ahl nahf-SHEH-noo

Then the waters had overwhelmed us, the stream had gone over our soul;

### 124:5

אֲזַי עָבַר עַל־נַפְשֵׁנוּ הַמַּיִם הַזֵּידוֹנִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-ZAHY ah-VAHR ahl nahf-SHEH-noo hah-MAH-yeem hah-zey-doh-NEEM

Then the proud waters Had gone over our soul.'

### 124:6

בָּרוּךְ יְהוָה שֶׁלֹּא נְתָנָנוּ טֶרֶף לְשִׁנֵּיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** bah-ROOKH ah-doh-NAI sheh-LOH ne-tah-NAH-noo teh-REHF le-shee-ney-HEHM

Blessed be the LORD, who hath not given us as a prey to their teeth.

### 124:7

נַפְשֵׁנוּ כְּצִפּוֹר נִמְלְטָה מִפַּח יוֹקְשִׁים הַפַּח נִשְׁבָּר וַאֲנַחְנוּ נִמְלָטְנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** nahf-SHEH-noo ke-tsee-POHR neem-le-TAH mee-PAHKH yoh-ke-SHEEM hah-PAHKH neesh-BAHR vah-ah-NAHKH-noo neem-LAH-te-noo

Our soul is escaped as a bird out of the snare of the fowlers; The snare is broken, and we are escaped.

### 124:8

עֶזְרֵנוּ בְּשֵׁם יְהוָה עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehz-reh-NOO be-SHEHM ah-doh-NAI oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-rehts

Our help is in the name of the LORD, Who made heaven and earth.

## Psalm 125 — Steadfast trust and peace for Israel

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.125) · Buckets: protection, peace

### 125:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת הַבֹּטְחִים בַּיהוָה כְּהַר־צִיּוֹן לֹא־יִמּוֹט לְעוֹלָם יֵשֵׁב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT hah-boh-te-KHEEM bah-ah-doh-NAI ke-hahr tsee-YOHN loh yee-MOHT le-oh-LAHM yeh-SHEHV

A Song of Ascents. They that trust in the LORD Are as mount Zion, which cannot be moved, but abideth for ever.

### 125:2

יְרוּשָׁלִַם הָרִים סָבִיב לָהּ וַיהוָה סָבִיב לְעַמּוֹ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד־עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-roo-shah-LAH-eem hah-REEM sah-VEEV LAHH vah-ah-doh-NAI sah-VEEV le-ah-MOH meh-ah-TAH ve-ahd oh-LAHM

As the mountains are round about Jerusalem, So the LORD is round about His people, From this time forth and for ever.

### 125:3

כִּי לֹא יָנוּחַ שֵׁבֶט הָרֶשַׁע עַל גּוֹרַל הַצַּדִּיקִים לְמַעַן לֹא־יִשְׁלְחוּ הַצַּדִּיקִים בְּעַוְלָתָה יְדֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE LOH yah-NOO-ahkh SHEH-veht hah-REH-shah AHL goh-RAHL hah-tsah-dee-KEEM le-MAH-ahn loh yeesh-le-KHOO hah-tsah-dee-KEEM be-ahv-LAH-tah ye-dey-HEHM

For the rod of wickedness shall not rest upon the lot of the righteous; That the righteous put not forth their hands unto iniquity.

### 125:4

הֵיטִיבָה יְהוָה לַטּוֹבִים וְלִישָׁרִים בְּלִבּוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hey-TEE-vah ah-doh-NAI lah-toh-VEEM ve-lee-shah-REEM be-lee-boh-TAHM

Do good, O LORD, unto the good, And to them that are upright in their hearts.

### 125:5

וְהַמַּטִּים עַקַלְקַלּוֹתָם יוֹלִיכֵם יְהוָה אֶת־פֹּעֲלֵי הָאָוֶן שָׁלוֹם עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-hah-mah-TEEM ah-kahl-kah-loh-TAHM yoh-lee-KHEHM ah-doh-NAI eht poh-ah-LEY hah-AH-vehn shah-LOHM ahl yees-rah-EHL

But as for such as turn aside unto their crooked ways, The LORD will lead them away with the workers of iniquity. Peace be upon Israel.

## Psalm 126 — Restoration, tears, and joy

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.126) · Buckets: hope, gratitude

### 126:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת בְּשׁוּב יְהוָה אֶת־שִׁיבַת צִיּוֹן הָיִינוּ כְּחֹלְמִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** SHEER hah-mah-ah-LOHT be-SHOOV ah-doh-NAI eht shee-VAHT tsee-YOHN hah-YEE-noo ke-khoh-le-MEEM

A Song of Ascents. When the LORD brought back those that returned to Zion, We were like unto them that dream.

### 126:2

אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם הִגְדִּיל יְהוָה לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם־אֵלֶּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHZ yee-mah-LEH se-KHOHK pee-NOO oo-le-shoh-NEH-noo ree-NAH AHZ yoh-me-ROO vah-goh-YEEM heeg-DEEL ah-doh-NAI lah-ah-SOHT eem EH-leh

### 126:3

הִגְדִּיל יְהוָה לַעֲשׂוֹת עִמָּנוּ הָיִינוּ שְׂמֵחִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** heeg-DEEL ah-doh-NAI lah-ah-SOHT ee-MAH-noo hah-YEE-noo se-meh-KHEEM

The LORD hath done great things with us; we are rejoiced.

### 126:4

שׁוּבָה יְהוָה אֶת־שבותנו [שְׁבִיתֵנוּ] כַּאֲפִיקִים בַּנֶּגֶב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shoo-VAH ah-doh-NAI ehsh-vee-TEH-noo kah-ah-fee-KEEM bah-NEH-gehv

### 126:5

הַזֹּרְעִים בְּדִמְעָה בְּרִנָּה יִקְצֹרוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-zoh-re-EEM be-deem-AH be-ree-NAH yeek-TSOH-roo

They that sow in tears Shall reap in joy.

### 126:6

הָלוֹךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָכֹה נֹשֵׂא מֶשֶׁךְ־הַזָּרַע בֹּא־יָבוֹא בְרִנָּה נֹשֵׂא אֲלֻמֹּתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-LOHKH yeh-LEHKH  oo-vah-KHOHH noh-SEH meh-shehkh hah-ZAH-rah BOH yah-VOH ve-ree-NAH noh-SEH ah-loo-moh-TAHV

Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves.

## Psalm 127 — Building, labor, rest, and children

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.127) · Buckets: opportunity, family, sleep

### 127:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת לִשְׁלֹמֹה אִם־יְהוָה לֹא־יִבְנֶה בַיִת שָׁוְא עָמְלוּ בוֹנָיו בּוֹ אִם־יְהוָה לֹא־יִשְׁמָר־עִיר שָׁוְא שָׁקַד שׁוֹמֵר׃

שָׁוְא לָכֶם מַשְׁכִּימֵי קוּם מְאַחֲרֵי־שֶׁבֶת אֹכְלֵי לֶחֶם הָעֲצָבִים כֵּן יִתֵּן לִידִידוֹ שֵׁנָא׃

### 127:3

הִנֵּה נַחֲלַת יְהוָה בָּנִים שָׂכָר פְּרִי הַבָּטֶן׃

As arrows in the hand of a mighty man, So are the children of one's youth.

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY hah-GEH-vehr ah-SHEHR mee-LEH eht ahsh-pah-TOH meh-HEHM loh yeh-VOH-shoo kee ye-dah-be-ROO eht oh-ye-VEEM bah-SHAH-ahr

### 128:1

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-re-eh vah-NEEM le-vah-NEY-khah shah-LOHM ahl yees-rah-EHL

## Psalm 132 — Covenant, Zion, and a resting place

### 132:1

**Pronunciation draft:** koo-MAH ah-doh-NAI leem-noo-khah-TEH-khah ah-TAH vah-ah-ROHN oo-ZEH-khah

### 132:11

## Psalm 133 — The goodness of people dwelling together

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.133) · Buckets: relationships, peace

### 133:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת לְדָוִד הִנֵּה מַה־טּוֹב וּמַה־נָּעִים שֶׁבֶת אַחִים גַּם־יָחַד׃

A Song of Ascents; of David. Behold, how good and how pleasant it is For brethren to dwell together in unity!

### 133:2

כַּשֶּׁמֶן הַטּוֹב עַל־הָרֹאשׁ יֹרֵד עַל־הַזָּקָן זְקַן־אַהֲרֹן שֶׁיֹּרֵד עַל־פִּי מִדּוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-SHEH-mehn hah-TOHV  ahl hah-ROHSH yoh-REHD ahl hah-zah-KAHN ze-kahn ah-hah-ROHN sheh-yoh-REHD ahl PEE mee-doh-TAHV

It is like the precious oil upon the head, Coming down upon the beard; Even Aaron's beard, That cometh down upon the collar of his garments;

### 133:3

כְּטַל־חֶרְמוֹן שֶׁיֹּרֵד עַל־הַרְרֵי צִיּוֹן כִּי שָׁם צִוָּה יְהוָה אֶת־הַבְּרָכָה חַיִּים עַד־הָעוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-tahl khehr-MOHN sheh-yoh-REHD ahl hahr-REY tsee-YOHN KEE SHAHM  tsee-VAH ah-doh-NAI eht hah-be-rah-KHAH khah-YEEM ahd hah-oh-LAHM

Like the dew of Hermon, That cometh down upon the mountains of Zion; For there the LORD commanded the blessing, Even life for ever.

## Psalm 134 — Blessing in the night

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.134) · Buckets: devotion, sleep

### 134:1

שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת הִנֵּה בָּרֲכוּ אֶת־יְהוָה כָּל־עַבְדֵי יְהוָה הָעֹמְדִים בְּבֵית־יְהוָה בַּלֵּילוֹת׃

A Song of Ascents. Behold, bless ye the LORD, all ye servants of the LORD, That stand in the house of the LORD in the night seasons.

### 134:2

שְׂאוּ־יְדֵכֶם קֹדֶשׁ וּבָרֲכוּ אֶת־יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** se-oo ye-deh-KHEHM KOH-dehsh oo-vah-rah-KHOO eht ah-doh-NAI

Lift up your hands to the sanctuary, And bless ye the LORD.

### 134:3

יְבָרֶכְךָ יְהוָה מִצִּיּוֹן עֹשֵׂה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-vah-rehkh-KHAH ah-doh-NAI mee-tsee-YOHN oh-SEH shah-MAH-yeem vah-AH-rehts

## Psalm 135 — Praise and communal memory

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.135) · Buckets: gratitude, community

### 135:1

הַלְלוּ יָהּ הַלְלוּ אֶת־שֵׁם יְהוָה הַלְלוּ עַבְדֵי יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HAH-le-loo YAHH  hah-le-LOO eht SHEHM ah-doh-NAI hah-le-LOO ahv-DEY ah-doh-NAI

Hallelujah. Praise ye the name of the LORD; Give praise, O ye servants of the LORD,

### 135:2

שֶׁעֹמְדִים בְּבֵית יְהוָה בְּחַצְרוֹת בֵּית אֱלֹהֵינוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheh-oh-me-DEEM be-VEYT ah-doh-NAI be-khahts-ROHT BEYT eh-loh-HEY-noo

Ye that stand in the house of the LORD, In the courts of the house of our God.

הַלְלוּ־יָהּ כִּי־טוֹב יְהוָה זַמְּרוּ לִשְׁמוֹ כִּי נָעִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-LOO YAHH kee TOHV ah-doh-NAI zah-me-ROO leesh-MOH KEE nah-EEM

Praise ye the LORD, for the LORD is good; Sing praises unto His name, for it is pleasant.

### 135:4

כִּי־יַעֲקֹב בָּחַר לוֹ יָהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לִסְגֻלָּתוֹ׃

For the LORD hath chosen Jacob unto Himself, And Israel for His own treasure.

### 135:5

כִּי אֲנִי יָדַעְתִּי כִּי־גָדוֹל יְהוָה וַאֲדֹנֵינוּ מִכָּל־אֱלֹהִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE ah-NEE yah-dah-TEE kee gah-DOHL ah-doh-NAI vah-ah-doh-NEY-noo mee-kohl eh-loh-HEEM

For I know that the LORD is great, And that our Lord is above all gods.

### 135:6

כֹּל אֲשֶׁר־חָפֵץ יְהוָה עָשָׂה בַּשָּׁמַיִם וּבָאָרֶץ בַּיַּמִּים וְכָל־תְּהוֹמוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOHL ah-shehr khah-FEHTS ah-doh-NAI ah-SAH bah-shah-MAH-yeem oo-vah-AH-rehts bah-yah-MEEM ve-khohl te-hoh-MOHT

Whatsoever the LORD pleased, that hath He done, In heaven and in earth, in the seas and in all deeps;

### 135:7

מַעֲלֶה נְשִׂאִים מִקְצֵה הָאָרֶץ בְּרָקִים לַמָּטָר עָשָׂה מוֹצֵא־רוּחַ מֵאוֹצְרוֹתָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mah-ah-LEH ne-see-EEM meek-TSEH hah-AH-rehts be-rah-KEEM lah-mah-TAHR ah-SAH moh-tseh roo-AHKH meh-oh-tse-roh-TAHV

Who causeth the vapours to ascend from the ends of the earth; He maketh lightnings for the rain; He bringeth forth the wind out of His treasuries.

### 135:8

שֶׁהִכָּה בְּכוֹרֵי מִצְרָיִם מֵאָדָם עַד־בְּהֵמָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheh-hee-KAH be-khoh-REY meets-RAH-yeem meh-ah-DAHM ahd be-heh-MAH

Who smote the first-born of Egypt, Both of man and beast.

### 135:9

שָׁלַח אֹתוֹת וּמֹפְתִים בְּתוֹכֵכִי מִצְרָיִם בְּפַרְעֹה וּבְכָל־עֲבָדָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-LAHKH  oh-TOHT oo-moh-fe-TEEM be-toh-KHEH-khee meets-RAH-yeem be-fahr-OHH oo-ve-khohl ah-vah-DAHV

### 135:10

שֶׁהִכָּה גּוֹיִם רַבִּים וְהָרַג מְלָכִים עֲצוּמִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** sheh-hee-KAH goh-YEEM rah-BEEM ve-hah-RAHG me-lah-KHEEM ah-tsoo-MEEM

Who smote many nations, And slew mighty kings:

### 135:11

לְסִיחוֹן מֶלֶךְ הָאֱמֹרִי וּלְעוֹג מֶלֶךְ הַבָּשָׁן וּלְכֹל מַמְלְכוֹת כְּנָעַן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-see-KHOHN  MEH-lehkh hah-eh-moh-REE oo-le-OHG MEH-lehkh hah-bah-SHAHN oo-le-KHOHL mahm-le-KHOHT ke-NAH-ahn

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-nah-TAHN ahr-TSAHM nah-khah-LAH nah-khah-LAH le-yees-rah-EHL ah-MOH

### 135:13

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI sheem-KHAH le-oh-LAHM ah-doh-NAI zeekh-re-KHAH le-dohr vah-DOHR

O LORD, Thy name endureth for ever; Thy memorial, O LORD, throughout all generations.

### 135:14

כִּי־יָדִין יְהוָה עַמּוֹ וְעַל־עֲבָדָיו יִתְנֶחָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee yah-DEEN ah-doh-NAI ah-MOH ve-ahl ah-vah-DAHV yeet-neh-KHAHM

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-tsah-BEY hah-goh-YEEM KEH-sehf ve-zah-HAHV mah-ah-SEH ye-DEY ah-DAHM

### 135:16

**Pronunciation draft:** peh lah-HEHM ve-LOH ye-dah-BEH-roo ey-NAH-yeem lah-HEHM ve-LOH yeer-OO

### 135:17

**Pronunciation draft:** ohz-NAH-yeem lah-HEHM ve-LOH yah-ah-ZEE-noo AHF eyn yehsh ROO-ahkh be-fee-HEHM

### 135:18

כְּמוֹהֶם יִהְיוּ עֹשֵׂיהֶם כֹּל אֲשֶׁר־בֹּטֵחַ בָּהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-moh-HEHM yeeh-YOO oh-sey-HEHM KOHL ah-shehr boh-TEH-ahkh bah-HEHM

### 135:19

בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּרֲכוּ אֶת־יְהוָה בֵּית אַהֲרֹן בָּרֲכוּ אֶת־יְהוָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** BEYT hah-leh-VEE bah-rah-KHOO eht ah-doh-NAI yee-re-EY ah-doh-NAI bah-rah-KHOO eht ah-doh-NAI

### 135:21

## Psalm 136 — Enduring lovingkindness

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.136) · Buckets: gratitude, provision

### 136:1

### 139:23

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-ke-REH-nee EHL ve-DAH le-vah-VEE be-khah-NEH-nee ve-DAH sahr-ah-PAHY

Search me, O God, and know my heart, Try me, and know my thoughts;

### 139:24

וּרְאֵה אִם־דֶּרֶךְ־עֹצֶב בִּי וּנְחֵנִי בְּדֶרֶךְ עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-re-EH eem deh-rehkh OH-tsehv BEE oo-ne-KHEH-nee be-DEH-rehkh oh-LAHM

And see if there be any way in me that is grievous, And lead me in the way everlasting.

## Psalm 140 — Protection from violence and harmful plans

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.140) · Buckets: protection, justice

### 140:1

לַמְנַצֵּחַ מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lahm-nah-TSEH-ahkh meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED

For the Leader. A Psalm of David.

### 140:2

חַלְּצֵנִי יְהוָה מֵאָדָם רָע מֵאִישׁ חֲמָסִים תִּנְצְרֵנִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-le-TSEH-nee ah-doh-NAI meh-ah-DAHM RAH meh-EESH khah-mah-SEEM teen-tse-REH-nee

Deliver me, O LORD, from the evil man; Preserve me from the violent man;

### 140:3

אֲשֶׁר חָשְׁבוּ רָעוֹת בְּלֵב כָּל־יוֹם יָגוּרוּ מִלְחָמוֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR khah-she-VOO rah-OHT be-LEHV kohl YOHM yah-GOO-roo meel-khah-MOHT

Who devise evil things in their heart; Every day do they stir up wars.

### 140:4

שָׁנֲנוּ לְשׁוֹנָם כְּמוֹ־נָחָשׁ חֲמַת עַכְשׁוּב תַּחַת שְׂפָתֵימוֹ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-nah-NOO le-shoh-NAHM ke-moh nah-KHAHSH khah-MAHT ahkh-SHOOV TAH-khaht se-fah-TEY-moh SEH-lah

They have sharpened their tongue like a serpent; Vipers' venom is under their lips. Selah

### 140:5

שָׁמְרֵנִי יְהוָה מִידֵי רָשָׁע מֵאִישׁ חֲמָסִים תִּנְצְרֵנִי אֲשֶׁר חָשְׁבוּ לִדְחוֹת פְּעָמָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-me-REH-nee ah-doh-NAI  mee-DEY rah-SHAH meh-EESH khah-mah-SEEM teen-tse-REH-nee ah-SHEHR khah-she-VOO leed-KHOHT pe-ah-MAHY

Keep me, O LORD, from the hands of the wicked; Preserve me from the violent man; Who have purposed to make my steps slip.

### 140:6

טָמְנוּ־גֵאִים פַּח לִי וַחֲבָלִים פָּרְשׂוּ רֶשֶׁת לְיַד־מַעְגָּל מֹקְשִׁים שָׁתוּ־לִי סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tah-me-noo geh-EEM  PAHKH LEE vah-khah-vah-LEEM PAH-re-soo reh-SHEHT le-yahd mah-GAHL moh-ke-SHEEM shah-too LEE SEH-lah

The proud have hid a snare for me, and cords; They have spread a net by the wayside; They have set gins for me. Selah

### 140:7

אָמַרְתִּי לַיהוָה אֵלִי אָתָּה הַאֲזִינָה יְהוָה קוֹל תַּחֲנוּנָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-MAHR-tee lah-ah-doh-NAI EH-lee AH-tah hah-ah-ZEE-nah ah-doh-NAI KOHL tah-khah-noo-NAHY

I have said unto the LORD: 'Thou art my God'; Give ear, O LORD, unto the voice of my supplications.

### 140:8

יְהֹוִה אֲדֹנָי עֹז יְשׁוּעָתִי סַכֹּתָה לְרֹאשִׁי בְּיוֹם נָשֶׁק׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM ah-doh-NAHY OHZ ye-shoo-ah-TEE sah-KOH-tah le-roh-SHEE be-YOHM NAH-shehk

O GOD the Lord, the strength of my salvation, Who hast screened my head in the day of battle,

### 140:9

אַל־תִּתֵּן יְהוָה מַאֲוַיֵּי רָשָׁע זְמָמוֹ אַל־תָּפֵק יָרוּמוּ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl tee-TEHN ah-doh-NAI mah-ah-vah-YEY rah-SHAH ze-mah-MOH ahl tah-FEHK yah-ROO-moo SEH-lah

Grant not, O LORD, the desires of the wicked; Further not his evil device, so that they exalt themselves. Selah

### 140:10

רֹאשׁ מְסִבָּי עֲמַל שְׂפָתֵימוֹ יכסומו [יְכַסֵּמוֹ׃]

**Pronunciation draft:** ROHSH me-see-BAHY ah-MAHL se-fah-TEY-moh ye-khah-SEH-moh

As for the head of those that compass me about, Let the mischief of their own lips cover them.

### 140:11

ימיטו [יִמּוֹטוּ] עֲלֵיהֶם גֶּחָלִים בָּאֵשׁ יַפִּלֵם בְּמַהֲמֹרוֹת בַּל־יָקוּמוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-MOH-too ah-ley-HEHM geh-khah-LEEM bah-EHSH yah-pee-LEHM be-mah-hah-moh-ROHT bahl yah-KOO-moo

### 140:12

אִישׁ לָשׁוֹן בַּל־יִכּוֹן בָּאָרֶץ אִישׁ־חָמָס רָע יְצוּדֶנּוּ לְמַדְחֵפֹת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** EESH lah-SHOHN bahl yee-KOHN bah-AH-rehts eesh khah-MAHS RAH ye-tsoo-DEH-noo le-mahd-kheh-FOHT

A slanderer shall not be established in the earth; The violent and evil man shall be hunted with thrust upon thrust.

### 140:13

ידעת [יָדַעְתִּי] כִּי־יַעֲשֶׂה יְהוָה דִּין עָנִי מִשְׁפַּט אֶבְיֹנִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-DAH-tee kee yah-ah-SEH ah-doh-NAI DEEN ah-NEE meesh-PAHT ehv-yoh-NEEM

I know that the LORD will maintain the cause of the poor, And the right of the needy.

Reading note: written ידעת → read יָדַ֗עְתִּי. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 140:14

אַךְ צַדִּיקִים יוֹדוּ לִשְׁמֶךָ יֵשְׁבוּ יְשָׁרִים אֶת־פָּנֶיךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** AHKH tsah-dee-KEEM yoh-DOO leesh-MEH-khah yeh-she-VOO ye-shah-REEM eht pah-NEY-khah

## Psalm 141 — Guarding speech and conduct

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.141) · Buckets: relationships, repentance

### 141:1

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד יְהוָה קְרָאתִיךָ חוּשָׁה לִּי הַאֲזִינָה קוֹלִי בְּקָרְאִי־לָךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED ah-doh-NAI ke-rah-tee-KHAH KHOO-shah LEE hah-ah-ZEE-nah koh-LEE be-kah-re-ee LAHKH

A Psalm of David. LORD, I have called Thee; make haste unto me; Give ear unto my voice, when I call unto Thee.

### 141:2

תִּכּוֹן תְּפִלָּתִי קְטֹרֶת לְפָנֶיךָ מַשְׂאַת כַּפַּי מִנְחַת־עָרֶב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tee-KOHN te-fee-lah-TEE ke-TOH-reht le-fah-NEY-khah mah-se-AHT kah-PAHY meen-khaht AH-rehv

Let my prayer be set forth as incense before Thee, The lifting up of my hands as the evening sacrifice.

### 141:3

שִׁיתָה יְהוָה שָׁמְרָה לְפִי נִצְּרָה עַל־דַּל שְׂפָתָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shee-TAH ah-doh-NAI shah-me-RAH le-FEE nee-tse-RAH ahl DAHL se-fah-TAHY

Set a guard, O LORD, to my mouth; Keep watch at the door of my lips.

### 141:4

אַל־תַּט־לִבִּי לְדָבָר רָע לְהִתְעוֹלֵל עֲלִלוֹת בְּרֶשַׁע אֶת־אִישִׁים פֹּעֲלֵי־אָוֶן וּבַל־אֶלְחַם בְּמַנְעַמֵּיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahl taht lee-BEE le-dah-VAHR  RAH le-heet-oh-LEHL ah-lee-LOHT  be-REH-shah eht ee-SHEEM poh-ah-ley AH-vehn oo-vahl ehl-KHAHM be-mahn-ah-mey-HEHM

Incline not my heart to any evil thing, To be occupied in deeds of wickedness With men that work iniquity; And let me not eat of their dainties.

### 141:5

יֶהֶלְמֵנִי־צַדִּיק חֶסֶד וְיוֹכִיחֵנִי שֶׁמֶן רֹאשׁ אַל־יָנִי רֹאשִׁי כִּי־עוֹד וּתְפִלָּתִי בְּרָעוֹתֵיהֶם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yeh-hehl-meh-nee tsah-DEEK  KHEH-sehd ve-yoh-khee-KHEH-nee SHEH-mehn ROHSH ahl yah-NEE roh-SHEE kee OHD oo-te-fee-lah-TEE be-rah-oh-tey-HEHM

Let the righteous smite me in kindness, and correct me; Oil so choice let not my head refuse; For still is my prayer because of their wickedness.

### 141:6

נִשְׁמְטוּ בִידֵי־סֶלַע שֹׁפְטֵיהֶם וְשָׁמְעוּ אֲמָרַי כִּי נָעֵמוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** neesh-me-TOO vee-dey seh-LAH shoh-fe-tey-HEHM ve-shah-me-OO ah-mah-RAHY KEE nah-EH-moo

Their judges are thrown down by the sides of the rock; And they shall hear my words, that they are sweet.

### 141:7

כְּמוֹ פֹלֵחַ וּבֹקֵעַ בָּאָרֶץ נִפְזְרוּ עֲצָמֵינוּ לְפִי שְׁאוֹל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-MOH foh-LEH-ahkh oo-voh-KEH-ah bah-AH-rehts neef-ze-ROO ah-tsah-MEY-noo le-FEE she-OHL

As when one cleaveth and breaketh up the earth, Our bones are scattered at the grave's mouth.

### 141:8

כִּי אֵלֶיךָ יְהֹוִה אֲדֹנָי עֵינָי בְּכָה חָסִיתִי אַל־תְּעַר נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE eh-LEY-khah  eh-loh-HEEM ah-doh-NAHY ey-NAHY be-KHAH khah-SEE-tee ahl te-AHR nahf-SHEE

For mine eyes are unto Thee, O GOD the Lord; In Thee have I taken refuge, O pour not out my soul.

### 141:9

שָׁמְרֵנִי מִידֵי פַח יָקְשׁוּ לִי וּמֹקְשׁוֹת פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** shah-me-REH-nee MEE-dey FAHKH YAH-ke-shoo LEE oo-moh-ke-SHOHT POH-ah-ley AH-vehn

Keep me from the snare which they have laid for me, And from the gins of the workers of iniquity.

### 141:10

יִפְּלוּ בְמַכְמֹרָיו רְשָׁעִים יַחַד אָנֹכִי עַד־אֶעֱבוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yee-pe-LOO ve-mahkh-moh-RAHV re-shah-EEM YAH-khahd ah-noh-KHEE ahd eh-eh-VOHR

Let the wicked fall into their own nets, Whilst I withal escape.

## Psalm 142 — Loneliness and an appeal for refuge

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.142) · Buckets: wellness, protection, health

### 142:1

מַשְׂכִּיל לְדָוִד בִּהְיוֹתוֹ בַמְּעָרָה תְפִלָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mahs-KEEL le-dah-VEED beeh-yoh-TOH vah-me-ah-RAH te-fee-LAH

Maschil of David, when he was in the cave; a Prayer.

### 142:2

קוֹלִי אֶל־יְהוָה אֶזְעָק קוֹלִי אֶל־יְהוָה אֶתְחַנָּן׃

**Pronunciation draft:** koh-LEE ehl ah-doh-NAI ehz-AHK koh-LEE ehl ah-doh-NAI eht-khah-NAHN

With my voice I cry unto the LORD; With my voice I make supplication unto the LORD.

### 142:3

אֶשְׁפֹּךְ לְפָנָיו שִׂיחִי צָרָתִי לְפָנָיו אַגִּיד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ehsh-POHKH le-fah-NAHV see-KHEE tsah-rah-TEE le-fah-NAHV ah-GEED

I pour out my complaint before Him, I declare before Him my trouble;

### 142:4

בְּהִתְעַטֵּף עָלַי רוּחִי וְאַתָּה יָדַעְתָּ נְתִיבָתִי בְּאֹרַח־זוּ אֲהַלֵּךְ טָמְנוּ פַח לִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-heet-ah-TEHF ah-LAHY  roo-KHEE ve-ah-TAH yah-DAH-tah ne-tee-vah-TEE be-oh-rahkh ZOO ah-hah-LEHKH tah-me-NOO FAHKH LEE

When my spirit fainteth within me— Thou knowest my path— In the way wherein I walk Have they hidden a snare for me.

### 142:5

הַבֵּיט יָמִין וּרְאֵה וְאֵין־לִי מַכִּיר אָבַד מָנוֹס מִמֶּנִּי אֵין דּוֹרֵשׁ לְנַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-BEYT yah-MEEN  oo-re-EH ve-eyn LEE mah-KEER ah-VAHD mah-NOHS mee-MEH-nee EYN doh-REHSH le-nahf-SHEE

Look on my right hand, and see, For there is no man that knoweth me; I have no way to flee; No man careth for my soul.

### 142:6

זָעַקְתִּי אֵלֶיךָ יְהוָה אָמַרְתִּי אַתָּה מַחְסִי חֶלְקִי בְּאֶרֶץ הַחַיִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-AHK-tee eh-LEY-khah ah-doh-NAI ah-mahr-TEE ah-TAH mahkh-SEE khehl-KEE be-EH-rehts HAH-khah-yeem

I have cried unto Thee, O LORD; I have said: 'Thou art my refuge, My portion in the land of the living.'

### 142:7

הַקְשִׁיבָה אֶל־רִנָּתִי כִּי־דַלּוֹתִי מְאֹד הַצִּילֵנִי מֵרֹדְפַי כִּי אָמְצוּ מִמֶּנִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hahk-SHEE-vah  ehl ree-nah-TEE kee dah-LOH-TEE me-OHD hah-tsee-LEH-nee meh-roh-de-FAHY KEE ah-me-TSOO mee-MEH-nee

Attend unto my cry; For I am brought very low; Deliver me from my persecutors; For they are too strong for me.

### 142:8

הוֹצִיאָה מִמַּסְגֵּר נַפְשִׁי לְהוֹדוֹת אֶת־שְׁמֶךָ בִּי יַכְתִּרוּ צַדִּיקִים כִּי תִגְמֹל עָלָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hoh-TSEE-ah mee-mahs-GEHR  nahf-SHEE le-hoh-DOHT eht she-MEH-khah BEE yahkh-TEE-roo tsah-dee-KEEM KEE teeg-MOHL ah-LAHY

Bring my soul out of prison, That I may give thanks unto Thy name; The righteous shall crown themselves because of me; For Thou wilt deal bountifully with me.

## Psalm 143 — Mercy, direction, and renewal

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.143) · Buckets: wisdom, wellness, health

### 143:1

מִזְמוֹר לְדָוִד יְהוָה שְׁמַע תְּפִלָּתִי הַאֲזִינָה אֶל־תַּחֲנוּנַי בֶּאֱמֻנָתְךָ עֲנֵנִי בְּצִדְקָתֶךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** meez-MOHR le-dah-VEED ah-doh-NAI  she-MAH te-fee-lah-TEE hah-ah-ZEE-nah ehl tah-khah-noo-NAHY beh-eh-moo-nah-te-KHAH ah-NEH-nee be-tseed-kah-TEH-khah

A Psalm of David. O LORD, hear my prayer, give ear to my supplications; In Thy faithfulness answer me, and in Thy righteousness.

### 143:2

וְאַל־תָּבוֹא בְמִשְׁפָּט אֶת־עַבְדֶּךָ כִּי לֹא־יִצְדַּק לְפָנֶיךָ כָל־חָי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ve-ahl tah-VOH ve-meesh-PAHT eht ahv-DEH-khah KEE loh yeets-DAHK le-fah-NEY-khah khohl KHAHY

And enter not into judgment with Thy servant; For in Thy sight shall no man living be justified.

### 143:3

כִּי רָדַף אוֹיֵב נַפְשִׁי דִּכָּא לָאָרֶץ חַיָּתִי הוֹשִׁיבַנִי בְמַחֲשַׁכִּים כְּמֵתֵי עוֹלָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KEE rah-DAHF oh-YEHV  nahf-SHEE dee-KAH lah-ah-REHTS khah-yah-TEE hoh-shee-VAH-nee ve-mah-khah-shah-KEEM ke-meh-TEY oh-LAHM

For the enemy hath persecuted my soul; He hath crushed my life down to the ground; He hath made me to dwell in darkness, as those that have been long dead.

### 143:4

וַתִּתְעַטֵּף עָלַי רוּחִי בְּתוֹכִי יִשְׁתּוֹמֵם לִבִּי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** vah-teet-ah-TEHF ah-LAHY roo-KHEE be-toh-KHEE yeesh-toh-MEHM lee-BEE

And my spirit fainteth within me; My heart within me is appalled.

### 143:5

זָכַרְתִּי יָמִים מִקֶּדֶם הָגִיתִי בְכָל־פָּעֳלֶךָ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיךָ אֲשׂוֹחֵחַ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** zah-KHAHR-tee yah-MEEM  mee-KEH-dehm hah-GEE-tee ve-khohl poh-oh-LEH-khah be-mah-ah-SEH yah-DEY-khah ah-soh-KHEH-ahkh

I remember the days of old; I meditate on all Thy doing; I muse on the work of Thy hands.

### 143:6

פֵּרַשְׂתִּי יָדַי אֵלֶיךָ נַפְשִׁי כְּאֶרֶץ־עֲיֵפָה לְךָ סֶלָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** peh-RAHS-tee yah-DAHY eh-LEY-khah nahf-SHEE  ke-eh-rehts ah-yeh-FAH le-KHAH SEH-lah

I spread forth my hands unto Thee; My soul [thirsteth] after Thee, as a weary land. Selah

### 143:7

מַהֵר עֲנֵנִי יְהוָה כָּלְתָה רוּחִי אַל־תַּסְתֵּר פָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי וְנִמְשַׁלְתִּי עִם־יֹרְדֵי בוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** mah-HEHR ah-NEH-nee  ah-doh-NAI kah-le-TAH roo-KHEE ahl tahs-TEHR pah-NEY-khah mee-MEH-nee ve-neem-SHAHL-tee eem YOH-re-dey VOHR

Answer me speedily, O LORD, My spirit faileth; Hide not Thy face from me; Lest I become like them that go down into the pit.

### 143:8

הַשְׁמִיעֵנִי בַבֹּקֶר חַסְדֶּךָ כִּי־בְךָ בָטָחְתִּי הוֹדִיעֵנִי דֶּרֶךְ־זוּ אֵלֵךְ כִּי־אֵלֶיךָ נָשָׂאתִי נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hahsh-mee-EH-nee vah-BOH-kehr  khahs-deh-KHAH kee ve-KHAH vah-TAH-khe-tee hoh-dee-EH-nee deh-rehkh ZOO eh-LEHKH kee eh-ley-KHAH nah-SAH-tee nahf-SHEE

Cause me to hear Thy lovingkindness in the morning, For in Thee do I trust; Cause me to know the way wherein I should walk, For unto Thee have I lifted up my soul.

### 143:9

הַצִּילֵנִי מֵאֹיְבַי יְהוָה אֵלֶיךָ כִסִּתִי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-tsee-LEH-nee meh-oh-ye-VAHY  ah-doh-NAI eh-LEY-khah khee-SEE-tee

Deliver me from mine enemies, O LORD; With Thee have I hidden myself.

### 143:10

לַמְּדֵנִי לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹנֶךָ כִּי־אַתָּה אֱלוֹהָי רוּחֲךָ טוֹבָה תַּנְחֵנִי בְּאֶרֶץ מִישׁוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-me-DEH-nee  lah-ah-SOHT re-tsoh-neh-KHAH kee ah-TAH eh-loh-HAHY roo-khah-KHAH toh-VAH tahn-KHEH-nee be-EH-rehts mee-SHOHR

Teach me to do Thy will, For Thou art my God; Let Thy good spirit Lead me in an even land.

### 143:11

לְמַעַן־שִׁמְךָ יְהוָה תְּחַיֵּנִי בְּצִדְקָתְךָ תוֹצִיא מִצָּרָה נַפְשִׁי׃

**Pronunciation draft:** le-mah-ahn sheem-KHAH ah-doh-NAI te-khah-YEH-nee be-tseed-kah-te-KHAH  toh-TSEE mee-tsah-RAH nahf-SHEE

For Thy name's sake, O LORD, quicken me; In Thy righteousness bring my soul out of trouble.

### 143:12

וּבְחַסְדְּךָ תַּצְמִית אֹיְבָי וְהַאֲבַדְתָּ כָּל־צֹרֲרֵי נַפְשִׁי כִּי אֲנִי עַבְדֶּךָ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** oo-ve-khahs-de-KHAH tahts-MEET oh-ye-VAHY ve-hah-ah-vahd-TAH kohl tsoh-rah-REY nahf-SHEE KEE ah-NEE ahv-DEH-khah

And in Thy mercy cut off mine enemies, And destroy all them that harass my soul; For I am Thy servant.

## Psalm 144 — Rescue, fruitful households, and communal well-being

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.144) · Buckets: family, provision, peace

### 144:1

לְדָוִד בָּרוּךְ יְהוָה צוּרִי הַמְלַמֵּד יָדַי לַקְרָב אֶצְבְּעוֹתַי לַמִּלְחָמָה׃

[A Psalm] of David. Blessed be the LORD my Rock, Who traineth my hands for war, And my fingers for battle;

### 144:2

חַסְדִּי וּמְצוּדָתִי מִשְׂגַּבִּי וּמְפַלְטִי לִי מָגִנִּי וּבוֹ חָסִיתִי הָרוֹדֵד עַמִּי תַחְתָּי׃

My lovingkindness, and my fortress, My high tower, and my deliverer; My shield, and He in whom I take refuge; Who subdueth my people under me.

### 144:3

יְהוָה מָה־אָדָם וַתֵּדָעֵהוּ בֶּן־אֱנוֹשׁ וַתְּחַשְּׁבֵהוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI mah ah-DAHM vah-teh-dah-EH-hoo behn eh-NOHSH vah-te-khah-she-VEH-hoo

LORD, what is man, that Thou takest knowledge of him? Or the son of man, that Thou makest account of him?

### 144:4

אָדָם לַהֶבֶל דָּמָה יָמָיו כְּצֵל עוֹבֵר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-DAHM lah-HEH-vehl dah-MAH yah-MAHV ke-TSEHL oh-VEHR

Man is like unto a breath; His days are as a shadow that passeth away.

### 144:5

יְהוָה הַט־שָׁמֶיךָ וְתֵרֵד גַּע בֶּהָרִים וְיֶעֱשָׁנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-doh-NAI haht shah-MEY-khah ve-teh-REHD GAH beh-hah-REEM ve-yeh-eh-SHAH-noo

O LORD, bow Thy heavens, and come down; Touch the mountains, that they may smoke.

### 144:6

בְּרוֹק בָּרָק וּתְפִיצֵם שְׁלַח חִצֶּיךָ וּתְהֻמֵּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-ROHK bah-RAHK oo-te-fee-TSEHM she-LAHKH khee-TSEH-khah oo-te-hoo-MEHM

Cast forth lightning, and scatter them; Send out Thine arrows, and discomfit them.

### 144:7

שְׁלַח יָדֶיךָ מִמָּרוֹם פְּצֵנִי וְהַצִּילֵנִי מִמַּיִם רַבִּים מִיַּד בְּנֵי נֵכָר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** she-LAHKH yah-DEY-khah mee-mah-ROHM pe-TSEH-nee ve-hah-tsee-leh-NEE mee-MAH-yeem rah-BEEM mee-YAHD be-NEY neh-KHAHR

Stretch forth Thy hands from on high; Rescue me, and deliver me out of many waters, Out of the hand of strangers;

### 144:8

אֲשֶׁר פִּיהֶם דִּבֶּר־שָׁוְא וִימִינָם יְמִין שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR pee-HEHM dee-behr SHAHV vee-mee-NAHM ye-MEEN SHAH-kehr

Whose mouth speaketh falsehood, And their right hand is a right hand of lying.

### 144:9

אֱ‍לֹהִים שִׁיר חָדָשׁ אָשִׁירָה לָּךְ בְּנֵבֶל עָשׂוֹר אֲזַמְּרָה־לָּךְ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** eh-loh-HEEM‍ SHEER khah-DAHSH ah-SHEE-rah LAHKH be-NEH-vehl ah-SOHR ah-zah-me-rah LAHKH

O God, I will sing a new song unto Thee, Upon a psaltery of ten strings will I sing praises unto Thee;

### 144:10

הַנּוֹתֵן תְּשׁוּעָה לַמְּלָכִים הַפּוֹצֶה אֶת־דָּוִד עַבְדּוֹ מֵחֶרֶב רָעָה׃

Who givest salvation unto kings, Who rescuest David Thy servant from the hurtful sword.

### 144:11

פְּצֵנִי וְהַצִּילֵנִי מִיַּד בְּנֵי־נֵכָר אֲשֶׁר פִּיהֶם דִּבֶּר־שָׁוְא וִימִינָם יְמִין שָׁקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** pe-TSEH-nee ve-hah-tsee-leh-NEE mee-YAHD be-ney neh-KHAHR ah-SHEHR pee-HEHM dee-behr SHAHV vee-mee-NAHM ye-MEEN SHAH-kehr

Rescue me, and deliver me out of the hand of strangers, Whose mouth speaketh falsehood, And their right hand is a right hand of lying.

### 144:12

אֲשֶׁר בָּנֵינוּ כִּנְטִעִים מְגֻדָּלִים בִּנְעוּרֵיהֶם בְּנוֹתֵינוּ כְזָוִיֹּת מְחֻטָּבוֹת תַּבְנִית הֵיכָל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ah-SHEHR bah-NEY-noo  keen-tee-EEM me-goo-dah-LEEM bee-ne-oo-rey-HEHM be-noh-TEY-noo khe-zah-vee-YOHT me-khoo-tah-VOHT tahv-NEET hey-KHAHL

We whose sons are as plants grown up in their youth; Whose daughters are as corner-pillars carved after the fashion of a palace;

### 144:13

מְזָוֵינוּ מְלֵאִים מְפִיקִים מִזַּן אֶל־זַן צֹאונֵנוּ מַאֲלִיפוֹת מְרֻבָּבוֹת בְּחוּצוֹתֵינוּ׃

Whose garners are full, affording all manner of store; Whose sheep increase by thousands and ten thousands in our fields;

### 144:14

אַלּוּפֵינוּ מְסֻבָּלִים אֵין־פֶּרֶץ וְאֵין יוֹצֵאת וְאֵין צְוָחָה בִּרְחֹבֹתֵינוּ׃

Whose oxen are well laden; With no breach, and no going forth, And no outcry in our broad places;

### 144:15

אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶׁכָּכָה לּוֹ אַשְׁרֵי הָעָם שֶׁיֲהוָה אֱלֹהָיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ahsh-REY hah-AHM sheh-KAH-khah LOH ah-she-REY hah-AHM sheh-ah-doh-NAI eh-loh-HAHV

## Psalm 145 — Praise, compassion, and sustenance

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.145) · Buckets: gratitude, provision

### 145:1

תְּהִלָּה לְדָוִד אֲרוֹמִמְךָ אֱלוֹהַי הַמֶּלֶךְ וַאֲבָרֲכָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** te-hee-LAH le-dah-VEED ah-roh-meem-KHAH eh-loh-HAHY hah-MEH-lehkh vah-ah-vah-rah-KHAH sheem-KHAH le-oh-LAHM vah-EHD

[A Psalm of] praise; of David. I will extol Thee, my God, O King; And I will bless Thy name for ever and ever.

### 145:2

בְּכָל־יוֹם אֲבָרֲכֶךָּ וַאֲהַלְלָה שִׁמְךָ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** be-khohl YOHM ah-vah-rah-KHEH-kah vah-ah-hah-le-LAH sheem-KHAH le-oh-LAHM vah-EHD

Every day will I bless Thee; And I will praise Thy name for ever and ever.

### 145:3

גָּדוֹל יְהוָה וּמְהֻלָּל מְאֹד וְלִגְדֻלָּתוֹ אֵין חֵקֶר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** gah-DOHL ah-doh-NAI oo-me-hoo-LAHL me-OHD ve-leeg-doo-lah-TOH EYN KHEH-kehr

Great is the LORD, and highly to be praised; And His greatness is unsearchable.

### 145:4

דּוֹר לְדוֹר יְשַׁבַּח מַעֲשֶׂיךָ וּגְבוּרֹתֶיךָ יַגִּידוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** DOHR le-DOHR ye-shah-BAHKH mah-ah-SEY-khah oo-ge-VOO-roh-TEY-khah yah-GEE-doo

One generation shall laud Thy works to another, And shall declare Thy mighty acts.

### 145:5

הֲדַר כְּבוֹד הוֹדֶךָ וְדִבְרֵי נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ אָשִׂיחָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-DAHR ke-VOHD hoh-DEH-khah ve-deev-REY neef-le-oh-TEY-khah ah-SEE-khah

The glorious splendour of Thy majesty, And Thy wondrous works, will I rehearse.

### 145:6

וֶעֱזוּז נוֹרְאֹתֶיךָ יֹאמֵרוּ וגדולתיך [וּגְדוּלָּתְךָ] אֲסַפְּרֶנָּה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** veh-eh-ZOOZ noh-re-oh-TEY-khah yoh-MEH-roo oo-ge-doo-lah-te-KHAH ah-sah-pe-REH-nah

And men shall speak of the might of Thy tremendous acts; And I will tell of Thy greatness.

Reading note: written וגדולתיך → read וּגְדוּלָּתְךָ֥. Original source spelling above is unchanged.

### 145:7

זֵכֶר רַב־טוּבְךָ יַבִּיעוּ וְצִדְקָתְךָ יְרַנֵּנוּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ZEH-khehr rahv too-ve-KHAH yah-BEE-oo ve-tseed-kah-te-KHAH ye-rah-NEH-noo

They shall utter the fame of Thy great goodness, And shall sing of Thy righteousness.

### 145:8

חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם יְהוָה אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם וּגְדָל־חָסֶד׃

**Pronunciation draft:** khah-NOON ve-rah-KHOOM ah-doh-NAI EH-rehkh ah-PAH-yeem oo-ge-dahl KHAH-sehd

The LORD is gracious, and full of compassion; Slow to anger, and of great mercy.

### 145:9

טוֹב־יְהוָה לַכֹּל וְרַחֲמָיו עַל־כָּל־מַעֲשָׂיו׃

**Pronunciation draft:** tohv ah-doh-NAI lah-KOHL ve-rah-khah-MAHV ahl kohl mah-ah-SAHV

**Pronunciation draft:** yoh-DOO-khah ah-doh-NAI kohl mah-ah-SEY-khah vah-khah-see-DEY-khah ye-vah-rah-KHOO-khah

All Thy works shall praise Thee, O LORD; And Thy saints shall bless Thee.

### 145:11

**Pronunciation draft:** ke-VOHD mahl-khoo-te-KHAH yoh-MEH-roo oo-ge-voo-rah-te-KHAH ye-dah-BEH-roo

### 145:12

לְהוֹדִיעַ לִבְנֵי הָאָדָם גְּבוּרֹתָיו וּכְבוֹד הֲדַר מַלְכוּתוֹ׃

### 145:13

מַלְכוּתְךָ מַלְכוּת כָּל־עֹלָמִים וּמֶמְשֶׁלְתְּךָ בְּכָל־דּוֹר וָדוֹר׃

Thy kingdom is a kingdom for all ages, And Thy dominion endureth throughout all generations.

### 145:14

סוֹמֵךְ יְהוָה לְכָל־הַנֹּפְלִים וְזוֹקֵף לְכָל־הַכְּפוּפִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** soh-MEHKH ah-doh-NAI le-khohl hah-noh-fe-LEEM ve-zoh-KEHF le-khohl hah-ke-foo-FEEM

### 145:15

עֵינֵי־כֹל אֵלֶיךָ יְשַׂבֵּרוּ וְאַתָּה נוֹתֵן־לָהֶם אֶת־אָכְלָם בְּעִתּוֹ׃

### 145:16

**Pronunciation draft:** poh-TEH-ahkh eht yah-DEH-khah oo-mahs-BEE-ah le-khohl KHAHY rah-TSOHN

### 145:17

**Pronunciation draft:** tsah-DEEK ah-doh-NAI be-khohl de-rah-KHAHV ve-khah-SEED be-khohl mah-ah-SAHV

The LORD is righteous in all His ways, And gracious in all His works.

### 145:18

קָרוֹב יְהוָה לְכָל־קֹרְאָיו לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָאֻהוּ בֶאֱמֶת׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kah-ROHV ah-doh-NAI le-khohl koh-re-AHV le-KHOHL ah-SHEHR yeek-rah-OO-hoo veh-eh-MEHT

And He hath lifted up a horn for His people, A praise for all His saints, Even for the children of Israel, a people near unto Him. Hallelujah.

## Psalm 149 — Communal rejoicing and judgment

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.149) · Buckets: community, justice

### 149:1

הַלְלוּ יָהּ שִׁירוּ לַיהוָה שִׁיר חָדָשׁ תְּהִלָּתוֹ בִּקְהַל חֲסִידִים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HAH-le-loo YAHH  SHEE-roo lah-ah-doh-NAI SHEER khah-DAHSH te-hee-lah-TOH beek-HAHL khah-see-DEEM

Hallelujah. Sing unto the LORD a new song, And His praise in the assembly of the saints.

### 149:2

יִשְׂמַח יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּעֹשָׂיו בְּנֵי־צִיּוֹן יָגִילוּ בְמַלְכָּם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yees-MAHKH yees-rah-EHL be-oh-SAHV be-ney tsee-YOHN yah-GEE-loo ve-mahl-KAHM

Let Israel rejoice in his Maker; Let the children of Zion be joyful in their King.

### 149:3

יְהַלְלוּ שְׁמוֹ בְמָחוֹל בְּתֹף וְכִנּוֹר יְזַמְּרוּ־לוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** ye-hah-le-LOO she-MOH ve-mah-KHOHL be-TOHF ve-khee-NOHR ye-zah-me-roo LOH

Let them praise His name in the dance; Let them sing praises unto Him with the timbrel and harp.

### 149:4

כִּי־רוֹצֶה יְהוָה בְּעַמּוֹ יְפָאֵר עֲנָוִים בִּישׁוּעָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** kee roh-TSEH ah-doh-NAI be-ah-MOH ye-fah-EHR ah-nah-VEEM bee-shoo-AH

For the LORD taketh pleasure in His people; He adorneth the humble with salvation.

### 149:5

יַעְלְזוּ חֲסִידִים בְּכָבוֹד יְרַנְּנוּ עַל־מִשְׁכְּבוֹתָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** yah-le-ZOO khah-see-DEEM be-khah-VOHD ye-rah-ne-NOO ahl meesh-ke-voh-TAHM

Let the saints exult in glory; Let them sing for joy upon their beds.

### 149:6

רוֹמְמוֹת אֵל בִּגְרוֹנָם וְחֶרֶב פִּיפִיּוֹת בְּיָדָם׃

**Pronunciation draft:** roh-me-MOHT EHL beeg-roh-NAHM ve-KHEH-rehv pee-fee-YOHT be-yah-DAHM

Let the high praises of God be in their mouth, And a two-edged sword in their hand;

### 149:7

לַעֲשׂוֹת נְקָמָה בַּגּוֹיִם תּוֹכֵחֹת בַּל־אֻמִּים׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-ah-SOHT ne-kah-MAH bah-goh-YEEM toh-kheh-KHOHT bahl oo-MEEM

To execute vengeance upon the nations, And chastisements upon the peoples;

### 149:8

לֶאְסֹר מַלְכֵיהֶם בְּזִקִּים וְנִכְבְּדֵיהֶם בְּכַבְלֵי בַרְזֶל׃

**Pronunciation draft:** leh-SOHR mahl-khey-HEHM be-zee-KEEM ve-neekh-be-dey-HEHM be-khahv-LEY vahr-ZEHL

To bind their kings with chains, And their nobles with fetters of iron;

### 149:9

לַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶם מִשְׁפָּט כָּתוּב הָדָר הוּא לְכָל־חֲסִידָיו הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** lah-ah-SOHT bah-HEHM  meesh-PAHT kah-TOOV hah-DAHR HOO le-khohl khah-see-DAHV hah-le-loo YAHH

To execute upon them the judgment written; He is the glory of all His saints. Hallelujah.

## Psalm 150 — Praise with instruments and every breath

[Source](https://www.sefaria.org/Psalms.150) · Buckets: gratitude, devotion

### 150:1

הַלְלוּ יָהּ הַלְלוּ־אֵל בְּקָדְשׁוֹ הַלְלוּהוּ בִּרְקִיעַ עֻזּוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** HAH-le-loo YAHH  hah-le-loo EHL be-kohd-SHOH hah-le-LOO-hoo beer-KEE-ah oo-ZOH

Hallelujah. Praise God in His sanctuary; Praise Him in the firmament of His power.

### 150:2

הַלְלוּהוּ בִגְבוּרֹתָיו הַלְלוּהוּ כְּרֹב גֻּדְלוֹ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-LOO-hoo veeg-voo-roh-TAHV hah-le-LOO-hoo ke-ROHV good-LOH

Praise Him for His mighty acts; Praise Him according to His abundant greatness.

### 150:3

הַלְלוּהוּ בְּתֵקַע שׁוֹפָר הַלְלוּהוּ בְּנֵבֶל וְכִנּוֹר׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-loo-HOO be-TEH-kah shoh-FAHR hah-le-LOO-hoo be-NEH-vehl ve-khee-NOHR

Praise Him with the blast of the horn; Praise Him with the psaltery and harp.

### 150:4

הַלְלוּהוּ בְתֹף וּמָחוֹל הַלְלוּהוּ בְּמִנִּים וְעוּגָב׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-loo-HOO ve-TOHF oo-mah-KHOHL hah-le-LOO-hoo be-mee-NEEM ve-oo-GAHV

Praise Him with the timbrel and dance; Praise Him with stringed instruments and the pipe.

### 150:5

הַלְלוּהוּ בְצִלְצְלֵי־שָׁמַע הַלְלוּהוּ בְּצִלְצְלֵי תְרוּעָה׃

**Pronunciation draft:** hah-le-LOO-hoo ve-tseel-tse-ley SHAH-mah hah-le-LOO-hoo be-tseel-tse-LEY te-roo-AH

Praise Him with the loud-sounding cymbals; Praise Him with the clanging cymbals.

### 150:6

כֹּל הַנְּשָׁמָה תְּהַלֵּל יָהּ הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃

**Pronunciation draft:** KOHL hah-ne-shah-MAH te-hah-LEHL YAHH hah-le-loo YAHH

Let every thing that hath breath praise the LORD. Hallelujah.
